Such dual mismatch leads to majority classes dominating the latent space and unknown class samples being overconfidently misclassified, degrading feature discriminability and pseudo-label quality. To address this, we propose a hub-spoke latent geometry, where known classes are uniformly distributed around a central hub and each class forms compact clusters around its prototype, while the hub provides an anchor for a low-evidence region specifically designed for high-uncertainty unknown class samples. Integrated with an evidence-based classifier, this geometry ultimately enhances feature discriminability and uncertainty separation by mitigating majority-class domination through structured feature organization and guiding high-uncertainty unknown class samples toward the hub. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with a maximum improvement of 3.25% across various settings.

</details>


### [102] [AFA-BANDIT: Provably Near-Optimal Online Multi-Feature Classification Under Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07615)
*AbdAlRahman Odeh,Teng-Hui Huang,Hesham El Gamal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将在线主动特征获取（AFA）建模为组合式 Bandits with Knapsacks（BwK）问题，提出低成本搜索的 LP-Chain 算法，并给出改进的遗憾上界，在合成数据上优于现有在线 AFA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 AFA 场景中，现有方法存在两类缺陷：基于深度强化学习的方法缺乏性能保证，而以成本调整奖励为目标的方法无法强制满足全局预算约束。作者希望在带全局预算、组合动作空间和结构化边信息的在线设定下，同时获得理论保证与实用算法。

Method: 将在线 AFA 形式化为组合 BwK 问题，耦合特征获取与预测决策；利用基数感知置信界（cardinality-aware confidence bound）和子集更新结构推导遗憾上界；为规避指数级动作空间，提出 LP-Chain，沿成本感知的特征子集链进行搜索，链长随特征数线性增长。

Result: 在该组合框架下取得优于标准 BwK 的遗憾上界；LP-Chain 在合成数据上性能与完整方法可比，并优于基于 HEDGE 的 BwK 与基于深度强化学习的在线 AFA 基线，且随特征数增加具有更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 在线 AFA 可通过组合 BwK 框架获得理论保证，LP-Chain 以线性搜索空间实现了有效近似，兼顾理论界与实证性能，为带预算约束的在线特征获取提供了新思路。

Abstract: Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) is a classification problem in which an agent decides which costly features to acquire before predicting each sample's label. Unlike batch AFA, which trains a fixed policy and classifier offline on fully observed data, online AFA updates its predictor from revealed labels as samples arrive. Existing online methods either use deep reinforcement learning (RL) without performance guarantees or maximize cost-adjusted reward rather than enforce a global budget. We formulate online AFA as a combinatorial Bandits with Knapsacks (BwK) problem that couples acquisition and prediction. Unlike prior bandit-based AFA and classical BwK, our setting has combinatorial complexity, evolving rewards, a global budget, and structured side information. We obtain an improved regret upper bound over standard BwK bounds in this framework, leveraging a cardinality-aware confidence bound and the subset update structure. To avoid an exponentially large action space, we propose \emph{LP-Chain}, a variant that searches a cost-aware chain of feature subsets with a size that grows linearly with the number of features. While the regret upper bound is specific to the combinatorial framework, \emph{LP-Chain} empirically achieves comparable predictive performance. On synthetic data, \emph{LP-Chain} outperforms HEDGE-based BwK and deep RL-based online AFA baselines and scales favorably to more features.

</details>


### [103] [Stateless Language Agents: Scaling Long-Horizon Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07625)
*Qizheng Zhang,Changxiu Ji,Isaac Sun,Yuetai Li,Shubhangi Upasani,Sherry Ruan,Boyuan Ma,Fenglu Hong,Vamsidhar Kamanuru,Yoonho Lee,Yuzhen Mao,Genghan Zhang,Rulin Shao,Qiuyang Mang,Andy Dimnaku,Changran Hu,Radha Poovendran,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无状态语言智能体（SLA）框架，核心是"有状态搜索+无状态智能体"：研究状态由框架持有，每次调用重构角色相关的全新上下文。在软件工程、内核优化、算法设计三类任务、最高十亿token预算下均取得最佳结果，并以减少84%以上token达到最强内核基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究系统长期运行LLM智能体时，更多推理并不等于更多进展：智能体会重复回放增长的历史、做重复工作，或在token持续消耗时停止实验。而现有评测多用短预算或早早饱和的基准，无法暴露这些失效模式。作者将失效根源归因于两个设计选择：研究状态存放在哪里，以及由谁决定下一步尝试什么。

Method: 提出无状态语言智能体（SLA），遵循"有状态搜索、无状态智能体"原则：智能体调用之间不携带对话历史，由框架持有研究状态（候选方案与实测结果），并为每次调用重构全新且角色特定的上下文。在SLA框架中实现：无状态Advisor读取框架汇总的跨搜索方向证据并分配具体实验，并行Worker执行实验。每个智能体所见内容成为显式设计选择，而非随运行增长的历史。

Result: 在软件工程、内核优化、算法设计任务上，以最高十亿token预算与三个近期框架对比：SLA在每个任务上均取得最佳最终结果，并以减少84%以上token达到最强内核基线的最终性能。基于共享检查点的消融显示，聚焦上下文与显式任务分配各自都有贡献，且效果在全流程中可累积；Advisor消耗的token不足0.6%。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用SLA，即将持久研究状态置于智能体对话之外；同时表明短评测周期可能误判研究系统及其组件的真实能力。

Abstract: Automated research systems increasingly run LLM agents over long horizons, but more inference does not by itself produce more progress: agents replay growing histories, duplicate one another's work, or stop experimenting while token consumption continues. Yet most evaluations use short budgets or benchmarks that saturate early, leaving these failure modes untested. We trace these failures to two choices: where research state lives and who decides what to try next. We introduce Stateless Language Agents (SLAs), built on the principle of stateful search with stateless agents: no agent carries its conversation across invocations; instead, the harness owns the research state (candidate solutions and measured outcomes) and reconstructs a fresh and role-specific context for every invocation. What each agent sees becomes an explicit design choice rather than a history that grows with the run. We implement this principle in the SLA framework, where a stateless Advisor reads harness-summarized evidence across search directions and assigns concrete experiments to parallel Workers. We evaluate SLA against three recent frameworks on software engineering, kernel optimization, and algorithm design at budgets of up to one billion tokens. SLA achieves the best final result on every task and reaches the strongest kernel baseline's final performance with over 84% fewer tokens. Ablations from shared checkpoints show that focused contexts and explicit assignments each contribute to SLA's progress, with effects that can compound over full runs, while the Advisor consumes less than 0.6% of tokens. These results argue for SLAs, which keep durable research state out of agent conversations, and show that short evaluation horizons can misjudge research systems and their components.

</details>


### [104] [Complementary Supervised and Self-Supervised Representations for Out-of-Distribution Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07628)
*Qingying Hao,Zikang Chen,Chuxuan Hu,Jinyuan Jia,Bo Li,Gang Wang,Carl Gunter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与骨干网络无关的框架（Co-Train 与 Dual-Space Retrieval），利用自监督表示补充监督表示，以提升图神经网络的 OOD 节点分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在分布外（OOD）泛化上面临挑战，因为图的分布会随时间和领域发生显著变化。监督学习与自监督图表示学习目标不同，可能提供互补的表示视角，因此作者希望探究自监督表示（SSL）能否为监督式 OOD 节点分类提供补充信号。

Method: 提出两种与骨干网络无关的框架：1）Co-Train，在训练阶段联合学习监督表示与自监督表示并自适应地融合；2）Dual-Space Retrieval，在推理阶段于两个表示空间中进行非参数预测，并通过置信度感知融合进行组合。监督编码器与自监督编码器分别参数化，无需共享相同的 GNN 结构。在多个 GNN 骨干与两种自监督目标（DGI、GRACE）上进行验证。

Result: 在涵盖时间与跨域分布偏移的四个图基准上，Co-Train 持续优于强监督 OOD 基线，Dual-Space Retrieval 作为灵活的非参数方案也取得了有竞争力的性能；不同骨干与自监督目标下的结果、表示分析与消融实验均表明自监督表示提供了互补信息。

Conclusion: 自监督表示能够为监督表示提供互补信息，在不同设定下均可提升 OOD 节点分类性能；所提框架具有骨干无关性与灵活性。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains challenging for graph neural networks (GNNs), as graph distributions can vary substantially across time and domains. Supervised and self-supervised graph representation learning are guided by distinct objectives and offer different perspectives on graph representations. In this work, we study whether self-supervised representations (SSL) can provide complementary signals to improve supervised OOD node classification. We develop two backbone-agnostic frameworks that exploit such information at different stages of learning and prediction. Co-Train jointly learns supervised and SSL representations and adaptively integrates them during training, while Dual-Space Retrieval performs non-parametric prediction in the two representation spaces and combines their predictions through confidence-aware fusion at inference time. The supervised and SSL encoders are separately parameterized and need not share the same GNN architecture.
  We evaluate multiple GNN backbones and two distinct SSL objectives, DGI and GRACE, on four graph benchmarks spanning temporal and cross-domain distribution shifts. Extensive experiments show that Co-Train consistently outperforms strong supervised OOD baselines, while Dual-Space Retrieval achieves competitive performance as a flexible non-parametric alternative. Results across different backbones and SSL objectives, together with representation analyses and ablations, demonstrate that SSL representations provide complementary information to supervised representations and can improve OOD node classification across diverse settings.

</details>


### [105] [Does On-Policy Distillation for Safety Pose Backdoor Risks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07654)
*Jian Luo,Kehan Qi,Qingqiao Hu,Meilong Xu,Jiacheng Qiu,Weimin Lyu,Jiawei Zhou,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了 on-policy distillation (OPD) 的安全隐患：一个安全对齐但被植入后门的教师模型可将隐藏的恶意行为传播给原本干净的学生模型，并提出简单缓解方法 Lazy Defense。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将 OPD 视为提升大语言模型安全性的有效工具，但普遍假设教师模型与训练数据是可信的。作者意图揭示这一被忽视的威胁：安全对齐的教师本身可能被植入后门，并在蒸馏过程中将后门传递给干净的学生模型，从而暴露 OPD 的安全风险。

Method: 作者建立了一个威胁模型，假设教师模型安全对齐但含有隐藏后门，通过 on-policy distillation 将能力传递给学生。实验中考察了投毒率、训练轮数（epochs）以及 KL 目标（top-k KL 与 sampled-token KL）对后门迁移的影响，并提出 Lazy Defense：通过裁剪 KL reward 来抑制学生模型的激进更新，从而减缓后门学习。

Result: 在低至 3% 的投毒率下，蒸馏后学生模型上的攻击成功率（ASR）最高可达 70%。增加训练轮数会放大风险：仅使用 10 个中毒样本，训练 16 个 epoch 后 ASR 即达 67%。常用的 top-k KL 会加速后门迁移，在多数设置下使触发条件相关的有害行为比 sampled-token KL 更早出现。Lazy Defense 在低投毒率设置下能延迟后门迁移。

Conclusion: OPD 能够传播后门，说明将 OPD 用作安全对齐工具时存在被滥用的风险。作者呼吁在 OPD 的安全应用中重视并缓解此类后门传播威胁，Lazy Defense 可作为初步缓解手段。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has attracted growing attention as an effective way to transfer capabilities from teacher models to student models. Recent studies further explore OPD as a tool for improving large language model safety with promising results. However, these approaches typically assume that the teacher and training data are trustworthy. In this paper, we uncover an overlooked threat to OPD for safety: a safety-aligned but backdoored teacher can propagate its hidden malicious behavior to an initially clean student. Under our threat model, a poisoning rate as low as 3% results in an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 70% on the distilled student. We further identify two training choices that can amplify this risk. First, increasing the number of training epochs can lead to high ASR even at low poisoning rates. With only 10 poisoned samples, ASR reaches 67% after 16 epochs. Second, the commonly used top-k KL can accelerate backdoor transfer, causing trigger-conditioned harmful behavior to emerge earlier than sampled-token KL in most settings. Alongside these findings, we explore a simple mitigation, Lazy Defense, which clips KL rewards to make student updates less aggressive, limiting aggressive updates and slowing backdoor learning. Experiments show that Lazy Defense delays backdoor transfer in low poisoning rate settings. Together, our findings reveal that OPD can propagate backdoors, highlighting the need to address the safety risks of OPD.

</details>


### [106] [MS-ECG-FM: Towards a More Universal Electrocardiogram Foundation Model for Health Monitoring using Multi-source Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07662)
*Robert A. Lewis,I-Min Chiu,Kyle Verrier,Karthik Jayaraman Raghuram,Francoise Marvel,Salar Abbaspourazad,Anshuman Mishra,Guillermo Sapiro,Andrew C. Miller,Joseph Futoma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-ECG-FM是一种心电图基础模型，通过对多种临床报告（心电图、超声心动图、放射、出院报告）进行对比对齐训练，突破了仅依赖心电报告监督的局限，在扩展的心电检测基准上全面优于现有方法，包括低导联配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心电图基础模型仅以心电图解读报告作为监督信号，而解读报告只涵盖临床医生常规识别的波形信息子集，导致表征学习忽略了心电图中更广泛的诊断信号。

Method: 提出MS-ECG-FM模型，通过对比对齐方式与多种不同类型的临床报告（心电图、超声心动图、放射、出院报告）进行多源对齐训练。

Result: 在扩展的心电检测基准上，MS-ECG-FM全面超越现有方法，覆盖心电图可检测的全部病种范围，包括低导联配置场景；不同报告对不同诊断领域提升表征效果，多源对齐可整合互补信息，在临床多样性任务上产出一致强健的表征。

Conclusion: 多源临床报告的对齐训练能克服单一监督的局限，为心电图基础模型提供跨临床任务的强健表征。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) records the electrical activity of the heart, aiding diagnosis by detecting abnormalities in cardiac function. ECG foundation models have demonstrated promising results, but are limited by a reliance on ECG interpretation reports as their sole supervision. Because interpretation reports only capture the subset of waveform information routinely recognized by clinicians, this constrains representation learning to overlook the broader diagnostic signals present in ECG. We introduce a new ECG foundation model --- MS-ECG-FM --- that is trained through contrastive alignment to multiple distinct clinical note types, including ECG, echocardiography, radiology, and discharge reports. We evaluate MS-ECG-FM on an extended set of ECG detection benchmarks, showing that it comprehensively outperforms existing methods on the full span of conditions that ECG can detect, including in reduced-lead configurations. Different reports improve representations for different diagnostic domains, while multi-source alignment captures their complementary information and produces consistently strong representations across clinically diverse tasks.

</details>


### [107] [Exact-Solution Volume and Length Generalization in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07676)
*Yijia Jessica Zhu,David Chiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化精确解体积（NESV）指标，衡量固定宽度单层 Transformer 在长度 n 下精确解参数区域占比，为四个任务给出渐近界，并据此定位 INDEX 的长度敏感误差来源以改进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 表达性研究只回答"能否求解某任务"，却几乎无法说明所获解能否泛化到更长输入长度。作者希望找到一种能刻画长度泛化难度的定量工具，并借此识别具体的长度敏感来源。

Method: 定义 NESV，即在有界参数区域内对所有长度 n 的输入都实现精确解的参数比例。针对固定宽度、单层、注意力按 log n 缩放的 Transformer，对 FIRST、MAJORITY、INDEX、PARITY 四个任务推导 NESV 的渐近界；并对 INDEX 进行体积分解，识别随 n 增长的误差项，进而设计在结构上消除其中一项的 Transformer 变体。

Result: NESV 渐近界分别为 FIRST：Θ(1)，MAJORITY：Θ(1/(n log n))，INDEX：Θ(1/n^3)，PARITY：0。该结果与既有经验结论一致：精确解体积随长度衰减越快，长度泛化越难。对 INDEX 的体积分析揭示两个随 n 增长的误差源；结构上消除其一的模型将理论界提升至 Θ(n^{-1})，在 10 倍训练长度上测试准确率达 85%，原模型为 60%。

Conclusion: 体积分析可作为识别长度敏感具体来源的有效方法，并能为任务相关的模型改进提供理论指引，从而提升长度泛化能力。

Abstract: Research on transformer expressivity shows whether a transformer is capable of solving a given task, but gives little indication of whether the solution, if learned, is generalizable to longer input lengths. We study this question through normalized exact-solution volume (NESV): the fraction of a bounded parameter region that achieves an exact solution on every input of length $n$. For fixed-width, single-layer transformers with $\log n$-scaled attention, we establish asymptotic bounds on NESV for four tasks: FIRST ($Θ(1)$), MAJORITY ($Θ(1/(n\log n))$), INDEX ($Θ(1/n^3)$), and PARITY ($0$). These results are consistent with previous empirical results: the faster the exact-solution volume decays with input length, the harder it is to length-generalize on that task. Looking deeper into INDEX, our volume analysis reveals two error sources that grow with $n$. Consequently, we study a transformer model that would structurally eliminate one of the terms, theoretically improving the NESV bound to $Θ(n^{-1})$, and empirically achieving 85% accuracy when tested at $10\times$ the training length, compared with the 60% accuracy of the original model. We conclude that volume analysis may be a useful approach to identify concrete sources of length sensitivity and thus provide insights into task-specific model refinements.

</details>


### [108] [Adaptive Model Inversion Attacks Generalize a Privacy-Robustness Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07677)
*Shailen Smith,Rasmus Torp,Adam Breuer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准的高分辨率模型反演攻击（MIA）评估显著低估了训练数据隐私泄露；经简单自适应攻击改动，泄露率提高1.16–6.59倍，且泄露与对抗鲁棒性密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIA评估可能将优化失败与测量失败误当作隐私保护效果，从而低估真实隐私泄露，并掩盖隐私与对抗鲁棒性之间的潜在关系。

Method: 在FaceScrub上对最先进隐私防御、MixUp与对抗训练等标准训练技术及无防御模型施加简单自适应攻击改动，并改变用于评估重建结果的外部分类器的特征基（如对抗训练Inception与标准Inception评估器）。

Result: 自适应攻击改动使训练图像泄露率提高1.16至6.59倍，且自报隐私最强的防御增幅最大；同一重建图像在不同评估器下目标身份识别率不同；调整攻击与评估器后，重建泄露与对抗鲁棒性紧密相关。

Conclusion: 标准MIA评估可能混淆优化与测量失败与隐私；对抗鲁棒性可作为攻击无关的重建脆弱性代理，适用范围比既有理论更广。由此提出开放问题：是否存在一种实用防御能在不付出对抗鲁棒性代价的情况下降低训练数据重建？

Abstract: In this paper, we show that standard evaluations of high-resolution Model Inversion Attacks (MIAs) significantly underestimate training-data privacy leakage. State-of-the-art privacy defenses, standard training techniques such as MixUp and Adversarial Training, and undefended models all leak training images at rates 1.16 to 6.59 times higher on FaceScrub under simple adaptive changes to the attack, with the largest increases among defenses reporting the strongest privacy. We further show that measured leakage depends on the feature basis of the external classifier used to evaluate reconstructions: for the same reconstructed images, an adversarially trained Inception evaluator identifies the targeted identity at different rates than the standard Inception evaluator. Our results suggest that standard MIA evaluation can mistake optimization and measurement failures for privacy.
  These underestimated leakage rates also concealed a broader relationship between privacy and adversarial robustness. Once we adapt the attack and vary the evaluator, reconstruction leakage closely tracks adversarial robustness across recent defenses and standard training regimes, suggesting that robustness provides an attack-agnostic proxy for reconstruction vulnerability that applies far more broadly than previously theorized. This raises an open question: can a practical defense reduce training-data reconstruction without paying a corresponding cost in adversarial robustness?

</details>


### [109] [Improving Synthetic Data Generation for Argument Mining via Adversarial Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07699)
*Zhijun Zhang,Qianlong Wang,Keyang Ding,Genan Dai,Bowen Zhang,Bin Liang,Ruifeng Xu,Yongsheng Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对抗强化学习框架用于论证挖掘（AM）的合成数据生成，通过生成器与判别器的对抗循环联合优化，同时提升生成数据的结构准确性与多样性，在三个基准数据集的全数据与低资源场景下均稳定提升 AM 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证挖掘受限于高质量结构标注数据的稀缺。尽管大语言模型在合成数据生成方面展现出潜力，但生成兼具结构准确性与足够多样性的 AM 合成数据仍是难题。

Method: 从一个新视角重新审视 AM 的合成数据生成，提出一种新颖的对抗强化学习数据合成框架。该框架在对抗循环中联合优化生成器与判别器：生成器产出结构化 AM 实例，判别器通过区分真实数据与合成候选提供学习信号，从而使生成器借助对抗反馈逐步提升论证数据的结构准确性，同时保持多样性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在三个基准数据集上，无论是在全数据还是低资源设置下，均能持续提升 AM 性能，验证了其有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 对抗强化学习框架能够有效缓解 AM 领域结构标注数据稀缺问题，兼顾结构准确性与多样性，并在多种数据条件下展现稳定的性能增益与可扩展性。

Abstract: Argument Mining (AM) is fundamentally constrained by the scarcity of high-quality structure-annotated datasets. While LLMs have shown promise in synthetic data generation, producing synthetic AM data that is both structurally accurate and sufficiently diverse remains a challenging problem. To address this problem, we revisit synthetic data generation for AM from a new perspective and propose a novel adversarial reinforcement learning framework for data synthesis. The proposed framework jointly optimizes the generator and the discriminator in an adversarial loop, in which the generator produces structured AM instances, and the discriminator provides learning signals by distinguishing real data from synthetic candidates. This enables the generator to progressively improve both the structural accuracy of generated argument data while maintaining diversity through adversarial feedback. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves AM performance on three benchmark datasets in both full-data and low-resource settings, validating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [110] [WASD: Wasserstein-based Knowledge Distillation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07706)
*Byeonghu Na,Donghyeok Shin,Yeongmin Kim,Mina Kang,Il-Chul Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WASD，一种基于Wasserstein距离的知识蒸馏方法，利用token嵌入构造代价矩阵引入语义信息，并通过Sinkhorn散度实现高效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大模型能力提升的同时，推理阶段的计算与内存开销显著增加。知识蒸馏可将大教师模型的知识迁移到小学生模型，但现有LLM知识蒸馏方法主要依赖逐词表索引上的概率值差异度量，未显式利用token级语义信息。

Method: 提出Wasserstein知识蒸馏（WASD），采用基于Wasserstein的距离并以token嵌入导出代价矩阵，从而融入token级语义信息。为保证计算可行性，采用Sinkhorn散度并推导梯度等价目标，无需额外网络即可高效优化。

Result: 在多个LLM家族与规模上的实验表明，WASD在指令跟随、数学推理和代码生成等多样任务上一致提升蒸馏性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明token空间中的语义信息对LLM蒸馏中的有效分布对齐至关重要。实现已公开于https://github.com/aailab-kaist/WASD。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language models (LLMs) have rapidly advanced in capability, but their increasing scale comes with substantial computational and memory costs at inference time. Knowledge distillation (KD) offers a practical solution by transferring knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student model via alignment of discrete probability distributions. However, existing KD methods for LLMs primarily rely on divergences that evaluate discrepancies through probability values at each vocabulary index, without explicitly leveraging token-level semantic information. We propose Wasserstein-based knowledge distillation (WASD) for LLMs, which incorporates token-level semantic information via the Wasserstein-based distance with a cost matrix derived from token embeddings. To ensure computational tractability, we adopt the Sinkhorn divergence and derive a gradient-equivalent objective that can be efficiently optimized without introducing additional networks. Experiments across multiple LLM families and scales show that WASD consistently improves distillation performance on diverse tasks, including instruction following, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our results highlight the importance of semantic information encoded in the token space for effective distribution alignment in LLM distillation. The implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/aailab-kaist/WASD .

</details>


### [111] [Neuromotor Hierarchy Network: Physiological Inductive Biases for Robust Generalization in sEMG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07713)
*He Wang,Hongyuan Qi,Zhaoxian Zhang,Jinbin Luo,Linyi He,Mehul Motani,Changsheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neuromotor Hierarchy Network (NHN)，从任务监督中学习紧凑的潜在神经运动状态，实现参数高效的sEMG解码，在emg2pose与emg2qwerty上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sEMG可穿戴非侵入，但群体规模解码困难：sEMG与神经肌肉活动的关系跨用户、跨会话变化，任务相关动态跨通道且涉及多时间尺度；仅从任务标签学习波形到输出的映射，使记录变异与协同运动活动的区分保持隐含。

Method: NHN以神经运动组织为启发构建层次结构：自适应记录统计量同时保留相对强度；时空编码器采用参数高效的通道交互，并用多时间尺度历史调制特征；生成学习到的运动基元的候选激活，经时间积分与连续加权形成潜在神经运动状态。理论分析刻画了核心机制的效率、时间行为与优化特性。

Result: emg2pose连续手部姿态估计：在Regression与Tracking三种泛化划分上，用户平均角度误差较Hadidi等最佳任务特定变体降低0.52%–2.84%，参数减少48.42%–48.51%。emg2qwerty触屏打字识别：beam-search字符错误率较SplashNet-Upscale零样本降低19.40%，微调后降低30.42%，参数减少65.86%。

Conclusion: 生理学引导的潜在神经运动状态推断可支撑参数高效的sEMG解码，验证了以神经运动组织为归纳偏置的层次化架构在跨用户、跨会话与多任务场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) provides a wearable, noninvasive interface to neuromuscular activity for movement decoding and human-computer interaction. Population-scale decoding remains difficult because the relationship between sEMG and neuromuscular activity varies across users and sessions, while task-relevant dynamics span channels and multiple timescales. Learning waveform-to-output mappings from task labels leaves the distinction between recording variability and coordinated motor activity implicit. We introduce the Neuromotor Hierarchy Network (NHN), which learns a compact latent neuromotor state from task supervision to represent task-relevant neuromuscular coordination. NHN constructs this latent state through a hierarchy inspired by neuromotor organization.It adapts recording statistics while preserving relative intensity.Its spatiotemporal encoder uses parameter-efficient channel interactions and modulates features with multi-timescale history. The resulting features yield candidate activations of learned motor primitives, which are temporally integrated and continuously weighted to form the state. Theoretical analysis characterizes the efficiency, temporal behavior, and optimization of NHN's core mechanisms. We evaluate the architecture for both continuous hand-pose estimation on emg2pose and touch-typing recognition on emg2qwerty. On emg2pose, NHN reduces user-averaged angular error by 0.52% to 2.84% across all three generalization splits in both Regression and Tracking relative to Hadidi et al.'s best task-specific variants, using 48.42% to 48.51% fewer parameters. On emg2qwerty, NHN reduces beam-search character error rate by 19.40% zero-shot and 30.42% after fine-tuning relative to SplashNet-Upscale, using 65.86% fewer parameters. Physiology-guided inference of a latent neuromotor state supports parameter-efficient sEMG decoding.

</details>


### [112] [Cite What You Explore: Budget-Aware LLM Reasoning over Medical KGs with Verifiable Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07739)
*Chen Chen,Dongjie Wang,Mei Liu,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BAR框架，通过预算感知的LLM推理在医学知识图谱上实现出院后风险预测，兼顾成本约束、证据质量区分与可引用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中连接出院观测与下游并发症的依赖关系（如共病级联、药物-疾病交互）常缺失，需借助外部医学知识图谱补充；但追踪这些依赖需同时满足三个性质：KG探索成本有界、检索证据按来源质量区分、推理依据可引用以供回溯审查。现有LLM方法无法同时满足。

Method: BAR包含三项贡献：(1) 将原始KG精炼为疾病特异性证据图，边携带支持分数与来源记录，使KG成为质量标注的推理空间；(2) LLM通过"规划-导航-验证"循环在图上游走，将问题分解为步骤，在患者特定预算下检索证据，验证失败时进行修正；(3) 以对比有无获取证据的预测、结合获取成本与引用完整性项构建奖励，训练推理策略。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，覆盖8种疾病与3个预测时间窗，BAR相比最强基线AUPRC提升3.4个百分点，引用精确率从59.8%提升至77.9%，预算消耗仅为上限的62-65%。

Conclusion: BAR验证了预算感知的LLM知识图谱推理可同时实现成本有界、证据质量区分与可引用性，显著提升出院后风险预测性能并保持低成本。

Abstract: Post-discharge risk prediction from electronic health records (EHRs) is difficult because many dependencies that link discharge-time observations to downstream complications, such as comorbidity cascades and drug-disease interactions, are absent from the record. External medical knowledge graphs (KGs) can supply these missing dependencies, but tracing them demands three properties: KG exploration must remain cost-bounded, retrieved evidence must be differentiated by source quality, and the resulting rationale must be citable for retrospective review. Large language models (LLMs) can plan and verify over structured evidence, making them natural candidates for KG reasoning, but existing LLM-based methods do not satisfy these three properties jointly. In this paper, we propose BAR, a Budget-Aware LLM Reasoning framework over medical KGs with three contributions. First, BAR refines the raw KG into disease-specific evidence graphs whose edges carry support scores and provenance records, turning the KG into a quality-annotated reasoning space rather than a static feature source. Second, an LLM then reasons over this graph through a plan-navigate-verify loop that decomposes the question into steps, retrieves evidence under a patient-specific budget, and revises when verification fails. Third, a reasoning policy is trained with a reward that compares predictions with and without acquired evidence, combined with acquisition cost and citation-integrity terms. Across 8 diseases and 3 prediction horizons on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, BAR improves AUPRC by 3.4 points over the strongest baseline, raises citation precision from 59.8% to 77.9%, and consumes only 62-65% of the budget cap.

</details>


### [113] [Adversarially Trained Linear Transformers Are Optimal Robust In-Context Learners for Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07754)
*Soichiro Kumano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗预训练的鲁棒性迁移问题，证明在混合高斯分类任务族上，足够深的线性Transformer经对抗预训练后可借助上下文学习在新任务上渐近达到鲁棒贝叶斯误差，而标准训练模型无法做到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练虽是最可靠的防御手段，但每个新任务都需重新付出高昂计算代价。鲁棒基础模型（一次对抗预训练后轻量适配）提供了替代方案，但一个根本问题尚未解决：预训练阶段获得的鲁棒性能否在不进行额外对抗训练的情况下迁移到未见任务？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 针对一族混合高斯分类任务，采用足够深的线性Transformer，在任务间进行对抗预训练，并通过上下文学习（in-context learning）利用干净示例进行推断。进一步在梯度流框架下分析收敛性，并考察精度—鲁棒性权衡与示例复杂度。

Result: 对抗预训练的线性Transformer可在未见任务上渐近达到鲁棒贝叶斯误差；而标准训练的模型无法做到。此外还给出了梯度流下的收敛性分析、精度—鲁棒性权衡刻画以及示例复杂度结果。

Conclusion: 预训练阶段习得的鲁棒性可以迁移到未见任务而无需额外对抗训练：规模化的对抗预训练单模型即可在新任务上实现最优鲁棒性，这为鲁棒基础模型的可行性提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Adversarial training is one of the most reliable defenses against adversarial attacks, but its high computational cost must generally be paid anew for each task. Robust foundation models offer a promising alternative: adversarially pretrain a model once and then transfer its robustness to downstream tasks through lightweight adaptation. However, a fundamental question remains open: can robustness acquired during pretraining transfer to unseen tasks without further adversarial training? In this study, we answer this question affirmatively. A single model adversarially pretrained at scale can achieve optimal robustness on new tasks without additional task-specific training. Specifically, we show that, for a family of Gaussian-mixture classification tasks, a sufficiently deep linear transformer adversarially trained across tasks can asymptotically attain the robust Bayes error on previously unseen tasks through in-context learning from clean demonstrations. By contrast, a standardly trained model cannot. We further analyze convergence under gradient flow, an accuracy--robustness trade-off, and demonstration complexity.

</details>


### [114] [TRACE: Rollout-Guided Quantization-Aware Training for FP4 Reinforcement Learning of MoE Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07767)
*Xin Wang,Hao Yu,Zhengyang Zhuge,Bochao Mao,Zheng Li,Junda Feng,Yuyan Luo,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Mi Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一个面向MoE大模型RL训练的FP4量化框架，通过rollout引导的量化感知训练对齐训练与rollout两条量化执行路径，并配合量化信息缓存降低开销，在四个大规模MoE模型上实现与BF16 rollout相当的RL性能，同时获得最高5.4倍rollout加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练LLM在rollout生成阶段带来巨大的计算与内存开销，促使研究者采用低精度rollout。但现有FP4 RL方法在训练路径与rollout路径上独立优化量化精度，未直接减小两条量化执行路径之间的差异（train-rollout discrepancy），导致性能受限。

Method: 提出TRACE（Train-Rollout Quantization Alignment via Compact GuidancE）FP4量化框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) rollout引导的量化感知训练（rollout-guided QAT），利用rollout端的量化结果指导训练端的FP4舍入决策，直接缩小训练与rollout的量化差异；(2) 高效的量化信息缓存方案，选择性保留深层网络的尾数（mantissa）与缩放因子（scale）信息，以降低rollout引导带来的存储与通信开销。评估对象为四个大规模MoE语言模型，覆盖推理、代码和长时程RL任务。

Result: TRACE支持FP4权重/激活与FP4 KV-cache联合rollout，RL性能与BF16 rollout相当，rollout加速最高达5.4倍；相比对BF16训练策略做事后FP4量化（post-hoc FP4 quantization），最终FP4性能显著更优。

Conclusion: 通过显式对齐训练与rollout的量化执行路径，并结合量化信息缓存控制额外开销，TRACE在MoE语言模型的RL训练中实现了精度与效率的良好权衡，验证了rollout引导QAT在低精度RL训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for post-training large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial computation and memory overhead during rollout generation, which motivates low-precision rollout for efficient RL training. However, existing FP4 RL methods suffer from a key limitation: they primarily optimize quantization accuracy on the training and rollout paths independently rather than directly reducing the discrepancy between the two quantized execution paths. In this work, we propose TRACE (Train-Rollout Quantization Alignment via Compact GuidancE), an FP4 quantization framework for RL training of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models that addresses the limitation of existing FP4 RL methods. TRACE incorporates rollout-guided quantization-aware training that uses rollout-side quantization outcomes to guide training-side FP4 rounding decisions, directly reducing train-rollout discrepancy. Moreover, TRACE adopts an efficient quantization-information caching scheme that selectively retains mantissa and scale information from deeper layers to reduce the storage and communication overhead introduced by rollout guidance. We evaluate TRACE on four large-scale MoE language models across reasoning, coding, and long-horizon RL tasks. Our results demonstrate that TRACE enables joint FP4 weight/activation and FP4 KV-cache rollout with RL performance comparable to BF16 rollout, while achieving up to 5.4xrollout speedup and strong final FP4 performance compared with post-hoc FP4 quantization of BF16-trained policies.

</details>


### [115] [Towards One-for-All Foundation Model for Attributed Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07778)
*Yunhui Liu,Xudong Jin,Kang Zhang,Danshuo An,Yu Xing,Te Song,Jia Liu,Tieke He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OFAG，一个面向属性图聚类的“一次训练、通用适用”基础模型，无需针对每张图单独训练、微调或调参即可直接推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性图聚类为无监督任务，模型选择与适配本身困难。现有方法通常为每张输入图训练并调参独立模型，流程成本高且脆弱，难以跨特征空间、结构模式和属性-结构关联不同的图迁移。

Method: OFAG 基于 Prior-data Fitted Networks，从潜聚类、节点属性与图结构的广泛先验下生成的合成属性图中学习可复用的聚类推理策略；采用维度无关的信号级图编码器，将每个特征通道视为图信号并建模其共享图滤波器响应；以超球面聚类目标训练，推理时单次前向即得聚类友好的节点表示。

Result: 在十个数据集上，单个冻结的 OFAG 模型在 NMI、ACC、ARI、F1 上取得最佳平均性能与最优平均排名；完成全部十数据集共耗时 12.43 分钟，比最快的次优基线快 6 倍以上，比聚类质量第二的方法快近 28 倍。

Conclusion: OFAG 验证了单一基础模型可免训练、免微调、免调参地直接应用于多样化属性图聚类，代码与预训练权重已开源，便于实际部署。

Abstract: Attributed graph clustering aims to discover node groups by jointly exploiting node attributes and graph topology, yet its unsupervised nature makes model selection and adaptation inherently difficult. Existing methods typically train and tune a separate model for each input graph, leading to costly and fragile pipelines that often fail to transfer across graphs with different feature spaces, structural patterns, and attribute-structure correlations. In this paper, we study a one-for-all alternative: can a single model be trained once and directly applied to diverse attributed graphs without graph-specific training, fine-tuning, or hyperparameter search? We propose OFAG, a foundation model for attributed graph clustering. Building upon Prior-data Fitted Networks, OFAG learns a reusable clustering inference strategy from synthetic attributed graphs generated under broad priors over latent clusters, node attributes, and graph structures. To handle incompatible feature spaces across graphs, OFAG adopts a dimension-agnostic signal-wise graph encoder that treats each feature channel as a graph signal and models its response to shared graph filters. The model is trained with a hyperspherical clustering objective, producing clustering-friendly node representations in a single forward pass at inference time. On ten datasets, one frozen OFAG model achieves the best mean performance and average rank across NMI, ACC, ARI, and F1, while completing all ten datasets in 12.43 minutes total---over 6* faster than the second-fastest baseline and nearly 28* faster than the second-best on clustering quality. Our code and pretrained checkpoint are available at https://github.com/Cloudy1225/OFAG, allowing practitioners to directly apply OFAG to their own attributed graph datasets without additional training or tuning.

</details>


### [116] [Extending Pathwise Gradients to Discrete Random Variables via Finite-Order Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07786)
*Donghan He,Luhuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离散随机变量路径梯度不可精确的问题，提出构造有限阶精确路径梯度估计器的通用框架，其在给定多项式类上无偏、唯一且最小化权重方差，无需温度调参、保留硬前向采样且实现简洁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径wise梯度对连续随机变量具有无偏、低方差、单样本可用等优点，但对离散变量，路径wise恒等式通常无法对任意可微函数精确成立。现有方法或用分类变量，或用增广表示近似非分类变量，带来额外方差与计算开销。

Method: 提出通用框架构造有限阶精确路径梯度估计器，适用于Poisson等常见离散变量；估计器取在所有对次数不超过给定阶的多项式无偏的解中的最小范数解，并推导超出该函数类时的非渐近偏差界。

Result: 理论上该估计器在可行解中唯一且最小化权重方差；实验中低阶方法在线性、非线性及层次潜变量模型上匹配或优于调优基线，且在每次运行时基准测试中快于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为离散变量提供了无需温度调参、保留硬前向采样、易于实现的精确路径梯度估计器，兼顾低方差与高效率，并给出可解释的偏差界限。

Abstract: Pathwise gradients are preferred for continuous random variables because they are unbiased, low variance, and work with a single sample. For discrete variables, however, the pathwise identity cannot generally be exact for every differentiable function. We propose a general framework to construct finite-order exact pathwise gradient estimators for a range of common discrete variables such as Poisson. The estimator is the least-norm solution among all solutions that are unbiased for polynomials of degree at most. The resulting estimators preserve the hard forward sample, require no temperature tuning, and can be implemented in a few lines of codes. Against other admissible solutions, our estimator is unique and minimizes weight variance; in contrast, prior works use categorical variables or augmented representations to approximate non-categorical variables that induces excess variance and computations. To understand approximation bias for functions beyond the prescribed class, we also derive a non-asymptotic bias bound. In experiments our low order methods match or improve tuned baselines across linear, nonlinear and hierarchical latent-variable models, while out-speeding competitors in every runtime benchmark.

</details>


### [117] [ServeLearnBench: How Well Can Agents Self-Improve from Serving Experience?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07792)
*Haizhong Zheng,Yizhuo Di,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Shuowei Jin,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出演化环境流式数据集(EESD)形式化设定与ServeLearnBench基准，覆盖零售、银行、销售话术生成三个领域、53个环境窗口、7718个任务，评测5种学习框架×6个模型共28个组合、252次学习运行，揭示任务能力与经验学习间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型智能体在真实环境中执行复杂任务时，所需知识往往是隐式的、未公开的且随时间变化。现有持续学习评测基准覆盖不完整：有的直接给出目标知识，有的假设静态环境，支持持续适应的又受限于规模和知识多样性，导致这些方法的有效性与局限缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 形式化定义"演化环境流式数据集"(EESD)：智能体必须从交互与结果反馈中推断、应用并修正潜在的环境知识，且隐藏策略会随时间演化。基于此构建ServeLearnBench基准，涵盖零售支持、银行、销售话术生成三类场景，包含53个环境窗口与7718个任务。评测5种学习框架(RAG、Mem0、SkillOpt、Continual Harness、Prime)与6个模型(GPT-5.6 Terra、Opus 5、Kimi K3、GLM-5.3、DeepSeek V4.1 Flash、GLM-5.3 Flash)，共28个模型-框架组合、252次学习运行。

Result: 三项主要发现：(1)任务能力与"从经验中学习"的能力之间存在显著差距；(2)持续适应代价高昂，且可能使原本正确的行为发生退化；(3)探索不足是有效适应的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: ServeLearnBench为诊断上述局限提供了受控测试平台，可用于跟踪智能体通过服务经验实现持续、可靠改进的进展。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed to perform complex tasks in real-world environments. However, the knowledge required for correct behavior in these environments is often implicit, undisclosed, and subject to change over time. Recent continual-learning harnesses seek to address this challenge by enabling agents to improve from serving experience. Yet the effectiveness and limitations of these methods are not yet well characterized. Existing benchmarks provide only partial coverage: some explicitly provide the target knowledge, others assume a static environment, and those that support continual adaptation remain limited in scale and knowledge diversity. To enable systematic evaluation, we formalize an evolving-environment streaming dataset (EESD), in which agents must infer, apply, and revise latent environment knowledge from interaction and outcome feedback as hidden policies evolve, and introduce ServeLearnBench, spanning retail support, banking, and sales-pitch generation with 53 environment windows and 7,718 tasks. We evaluate five learning harnesses (RAG, Mem0, SkillOpt, Continual Harness, and Prime) across six models (GPT-5.6 Terra, Opus 5, Kimi K3, GLM-5.3, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, and GLM-5.3 Flash), covering 28 model-harness pairs and 252 learning runs. Our evaluation reveals three main findings: a substantial gap remains between task capability and learning from experience; continual adaptation is costly and can degrade already-correct behavior; and insufficient exploration emerges as a key bottleneck to effective adaptation. Overall, ServeLearnBench provides a controlled testbed for diagnosing these limitations and tracking progress toward agents that continually and reliably improve through serving experience.

</details>


### [118] [The Geometry of Empowerment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07796)
*Catherine Ji,Vivek Myers,Sergey Levine,Benjamin Eysenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将赋权（empowerment）最大化与技能学习联系起来，提出新的几何视角来解释赋权，并厘清了赋权与结构中心性之间的关系，同时揭示了信息几何与奖励几何的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赋权作为衡量智能体主动控制环境能力的信息论量虽具吸引力，但其与"能广泛通达未来结果的结构中心状态"之间的联系一直是未解问题。

Method: 将赋权最大化与技能学习（skill-learning）方法相衔接，构建新的几何框架来刻画和分析赋权。

Result: 回答了解赋权与结构中心性之间关系的长期开放问题，并揭示信息几何与奖励几何之间的区别。

Conclusion: 研究为构建可扩展的赋权最大化方法提供了重要的理论启示，相关网站与代码已公开。

Abstract: Empowerment captures the capacity for an agent to actively control its environment. While conceptually appealing as an information-theoretic quantity, the connection between empowerment and structurally central states that provide broad access to future outcomes has remained an open question. In this work, we link empowerment maximization and skill-learning methods to provide new geometries for interpreting and analyzing empowerment. Our analyses answer longstanding open questions on the connections between empowerment and structural centrality. Our analyses also reveal distinctions between information and reward geometries, highlighting important theoretical implications to build scalable empowerment-maximization methods. Website and code can be found at https://empowerment-geometry.github.io/.

</details>


### [119] [Adaptive Mean Estimation by In-Context Learning: A Gradient-Flow Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07804)
*Martin Eppert,Krishna Balasubramanian,Subhro Ghosh,Jason Klusowski,Yan Shuo Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 Prior Fitted Networks 的统计自适应性如何被学习：在位置估计任务中，softmax 注意力可计算经验累积生成函数导数，从而同时区分数据族并形成介于样本均值与中点极差之间的估计器；经混合专家（softmax 门控或 GLU）聚合与逐阶段梯度流分析，在约 n^{1+ε} 个预训练任务下，学习到的估计器在 Gaussian 上渐近有效、在 uniform 上达到 minimax 率的 n^ε 因子内，并在收缩方差混合模型上阶最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFNs（如 TabPFN）在预测与估计任务上已可与经典统计方法竞争，其可能原因是具备统计自适应性：在未知真实数据生成模型的情况下，对异质模型集合仍接近按真实模型定制的方法。作者希望解释这种自适应性是如何从架构与训练中被学到的，因此选择一个可控的位置估计问题来剖析机制。

Method: 任务为未标注样本且族别隐藏：Gaussian 数据宜用均值（误差阶 n^{-1}），uniform 数据宜用极差估计（更快阶 n^{-2}），并给出对称 Gaussian 混合例子可达 σ_n^2/n 率。标量输入下，softmax 注意力计算经验累积生成函数的导数，该单一原语既提供区分族的特征，又形成在样本均值与 mid-range 之间插值的估计器。通过 softmax 混合专家或 gated linear unit（GLU）组合注意力专家，并分析逐阶段梯度流。

Result: 在约 \widetildeΩ(n^{1+ε}) 个预训练任务下，学习到的估计器在 Gaussian 任务上渐近有效，在 uniform 任务上处于 minimax 率的 n^ε 因子内，并在收缩方差机制的混合模型上阶最优；这些保证可推广到新位置与更长上下文。风险分解将专家误差、路由误差与归一化误差分离，说明架构差异：softmax 门控强制归一化与精确平移等变性，而 GLU 必须学习该性质，其动力学分为快速偏差消除与缓慢专家选择两阶段。端到端实验复现了预测的特化现象。

Conclusion: PFN 的统计自适应性可由 softmax 注意力的 cumulant 导数原语与门控专家组合共同解释；softmax 门控在归一化与平移等变性上具架构优势，GLU 需通过分阶段学习获得类似行为。该分析在位置估计设定中给出了可证明的率保证，并得到实验验证。

Abstract: Prior Fitted Networks (PFNs) such as TabPFN now rival established statistical procedures across prediction and estimation tasks. A natural explanation is that PFNs have the property of statistical adaptivity, that is, they perform nearly as well as a method tailored to the true data-generating model for a heterogeneous set of models, while not being told which model the data comes from. We study how such adaptivity is learned in a controlled location-estimation problem. Each task is an unlabeled sample whose family is hidden: Gaussian data call for averaging, with error of order $n^{-1}$, whereas uniform data are best estimated from their extremes, at the faster rate $n^{-2}$. We also provide the example of a symmetric Gaussian mixture, for which a rate of $σ^2_n/n$ can be attained. On scalar inputs, softmax attention computes the derivative of the empirical cumulant-generating function. A single primitive therefore both supplies features that distinguish the families and forms estimators interpolating between the sample mean and the mid-range. We combine attention experts through either a softmax mixture of experts or a gated linear unit (GLU), and analyze stagewise gradient flow. With $\widetildeΩ(n^{1+ε})$ pretraining tasks, the learned estimator is asymptotically efficient on Gaussian tasks, within a factor $n^ε$ of the minimax rate on uniform tasks, and order-optimal on mixtures in a shrinking-variance regime. These guarantees extend to new locations and longer contexts. A risk decomposition separates expert error, routing error and normalization error, which clarifies the architectural contrast. Softmax gating enforces normalization and exact translation equivariance, whereas the GLU must learn it: its dynamics separate into fast bias removal followed by slow expert selection. End-to-end experiments recover the predicted specialization.

</details>


### [120] [MASKerade: Token-Routed Mask Experts for Dense-to-MoE Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07809)
*Mingyuan Zhang,Yue Bai,Zhongruo Wang,Yupin Huang,Yiyang Huang,Hailing Wang,Huimin Zeng,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MASKerade：将MoE专家建模为冻结预训练FFN内可学习的稀疏子网络（二值掩码），联合优化路由器与掩码分数，在五个视觉语言基准上取得较优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏激活MoE可在不按比例增加每token计算量的前提下提升模型容量；现有dense-to-MoE upcycling通常把FFN复制为独立训练的专家，需要多份独立专家权重矩阵，参数开销大。作者希望探索以掩码学习方式复用单个冻结FFN来构造专家。

Method: 每个专家由学习到的二值掩码定义，token级路由器选择并组合被掩码的FFN；路由器与掩码分数联合优化，底层FFN权重保持冻结。该框架统一支持神经元结构、半结构化（2:4）与非结构化专家。主配置为四个2:4专家、top-2路由，两次半稠密专家前向的名义FFN算力等于一次稠密前向，且无需独立专家权重矩阵。实验在Qwen与Gemma骨干的五个视觉语言基准上，并设置掩码粒度、路由干预与算力匹配对照。

Result: 在五个视觉语言基准、Qwen与Gemma骨干上，该主配置性能优于所比较的基线；掩码粒度、路由干预与算力匹配对照实验区分了学习到的连接性与专家激活数量的作用。

Conclusion: 在冻结权重上进行掩码学习，可作为构建token路由MoE专家的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Sparsely activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models increase model capacity without a proportional increase in per-token computation. Dense-to-MoE upcycling reuses pretrained dense models to construct such systems, commonly by copying feed-forward networks (FFNs) into independently trained experts. We introduce MASKerade, a dense-to-MoE training method that instead learns experts as sparse subnetworks of a frozen pretrained FFN. Each expert is defined by a learned binary mask, and a token-level router selects which masked FFNs to execute and combine. The router and mask scores are optimized jointly, while the underlying FFN weight values remain unchanged. This formulation supports neuron-structured, semi-structured, and unstructured experts within the same routing architecture. Our main configuration uses four 2:4 experts with top-2 routing, where two half-dense expert passes have the nominal FFN arithmetic of one dense pass, without requiring independent expert weight matrices. On five vision-language benchmarks with Qwen and Gemma backbones, this configuration achieves the highest performance among the compared baselines. Comparisons across mask granularities, routing interventions, and compute-matched controls distinguish the effects of learned connectivity from expert activation count. These results establish mask learning over frozen weights as a practical alternative for constructing token-routed MoE experts.

</details>


### [121] [SIFT: Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer for Multi-Task Personalized Filter Ranking at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07810)
*Shashank Dabriwal,Tanya Piplani,Hao Li,Yiwei Wang,Ashish Jain,Kedar Bellare,Stephanie Moyerman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIFT 是一个基于 Transformer 的搜索筛选器排序模型，直接用原始行为序列学习用户偏好，取代人工特征工程，并已全量上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产环境的筛选器排序系统依赖 ETL 流水线生成的人工、预聚合特征来表示用户，维护成本高，且难以扩展新的筛选器类型或情境维度（如行程长度、同行人数）。

Method: 提出 SIFT（Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer）：用统一用户表示替代人工特征，直接建模原始行为序列，并通过多头结构同时预测预订概率、筛选器交互与有序容量阈值（如 2+ 卧室），兼容布尔型和数值区间型筛选器；新增筛选器只需增加预测头。该用户表示以日频离线计算，以保证线上服务速度。

Result: 离线：预订与设施交互 PR-AUC 分别较生产基线提升 +51.9% 与 +62.8%。线上 A/B：推荐筛选器交互 +20.0%，搜索用户整体筛选器使用 +0.72%，新支持的卧室、浴室、床位筛选器使用分别 +3.9%、+10.7%、+0.52%。扩展性验证：快速接入酒店意图筛选器，未取消酒店预订 +3.8%，整体市场预订 +0.76%。

Conclusion: SIFT 以共享用户表示统一多任务排序，兼顾可扩展性与服务效率，已全量部署，为数百万用户提供规模化个性化。

Abstract: Search filters help guests navigate vast catalogs in two-sided marketplaces like Airbnb, and recommending the right filters can meaningfully lift booking conversion. Many such production filter-ranking systems, however, represent the guest through hand-engineered, pre-aggregated features generated by ETL pipelines. This makes it expensive to maintain and difficult to extend for new filter types or contextual dimensions (trip length, group size). We present SIFT (Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer), a ranking model built on transformers that learns guest preferences directly from raw behavioral sequences. SIFT replaces manual feature engineering with a unified guest representation that feeds multiple prediction tasks, including booking likelihood, filter engagement, and ordinal capacity thresholds (e.g., 2+ bedrooms) -- a general framework for filter ranking in two-sided marketplaces that accommodates both boolean and numeric-range filter types. Extending SIFT to new filters requires only adding a new head, not a new feature pipeline. To keep serving fast, this guest representation is computed offline on a daily cadence rather than at request time. Offline, SIFT improves booking and amenity-engagement PR-AUC by +51.9% and +62.8% respectively over the production baseline. In online A/B testing, SIFT increased engagement with recommended filters by +20.0%, overall filter usage among searchers by +0.72%, and usage of the newly-supported bedroom, bathroom, and bed filters by +3.9%, +10.7%, and +0.52% respectively. Demonstrating the system's extensibility, we rapidly integrated a novel hotel-intent filter using the same shared representation, driving a +3.8% lift in uncancelled hotel bookings and a +0.76% lift in overall marketplace bookings. SIFT is now fully deployed in production, serving scalable personalization to millions of guests.

</details>


### [122] [$α$Transfer: Coefficient Transfer for Efficient Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07819)
*Shih-Cheng Huang,Zhi Rui Tam,Chieh-Yen Lin,Yun-Nung Chen,Hung-yi Lee,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出αTransfer范式：在同族模型间，合并系数的性能分布高度一致，因此可在小代理模型上搜索最优系数并直接迁移到大模型，显著降低搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并需通过参数算术把多个微调检查点合成单一模型，但寻找最优合并系数需大量搜索；随模型规模与数量增长，内存开销高、搜索空间组合爆炸，使搜索成本难以承受。

Method: 发现同一模型族内不同规模模型在合并系数上的性能分布高度一致（分布相似性）；据此提出αTransfer：在小代理模型上搜索最优合并系数，再直接迁移到更大的目标模型；并在多种合并方法、模型族和任务上验证。

Result: 视觉Transformer上实现6倍加速与70%内存降低；大语言模型上实现20倍加速与85%内存降低，同时保持可比性能。

Conclusion: αTransfer是一种高效且可泛化的模型合并扩展方法，可缓解大规模模型合并的系数搜索瓶颈。

Abstract: Model merging offers a promising solution for combining multiple fine-tuned checkpoints into a single model through parameter arithmetic. However, finding optimal merging coefficients requires an extensive search that becomes prohibitively expensive as models scale in both size and number, due to high memory requirements and combinatorial growth in the search space. We show that, within the same model family, models exhibit highly congruent performance distributions over merging coefficients across different model sizes. This distributional similarity enables a practical paradigm we call \textit{$α$Transfer}: searching for optimal coefficients on a small proxy model, then directly transfer them to larger target models. We verify $α$Transfer across multiple merging methods, model families, and tasks. Experimental results demonstrate a 6$\times$ speedup and 70\% memory reduction on vision transformers, and a 20$\times$ speedup and 85\% memory reduction on large language models, while maintaining comparable performance. Our findings establish $α$Transfer as an efficient and generalizable approach to scaling model merging.

</details>


### [123] [TTNet: Multi-Task Deep Learning for Table Tennis Player Analysis with Smart Racket](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07823)
*Ko-Hsun Chen,Xiang-Wei Ke,Hsien-Cheng Huang,Shang-Kuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTNet多任务深度学习模型，基于智能乒乓球拍的六轴传感器数据分析选手属性，在AI CUP 2025竞赛中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能乒乓球拍可采集大量挥拍数据，为乒乓球大数据研究提供基础，有助于深入分析回球技术与挥拍力量一致性，并提升选手技能评估的准确性，因此需要有效的分析方法。

Method: TTNet融合卷积神经网络（CNN）、残差网络（ResNet）与自注意力机制，同时预测性别、持拍手、球龄和技能水平四个选手属性；采用两阶段训练策略，结合数据增强与任务特定损失函数以提升在不平衡数据上的泛化能力。

Result: 该方法在官方竞赛排行榜上取得第二名。

Conclusion: TTNet能够有效推进乒乓球数据分析及相关应用，验证了多任务学习结合CNN、ResNet与自注意力机制在该任务上的有效性。

Abstract: The AI CUP 2025 Precise Analysis of Table Tennis Smart Racket Data Competition introduced smart table tennis rackets that collect extensive player swing data, enabling research on table tennis big data. These data support in-depth analysis of players' return techniques and swing-force consistency, improving the accuracy of player skill assessment. This study focuses on six-axis sensor data collected by smart table tennis rackets and proposes TTNet, a novel deep learning model with multitask learning capabilities, to advance table tennis data analysis and related applications. TTNet combines convolutional neural networks (CNNs), residual networks (ResNet), and self-attention mechanisms to simultaneously predict four player attributes: gender, playing hand, years of experience, and skill level. We adopt a two-stage training strategy that incorporates data augmentation and task-specific loss functions to improve generalization on imbalanced data. Our approach achieved second place on the official competition leaderboard.

</details>


### [124] [CANDLE: Cortical Null-Space Decomposition for Noninvasive Brain Source Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07824)
*Shuntaro Suzuki,Yuiga Wada,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CANDLE，一种基于学习的电生理源成像模型，通过在被试个体皮层几何上学习零空间先验，仅在仿真数据上训练即超越现有方法，并泛化至颅内刺激定位与癫痫灶估计任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电生理源成像（ESI）本质上是病态逆问题：源活动的维度远高于传感器观测维度，导致解不唯一。现有基于学习的方法虽引入数据驱动的源先验以缓解歧义，但难以泛化到被试个体化的皮层几何结构。

Method: 提出CANDLE模型，直接在个体皮层几何上估计源活动。其核心是学习由T1加权MRI导出的源-传感器映射所诱导零空间上的先验，将学习限制在不可观测的源分量上，同时保持几何约束。为训练该模型，构建了覆盖1100余个个体皮层几何、源配置源自26000余张统计脑图的全脑仿真器。

Result: 仅用仿真数据训练，CANDLE在仿真源活动估计上优于既有ESI方法，并泛化到两项实证任务：（i）基于同步头皮脑电的颅内刺激定位；（ii）基于术前发作间期脑电的致痫区估计。

Conclusion: 通过学习受试者个体皮层几何上的零空间先验，CANDLE在避免依赖真实标注的前提下实现了跨几何泛化，展现出在临床ESI任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Electrophysiological source imaging (ESI) aims to estimate cortical source activity from noninvasive electrophysiological measurements such as electroencephalogram (EEG). However, ESI is fundamentally ill-posed because source activity is substantially higher-dimensional than sensor observations, resulting in non-unique solutions. Recent learning-based approaches address this ambiguity by learning data-driven source priors, yet they often struggle to generalize across subject-specific cortical geometries. To address this, we propose CANDLE, a learning-based ESI model that estimates source activity on subject-specific cortical geometries. CANDLE learns a prior over the null space induced by the source-to-sensor mapping derived from T1-weighted MRI, restricting learning to unobservable source components while preserving geometric constraints. To train CANDLE, we develop a whole-brain simulator spanning over 1,100 subject-specific cortical geometries with source configurations derived from over 26,000 statistical brain maps. Trained exclusively on simulated data, CANDLE outperformed prior ESI methods on simulated source activity estimation and generalized to two empirical tasks: (i) intracranial stimulation localization from simultaneously recorded scalp EEG and (ii) epileptogenic zone estimation from presurgical interictal EEG. Our project page is available at https://candle-esi.pages.dev}{https://candle-esi.pages.dev.

</details>


### [125] [Retrieval Is Not Enough: Refreshing Memory for Frozen Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07834)
*Chao He,Jianyu Xu,Xinyi Guo,Ruiqi Liu,Haobin Ding,Ruiqi He,Dongqing Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FreshCast插件式检索框架：冻结预测器，持续用新观测更新非参数记忆，通过关系核回归形成记忆预测，并在验证段闭式校准其权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强时间序列预测多在训练段一次性构建记忆，部署后新观测无法作为参考，且未校准检索信息对冻结预测器的影响程度。论文识别出检索效用的两个关键决定因素：历史是否仍反映当前状态，以及检索诱导的修正是否与预测器残差误差对齐。

Method: FreshCast保持预测器冻结；持续用新观测更新非参数记忆；通过关系核回归生成记忆预测；在验证段以闭式解校准记忆预测的融合权重。同时在简化生成模型下刻画最优组合增益。

Result: 在七个基准与十种预测架构上，FreshCast对每个预测器和输入长度均降低平均MSE，输入长度96和720时分别降低14.6%和5.6%，并在相同比较设置下优于所评估的检索增强与在线基线。消融显示训练结束时冻结记忆会消除大部分增益。

Conclusion: 对冻结预测器而言，有用的历史参考必须保持时效性，并提供有助于修正其剩余误差的信息。部署后观测是性能提升的主要来源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented time-series forecasting uses the continuations of historical segments similar to the current context as references for a forecaster. Most existing methods build the retrieval memory once from the training segment, leaving observations revealed after deployment unavailable as references, and generally do not calibrate how much the retrieved information should influence a frozen forecaster. We identify two key determinants of retrieval utility for a frozen forecaster: whether the history still reflects the current state, and whether the correction it induces aligns with the forecaster's residual errors, an alignment that can shift between validation and deployment when the memory becomes stale. We propose FreshCast, a plug-in retrieval framework that keeps the forecaster frozen, continuously updates a non-parametric memory with new observations, forms a memory forecast through relational kernel regression, and calibrates its weight in closed form on the validation segment. Under a simplified generative model, we characterize the optimal combination gain through the second-order relation between forecaster error and memory correction, and show that a sufficiently long look-back can make periodic memory information redundant. Across seven benchmarks and ten forecasting architectures, FreshCast reduces average MSE for every evaluated forecaster and input length, by 14.6% and 5.6% at input lengths 96 and 720, and achieves lower MSE than the evaluated retrieval-augmented and online baselines in their comparison settings. Ablations show that freezing the memory at the end of training removes most of the gain, identifying post-training observations as a primary source of improvement. For a frozen forecaster, useful historical references must remain timely and provide information that helps correct its remaining errors.

</details>


### [126] [Privileged Context as Drift in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07842)
*Ravenor Davion,Nick Rui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统考察了on-policy自蒸馏(OPSD)中特权上下文设计对策略漂移的影响，发现内容(content)比来源(source)对策略偏移的影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有OPSD工作同时变动特权上下文、模型、数据与训练设置，导致难以单独分离出"特权上下文设计"的作用。受持续学习中减少灾难性遗忘的启发，作者希望厘清特权上下文各设计轴对策略漂移的影响。

Method: 沿两个轴变化特权上下文：内容（示范、反馈、改写）与来源（外部、自生成带验证器、自生成不带验证器），共9种组合。在3个数据集上用OPSD训练Qwen2.5-7B，测量目标任务准确率、先前任务保持率、相对基础策略的反向KL，以及参数更新几何。

Result: 固定来源时改变内容引起的中位KL范围比固定内容时改变来源更宽，比值对每token KL为5.1×，对每序列KL为2.2×。参数更新几何呈相同模式：共享内容的适配器更新对齐度更高（平均余弦0.571），共享来源的仅0.255。

Conclusion: 特权上下文应被视为OPSD稳定性设计的一部分，因为它与策略移动的距离和方向相关；对持续学习而言，设计需重视内容维度。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a language model to match a copy of itself conditioned on privileged context. Existing work varies what privileged context contains and how it is produced while also changing models, data, and training setups, making the effects of privileged context design difficult to isolate. Motivated by efforts in continual learning to reduce catastrophic forgetting, we study how the choice of privileged context affects policy drift. Specifically, we vary two axes: content (a demonstration, feedback, or rephrase) and source (external, self-generated with a verifier, or self-generated without a verifier). We train Qwen2.5-7B with OPSD across these nine combinations and three datasets, measuring target-task accuracy, prior-task retention, reverse KL from the base policy, and parameter-update geometry. Holding source fixed, changing content spans a wider median KL range than holding content fixed and changing source. The ratio between these ranges is $5.1\times$ for per-token KL and $2.2\times$ for per-sequence KL. Parameter-update geometry shows the same pattern: updates from adapters that share content are more closely aligned (mean cosine $0.571$) than updates from adapters that share source ($0.255$). For continual learning, these findings suggest that privileged context should be treated as part of OPSD's stability design because it is associated with how far and in what direction the policy moves.

</details>


### [127] [Lost in the bf16 Cast: Exporting Ternary Language Models Can Revert Most Low-Learning-Rate Code Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07853)
*Avichal Sahai,Nishant Raj,Animesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 审计三个实验室的三值语言模型部署流程发现：文档化的bf16导出步骤会因舍入恰好落在阈值上（ties-to-even归零）而产生与训练量化器代码不一致的三值码，导致微调端点严格准确率大幅崩塌（如Falcon-E-1B-Base从58.79%降至0.78%），而直接写入训练量化器代码或调整bf16输入两种兼容性补救均满足4点严格准确率非劣性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三值语言模型（如BitNet b1.58、Falcon-E、BitCPM）以高精度潜权重微调、再经导出步骤转为三值码部署，而各实验室文档化流程均先将潜权重转换为bf16。作者怀疑这一导出步骤可能引入系统性的量化不一致，从而损害模型性能，因此对三个实验室的发布流程进行审计，以验证导出环节的正确性并寻找补救方案。

Method: 对三个实验室的发布检查点进行审计：比较fp32量化的"已发布潜权重"与"实际部署代码"的一致性；分析不一致代码的成因（bf16舍入恰好落在阈值上、ties-to-even映射为零）；用未修改的onebitllms导出器复现Falcon-E发布版本。在微调端点上，采用匹配名义学习率与bf16-ULP比值的设定进行实验，测量文档化导出前后贪婪GSM8K严格准确率与末位数字准确率的变化；并评估两种兼容性补救（直接写入训练量化器代码、调整bf16输入直到原工具输出目标代码）相对于在线评估的4点严格准确率非劣性；最后通过随机化初始阈值距离的干预实验，检验代码选择对阈值距离的依赖性。

Result: 在发布的检查点中，对已发布潜权重做fp32量化与实际部署代码在Falcon-E和BitCPM上不一致比例为0.83–1.77%，在BitNet 2B-4T上为1.530%；Falcon-E和BitCPM的大部分不一致是bf16舍入恰好落在阈值上、被ties-to-even映射为零的乘积；未修改的onebitllms导出器可逐字节复现全部四个Falcon-E发布版本。在微调端点，文档化导出使Falcon-E-1B-Base的贪婪GSM8K严格准确率从58.79%降至0.78%，使BitCPM-CANN-0.5B从36.13%降至0.39%；bf16保存并重载使BitNet 2B-4T严格准确率下降27.54个百分点，同时末位数字准确率上升。两种兼容性补救在三个模型上均满足相对在线评估的4点严格准确率非劣性标准。在两个模型族中，对初始阈值距离的随机化干预支持了"微调所改变的代码是距离依赖选择"这一结论。

Conclusion: 文档化的bf16导出步骤在三个实验室的三值语言模型流程中引入系统性量化不一致，导致部署性能大幅下降；该问题源于bf16舍入与阈值及ties-to-even规则的交互。通过直接写入训练量化器代码或调整bf16输入以适配原工具，可在不改动工具的前提下恢复性能，满足非劣性标准。研究还表明微调对代码的改变与初始阈值距离相关，提示导出精度与阈值处理应作为三值模型部署的关键环节加以审慎处理。

Abstract: Ternary language models such as BitNet b1.58, Falcon-E and BitCPM are fine-tuned with higher-precision latent weights and deployed as ternary codes produced by an export step that, in the labs' documented pipelines, first casts the latents to bf16. We audit those pipelines across three labs. In released checkpoints, fp32 quantization of the shipped latents disagrees with the deployed codes on 0.83-1.77% of codes in Falcon-E and BitCPM and on 1.530% in BitNet 2B-4T; for Falcon-E and BitCPM most disagreements are products that bf16 rounding lands exactly on the threshold, which ties-to-even maps to zero, and the unmodified onebitllms exporter reproduces all four Falcon-E releases byte for byte. At fine-tuned endpoints, with learning rates selected to match a nominal learning-rate-to-bf16-ULP ratio, the documented export lowers greedy GSM8K strict accuracy from 58.79% to 0.78% for Falcon-E-1B-Base and from 36.13% to 0.39% for BitCPM-CANN-0.5B, and a bf16 save and reload lowers BitNet 2B-4T's strict accuracy by 27.54 points while its last-number accuracy rises. Two compatibility remedies, writing the training quantizer's codes directly or adjusting the bf16 inputs until the unchanged tools emit them, each met a 4-point strict-accuracy non-inferiority criterion against online evaluation in all three models. In two model families, randomized interventions on the initial distance from the threshold support distance-dependent selection of the codes that fine-tuning changes.

</details>


### [128] [A Decision-Focused Neural Optimization Framework for Personalized Route Reproduction from Vehicle Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07857)
*Gyeongjun Kim,Yeseul Kang,Keemin Sohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将个体路径复现建模为基于学习的驾驶员特异性隐式路段成本的最短路径问题，提出结合感知编码器、约束优化层与决策导向学习的神经流水线，实现端到端训练并优于基线路径选择模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路径选择建模通常需枚举备选路径集合，难以刻画个体异质性。作者希望在无需枚举备选路径的前提下，直接从上下文信息推断个体化隐式路段成本，从而复现观测路径，并解释异质性路径选择行为。

Method: 提出神经流水线：感知模型将上下文协变量（个体特征、出行属性、网络级交通状态）嵌入为个性化路段成本；随后由约束优化（CO）层基于估计成本求最短路径（SP）。为支持端到端训练，采用决策导向学习对齐预测最短路径与观测路径，并用隐式最大似然估计（iMLE）为包含不可微 CO 层的损失提供近似梯度。此外引入正则项，将隐式成本分布锚定到观测路段行程时间的经验尺度，以缓解最短路径监督中的尺度模糊性。

Result: 实证评估表明，该框架在路径复现方面优于基线路径选择模型；学习到的隐式成本可解释为感知出行成本的代理，为异质性路径选择提供合理解释。

Conclusion: 该方法通过隐式成本学习与决策导向训练，在无需枚举备选路径的情况下有效复现个体路径，并具备行为可解释性。

Abstract: This study formulates individual route reproduction as a shortest-path problem over learned driver-specific latent link costs. The central idea is that, once such latent costs are inferred from contextual information, observed routes can be reproduced without enumerating alternative route sets. We propose a neural pipeline that includes a perception model that embeds context covariates, which comprises individual characteristics, trip-specific attributes, and network-level traffic states, into the personalized link costs. A constrained optimization (CO) layer, which determines the shortest path (SP) based on these estimated costs, follows the perception encoder. To enable end-to-end training, we employ decision-focused learning to align the predicted shortest paths with observed routes. The implicit maximum likelihood estimation (iMLE) provides an approximate gradient of the loss function that contains the non-differentiable CO layer. Furthermore, a regularization term anchors the latent cost distribution to the empirical scale of observed link travel times, mitigating the scale ambiguity inherent in shortest-path supervision. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms baseline route choice models in path reproduction. The learned latent costs, interpreted as proxies for perceived travel costs, provide plausible explanations for heterogeneous route choices.

</details>


### [129] [Tram-FL: Reducing Communication and Computation Costs through Sequential Model Circulation in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07859)
*Kota Maejima,Takayuki Nishio,Asato Yamazaki,Yuko Hara-Azumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tram-FL机制，通过让单一模型在节点间循环以最小化去中心化联邦学习的计算与通信开销，并借助量化动量在非IID数据下高效收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统去中心化联邦学习以客户端为中心，各客户端维护模型副本并独立更新与交换，虽能利用算力缩短训练时间，但计算与通信浪费严重，尤其在非IID数据下需额外资源才能达到高精度。因此本文转向以模型为中心，追求最小计算与通信成本的DFL。

Method: 提出Tram-FL（面向去中心化联邦学习的巡回模型训练机制），通过让单一模型在节点间循环依次训练。针对模型循环训练的调度问题，即确定哪些节点更新模型及更新次数，从模型巡回路径与更新迭代分配两方面提出简洁有效的方法；同时引入量化动量，减少模型循环次数并控制单次传输的通信负载。

Result: 实验表明，所提算法在非IID数据下仍能收敛到全局模型，且通信与计算开销更低。

Conclusion: 以模型为中心的巡回训练机制Tram-FL可在非IID场景下以更少的通信和计算实现有效的去中心化联邦学习。

Abstract: Conventional decentralized federated learning (DFL) often focuses on clients, with each client maintaining a model copy, performing updates individually, and undertaking model exchange and integration. While fully leveraging computational resources can shorten training times, it can also lead to significant computational and communication waste. This is especially pronounced with non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data, where achieving high model accuracy demands extra resources. This research shifts focus to the model itself, aiming to realize DFL with minimal computation and communication costs. To this end, we propose Tram-FL (Traveling Model Training Mechanism for Decentralized Federated Learning), a mechanism designed to efficiently address these challenges. It sequentially trains a single model by circulating it among nodes. We address the training scheduling problem in model circulation-based training, specifically determining which nodes should update the model and the number of updates to perform. This is approached by considering the model's circulation route and update iteration allocation, for which we propose simple yet effective methods. Additionally, with quantized momentum, Tram-FL achieves high accuracy with fewer model circulations while controlling communication load per transmission. Experimental results show that the proposed algorithm, even with non-IID data, converges to a global model with reduced communication and computation.

</details>


### [130] [On-Policy Distillation with Negative-Policy Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07874)
*Jaehui Hwang,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun,Byeongho Heo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Negative-Policy OPD (NP-OPD)，通过在 rollout 阶段引入低能力的负策略(negative policy)为学生提供显式负信号，与教师的正向监督互补，从而缓解教师与学生分布重叠不足导致学习信号不充分的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy distillation (OPD) 的目标本质仍是模仿教师，依赖正向后验(positive guidance)。当更强的教师与学生的分布重叠有限时，这种正向指导提供的学习信号不足，导致蒸馏效果受限。作者希望在不改变蒸馏奖励形式的前提下，补充一个显式的负信号来源。

Method: 提出 NP-OPD：在 rollout 阶段引入一个表现更差、能力更低的负策略，将其与教师监督互补。具体做法是持续提供那些负策略比教师更偏好的 token，使其在训练全过程中仍暴露于教师监督之下，从而通过负策略的 rollout 给出显式负信号，同时保留 OPD 原有的正向教师监督。该方法不修改蒸馏奖励公式，而是在 rollout 阶段引入负策略。

Result: 在多种模型规模、生成模式、推理领域以及不同 OPD 变体上的大量实验表明，NP-OPD 均能提升 OPD 表现。分析进一步显示，NP-OPD 能有效抑制那些负策略比教师更偏好的 token，并推动学生远离负策略。

Conclusion: 结果支持通过负策略 rollout 引入负信号的设计，并为 OPD 中 rollout 策略所扮演的角色提供了新的洞见。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has been widely studied as a post-training method in which a student model obtains token-level supervision from a stronger teacher on its own rollouts. Recent studies have improved OPD through alternative distillation reward formulations and teacher configurations, while the objective of distillation remains centered on mimicking the teacher. However, when a stronger teacher has limited distributional overlap with the student, such positive guidance can provide insufficient learning signals. In this work, we introduce Negative-Policy OPD (NP-OPD), which complements teacher supervision with rollouts from a lower-performing, lower-capability negative policy that serves as a negative reference for the student. Rather than modifying the distillation reward formulation, NP-OPD introduces the negative policy at the rollout stage, continuously supplying tokens preferred by the negative policy over the teacher so that they remain exposed to teacher supervision throughout training. This provides an explicit negative signal through negative-policy rollouts while preserving the positive teacher supervision used in OPD. Through extensive experiments, we show that NP-OPD improves OPD across model scales, generation modes, reasoning domains, and different OPD variants. Furthermore, our analyses show that NP-OPD effectively suppresses tokens preferred by the negative policy over the teacher and moves the student away from the negative policy. These results support our design of introducing negative signals through negative-policy rollouts and provide new insight into the role of the rollout policy in OPD. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/np-opd.

</details>


### [131] [Label-Efficient Deep Learning for ECG Delineation: A Multi-Dataset Benchmark against Widely Used Delineation Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07885)
*Jeonghwa Lim,Minje Park,Yeongyeon Na,Yujin Eom,Soyeon Lim,Young Ho Lee,Yu Jeong Kim,Sunghoon Joo,Ki Hong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了自监督预训练与半监督微调在ECG波形 delineation 中的有效性，并将所得深度学习模型与主流开源及商业 delineation 工具对比，证明其在多数据集上全面领先且对心律失常更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG波形边界识别（delineation）是临床测量的基础步骤，深度学习虽推进了该任务，但依赖昂贵专家标注。自监督预训练与半监督学习等标签高效策略虽被寄予厚望，但其能否产生可靠的delineation、所得模型能否超越实际使用的工具尚不明确。

Method: 研究分两阶段：第一，在1个内部和4个外部数据集上比较不同自监督目标与有监督/半监督微调组合；第二，用三种互补指标将选定深度学习模型与开源工具（NeuroKit2、Prominence、ECGdeli）及商业工具（CalECG）进行基准测试，并进行节律分层与逐点分析。

Result: 预训练有帮助但目标函数至关重要，半监督微调的价值取决于预训练目标。所选模型在所有指标和数据集上均排名第一，在节律多样集上明显超越最强工具（mIoU 71.3% vs. 54.8%；平均逐点敏感度92.6% vs. 76.4%），且从窦性心律到心律失常的退化最小。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练对ECG delineation有效，可借助大量无标注数据实现标签高效训练，使深度学习模型超越广泛使用的delineation工具，支持在多样真实临床场景中采用此类模型。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) delineation, the identification of waveform boundaries, is a foundational step that translates raw ECG signals into clinically interpretable measurements. Deep learning has advanced this task but remains dependent on costly expert annotations. Label-efficient strategies such as self-supervised pretraining and semi-supervised learning are expected to ease this burden, yet it remains unclear whether they yield reliable delineation and whether the deep models they produce outperform the delineation tools used in practice. We address this in two stages. First, comparing self-supervised objectives with supervised or semi-supervised fine-tuning across one internal and four external datasets, we find that pretraining helps but the objective matters, and that the value of semi-supervised fine-tuning depends on the pretraining objective. Second, we benchmark the selected deep learning model against widely used open-source (NeuroKit2, Prominence, ECGdeli) and commercial (CalECG) tools using three complementary metrics. The model ranks best on every metric and dataset, outperforming the strongest tool by a clear margin on the rhythm-diverse set (mIoU 71.3 vs. 54.8%; averaged point-wise sensitivity 92.6 vs. 76.4%), and degrades the least from sinus to arrhythmia. A rhythm-stratified and point-wise analysis further characterizes the distinctive behavior of each tool, yielding practical guidance for tool selection. These results provide systematic, multi-dataset evidence that self-supervised pretraining is effective for ECG delineation and enables a label-efficiently trained deep learning model to outperform widely used delineation tools by leveraging abundant unlabeled data. This supports adopting such models in diverse, real-world clinical settings.

</details>


### [132] [Variance-Averse $n$-Step Offline Reinforcement Learning for Sparse Long-Horizon Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07899)
*Guhyeon Kang,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VAN-Flow框架，通过类别分布式评论家、方差规避期望算子和流匹配生成actor，在生成式离线RL中促进可靠动作选择，在D4RL和OGBench的超40个任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式actor虽能建模复杂动作分布，但在异构数据集上会复制不可靠的动作模式，其回报分布方差高、偶发高回报但缺乏一致性，因此仅最大化期望Q值不足以识别可靠动作。

Method: VAN-Flow包含三部分：(i) 类别分布式评论家；(ii) 方差规避期望算子，通过平滑重加权原子概率，偏好兼具高回报与低离散度的动作；(iii) 经拒绝采样引导的流匹配生成actor。该算子不采用硬截断或辅助惩罚项，而是在类别回报分布上重新分配概率质量。

Result: 在D4RL和OGBench的40多个任务上，VAN-Flow持续超越强基线，在长时程和高方差场景中增益最大。

Conclusion: VAN-Flow通过方差规避与生成式建模结合，有效提升离线RL中可靠动作的选择能力，尤其在可靠动作选择至关重要的长时程、高方差任务中优势显著。

Abstract: Generative actors are transforming offline reinforcement learning (RL) by enabling expressive policy classes that model complex action distributions. However, this expressiveness also exposes a key challenge in heterogeneous datasets: generative policies can reproduce unreliable action modes whose return distributions exhibit high variance, occasionally yielding high returns by chance but lacking consistency. Consequently, maximizing the expected $Q$-value alone is insufficient for identifying reliable actions. We propose VAN-Flow (Variance-Averse $n$-step Flow), a framework that promotes reliable actions in generative offline RL. VAN-Flow combines (i) a categorical distributional critic, (ii) a variance-averse expectation operator that smoothly reweights atom probabilities to favor actions with both high returns and low dispersion, and (iii) a flow-matching generative actor guided via rejection sampling. Unlike CVaR or mean-variance objectives, the operator redistributes probability mass over the categorical return distribution without hard truncation or auxiliary penalty terms. Across more than 40 tasks from D4RL and OGBench, VAN-Flow consistently outperforms strong baselines, with the largest gains in long-horizon and high-variance regimes where reliable action selection becomes critical.

</details>


### [133] [ApexQuant: Data-Free Elastic Quantization by Residual Re-Isotropization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07904)
*Aksel Fristrup,Sumit Pandey,Ankit Kariryaa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ApexQuant 是一种免校准的残差递归重量化方法，可作为现有量化器的精化层，实现无数据场景下的高精度低比特量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私限制或数据许可受限的领域中，分布内数据往往无法获取，导致依赖校准数据的量化方法难以适用；需要一种完全无数据（data-free）的量化方案，同时支持多精度部署。

Method: 提出 ApexQuant：递归地对残差误差进行重量化。理论上证明新的随机旋转使每个残差回归超球面上的均匀分布，据此刻画逐次传递中渐进误差衰减速率。该结果可在读取任何权重前，确定某层达到目标权重空间误差所需的传递次数。每个前缀本身即为有效的低比特率模型，因此单一产物可服务多种精度。实例化时采用可互换的三个阶段：标量、E8 与格状（trellis）量化。

Result: 在四个开放权重 LLM 以及地球观测和医学领域（这些领域常因影像许可限制或患者隐私约束而无法获得分布内数据）上验证。渐进再各向同性化在四比特下接近全精度（误差在几个百分点内），并在完全无数据设定下取得所测最佳二比特方案。

Conclusion: ApexQuant 通过递归残差重量化与随机旋转带来的再各向同性化，实现了免校准、无数据、渐进多精度的量化，兼顾高精度与低比特性能，适用于数据受限的实际场景。

Abstract: We introduce ApexQuant, a calibration-free quantization method that recursively re-quantizes the residual error, serving as a refinement layer on top of existing quantizers. We establish that a fresh random rotation returns each residual to the uniform distribution on the hypersphere, which characterizes the rate of progressive error decay across successive passes. This result lets us determine, before any weight is read, how many passes a layer needs for a target weight-space error. Every prefix is itself a valid lower-rate model, so one artifact serves several precisions. We instantiate ApexQuant with three interchangeable stages, scalar, $E_8$ and trellis, and validate it on four open-weight LLMs and on Earth-observation and medical domains where in-distribution data is often unattainable as imagery arrives under restrictive licences or due to patient material under privacy constraints. Progressive re-isotropization comes within a few percent of full precision at four bits and gives the best two-bit arm we measure, in a completely data-free setting.

</details>


### [134] [Revisiting Temporal Regularization for Smooth Control in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07910)
*SungJae Ahn,Jeong Woon Lee,Kyoleen Kwak,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CATS方法，通过证明时间惩罚能约束下一状态相同但当前状态不同的动作差异，将时间平滑正则化与线性递增策略结合，在仿真和真实机器人上显著减少动作振荡且不降低任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略会产生非平滑的动作振荡，妨碍在物理机器人上的部署。现有架构设计和惩罚类方法追求空间平滑，但直接降低对状态输入变化的敏感性，强平滑会损害任务性能；而时间正则化被认为在观测噪声下无法提供所需的空间平滑。

Method: 作者重新审视上述假设，证明时间惩罚能够约束那些共享同一后继状态的当前状态之间的期望动作差异，揭示出可经验性扩展到空间平滑的空间效应。基于此提出CATS（Conditioning for Action using only Temporal Smoothness），将时间惩罚与线性递增（linear ramp-up）相结合，使策略先学习有回报的行为再逐步平滑动作。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，CATS显著减少动作振荡且不降低任务性能，计算开销很小，同时提升了回报保持以及时间与空间平滑性。

Conclusion: 时间正则化能够在比显式空间正则化更好保留任务性能的同时提供空间平滑性；通过线性递增，CATS在逐步平滑动作的同时改善回报保持和时空平滑性。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning policies can produce nonsmooth action oscillations that hinder deployment on physical robots. Existing architectural and penalty-based approaches seek spatial smoothness by directly reducing sensitivity to changes in state inputs, but their broad constraints can degrade task performance as stronger smoothing is pursued. Temporal regularization instead constrains action differences along observed transitions, but has been considered unable to provide the spatial smoothness needed under observation noise. We revisit this assumption by proving that the temporal penalty bounds the expected action differences between current states sharing a next state, revealing a spatial effect that empirically extends to spatial smoothness. Building on this finding, we propose Conditioning for Action using only Temporal Smoothness (CATS), which combines a temporal penalty with linear ramp-up. We highlight temporal regularization's ability to provide spatial smoothness while better preserving task performance than explicit spatial regularization. Through linear ramp-up, CATS allows the policy to learn rewarding behavior before progressively smoothing its actions, improving return preservation and both temporal and spatial smoothness. Experiments in both simulation and the real world show that CATS substantially reduces action oscillation without degrading task performance, with little computational overhead.

</details>


### [135] [Generalized Matheron Variational Implicit Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07938)
*Luis A. Ortega,Andrés R. Masegosa,Thomas D. Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GMVIP 提出一种路径式变分族，用于对隐式过程先验进行后验推断，通过在诱导输入处对先验样本施加校正来构造后验样本，并获得可处理的系数空间 KL 散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式过程先验（如贝叶斯神经网络、随机模拟器）通过样本前向机制定义函数分布，但其函数空间密度通常不可用，导致难以直接进行变分后验推断。因此需要一个既能利用先验样本、又具备可处理推断目标的变分框架。

Method: 提出广义 Matheron 变分隐式过程（GMVIP）。其核心是路径式变分构造：从先验中抽取函数，并在一组诱导输入处施加校正。对于高斯过程先验，GMVIP 退化为标准诱导变量变分 GP 构造；对于一般隐式先验，其经验协方差构造在总体极限下保持先验均值与协方差。替代先验与变分后验采用相同路径构造，仅白化诱导系数的分布不同，从而得到可处理的系数空间 KL 散度。远离诱导输入处的校正效果直接由先验样本决定。

Result: 在回归、分类和预测任务上，使用模拟器定义以及检索条件化的经验轨迹先验进行实验，GMVIP 与现有方法相比具有广泛竞争力。

Conclusion: GMVIP 为隐式过程先验提供了一种通用且可处理的路径式变分推断方法，在保持先验结构与变异性的同时，可兼容 GP 先验并扩展到一般隐式先验，实验验证了其竞争性表现。

Abstract: Implicit-process priors specify distributions over functions through sample-forward mechanisms such as Bayesian neural networks and stochastic simulators, but their function-space densities are typically unavailable. We introduce Generalized Matheron Variational Implicit Processes (GMVIP), a pathwise variational family for posterior inference with such priors. For Gaussian-process priors, GMVIP recovers the standard inducing-variable variational GP construction; for general implicit priors, its empirical covariance construction preserves the prior mean and covariance in the population limit.
