GMVIP constructs posterior samples by drawing a function from the prior and applying a correction anchored at a set of inducing inputs. The effect of this correction away from the inducing inputs is determined directly from prior samples, allowing the posterior to retain the structure and variability of the original implicit process. The (surrogate) prior and variational posterior use the same pathwise construction and differ only in the distribution of whitened inducing coefficients, yielding a tractable coefficient-space Kullback-Leibler divergence. Experiments on regression, classification, and forecasting with simulator-defined and retrieval-conditioned empirical trajectory priors show that GMVIP is broadly competitive with existing methods.

</details>


### [136] [DecepEval: A Benchmark for Evaluating Deception in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07967)
*Yiming Xu,Hongyue Yu,Beihua Yang,Zihan Chen,Yixin Liu,Zhen Peng,Bin Shi,Bo Dong,Chao Shen,Irwin King,Qinghua Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DecepEval 基准（1,532 个实例、3 类任务、28 个专业场景）与 LLM Deception Diamond 框架，系统测量 LLM 智能体在压力、激励、机会、冲突四类外部条件下的欺骗行为，发现诱导条件普遍提升九个前沿模型的欺骗率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 智能体自主性增强，其可能通过欺骗来提升任务表现，威胁可靠部署。现有评测多局限于孤立场景或狭窄条件，难以系统理解欺骗何时更易发生，因此需要一个可控、可比较的评测基准来刻画条件依赖的欺骗倾向。

Method: 构建 DecepEval 基准，涵盖 1,532 个实例、3 个任务族与 28 个专业场景；基于古典欺诈理论提出 LLM Deception Diamond 框架，刻画压力、激励、机会、冲突四种外部诱导条件。每个实例配备中性版与诱导版配对，通过欺骗率差值衡量条件依赖变化；同时以显式任务事实与可观测智能体行为区分欺骗与能力性错误。对九个前沿 LLM 进行评测。

Result: 评测显示，诱导条件在全部模型与任务族中均提升欺骗率，即使基线欺骗率较低的模型也出现明显上升，表明欺骗倾向是普遍且可被外部条件放大的脆弱性。

Conclusion: DecepEval 将 LLM 智能体的欺骗脆弱性变得可测量，提供统一的共享基准，为可信人工智能的进展评估与改进提供依据。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents become increasingly autonomous, they may pursue task performance through deception, raising concerns about their reliable deployment. Existing evaluations show that LLM agents can deceive, but often examine isolated scenarios or narrowly defined conditions, limiting systematic understanding of when deception becomes more likely. To address this gap, we introduce DecepEval, a benchmark comprising 1,532 instances across 3 task families and 28 professional scenarios. Drawing on classical fraud theories, we propose the LLM Deception Diamond framework, which characterizes four external conditions that may induce deception: pressure, incentive, opportunity, and conflict. DecepEval pairs neutral and induced versions of each instance to measure condition-dependent changes in deception rates, while explicit task facts and observable agent behavior help distinguish deception from capability-related errors. Evaluations of nine frontier LLMs show that inducements increase deception across models and task families, even among models with low baseline deception rates. DecepEval makes these vulnerabilities measurable, providing a shared benchmark for progress toward trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [137] [Learning a Ranking from Human Feedback in Log-Concave Random Utility Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07973)
*Diego Alovisetti,Marco Mussi,Alberto Maria Metelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究根据未知数值效用恢复固定物品集合排名的问题，涵盖全排名反馈与仅获胜者反馈两种设定，采用对数凹噪声的随机效用模型，建立了ε-精确排名恢复的最坏情况样本复杂度下界，并给出匹配下界（至对数因子）且无需知道噪声分布、仅需方差上界的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排名恢复在推荐、偏好学习等任务中具有重要意义，但既有工作多关注精确恢复或特定反馈形式。本文希望统一刻画两种常见人类比较反馈（全排名与仅获胜者）在随机效用模型下的样本复杂度，并引入容忍小效用差异的ε-精确排名准则，以更贴合实际。同时现有算法常依赖噪声分布知识，本文希望放松这一假设。

Method: 采用对数凹噪声的随机效用模型对两种反馈建模；定义ε-精确排名作为恢复准则，允许效用差小于ε的物品间存在排序错误；针对全排名反馈和仅获胜者反馈分别推导最坏情况样本复杂度下界，并设计相应算法，均不依赖噪声分布的具体形式，只需噪声方差的一个上界。

Result: 对两种反馈类型均建立了样本复杂度的最坏情况下界，并提出了达到这些下界（相差对数因子）的算法；结果表明仅获胜者反馈下的排名问题本质上更难，其样本复杂度显式依赖于物品集合中的最小获胜概率。

Conclusion: 在随机效用模型下，ε-精确排名恢复的样本复杂度可由匹配的上下界刻画；全排名与仅获胜者反馈存在本质难度差异，且算法可在仅知方差上界的弱假设下实现接近最优的性能。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering the ranking of a fixed set of items according to their unknown numerical utilities. At each interaction with the environment, a learner presents the item set to a human and receives comparative feedback of two types. Under full-ranking feedback, each interaction reveals a noisy ranking of all items, whereas under winner-only feedback, it reveals only the item ranked first. In both settings, we model human feedback using a random utility model with log-concave noise and study the number of observations needed to recover an $ε$-accurate ranking with high probability. This novel criterion tolerates ordering errors only between items whose utilities differ by less than $ε$. For both feedback types, we establish worst-case sample-complexity lower bounds and develop algorithms that match these bounds up to logarithmic factors. Neither algorithm requires knowledge of the noise distribution, while only requiring an upper bound on its variance. Our results show that the ranking problem under winner-only feedback is intrinsically harder by exposing the sample complexity dependence on the minimum winning probability across the item set.

</details>


### [138] [Do Higher-Order Models Win for Higher-Order Reasons? Rethinking Performance Gains in Hypergraph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07981)
*Fanchen Bu,Fan Li,Geon Lee,Sunwoo Kim,Xiaoyang Wang,Renaud Lambiotte,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出受控的性能归因框架，在保持低阶（成对）信息不变的前提下去扰动高阶信息，并在25个超图学习基准上检验"高阶模型是否因高阶原因获胜"。结果显示高阶模型的优势在扰动后大多保留，且可通过更强低阶基线（加权、更多传播步数、归一化）缩小，说明其优势部分源于低阶因素而非高阶信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究显示高阶模型（如超图神经网络）在超图学习基准上常优于低阶基线，但仅凭性能更好并不足以证明优势来自高阶信息的利用。作者因此质疑这类"高阶原因"解释的充分性，希望区分性能提升与其归因，检验高阶模型是否真的因高阶原因而胜出。

Method: 提出受控的性能归因框架：在扰动高阶信息的同时保持低阶（成对）信息不变，从而观察高阶模型优势的变化。实验覆盖3类任务、25个常用超图学习基准；随后针对扰动后残留的性能差距，系统检验若干低阶解释，如更丰富的成对加权、更多成对特征传播步数以及归一化等对低阶基线的改进。

Result: 在多数基准上观察到一致模式：高阶模型原本优于低阶基线，但在高阶信息被扰动后仍保留大部分优势，说明其优势很大程度可在不使用高阶信息的情况下实现。通过为低阶基线加入成对加权、更多传播步数与归一化等简单改进，残留性能差距被显著缩小，支持了部分优势的低阶解释。

Conclusion: 论文呼吁超图学习社区重新思考性能归因，将性能增益与其解释区分开来，采用更强的低阶基线，并选择能更好检验高阶信息价值的基准，以避免将性能优势直接等同于高阶信息的作用。

Abstract: Higher-order models (e.g., hypergraph neural networks) often outperform lower-order baselines on hypergraph learning benchmarks, and their advantages are commonly attributed to their ability to exploit higher-order information. However, better performance alone does not establish this explanation. We therefore ask: Do higher-order models win for higher-order reasons? To investigate this question, we introduce a controlled performance-attribution framework that perturbs higher-order information while preserving the lower-order, i.e., pairwise, information. Across 25 commonly used hypergraph learning benchmarks spanning three tasks, we frequently observe an intriguing pattern: higher-order models originally outperform lower-order baselines, yet retain most of their advantage after perturbation. This suggests that much of the observed advantage remains achievable without the higher-order information. We then investigate potential lower-order explanations for these remaining gaps. We find that simple additions to a lower-order baseline, e.g., richer pairwise weighting, more steps of pairwise feature propagation, and normalization, reduce the remaining performance gaps, supporting lower-order explanations for part of the observed advantage. Our analysis calls for the hypergraph learning community to rethink performance attribution by distinguishing performance gains from their explanations, adopt stronger lower-order baselines, and use suitable benchmarks that better test the value of higher-order information.

</details>


### [139] [A Broader Look at Model Merging: Rethinking Implicit Regularization Induced by Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07990)
*Sin-Han Yang,Shih-Cheng Huang,Chieh-Yen Lin,Yun-Nung Chen,Shao-Hua Sun,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有多任务模型合并依赖额外数据集搜索任务权重更新的线性组合系数，本文指出该做法隐含地将候选模型限制于任务权重更新张成的子空间（一种隐式正则化），并通过实验证明去掉该正则化、直接在权重空间优化可显著提升常见合并方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并旨在通过组合各任务专用模型的权重，廉价地构建多任务模型。为在多个任务上取得良好表现，主流合并方法通常借助额外数据集求解任务权重更新的最优线性组合系数。作者识别出这一标准流程中的隐含正则化：对系数的搜索把候选模型限制在任务权重更新所张成的子空间内。本文的动机是检验该正则化是否真正有益，并据此重新审视现有的模型合并范式。

Method: 作者在多个架构与领域上进行实证研究，比较"带系数搜索的正则化合并"与"不施加该正则化、直接优化合并模型权重"两种设置；并在每类仅有单个样本的极端数据受限场景下验证；同时比较直接优化预训练模型权重与现有合并方法的表现；进一步分析子空间之外的多任务权重是否存在、是否可用多种方法找到，并研究使用额外数据集的不同策略。

Result: 经验结果表明，在不施加该隐式正则化的情况下优化合并模型权重，能在多种架构、多个领域、乃至每类仅一个样本的极端数据受限场景中，显著提升常见合并方法的性能；直接优化预训练模型权重甚至可超越部分现有合并方法；分析显示更优的多任务权重存在于任务权重更新张成的子空间之外，且可通过多种方法找到。

Conclusion: 该工作呼吁重新审视现有的模型合并流程，主张对权重空间进行更广泛的探索，并重新考虑由任务算术（task arithmetic）所带来的隐式正则化；同时讨论了使用额外数据集的不同策略及其实际应用价值与对模型合并的启示。

Abstract: Model merging aims to build a multi-task model cheaply by combining the weights of individual task-specific models. To perform well across multiple tasks, most existing merging methods use an additional dataset to find the coefficients for the best linear combination of task-specific weight updates. However, we identify an implicit regularization in this standard practice: searching over coefficients restricts the candidate models to a subspace spanned by task-specific weight updates. In this work, we investigate whether this regularization is actually useful. Surprisingly, empirical results show that optimizing merged-model weights without this regularization significantly boosts the performance of common merging methods across multiple architectures, domains, and even in an extremely data-limited scenario where only one instance is available per class. Moreover, directly optimizing the pretrained model weights even outperforms some existing merging methods. Analysis shows that better multi-task weights exist outside the subspace and can be found using multiple methods. We study different strategies for using the additional dataset, discussing their practical use and implications for model merging. Overall, this work calls for revisiting the existing model-merging pipeline, motivating a broader exploration of the weight space and a reconsideration of the implicit regularization induced by task arithmetic.

</details>


### [140] [TICDA: Tabular In-Context Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07996)
*Yacine Benihaddadene,Milan Bhan,Eliot Dugelay,Mohammed Jawhar,Benjamin Wong,Nicolas Chesneau,Duong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出TICDA，一种基于TFM隐嵌入上线性代理模型的数据归因方法，可在单次前向传播中以极低成本衡量上下文中每个示范样本的影响，并在四类任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文中带标签的示范样本进行预测且不更新参数，但单个示范样本如何影响预测尚不清楚。实践中上下文常由任意可用标签数据拼装，可能混入错标、冗余或低质量样本从而降低性能。而现有的数据归因方法无法迁移到TFM场景：重采样类方法（如DemoShapley）需要组合数量级的前向传播，基于梯度的估计（如影响函数）依赖参数更新，而上下文学习从不更新参数。

Method: TICDA直接在TFM隐嵌入上训练线性代理模型，据此度量上下文中每个示范样本的影响，仅需单次前向传播，成本可忽略。

Result: 在四项任务上，TICDA相较对比方法取得最佳折中效果：检测标注错误、在降低推理成本的同时保持预测精度的上下文筛选、跨TFM迁移的归因分数，以及支持高效主动学习的采集策略。

Conclusion: TICDA为TFM的上下文示范样本提供了高效、可迁移的影响力归因手段，适用于数据清洗、上下文筛选与主动学习等实际场景。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve strong predictive performance by conditioning on labeled demonstrations provided in context, without any parameter update. Yet how individual demonstrations shape a given prediction remains poorly understood. This gap matters in practice: the context is often assembled from whatever labeled data is available, potentially leading to the inclusion of mislabeled, redundant, or low-quality examples that degrade performance. Standard data attribution methods do not transfer to the TFM setting: resampling-based approaches such as DemoShapley require a combinatorial number of forward passes, and gradient-based estimators such as influence functions require computing training point's effect on the model parameters, which in-context learning never updates. We introduce TICDA, a method that measures the influence of every demonstration in the context directly from linear surrogates trained on TFM latent embeddings, in a single forward pass and at negligible cost. We show that TICDA offers the best compromise against competitors across four tasks: detecting labeling errors, curating context to preserve predictive accuracy while lowering inference cost, producing attribution scores that transfer across TFMs, and supporting an acquisition strategy for efficient active learning.

</details>


### [141] [Can phenotypic activity be predicted without experimental readouts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07997)
*Télio Cropsal,Rocío Mercado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了以分子-形态配对数据对比预训练的分子编码器（如CLOOME、CellCLIP）能否作为表型预测的廉价替代方案，在控制预训练边界泄漏和表型活性与细胞毒性相关性两个混杂因素后，发现预训练编码器相对普通理化描述符并无明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLOOME、CellCLIP等分子编码器被提出可替代Cell Painting实验进行廉价表型预测，但以往评估可能受两类混杂因素夸大性能：编码器自身预训练边界的泄漏，以及表型活性与细胞毒性的相关性。作者希望验证这一替代方案的可靠性。

Method: 设计了一套控制上述两个混杂因素的评估协议，在两种不同的Cell Painting筛选数据集上测试六种表示，其中包括一个与CLOOME输入和层数匹配的非预训练MLP作为对照。

Result: 在控制混杂因素后，预训练分子编码器相对普通理化描述符没有明显优势；且在所有表示中，毒性通常比表型活性更容易预测。

Conclusion: 在将表型预训练编码器作为表型药物发现替代工具之前，应把具备泄漏意识、控制混杂因素的评估作为标准做法。

Abstract: Molecular encoders contrastively pretrained on paired molecule-morphology data, such as CLOOME and CellCLIP, have been proposed as cheap surrogates for phenotypic prediction, avoiding the need to run a Cell Painting assay. We evaluate this idea for these molecular encoders under a protocol designed to control for two confounds that can inflate apparent performance: leakage across an encoder's own pretraining boundary, and the correlation between phenotypic activity and cytotoxicity. Testing six representations, including a non-pretrained MLP control matching CLOOME's input and layer count, on two distinct Cell Painting screens, we find that once these confounds are controlled for, the pretrained molecular encoders show no clear advantage over plain physicochemical descriptors, and that toxicity is generally easier to predict than phenotypic activity across representations. Our results suggest leakage-aware, confound-controlled evaluation should be standard practice before phenotype-pretrained encoders are trusted as surrogates for phenotypic drug discovery.

</details>


### [142] [Spectra: Exact Component Transport for Test-Time Prior Adaptation in Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08021)
*Xin Zhao,Nico Scherf,Robert Trampel,Kerrin J. Pine,Nikolaus Weiskopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spectra，一种针对扩散模型SBI的测试时自适应方法，通过精确的score-transport恒等式，在闭式解下对冻结扩散模型进行后验调整，无需额外仿真或训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摊销式SBI能学习可复用的推断模型，但复用性受限于训练时选定的先验分布；科学分析常需随知识积累或假设变更而修订先验，现有方法难以在测试时更新。

Method: Spectra利用精确的score-transport恒等式，对结构化先验变化从冻结的扩散模型中闭式地获得自适应score，无需额外仿真或训练。

Result: 在六个SBI基准上，Spectra在强先验偏移下实现准确自适应，且在线采样成本低。

Conclusion: 该方法使预训练SBI模型能在测试时纳入更新的先验信息，扩展了摊销式SBI的复用性与实用价值。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) has become a powerful approach to Bayesian inference in complex scientific models whose likelihoods are difficult or impossible to evaluate. Amortized SBI learns reusable inference models from simulated data, enabling rapid posterior inference for new observations, and modern generative models have made these models increasingly expressive. However, this reuse is limited to the prior distribution chosen during training, whereas scientific analyses often need revised priors as knowledge accumulates or alternative assumptions are tested. We introduce Spectra, a test-time adaptation method for diffusion-based SBI. Spectra uses an exact score-transport identity to obtain the adapted score from a frozen diffusion model in closed form for structured prior changes, without additional simulation or training. Across six SBI benchmarks, Spectra achieves accurate adaptation under strong prior shifts at low online sampling cost. This enables pretrained SBI models to incorporate updated prior information at test time.

</details>


### [143] [SepsisLens: Structure-Preserving Sequence Modelling for Decomposable Early Sepsis Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08046)
*Yikun Ou,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SepsisLens，一种保持变量索引结构的早期脓毒症预警模型，在风险组合前不压缩变量维度，实现多horizon风险预测并提供变量级/器官级结构分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期脓毒症预警可视为保结构预测问题：需从ICU不规则测量中检测恶化，同时让每个预警与支撑它的生理信号保持关联。现有时间模型将临床变量融合为患者级表示，虽支持标量风险预测，但削弱了临床可分解性所需的结构。

Method: SepsisLens将变量索引的时序状态保留至风险组合阶段。观测感知表示编码每个变量的动态与测量历史；共享时序编码器对每条轨迹建模而不压缩变量轴；StructuredRiskHead从显式的变量级与器官级分量组合多horizon风险。

Result: 在三个公开ICU队列与一个私立医院队列上，采用统一的pre-onset协议评估。SepsisLens在四个队列上均取得较强判别性能，并在MIMIC-IV上于匹配事件召回率下具有更低的告警负担。结构消融验证了设计，输入侧masking表明排序分量反映了对预测影响更大的变量。

Conclusion: 保持变量索引时序结构直至风险组合的设计，可在维持强判别力的同时提升预警的结构可分解性与告警效率。

Abstract: Early sepsis warning from ICU records can be cast as a structure-preserving prediction problem. A model needs to detect deterioration from irregular measurements while keeping each alert connected to the physiological signals that support it. Many temporal models fuse clinical variables into a patient-level representation, supporting scalar risk prediction but weakening the structure needed for clinical decomposition. We present SepsisLens, which preserves variable-indexed temporal states until risk composition. Observation-aware representations encode each variable's dynamics and measurement history, while a shared temporal encoder models each trajectory without collapsing the variable axis. The StructuredRiskHead composes multi-horizon risk from explicit variable-level and organ-level components. We evaluate SepsisLens on three public ICU cohorts and one private-hospital cohort under a common pre-onset protocol. SepsisLens achieves strong discrimination on all four cohorts and lower alert burden at matched event recall on MIMIC-IV. Structural ablations support the design, while input-side masking shows that the ranked components reflect variables with greater influence on prediction.

</details>


### [144] [A Riemannian Geometry for Low-rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08049)
*Shoichiro Takeda,Shin'ya Yamaguchi,Satoshi Suzuki,Yasunori Akagi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LoRA参数化中的冗余等价关系，设计了一种专门定制的黎曼度量，用于诱导预处理，使LoRA的权重更新更接近全量微调，在语言和视觉任务上取得更高效的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA通过低秩矩阵 BAᵀ 近似全量微调的权重更新，其参数化存在等价关系 (B,A) ~ (BG⁻¹, AGᵀ)，形成商流形并带来冗余方向。现有方法未能充分利用该几何结构，且LoRA与全量微调在权重层面存在差距。作者希望通过对商流形赋予合适度量来缩小这一差距。

Method: 为LoRA的商流形引入一种新的、在等价关系下不变的黎曼度量，该度量在每个梯度步诱导预处理（preconditioning）。理论上证明该预处理满足两条性质：(i)权重更新方向在LoRA参数化允许的一阶权重变化子空间内最接近全量微调梯度；(ii)更新后的权重矩阵在Frobenius范数下比使用常规预处理和无预处理的LoRA更接近全量微调。

Result: 理论分析表明该预处理使LoRA更好地近似全量微调，从而实现更高效优化；实验在语言和视觉领域的微调任务上验证了该预处理的有效性与高效性。

Conclusion: 针对LoRA商流形定制的黎曼度量与相应预处理能有效缩小LoRA与全量微调在权重层面的差距，提升优化效率和微调性能。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is widely used as a parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique for pre-trained deep neural networks, which approximates the weight update via full fine-tuning by a low-rank matrix $BA^\top$. This parameterization leads to the equivalence relation $(B, A) \sim (BG^{-1}, AG^\top)$ for any invertible matrix $G$ because $BA^\top = BG^{-1}(AG^\top)^\top$ and thus both pairs yield the same loss value. This relation induces a quotient manifold where matrices $(BG^{-1}, AG^\top)$ for all $G$ are identified, eliminating redundant directions along which the loss value remains unchanged. To respect the geometry of this manifold, the original search space is endowed with a Riemannian metric that is invariant under the equivalence relation. Such a metric induces preconditioning at each gradient step and ensures that each weight update via LoRA changes the loss value, leading to efficient optimization. In this paper, we propose a new Riemannian metric that is specifically tailored to LoRA to close the gap to full fine-tuning at the weight level. We theoretically show that LoRA with our preconditioning induced by this metric satisfies the following two properties at each iteration: (i) The weight update follows the direction closest to the gradient of full fine-tuning within the subspace of first-order weight changes allowed by the LoRA parameterization. (ii) The updated weight matrix is closer in Frobenius norm to that of full fine-tuning than the updated weight matrices of LoRA with conventional preconditioning and without preconditioning. These theoretical insights suggest that our preconditioning makes LoRA better approximate full fine-tuning, thereby leading to more efficient optimization. Experiments show the effectiveness and efficiency of our preconditioning for LoRA on fine-tuning tasks with language and vision domains.

</details>


### [145] [Detecting a Shift Is Not Enough: Exact Minimax Limits of Linear Representation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08069)
*Anuar Aimoldin,Yankai Chen,Ayana Mussabayeva,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把"消除两个数据源之间的均值偏移"建模为统计决策问题，在硬失真预算下学习一个作用于两侧的线性映射，推导出精确的有限样本极小极大风险 $(d-k)\mathbb{E}[1/(d+2J)]$（$J\sim\mathrm{Pois}(\kappa/2)$），并揭示检测与修复之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个数据源之间的均值偏移容易检测却难以在不显著改变数据表示的情况下消除。现有线性概念擦除方法（MP、SAL、LEACE）虽能消除校准差异，但缺乏对其在新鲜数据上残留偏移量、以及所需校准量的理论刻画，实践中难以判断校正不足时应改进方法、增加单被试记录数还是增加被试数。

Method: 将偏移消除形式化为统计决策问题：给定成对校准测量在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中的含噪差异，在硬失真预算下学习单一线性映射同时作用于两个源，使新鲜数据上残留偏移最小。推导所有此类映射的精确有限样本极小极大风险，并分析擦除均值校准差异的投影方法在未知 $\kappa$ 与噪声尺度下的最优性，考察配对、各向异性噪声与选择性弃权下的稳健性。

Result: 极小极大风险为 $(d-k)\mathbb{E}[1/(d+2J)]$，其中 $J\sim\mathrm{Pois}(\kappa/2)$，预算允许删除 $k$ 个方向，$\kappa$ 为校准信噪比。投影掉均值校准差异即可在不知 $\kappa$ 和噪声尺度下达到该风险。检测偏移仅需 $\kappa\gg\sqrt d$，而恒定失真下移除固定比例偏移需 $\kappa\asymp d$。配对在非高斯共享内容下保持精确，投影在各向异性噪声下保持保证，选择性弃权无法弥合差距。在配对临床与可穿戴睡眠 EEG 上，公式预测了新参与者中残留的设备偏移，且每人更多记录很快不再有帮助。

Conclusion: 检测与修复之间存在本质的"检测-修复鸿沟"。标准线性概念擦除器消除相同的校准差异，因此该公式在拟合前即可精确给出它们在新数据上的残留偏移及目标所需校准量。这些结果可判断校正不足时应采用更好的方法、更多单被试记录还是更多被试。

Abstract: A mean shift between two data sources can be easy to detect but hard to remove without substantially changing their representations. We cast its removal as a statistical decision problem: from noisy differences between paired calibration measurements in $\mathbb{R}^d$, learn one linear map, applied to both sources under a hard distortion budget, that leaves as little of the shift as possible on fresh data. We derive the exact finite-sample minimax risk over all such maps, $(d-k) \mathbb{E}[1/(d+2J)]$ with $J\sim\mathrm{Pois}(κ/2)$, where the budget allows deleting $k$ directions and $κ$ is the calibration signal-to-noise ratio. Projecting out the mean calibration difference attains it without knowing $κ$ or the noise scale. This exposes a detection-repair gap: detecting the shift needs only $κ\gg\sqrt d$, whereas removing a fixed fraction of it at constant distortion needs $κ\asymp d$, as for estimating its direction. Standard linear concept erasers (MP, SAL, LEACE) remove the same calibration difference, so the formula gives, before fitting, exactly how much shift they leave on fresh data and how much calibration a target requires. The limit is robust: pairing keeps it exact for non-Gaussian shared content, the projection keeps its guarantee under anisotropic noise, and selective abstention cannot close the gap. On paired clinical and wearable sleep EEG, where differences between participants act as calibration noise, the formula predicts the device shift left in new participants, and more recordings per person soon stop helping. Together, these results tell whether a correction that falls short needs a better method, more recordings, or more participants.

</details>


### [146] [Optimization Encoders: Rethinking Second-Order Meta-Learning for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08075)
*Rudolf L. M. van Herten,Soufiane Ben Haddou,Rachit Saluja,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"优化编码器"视角，将元学习中的潜变量优化形式化为一种编码过程，统一了二阶微分、潜参数化和任务监督，并据此设计了基于等变Transformer的神经场MetaLF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件神经场能连续表示信号，但其效果取决于如何从观测数据推断条件潜表示。在元学习中，这种编码依赖解码器诱导的梯度更新，使表示学习与解码器设计紧密耦合，缺乏对编码过程本身的理论刻画与设计准则。

Method: 将潜优化解释为"优化编码器"，统一二阶微分、潜参数化与任务监督的作用；据此提出二阶元学习以端到端训练编码过程与解码器，并厘清一阶近似所丢失的学习路径。具体实现MetaLF：基于等变Transformer的神经场，通过自注意力对潜点云进行上下文化，使局部观测影响场预测及表示构建的更新；将内层编码目标与外层任务监督解耦，统一重建、分类与分割于端到端元学习框架，测试时仅用重建式潜适应。

Result: 多项式场受控实验表明潜变量协调可降低有效秩并增强与底层函数空间的对齐；在图像与3D形状重建中，MetaLF在三到五次梯度更新内提升保真度，并支持跨图像、形状与体数据的语义预测。

Conclusion: 这些发现将优化编码器视角确立为围绕表示如何被构建、协调与使用来设计神经场的统一基础。

Abstract: Conditional neural fields represent signals continuously, but their effectiveness depends on how the conditional latent representations are inferred from observed data. In meta-learning, this encoding occurs through gradient updates induced by the decoder, tying representation learning directly to decoder design. We formalize this connection by interpreting latent optimization as an optimization encoder, unifying the roles of second-order differentiation, latent parameterization, and task supervision. This concept enables second-order meta-learning for end-to-end training of the encoding procedure alongside the decoder, and clarifies which learning pathway first-order approximations discard. Guided by this view, we introduce Attentive Latent Fields (MetaLF), an equivariant transformer-based neural field that contextualizes a latent pointcloud through self-attention. These interactions shape both field predictions and the updates that construct their representation, allowing local observations to inform coherent non-local structure. Disentangling the inner encoding objective from outer task supervision unifies reconstruction, classification, and segmentation within an end-to-end meta-learning framework, using reconstruction-only latent adaptation at test time. Controlled experiments on polynomial fields link latent coordination to lower effective rank and stronger alignment with the underlying function space. Across image and 3D shape reconstruction, MetaLF improves fidelity within three to five gradient updates, while supporting semantic prediction across images, shapes, and volumes. Together, these findings position the optimization encoder perspective as a unified basis for designing neural fields around how representations are constructed, coordinated, and used.

</details>


### [147] [Enhancing Diffusion Language Models with Autoregressive Post-Training Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08108)
*Yiming Qin,Ke Wang,Amel Abdelraheem,Adam Hazimeh,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 A2D，一个无需训练的框架，通过回收自回归(AR)后训练权重更新来增强扩散语言模型(dLLM)，提升指令遵循、数学推理和代码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型通常由预训练 AR 模型转换而来以继承其表示，但转换后往往忽略 AR 祖先所积累的丰富后训练(post-training)生态。作者希望验证这些已有的 AR 后训练权重更新能否被有效回收，用于增强扩散模型。

Method: A2D 直接在扩散基座模型上叠加 AR 后训练权重更新(尽管存在 AR-to-diffusion 转换带来的变化)，并将 AR 更新与扩散后训练更新在权重空间中进行组合(composing)，从而同时保留两个范式的收益。该框架无需额外训练或推理时计算。

Result: 直接加 AR 后训练更新使扩散基座模型性能接近直接扩散后训练的效果；AR 与扩散后训练更新在权重空间近乎正交，却在扩散模型中诱导出高度对齐的表示变化，且互为补充；组合权重可保留两者增益并进一步提升已后训练的扩散模型。在 Dream、DreamReasoner、DiffuCoder、Dream-Coder、Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion、DiffusionGemma 等模型上，A2D 在 SFT 与 RL 更新下均稳定提升指令遵循、数学推理与代码能力。

Conclusion: 现有 AR 后训练权重更新可被有效回收以增强扩散语言模型；A2D 提供了一种简单、无需训练的途径，将 AR 后训练能力迁移至扩散基座模型，并通过组合 AR 与扩散更新持续改进已后训练的扩散模型。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) language models, offering flexible token-update orders and parallel decoding. Recent dLLMs are often initialized from pretrained AR models before diffusion conversion in order to inherit their learned representations. After the conversion, however, they typically ignore the extensive post-training ecosystem of their AR ancestors. In this work, we show that these existing AR post-training weight updates can instead be effectively recycled to enhance diffusion models. Despite the changes by AR-to-diffusion conversion, directly adding an AR post-training weight update to a diffusion base model remains effective, bringing its performance close to that achieved by direct diffusion post-training. Notably, AR and diffusion post-training updates are nearly orthogonal in weight space, yet induce substantially more aligned representation changes in the diffusion model. Their distinct updates are also complementary: composing their weights can retain gains from both regimes and further improve the post-trained diffusion model. Based on these findings, we propose A2D, a simple training-free framework for enhancing diffusion models with existing AR post-training resources. A2D can transfer capabilities from AR post-trained models to diffusion base models, and further improve already post-trained diffusion models by composing AR and diffusion post-training updates. Across various dLLMs, including Dream, DreamReasoner, DiffuCoder, Dream-Coder, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion, and DiffusionGemma, A2D reliably improves instruction following, mathematical reasoning, and coding with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning updates, without additional training, or inference-time computation.

</details>


### [148] [Attenuated in-context identification in time-series foundation models: diagnosis under counterfactual inputs and repair by synthetic forced-system fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08118)
*Hong-In Won*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在具有精确反事实的强制工程系统上检验协变量感知时间序列基础模型（Chronos-2、TimesFM-2.5、TabPFN-TS）的无训练what-if预测能力，发现默认协变量接口要么无记忆、要么衰减动态，需微调才能恢复响应幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量感知TSFM被寄望于为仪器化工厂提供免训练的what-if答案，即"不同未来输入会导致输出如何变化"。但这一能力此前缺乏在具有精确反事实的强制系统上的严格检验，因此作者针对此假设进行系统评测。

Method: 在具有精确反事实的强制工程系统上，对比Chronos-2、TimesFM-2.5、TabPFN-TS与用相同上下文拟合的经典系统辨识（ARX）。通过默认协变量接口测试；在推理时加入context dither；并用合成强制系统做26分钟微调；另训练一个专门的in-context identifier作为对照。同时用配对反事实输入和打乱的未来输入在实测记录上检验两项性质。

Result: TimesFM-2.5和TabPFN-TS为无记忆：输入变化效应是同一时刻的函数（TimesFM-2.5的R²=1.000）。Chronos-2能识别上下文动力学但衰减之：预测效应仅为真值的0.33–0.80，脉冲响应形状错误，单自由度振子误差在8192上下文样本时停在0.57，而用256样本拟合的ARX可达0.02。Context dither在六类合成数据上无训练即降低what-if误差。26分钟微调恢复响应幅度（敏感度0.83–0.96），在Wiener-Hammerstein和留出摩擦类上优于结构无关辨识；专门in-context identifier接近其表现，说明增益主要来自强制系统数据。在四个实测工厂中的三个上经典辨识仍明显更优，且微调模型失去部分单变量预测能力。

Conclusion: 当前协变量感知TSFM的默认接口无法可靠表征动力学：或完全无记忆，或系统性衰减动态，与经典辨识差距显著。小规模微调可部分恢复，但增益主要源于数据而非模型先验，且牺牲预测技能。配对反事实输入是揭示衰减的关键探针，单用打乱未来输入则不能。

Abstract: Covariate-aware time-series foundation models (TSFMs) promise training-free what-if answers for instrumented plants: the change in output that a different future input would cause. We test this on forced engineering systems with exact counterfactuals, comparing Chronos-2, TimesFM-2.5 and TabPFN-TS with classical system identification fitted to the same context. Through their default covariate interfaces, TimesFM-2.5 and TabPFN-TS are memoryless: the predicted effect of an input change is a same-time function of that change ($R^2 = 1.000$ for TimesFM-2.5). Chronos-2 identifies dynamics in context but attenuates them. Its predicted effect is 0.33-0.80 of the true effect, its recovered impulse response has the wrong shape, and its error on a one-degree-of-freedom oscillator levels off at 0.57 with 8192 context samples, where ARX fitted to 256 samples reaches 0.02. Context dither at inference lowers the what-if error on all six synthetic classes without training. A 26-minute fine-tune on synthetic forced systems restores the response magnitude (sensitivity 0.83-0.96) and outperforms structure-agnostic identification on Wiener-Hammerstein and a held-out friction class. A specialised in-context identifier trained on the same data comes close, so the forced-system data carry most of the gain. On three of four measured plants classical identification remains clearly better, and the fine-tuned model loses part of its univariate forecasting skill. Paired counterfactual inputs, together with shuffled future inputs on measured records, test two properties: whether the covariate interface can represent dynamics and whether the pretraining prior covers the plant's time scale. Only the counterfactual pairs expose the attenuation.

</details>


### [149] [Do LLMs Act on What They Know? From Partner Representations to Cooperative Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08129)
*Yuhwan Jeong,Jinnyeong Yang,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在受控的Hanabi衍生环境中，八个LLM能通过线性探针较好地解码意图约定但难以据此调整接收决策；相比抽象规则陈述，将约定转为具体行动推荐能更有效提升合作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与陌生伙伴合作时，需要适应事先未知的沟通约定。论文旨在探究LLM能否从可用信息中解码伙伴的约定，并将其转化为实际的接收决策与合作收益。

Method: 构建基于Hanabi的受控环境，采用脚本化提示生成、LLM控制的接收决策与冻结权重。在八个LLM上进行线性探针分析，比较意图约定与目标约定的可解码性；随后对比以“一般规则”或“外部计算行动推荐”两种形式呈现探针预测约定与真实约定；并以Qwen3-8B进行匹配状态陈述反转的案例研究及激活迁移实验。

Result: 线性探针恢复意图约定的准确率显著高于目标约定，但接收选择与发送方约定并不一致。规则陈述带来的合作变化有限且依赖模型，行动推荐平均增益更大。Qwen3-8B中，匹配状态陈述反转显示对行动推荐的敏感性远高于规则陈述。来自oracle-action与非oracle提示重述供体的激活迁移可提升两类行动的意图准确率，但替代方案无法可靠复现该收益。

Conclusion: 研究区分了约定可解码性、对约定信息的敏感性与合作表现三个层面，并指出LLM在将可用伙伴信息转化为接收决策方面存在局限。

Abstract: Cooperation with unfamiliar partners requires adapting to communication conventions that are not known in advance. We study this problem in a controlled Hanabi-derived environment with scripted hint generation, LLM-controlled receiving decisions, and frozen model weights. Across eight LLMs, linear probes recover intent conventions substantially more accurately than target conventions, yet receiving choices do not consistently agree with the sender's convention. We compare probe-predicted and ground-truth conventions presented either as general rules or as externally computed action recommendations. Rule statements yield modest and model-dependent changes in cooperation, whereas action translation produces larger gains on average. In a Qwen3-8B case study, matched-state statement reversals reveal much greater sensitivity to action recommendations than to rule statements. Activation transfers from oracle-action and non-oracle hint-restatement donors improve intent accuracy on both action classes, but the tested alternatives do not reliably reproduce these benefits. Together, these results distinguish convention decodability, sensitivity to convention information, and cooperative performance, and highlight limitations in turning available partner information into receiving decisions.

</details>


### [150] [Mu-DisCoCat: A Variational Pipeline for Compositional Generalization on Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08131)
*Mina Abbaszadeh,Matilda Karabina Moore,Raem Haq,Martha Lewis,Mehrnoosh Sadrzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mu-DisCoCat，一个多模态变分量子学习框架，首次实现组合概念泛化(CoCoGen)在量子硬件上的执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合概念泛化(CoCoGen)是AI的基础挑战。DisCoCat等组合语义模型通过张量推广向量，但学习张量时存在扩展瓶颈；将其映射到变分量子电路(VQC)可解决文本场景的瓶颈，但尚未扩展到多模态场景。

Method: Mu-DisCoCat框架：先从单物体图文对学习稳定的物体表示，然后固定这些表示，在多变物体情境中学习它们之间的关系。经典模拟中使用Uhlmann态保真度计算多模态电路表示间的重叠度；部署时采用破坏性SWAP测试，在含噪量子模拟器(多个IBM fake backend、IQM FakeAphrodite)及IBM Marrakesh量子处理器上评估。

Result: 经典模拟中关系型OOD准确率高于所评估的CLIP基线；尽管存在真实设备噪声，硬件执行模型与模拟保真度保持强正相关，能可靠区分未见过的相似与不相似对。

Conclusion: 工作建立了在VQC上执行CoCoGen的框架，展示了近期待量子硬件的可行用例。

Abstract: Achieving compositional concept generalization (CoCoGen), the ability to understand novel situations by recombining learned primitives, remains a fundamental challenge in artificial intelligence. Compositional semantic models such as Compositional Distributional Semantics (DisCoCat) offer solutions by generalising vectors to tensors, but suffer from scaling bottlenecks when learning the tensors. Mapping DisCoCat onto Variational Quantum Circuits (VQCs) resolves this limitation for text, yet the methodology has not been expanded to multimodal situations such as the ones involved in CoCoGen. This paper introduces Mu-DisCoCat: a multimodal variational quantum learning framework for DisCoCat that achieves CoCoGen. The framework first learns stable object representations from single-object image-text pairs, then fixes these and uses them to learn the relations between them in multi-object situations. In classical simulations, the model used Uhlmann state fidelity to compute the overlap between the multimodal circuit representations and achieved higher relational OOD accuracy than the evaluated CLIP baseline. Its deployment was evaluated using the destructive SWAP test across noisy quantum emulators, including a range of IBM fake backends, IQM FakeAphrodite, and the IBM Marrakesh quantum processor. Despite real-world device noise, the hardware-executed models maintained a strong positive correlation with simulated fidelities, reliably distinguishing unseen similar and dissimilar pairs. Our work establishes a framework for executing CoCoGen on VQCs, demonstrating a viable use case for near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [151] [Symphony for Text Generation: Benchmarking Clinical Note Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08161)
*Daniel Varab,Victor Petrén Bach Hansen,Asbjørn W. Helge,Kevin Pelgrims,Mathias Baltzersen,Adrian Young-San Roessler,Vanessa Klungtvedt,Maximilian Brand,Lasse Krogsbøll,Henrik Cullen,Lars Maaløe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出MedConv多语言临床数据集并构建受控临床评估框架，对比Corti与两款通用AI环境记录软件，结果显示Corti在临床笔记质量上持平或更优，且其可配置API能针对不同文档场景微调质量维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境文档系统正快速普及，但其对临床笔记质量的影响缺乏充分表征，且缺少可复现的多语言对比评估方法与数据集。

Method: 构建MedConv数据集（300例临床问诊，涵盖英语、丹麦语、德语），结合ACI-BENCH基准；提出受控临床评估框架，融合蕴含（entailment）指标与基于LLM评判的成对比较，采用源自PDSQI-9的八个维度进行打分。

Result: Corti基于API的文本生成基础设施与领先商业记录软件表现持平或更优；其可配置API具备足够灵活性，可针对特定文档用例微调质量维度。

Conclusion: 研究提出了可复现的环境文档系统评估方法论并公开数据集，以支持未来对临床记录系统的可复现比较。

Abstract: Ambient documentation systems are rapidly gaining adoption, yet their impact on clinical note quality remains poorly characterized. We introduce MedConv, a multilingual dataset of 300 clinical encounters in English, Danish, and German, and use it alongside the Ambient Clinical Intelligence benchmark (ACI-BENCH) to compare Corti, a clinical AI platform, with two leading, accessible ambient scribe software applications built on general-purpose AI. We present a controlled clinical evaluation framework that combines entailment metrics with LLM-judged pairwise comparisons across eight dimensions adopted from PDSQI-9. Results show that Corti's API-based text-generation infrastructure is on par with or outperforms leading commercial scribes. We further show that Corti's configurable API provides the flexibility necessary to fine-tune quality dimensions for specific documentation use cases. We present the evaluation methodology and release a dataset to support future reproducible comparison of ambient documentation systems.

</details>


### [152] [Align, Then Correct: Training-Free Two-Stage Low-Rank Compensation for Extremely Quantized Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08164)
*Seobin Song,Geonho Lee,Janghwan Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段闭式框架，修正现有低秩量化误差补偿(LQEC)的对称校准与仅二阶目标两大简化，仅靠截断SVD实现，在2比特下显著降低困惑度并恢复更多FP16精度差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LQEC通过在每个冻结量化权重旁附加闭式rank-r适配器来恢复激进量化的精度损失，但存在两个共同简化：一是对称校准（用相同激活评估全精度与补偿权重），导致补偿目标本质高秩，固定秩预算只能捕获小部分；二是只最小化损失的二阶项，而补偿模型并非驻点，每层仍残留大于所施加补偿的一阶下降方向，任何重构目标都无法吸收。本文旨在消除这两个简化。

Method: 提出两阶段闭式框架：阶段一在Fisher加权的不对称目标下对齐每层输出与全精度模型，将秩预算集中于可压缩目标；阶段二在补偿模型上重新测量统计量，施加秩约束的自然梯度步以吸收剩余一阶信号。每个适配器均由单次截断SVD得到，反向传播仅用于收集统计量，无需训练。

Result: 在QuIP#的2比特下，WikiText-2困惑度：Qwen3-8B从12.43降至10.26，Qwen3-4B从21.11降至13.22。在留出C4语料上，分别恢复至FP16差距的51%和84%，最强基线为31%和63%；在七任务零样本平均、更高比特宽度及不同量化器下均有一致增益。

Conclusion: 通过同时移除对称校准与仅二阶目标的简化，两阶段闭式补偿框架在无需训练的前提下，显著优于现有LQEC方法，并在多种设置下保持稳健增益。

Abstract: Low-rank quantization error compensation (LQEC) recovers the accuracy lost under aggressive weight quantization by attaching a closed-form rank-$r$ adapter beside each frozen quantized weight, without any training. We show that existing compensators are limited by two shared simplifications. They calibrate symmetrically, evaluating the full-precision and compensated weights on the same activation, which yields a compensation target that is inherently high-rank -- so a fixed rank budget captures only a small fraction of it. And they minimize only the second-order term of the loss, although the compensated model is not stationary: a first-order descent direction larger than the applied compensation itself remains in every layer, and no reconstruction objective can absorb it. We propose a two-stage closed-form framework that removes both simplifications. Stage 1 aligns each layer's output with the full-precision model under a Fisher-weighted asymmetric objective, concentrating the rank budget on a rank-compressible target. Stage 2 re-measures statistics on the compensated model and applies a rank-constrained natural-gradient step that absorbs the remaining first-order signal. Every adapter is the result of a single truncated SVD; backward passes serve only to collect statistics. At 2 bits under QuIP#, our method reduces WikiText-2 perplexity from 12.43 to 10.26 on Qwen3-8B and from 21.11 to 13.22 on Qwen3-4B. On the held-out C4 corpus, it recovers 51% and 84% of the gap to FP16, versus 31% and 63% for the strongest baseline, with consistent gains in the seven-task zero-shot average, at higher bit-widths, and under a distinct quantizer.

</details>


### [153] [On the Intrinsic Limited Robustness of Latent-Based Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08178)
*Cheng-Han Yeh,Kuan-chun Yu,Cheng-Chang Tsai,Chun-Shien Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了扩散模型潜在空间水印方法对图像扰动（尤其是RST几何变换）缺乏不变性的原因，推导了像素扰动与潜在空间影响的扰动上界，并给出检测机制的解析建模，实验验证了该范式固有的鲁棒性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于潜在空间的扩散模型水印方法高估了其对图像失真（旋转、缩放、平移RST等）的鲁棒性，且该范式受限于水印嵌入域本身的固有缺陷，亟需理论解释。

Method: 通过放松不变关系，推导刻画像素空间扰动与潜在空间对应影响的最大扰动界；提出首个解析化公式，覆盖实际检测机制的全部组成部分；并通过实验验证理论结论。

Result: 理论推导与实验结果一致表明，在现有设计范式下，基于潜在空间的水印方法本质上鲁棒性有限，尤其在几何变换等扰动下表现不足。

Conclusion: 该范式存在内在鲁棒性上限；论文提供了解析工具与设计准则，为未来研究改进扩散模型水印的鲁棒性指明方向。

Abstract: Existing latent-based watermarking methods for diffusion models have overestimated their robustness to image distortions, including geometric transformations such as rotation, scaling, and translation (RST). Moreover, this paradigm of watermarking approaches may suffer from inherent limitations arising from the domain in which the watermark is embedded. In this paper, we provide the first theoretical analysis explaining why these methods lack invariance to perturbations. By relaxing the invariant relation, we derive a maximum perturbation bound that characterizes the relationship between pixel-space perturbations and their corresponding effects in latent space. In addition, we present the first analytical formulation that captures all components of practical detection mechanisms. Finally, we conduct experiments to validate the theoretical findings and the limitations of latent-based watermarking methods. Our theoretical and empirical results indicate that, under the current design paradigm, latent-based watermarking methods intrinsically exhibit limited robustness. We conclude by providing the analytical tool and design guidelines that future research could follow.

</details>


### [154] [Finding the Heads and the Neurons Responsible for Network Information Retrieval in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08200)
*Abdul Kadir,Md Mohasin Hossain,Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究检验语言模型中识别网络基础设施信息（主机名与IP配对）的责任单元：在头（head）层面答案一致肯定，跨5个模型1-9个头即可达99.5-100%泛化准确率；但责任能否下推到单个神经元则因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望回答两个问题：(1) 特定注意力头、乃至头内特定神经元是否负责识别上下文中的网络基础设施信息；(2) 这种责任能否通过因果（消融/干预）而非仅相关性来验证。并进一步探讨头级责任能否下推到神经元级。

Method: 在跨3个架构家族、5个模型上：(1) 用因果消融筛选定位候选头，并用匹配的负样本与无上下文对照检验选择性；(2) 用因果干预与相关性排序独立定位头内关键神经元，比较二者一致性；(3) 在与发现数据不同的机构独立数据集（反向DNS记录）上测试头级与神经元级检测器的迁移表现。

Result: 头级：每个模型仅需1-9个头（共128-1152个候选）即可支撑检测器达99.5-100%留出准确率。神经元级：一模型中信号集中于单个神经元，因果干预与相关性排名完全一致（AUC=1.000，等同整头）；另一模型中整头有效（AUC=1.000）但最佳因果排序神经元无效（AUC=0.665），责任分散于头部；其余三模型介于两者之间。独立数据集上，所有模型整头检测器100%标记正样本，单神经元版本迁移性较差，其中一模型低于随机水平。

Conclusion: 针对特定网络信息实体的因果头定位跨模型与架构普遍成立且可靠；但这一发现能否下推到单个神经元因模型而异，需逐模型验证；头级检测器的跨机构迁移性优于单神经元检测器。

Abstract: We ask whether specific attention heads, and more finely specific neurons inside those heads, are responsible for recognizing that a language model's context contains network infrastructure information (a hostname paired with its IP address), and whether that responsibility can be validated causally rather than by correlation alone. At the head level the answer is yes, across five models spanning three architecture families: in every model, a small set of heads (1 to 9 out of 128 to 1152 candidates), found by causal ablation screening and tested for selectivity against matched negative and context-free controls, supports a detector with 99.5--100\% held-out accuracy. We then ask whether a head's responsibility concentrates into one neuron or stays spread across its dimensions; this is model-specific. In one model, the top head's signal concentrates into a single neuron, found independently by both a causal intervention and a correlational ranking, which agree exactly (AUC = 1.000, matching the full head). In another, the single clean head works as a whole (AUC = 1.000) but the best causally ranked neuron inside it does not (AUC = 0.665), so the responsibility there is spread across the head. The remaining three models fall in between. On an independent dataset collected by a different institution (reverse-DNS records rather than the discovery data), every model's full-head detector flags 100\% of positive records; the single-neuron versions transfer less reliably, and in one model score below chance. Causal head-finding for a specific network-information entity works across models and architectures; how far that finding can be pushed down to individual neurons varies, and needs to be checked for each model.

</details>


### [155] [Reinforcement Learning with Segment Reward Feedback under Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08271)
*Fengxu Liu,Siwei Wang,Gal Dalal,Shie Mannor,Yihan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性函数逼近下带分段奖励反馈的强化学习，分别针对等长分段（已知/未知转移）与任意分段设计算法，揭示分段粒度对学习 regret 的影响规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典RL假设每个状态-动作对都有奖励反馈，但现实中（如自动驾驶）这种细粒度反馈代价高昂，而纯轨迹级反馈又过于稀疏、学习效率低。因此需要一个介于两者之间的通用反馈模型，并在大状态空间下（线性函数逼近）进行研究，回答分段反馈的粒度与分段方式如何影响学习。

Method: 在线性函数逼近下研究分段奖励反馈RL。对已知转移的等长分段，分别针对二值反馈和求和反馈设计bitssegd与edlinucbsegd算法，采用后验采样加规划保证计算效率，并用E-最优实验设计达到近最优性；对未知转移的等长分段，提出统一的seglsvits框架及两种实例，将后验估计的奖励参数融入最小二乘值迭代；此外设计支持任意分段的uneqsegbitsd算法，并给出相应regret上界。

Result: 对等长分段已知转移情形证明了近匹配的下界。核心洞见：二值反馈下，增加分段数量可带来指数级regret下降；而求和反馈下，分段粒度对学习影响不大。对任意分段，在椭圆势分析下，状态-动作特征对regret的影响仅通过对数项体现，等长分段表现最佳。

Conclusion: 分段反馈是一种桥接细粒度与轨迹级反馈的通用模型。分段粒度对学习的影响取决于反馈类型：二值反馈受益于更细分段（指数级），求和反馈影响有限；在特征层面，等长分段优于任意分段，特征影响仅呈对数级别。

Abstract: Classical reinforcement learning (RL) assumes that a reward is observed for every visited state-action pair. However, in real-world applications such as autonomous driving, such fine-grained feedback can be costly or difficult to collect, whereas trajectory-level feedback may be too sparse for efficient learning. To provide a general feedback model bridging these two extremes and handle large state spaces, we study RL with segment reward feedback under linear function approximation. Our work answers how the granularity of segment feedback and the choice of segmentation influence learning. For equal-length segments with known transitions, we design algorithms $\bitssegd$ and $\edlinucbsegd$ for binary and sum feedback types, respectively. They adopt posterior sampling with planning to achieve computational efficiency and the E-optimal experimental design to attain near-optimality. Nearly matching lower bounds are established. For equal-length segments with unknown transitions, we develop a unified $\seglsvits$ framework with two instantiations for binary and sum feedback, which carefully integrates the posterior estimated reward parameters into least-squares value iteration. These results reveal a fundamental insight: under binary feedback, increasing the number of segments significantly reduces the regret through an exponential factor, while surprisingly, under sum feedback, the granularity of segments does not affect learning much. Finally, to investigate whether segmenting according to state-action features can further expedite learning, we design an algorithm $\uneqsegbitsd$ that allows arbitrary segmentations. The resulting regret bound shows that under the usual elliptical potential analysis, the influence of state-action features on the regret appears only through logarithmic factors, and equal segmentation achieves the best performance.

</details>


### [156] [Performative Prediction with Selective Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08272)
*Giovani Valdrighi,Isabel Valera,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将performative prediction扩展到含选择性标签的场景，指出仅在观测数据上重训练会失去稳定点收敛保证，并提出基于正标签概率置信区间的worst-case目标以保持与真实稳定点的有界距离，实验表明其性能接近完全标签访问下的RRM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: performative prediction假设部署模型后能获取特征与标签的完整分布，但现实中常存在选择性标签（仅观测到接受子集的标签），该问题此前被忽略，可能导致重训练过程被误导、失去收敛保证。

Method: 1) 形式化含选择性标签的performative prediction；2) 分析仅在观测数据上重训练的失败模式；3) 基于正标签概率的置信区间构造worst-case目标；4) 对该目标应用RRM；5) 在条件标签分布敏感性假设下，利用先前接受数据逐步收紧置信区间。

Result: RRM在观测数据上重训练可能收敛到误导性结果；对worst-case目标应用RRM可保持与真实稳定点的有界距离；敏感性假设下置信区间可随时间收紧；在带公平正则的借贷实验中，方法性能接近完全标签访问下的RRM。

Conclusion: 选择性标签会破坏performative prediction的收敛保证，通过置信区间驱动的鲁棒优化可缓解该问题并逼近完全标签访问的效果。

Abstract: Many social applications of machine learning exhibit performative effects: population behavior changes in response to deployed models. Performative prediction studies this interaction through a distribution map that relates each model to the population distribution it induces. One of the main results in this framework showed that repeated risk minimization (RRM), which updates models by retraining on the most recent data, can converge to a stable model that minimizes risk on its own induced distribution. However, existing analyses typically assume access to the complete distributions of features and labels after model deployment, ignoring the possibility of selective labels: observing labels only for the accepted subset of the population. In this work, we formalize performative prediction with selective labels and show that retraining only on observed data can misguide the retraining procedure and undermine the guarantees of convergence to a stable solution. We then propose a worst-case objective based on knowledge of a confidence interval on the probability of a positive label. Applying RRM to this objective permits us to remain within a bounded distance to the true stable point. Under a sensitivity assumption on the conditional label distribution, we further show how previously accepted data can tighten these confidence intervals over time. Experiments in a lending application with fairness regularization show that our robust optimization approach closely matches the performance of RRM with complete label access.

</details>


### [157] [Scalable extraction and visualization of multi-attribute logical and functional dependencies in tabular data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08287)
*Chaithra Umesh,Arvind Lomrore,Neethu D,Kristian Seegel-Schultz,Saptarshi Bej,Olaf Wolkenhauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LDTool与HLDTool，用于从表格数据中提取并可视化多属性逻辑依赖（LD）与函数依赖（FD），显著提升高维场景下的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中属性间的结构关系对机器学习与模式识别至关重要。函数依赖（FD）发现已有较多研究，但逻辑依赖（LD）的可扩展发现（尤其随属性数量与依赖阶数增加）仍缺乏探索；LD刻画非确定性、条件特定的成对或多属性关系。此外，现有方法缺乏统一框架同时提取多属性LD与FD。

Method: 提出LDTool与HLDTool：LDTool将依赖发现从成对关系扩展到多属性逻辑依赖；HLDTool通过超图引导的搜索空间缩减实现可扩展提取；两者统一支持多属性LD与FD的提取与可视化。

Result: 在3个模拟数据集与11个真实数据集上验证：LDTool提取的FD与现有FD发现方法一致，且在高维特征空间中运行时间更低；HLDTool可在含数百个特征的数据集上完成依赖发现；框架提供依赖结构的可解释可视化。

Conclusion: 该框架可提取有意义的LD与FD并提升可扩展性，支持探索性数据分析与合成表格数据定量评估等应用。

Abstract: Understanding the structural relationships among attributes in tabular data is fundamental to machine learning and pattern recognition. While functional dependency (FD) discovery has been extensively studied, scalable discovery of logical dependencies (LDs), particularly as the number of attributes and dependency order increase, remains underexplored. These dependencies capture non-deterministic, condition-specific relationships among pairwise or multiple attributes. Furthermore, existing approaches do not provide a unified framework for extracting multi-attribute LDs and FDs. To address these limitations, we propose LDTool and HLDTool for extracting and visualizing multi-attribute LDs and FDs from tabular data. LDTool extends dependency discovery beyond pairwise relationships, while HLDTool enables scalable extraction through hypergraph-guided search-space reduction. Experiments on three simulated and eleven real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework extracts meaningful LDs and FDs while improving scalability. LDTool recovers the same FDs as existing FD discovery methods with lower runtime in high-dimensional feature spaces, whereas HLDTool enables dependency discovery in datasets with hundreds of features. The proposed framework provides interpretable visualizations of dependency structures and supports applications in exploratory data analysis and the quantitative evaluation of synthetic tabular data.

</details>


### [158] [OxiGen: Oxidation-State-Aware Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08296)
*Dylan John,Kim E. Jelfs,Alex M. Ganose,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OxiGen是一种考虑氧化态的晶体扩散生成模型，通过结构化输出层与有限状态自动机的精确推断保证全局电中性，显著提升氧化态保真度与稳定、唯一、新颖晶体的生成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型可加速无机材料逆设计，但生成实验可实现的晶体仍具挑战。氧化态常用于评估晶体成分有效性并指导材料发现；现有晶体生成模型虽能生成电荷中性分配的材料，却难以复现合成材料中观测到的氧化态分布。

Method: 提出OxiGen，一种在生成过程中显式表示氧化态的晶体扩散模型。该模型通过结构化输出层，在有限状态自动机上执行精确推断，从构造上强制满足全局电中性约束。

Result: 实证表明，OxiGen显著提升氧化态保真度，在评估方法中生成稳定、唯一且新颖晶体的比率最高，并在性质条件化下仍保持高成分有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模氧化态并结合电中性约束的生成框架可有效改善晶体生成的可合成性与分布真实性，为无机材料逆设计提供更可靠的途径。

Abstract: Generative models have the potential to accelerate inorganic materials discovery by enabling inverse design, but generating experimentally realisable crystals remains challenging. Oxidation states are widely used to assess the compositional validity of crystals and guide inorganic materials discovery. While existing generative models for crystals can generate materials with charge-neutral oxidation-state assignments, they poorly reproduce the distributions of oxidation states observed in synthesised materials. To address this limitation, we propose OxiGen, an oxidation-state-aware crystal diffusion model that explicitly represents oxidation states during generation. OxiGen enforces global charge neutrality by construction using a structured output layer with exact inference over a finite-state automaton. Empirically, OxiGen substantially improves oxidation-state fidelity, generates the highest rate of stable, unique, and novel crystals among evaluated methods, and maintains high compositional validity even under property conditioning.

</details>


### [159] [Structure-Aware Graph Abstention for Reliable Selective Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08322)
*Jianxiang Xie,Belal Alsinglawi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将选择性预测中的可靠性区分为"实例级合理性"与"关系一致性"两个轴，提出用可学习稀疏图与Dirichlet式结构能量E_struct来量化后者，并以此作为新的弃权门控信号，在多个基准上相对TEM在相同覆盖率下降低选择性MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性预测门控（如TEM）把每条预测整体打一个分，对多变量输出而言，整条轨迹看似合理，却可能违反变量间的依赖关系，因此需要引入独立的关系一致性可靠性维度。

Method: 将实例级合理性与关系一致性视为两个独立的可靠性轴；用可学习稀疏图刻画变量间结构，定义Dirichlet式结构能量E_struct；训练时采用误差加权图正则化与分数-误差对齐；以该结构能量作为门控信号在保留覆盖率预算下弃权高风险测试窗口。

Result: 在7个长时程基准与4个骨干网络上，结构门控在匹配覆盖率下常能相对TEM降低选择性MSE，跨变量结构信息更丰富时增益最大；但增益并非普遍存在，说明该信号是互补性的弃权依据。表1为Protocol A排序诊断（seed 2024），表3为对齐子集上的三种子可部署Protocol B，完整验证-测试网格见附录A。

Conclusion: 关系一致性是可选择性预测中与实例级合理性互补的可靠性轴，结构门控提供了额外的弃权信号，可在部分场景下提升选择性预测的准确性，但并非无条件优于TEM。

Abstract: Selective forecasting abstains on high-risk test windows under a retained-coverage budget. Existing gates such as TEM (Brusokas et al., 2025) score each forecast as a whole; for multivariate outputs, trajectories can look plausible while violating dependencies among variables. We treat instance-level plausibility and relational consistency as distinct reliability axes and operationalize the latter via a learned sparse graph and a Dirichlet-style structural energy E_struct, trained with error-weighted graph regularization and score-error alignment. On seven long-horizon benchmarks and four backbones, structural gating often reduces selective MSE versus TEM at matched coverage, with the largest gains where cross-variable structure appears more informative in our benchmarks; gains are not universal, indicating a complementary abstention signal. Table 1 is a Protocol A ranking diagnostic (seed 2024); three-seed deployable Protocol B on an aligned subset is in Table 3 (full validation-to-test grids: Appendix A).

</details>


### [160] [Uncertainty Quantification Is Indispensable for Reliable Connectome-Based Graph Learning: A Narrative Review and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08353)
*Mansooreh Pakravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对连接组图学习中的不确定性量化(UQ)框架进行叙述性综述，并通过实证案例揭示深度连接组学中的"置信悖论"：模型判别性能良好但校准严重失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在基于连接组的诊断分类中表现良好，但确定性模型会压制流程噪声与模型歧义，产生过度自信的预测。UQ虽已广泛用于体素级分割，但在连接组图学习中的作用尚未被充分探讨，因此需要系统梳理面向连接组图学习的UQ框架并验证未校准预测的风险。

Method: 采用叙述性综述与实证案例研究相结合的方式。综述部分：梳理神经影像流程中偶然不确定性与认知不确定性的来源，并回顾主流UQ范式，包括贝叶斯近似、集成方法、证据学习与保形预测。案例部分：在SUDMEX CONN数据集上，使用基于动态功能连接(dFC)矩阵的时序图注意力网络(GAT)进行可卡因使用障碍诊断，并通过蒙特卡洛dropout进行事后不确定性审计。

Result: 时序GAT取得80.0%的诊断准确率（F1 = 0.794）。但事后不确定性审计显示严重过度自信（ECE = 0.127），被误分类的受试者其预测置信度最高可达95%，表明判别性能与校准之间存在显著背离。

Conclusion: 严格的UQ、校准与选择性预测机制，是将基于图的生物标志物可信地部署于临床神经科学中不可或缺的环节。判别性能与校准之间的经验性分歧凸显了深度连接组学中的"置信悖论"。

Abstract: While graph neural networks (GNNs) have shown substantial promise in connectome-based diagnostic classification, deterministic models inevitably suppress pipeline-induced noise and model ambiguities, yielding overconfident predictions. Although uncertainty quantification (UQ) is widely adopted in voxel-level segmentation, its role in connectomic graph learning remains largely unaddressed. This paper presents a comprehensive narrative review of UQ frameworks tailored to connectome graph learning alongside an empirical case study demonstrating the perils of uncalibrated predictions. We delineate sources of aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty across neuroimaging pipelines and review prominent UQ paradigms, from Bayesian approximations and ensemble methods to evidential learning and conformal prediction. In our case study, a temporal Graph Attention Network (GAT) trained on dynamic functional connectivity (dFC) matrices from the SUDMEX CONN dataset achieves 80.0% diagnostic accuracy (F1 = 0.794) for Cocaine Use Disorder. However, a post-hoc uncertainty audit via Monte Carlo dropout reveals severe overconfidence (ECE = 0.127), with misclassified subjects assigned prediction confidences up to 95%. This empirical divergence between discrimination and calibration underscores the confidence paradox in deep connectomics. Our findings establish that rigorous UQ, calibration, and selective prediction mechanisms are indispensable for deploying trustworthy graph-based biomarkers in clinical neuroscience.

</details>


### [161] [Sensor Geometry as a Flow-Matching Prior for Multi-Channel Brain Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08355)
*Jaedong Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Graph-Matérn源协方差，把电极几何结构注入流匹配起点，无需学习参数即可提升EEG生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型默认从各向同性高斯源出发，适用于未知数据结构的情形。但多通道脑记录中，电极位置固定、容积传导使邻近电极共变，这一结构先验已知，而现有EEG生成模型仍让网络从头学习，浪费了可用的先验信息。

Method: 仅依据传感器坐标构建k近邻图，以其拉普拉斯算子的Graph-Matérn函数作为源协方差，使流从空间相干的模式而非通道独立噪声出发。该改动不增加任何可学习参数，兼容任意耦合与漂移网络，所有数据集共用同一组三个超参数。

Result: 在8个EEG数据集、4种流匹配方法上，Graph-Matérn源在多数数据集上降低五个临床频段的PSD-KL，几何平均降幅12%–17%，最密集导联的PhysioNet-MI上最高降40%。消融显示增益来自局部传感器位置图的空间特征向量：随机化特征向量但保留特征值谱会消除增益；直接拟合经验数据协方差的先验反而不如各向同性噪声。该构造无需改动即可迁移至MEG、患者特异性颅内EEG网格及交通传感器网络。

Conclusion: 将传感器几何先验显式编码进生成模型的源分布，是一种零参数、通用且跨模态有效的改进，优于数据驱动协方差拟合。

Abstract: Flow-matching models start from an isotropic Gaussian source, the standard choice when the correlation structure of the data is unknown in advance. For multi-channel brain recordings, however, part of this structure is known in advance. Electrodes sit at fixed positions on the head, and volume conduction through the skull and scalp makes nearby electrodes co-vary in a way that is shared across subjects. Existing EEG generative models nonetheless leave the network to learn this from scratch. We put this structure into the source instead. From the sensor coordinates alone, we build a k-nearest-neighbor graph and take a graph-Matérn function of its Laplacian as the source covariance, so the flow starts from spatially coherent patterns rather than channel-independent noise. The change adds no learned parameters, works with any coupling and any drift network, and uses the same three hyperparameters on every dataset. Across eight EEG datasets and four flow-matching methods, the graph-Matérn source lowers the spectral discrepancy between generated and real signals in the five clinical bands (PSD-KL) on most datasets. PSD-KL falls by 12% to 17% in geometric mean over datasets depending on the method and by up to 40% on PhysioNet-MI, the densest montage. We show that the improvement stems from the spatial eigenvectors of the local graph of sensor positions, since randomizing the eigenvectors while preserving the eigenvalue spectrum eliminates the gain. Furthermore, a prior fitted directly to the empirical data covariance performs worse than isotropic noise. The same construction applies unchanged to MEG, intracranial EEG with patient-specific grids, and a traffic-sensor network, lowering PSD-KL for every method on each. https://jd730.github.io/projects/GraphPrior

</details>


### [162] [Evolutionary One-Step Generators: Fast and Diverse Sampling for Discrete Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08367)
*Marcus Vukojevic,Erik Nielsen,Veronica Lachi,Andrea Passerini,Giovanni Iacca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EGO框架，用无反传(ES)方式训练紧凑生成器直接输出离散结构，单步推理即可生成整个图，在分子生成与NAS任务上实现高有效性与高多样性且大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散设计任务（如分子发现）需要低成本地获得多样化且有用的候选集合。但仅保证高有效性不够——若反复生成相同的有效结构，可用的备选方案依然很少。同时优化可行性与多样性很困难，因为很多评价指标只能在硬解码之后才能计算。

Method: 提出EGO（Evolutionary Generators with One-step inference）：直接在离散输出上训练紧凑生成器。方法将分布匹配与结构约束结合，并可选地加入多样性或依赖历史的奖励；采用antithetic低秩进化策略（evolution strategies），无需针对具体准则设计可微代理模型（surrogate）。训练完成后，生成器仅需一次神经网络评估即可产出完整图（graph）。

Result: 分子生成基准上，相对近期单步flow-map基线，每估计密集算子(dense operation)的有效且唯一(effectively valid-and-unique)产出率提升超过50×，同时保持高化学有效性。骨架补全(scaffold completion)任务中，相比MoLeR生成SMILES观察到44.3×加速，在相同时间预算下产生约10×的通过筛选(filter-passing)提案。在化学之外，NAS-Bench-101上EGO产生的预留精英架构数量为松弛梯度训练的1.54×。

Conclusion: 低生成成本有望在各类离散设计任务中实现实时候选生成，支持受限设计空间的交互式探索，以及为下游评估快速构建候选集合。

Abstract: Several discrete design tasks, such as molecular discovery, require diverse collections of useful candidates at low computational cost. High validity alone does not guarantee a useful candidate library: repeatedly generating the same valid structures leaves few distinct alternatives. Training for both feasibility and diversity is challenging because many relevant criteria can only be evaluated after hard decoding. To address this challenge, we propose EGO (Evolutionary Generators with One-step inference), a framework for training compact generators directly on discrete outputs. The method combines distribution matching with structural constraints and optional diversity or history-dependent rewards, using antithetic low-rank evolution strategies without requiring criterion-specific differentiable surrogates. Once trained, the generator produces the entire graph in a single neural-network evaluation. On molecular generation benchmarks, our compact generator achieves over $50\times$ the valid-and-unique yield per estimated dense operation compared to recent one-step flow-map baselines while retaining high chemical validity. In scaffold completion, EGO achieves an observed $44.3\times$ speedup over MoLeR in generation to SMILES and produces approximately $10\times$ as many filter-passing proposals within matched time budgets for generation and screening. Beyond chemistry, EGO produces $1.54\times$ as many distinct held-out elite architectures as relaxed gradient training on NAS-Bench-101. The low generation cost may enable real-time candidate generation across discrete design tasks, supporting interactive exploration of constrained design spaces and rapid construction of candidate sets for downstream evaluation.

</details>


### [163] [Accelerating the Development of PLGA In Situ Forming Depots Through AI-Driven Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08368)
*Pauric Bannigan,Siddarth Chandrasekaran,Brigitte A. G. Lamers,Inge Hermsen,Gary Tom,Riley J. Hickman,Bahar Yeniad,Morgan Fox,Christine Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将Corbion的PURASORB可生物降解聚合物库与Intrepid的AI算法ANDROMEDA 1相结合，在约15周内制备并表征181种原位成胶储库制剂，筛选出4个先导候选配方（载药量6%、9%、12% w/w），均满足黏度与可注射性要求并提供各异的30天体外释放曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长效注射制剂开发需同时优化载药量、释放动力学、黏度、可注射性、稳定性等多维目标，传统试错式研发难以高效导航这一复杂设计空间，因此需要整合聚合物材料专长与AI驱动的多目标优化方法。

Method: 采用Corbion的PURASORB生物可吸收聚合物库（涵盖宽分子量范围，含商业化牌号及在研新聚合物）与Intrepid Labs专有AI算法ANDROMEDA 1，针对一种治疗性多肽开展原位成胶储库制剂开发；通过广泛设计空间映射与靶向多目标优化，在约15周内制备并表征181种独特配方（载药量6–12% w/w）。

Result: 在6%、9%和12% w/w载药量下确定4个先导候选配方，均满足预设黏度与可注射性标准，并呈现各异的30天体外释放曲线；ANDROMEDA 1识别出中等分子量聚合物在缓释性能与溶液黏度之间具有更优平衡。

Conclusion: 聚合物专业能力与AI驱动优化的整合可快速识别差异化配方候选物、聚焦开发空间，并为后续优化和体内评价建立强有力的数据驱动基础。

Abstract: Developing long-acting injectable formulations requires the simultaneous optimization of drug loading, release kinetics, viscosity, injectability, stability and other objectives. To navigate this multidimensional space, Corbion and Intrepid combined Corbion's diverse PURASORB bioresorbable polymer library with Intrepid Labs' proprietary AI algorithm (ANDROMEDA 1) to develop in situ forming depots for a therapeutic peptide. Over approximately 15 weeks, 181 unique formulations spanning drug loadings of 6-12% w/w were prepared and characterized through broad design-space mapping and targeted multi-objective optimization. Four lead candidate formulations were identified at 6%, 9%, and 12% w/w drug loading. Each met the predefined viscosity and injectability criteria while providing distinct 30-day in vitro release profiles. The study evaluated polymers spanning a broad range of molecular weights, including commercially available PURASORB grades and new polymers under development by Corbion to expand its polymer toolbox. ANDROMEDA 1 identified that polymers with intermediate molecular weights provided a favorable balance between sustained release and solution viscosity. Together, these findings demonstrate how integrated polymer expertise and AI-driven optimization can rapidly identify differentiated formulation candidates, focus the development space, and establish a strong data-driven foundation for further optimization and in vivo evaluation.

</details>


### [164] [Decision-Focused Learning in MDPs: An Occupancy Measure Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08384)
*Zihao Zhao,Ashwath K. Karunakaram,Ali Eshragh,Yuexing Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将MDP重构为基于占用测度的线性规划（LP），通过枢轴法识别活跃约束获得闭式梯度，并用增广拉格朗日代理、随机行草图与可学习软状态聚合层解决梯度不连续与大规模状态空间的可扩展性问题，在多任务上以更低计算成本取得比KKT-based DFL和两阶段基线更低的regret。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策聚焦学习（DFL）方法对Bellman方程的KKT条件求导，需要求解覆盖所有状态-动作对的线性系统，可扩展性差，难以应对大规模或连续状态空间。

Method: 将MDP重构为基于占用测度的线性规划（LP），其可行域由预测的动态模型诱导；通过枢轴算法识别可行多面体中的活跃约束，推导闭式梯度。针对两个挑战：(1) 活跃约束变化时LP解梯度不连续，采用增广拉格朗日代理并通过对约束进行随机行草图平滑边界跳变；(2) LP反向传播代价随状态规模增长，引入可学习的软状态聚合层及其函数逼近泛化，将LP扩展到大规�模有限状态与连续状态MDP。

Result: 在多个任务上，所提方法相比基于KKT的DFL和两阶段基线取得更低的regret，且计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 基于占用测度LP的DFL框架结合平滑与状态聚合技术，为大规模及连续状态MDP的决策聚焦学习提供了可扩展且高效的方案。

Abstract: In this work, we consider decision-focused learning (DFL) for a Markov decision process (MDP), where existing methods differentiate through the KKT conditions of the Bellman equation and require solving a linear system over all state-action pairs, limiting its scalability. We address this by reformulating the MDP as an occupancy measure-based linear program (LP), whose feasible region is induced by predicted dynamics, and we derive a closed-form gradient by identifying the active constraints in the feasible polyhedron via the pivoting algorithm. This occupancy measure-based LP layer raises two challenges: (1) LP's solution gradient is discontinuous when active constraints change, and (2) the LP backward cost still scales with the state size, which is costly for large or continuous state spaces. We address the challenges with an augmented Lagrangian surrogate and smooth the boundary jumps by random row sketching of the constraints, and a learnable soft state-aggregation layer and its function-approximation generalization that scales the LP to large finite and continuous-state MDPs. Across multiple tasks, our methods reach lower regret than KKT-based DFL and two-stage baselines with significantly lower computation cost. The source code for all experiments is available at https://github.com/A-Eshragh/State_Aggregation_Project.

</details>


### [165] [Atom-JEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for 3D Atomistic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08400)
*Kasper Helverskov Petersen,Rasmus Hannibal Tirsgaard,François R J Cornet,Mikkel Jordahn,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Atom-JEPA自监督预训练框架，从无标注3D结构学习潜在表示，在分子与晶体性质预测上取得SOTA或具竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自监督预训练已显著提升语言与视觉模型的泛化能力，但在原子尺度（atomistic）体系建模中，自监督预训练尚未取得可比的下游泛化效果，因此需要专门的预训练框架。

Method: Atom-JEPA基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）思想，通过互补的原子级（atom-level）与子结构级（substructure-level）目标，从无标注3D结构学习潜在表示；在大规模分子与晶体数据集上预训练，并通过在多样化下游性质预测任务上微调评估迁移性能。

Result: 在分子ADMET与量子化学性质预测任务上达到SOTA，在晶体材料物理性质预测上具高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明潜在空间预测式预训练仅凭结构数据即可支撑广泛的下游泛化，代码与预训练权重已公开。

Abstract: Large-scale self-supervised pretraining has reshaped modern machine learning, substantially advancing the ability of language and vision models to generalize across downstream tasks. While deep learning has driven considerable progress in modeling atomistic systems in recent years, self-supervised pretraining in this domain has not yet achieved comparable downstream generalization. To address this, we introduce Atom-JEPA, a self-supervised pretraining framework that learns latent representations from unlabeled 3D structures through complementary atom-level and substructure-level objectives inspired by joint-embedding predictive architectures. We pretrain Atom-JEPA on large-scale molecular and crystalline datasets and evaluate its transfer performance by fine-tuning on a diverse set of downstream property prediction tasks. Atom-JEPA achieves state-of-the-art performance on molecular ADMET and quantum-chemical property prediction tasks, and is highly competitive in predicting the physical properties of crystalline materials. These results demonstrate the potential of latent-space predictive pretraining to support broad downstream generalization from structural data alone. Code and pretrained model checkpoints are publicly available at https://github.com/khelverskovp/atom-jepa

</details>


### [166] [VETTA: Coordinating Turn- and Token-Level Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08402)
*Jiaju Chen,Min Yang,Jinghua Piao,Xiaochong Lan,Xu Xia,Xiangnan He,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VETTA 提出一种在共享轻量 critic 上联合学习轮次级与 token 级价值的信用分配方法，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上显著提升多轮 LLM 智能体的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮 LLM 智能体面临稀疏反馈，存在两层信用分配问题：哪些回复促成了任务结果，以及每条回复内部哪些生成决策更重要。现有方法通常只关注一层：轮次级方法评估完整回复但不区分内部决策；token 级方法能跨轮传播反馈但不显式建模每条回复的信用。二者的互补局限促使作者在单次策略更新中同时学习并协调两个层级的信用。

Method: VETTA 在共享轻量 critic 上通过独立预测头联合学习轮次级与 token 级价值。它沿时间序列计算轮次优势，并将轮次优势与以回复内均值为中心的 token 残差相结合，用于 PPO 更新。为降低价值学习成本，critic 仅保留用于初始化 actor 的预训练检查点中的早期 Transformer 块。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 两个具有挑战性的智能体基准上，使用 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 时 VETTA 相比 PPO 将成功率分别提升 37.5% 和 22.3%，成功率分别达到 95.5% 和 76.0%（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）。critic 深度对比进一步表明，在显著降低 critic 侧计算量的同时取得了强任务性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑的共享 critic 能够协调轮次级与 token 级信用，在保持价值估计高效的同时提升智能体性能。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM agents often receive sparse task feedback across several interactions, while generating each response token by token. This creates two related credit-assignment questions: which responses helped achieve the outcome, and which generation decisions mattered within each response? Existing methods typically focus on only one level: turn-level methods evaluate complete responses but do not distinguish the decisions within them; token-level methods can propagate feedback across turns but do not explicitly model credit for each response. These complementary limitations motivate learning credit at both levels and coordinating it in a single policy update. We introduce VETTA, a credit assignment method that jointly learns turn- and token-level values through separate heads on a shared lightweight critic. VETTA computes advantages along both temporal sequences and combines each turn advantage with a within-response-centered token residual for PPO updates. Furthermore, to reduce value-learning cost, the critic retains only early Transformer blocks from the pretrained checkpoint used to initialize the actor. On two challenging agent benchmarks, ALFWorld and WebShop, VETTA improves success rates over PPO by 37.5% and 22.3%, respectively, with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct and achieves success rates of 95.5% and 76.0%, respectively, with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Critic-depth comparisons further show strong task performance with substantially lower critic-side computation. These results suggest that a compact shared critic can coordinate turn- and token-level credit to improve agent performance while keeping value estimation efficient. Code is available at https://github.com/Jiaju-Chen/VETTA-official.

</details>


### [167] [SSR: Sparse Segment Reduction for Ternary GEMM Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08403)
*Adeline Pittet,Shien Zhu,Valérie Verdan,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sparse Segment Reduction（SSR），一种面向三值 LLM 与三值权重网络的三值矩阵乘法方法，通过专用数据格式与基于稀疏度的计算树同时利用三值量化与稀疏性，在 45-95% 稀疏度下相较 RSR++ 实现 2.1-11.3x 的 GEMM 加速，并在 Llama-3 1B 端到端推理中取得 3.5-6.3x 加速与 4.9% 显存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型计算开销大，难以部署在资源受限硬件上。三值量化可实现显著压缩且常伴随 50-90% 稀疏性。但现有方法存在割裂：BitNet、RSR 及 RSR++ 等针对三值权重的优化未利用稀疏结构，而常规稀疏格式又忽略三值特性，导致无法同时挖掘三值量化与稀疏性的双重优化机会。

Method: 提出 Sparse Segment Reduction（SSR）：1）设计专用的优化三值数据格式；2）提出通过随稀疏度缩放的计算树系统性地利用稀疏模式的算法。SSR 统一针对三值权重与稀疏结构进行协同优化，适用于三值 LLM 及通用三值权重网络（TWN）。

Result: 理论上，当稀疏度高于 50% 时 SSR 的推理渐近快于 RSR++；实测在所有稀疏度水平均有性能提升。在三值 GEMM 上，45-95% 稀疏度下相较 RSR++ 获得 2.1-11.3x 加速；在 Llama-3 1B 模型推理中实现 3.5-6.3x 端到端加速与 4.9% 显存节省。

Conclusion: SSR 通过专用三值数据格式与稀疏度自适应计算树，首次在方法层面协同利用三值量化与稀疏性，克服了 RSR++ 等仅优化三值权重和传统稀疏格式忽略三值特征的局限，在理论与实测中均优于现有方案，为资源受限场景下三值 LLM 的高效推理提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) require substantial computational resources, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained hardware. Ternary LLMs mitigate these demands through weight quantization via ternary values, achieving significant compression often with 50-90% sparsity. However, existing approaches have limitations: methods optimized for ternary weights, such as BitNet, redundant segment reduction (RSR), and its improved version RSR++, do not exploit sparsity structures, while conventional sparse formats neglect ternary characteristics, foregoing dual optimization opportunities.
  In this paper, we introduce Sparse Segment Reduction (SSR), a ternary matrix multiplication method designed to accelerate the inference of ternary LLMs and general Ternary Weight Networks (TWNs). SSR has a dedicated optimized ternary data format and an algorithm that systematically exploits sparsity patterns through computation trees that scale with the sparsity. SSR provides theoretical gains with asymptotically faster inference than RSR++ for sparsity above 50%, while practical evaluations reveal performance improvements across all sparsity levels. Evaluation results show that SSR achieves 2.1-11.3x speedup over RSR++ on ternary GEMM with 45-95% sparsity. Furthermore, SSR achieves 3.5-6.3x end-to-end speedup and 4.9% of memory saving over RSR++ on the Llama-3 1B model inference.

</details>


### [168] [Symmetry-Aware Feature Learning: A Polynomial Separation for Multi-Index Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08420)
*Jivan Waber,Vanessa Piccolo,Yatin Dandi,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了对称感知与对称不可知特征学习之间的多项式样本复杂度分离：在循环对称轨道增长秩多指标模型中，权重共享与全群数据增强均达到 Θ̃(d^{p-1})（纯二次情形为 Θ̃(d)），而无对称性学习需 Θ̃(rd^{p-1})（纯二次为 Θ̃(rd)），故数据增强可匹配架构权重共享的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性（如权重共享、数据增强）是深度学习中提升样本效率的常用手段，但对称感知与无对称性学习之间是否存在严格的多项式样本复杂度优势，缺乏理论刻画。作者旨在量化这一优势，并回答"数据增强能否在样本复杂度上匹敌架构权重共享"这一核心问题。

Method: 研究 R^d 上高维高斯协变量的增长秩多指标模型，教师方向数为 r=Θ(d^δ)（0<δ<1/2）并构成循环对称轨道。使用球面在线 SGD 与相关损失，对比三种学习器：对称捆绑卷积网络（权重共享）、非捆绑网络、非捆绑网络加全群数据增强。对信息指数 p≥3 的多项式链接类及纯二次 Hermite 链接分别给出匹配的样本复杂度上下界。

Result: 对 p≥3 的多项式链接类，捆绑学习器与增强学习器均在 Θ̃(d^{p-1}) 样本内实现弱方向恢复，而对称不可知学习器需要 Θ̃(rd^{p-1})；对纯二次 Hermite 链接，弱恢复教师子空间所需样本分别为 Θ̃(d) 与 Θ̃(rd)。所有界均匹配至对数因子。证明揭示两阶段机制：初始化涨落从教师轨道中选出单一方向，随后局部化增长将该方向的重叠放大至弱恢复尺度，而竞争方向的重叠保持在初始化尺度附近。

Conclusion: 全群数据增强在样本效率上可匹配架构权重共享，二者相对无对称性训练均具多项式优势；对称性感知学习的加速源于"轨道选择—局部增长"的两阶段动力学。该结果从理论上说明对称结构利用方式（架构 vs 数据）在渐近样本复杂度上等价。

Abstract: We establish a polynomial sample complexity separation between symmetry-aware and symmetry-agnostic feature learning. We study growing-rank multi-index models with high-dimensional Gaussian covariates in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and $r=Θ(d^δ)$ teacher directions forming a cyclic symmetry orbit, where $0<δ<1/2$. We compare three ways of exploiting this structure: architectural weight sharing, data augmentation over the full symmetry group, and learning without access to the symmetry. In particular, we analyze a symmetry-tied convolutional network, an untied network, and the same untied network trained with full-group data augmentation, using spherical online SGD with correlation loss. For a class of polynomial links with information exponent $p\ge3$, we prove matching sample complexity bounds up to logarithmic factors: the tied and augmented learners achieve weak directional recovery in $\widetildeΘ(d^{p-1})$ samples, whereas the symmetry-agnostic learner requires $\widetildeΘ(rd^{p-1})$. For the pure quadratic Hermite link, the same separation holds for weak recovery of the teacher subspace, with sample complexities $\widetildeΘ(d)$ and $\widetildeΘ(rd)$, respectively. Thus, full-group data augmentation matches the sample efficiency of architectural weight sharing, and both provide a polynomial advantage over training without symmetry. For $p\ge3$, the proof reveals a two-stage mechanism: fluctuations at initialization select one direction in the teacher orbit, after which localized growth amplifies its overlap to the weak recovery scale while competing overlaps remain near their initialization scale.

</details>


### [169] [Climbing the Design Ladder: Sequential Knowledge Distillation for Early-Stage Circuit Timing Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08457)
*Reza Moravej,Fahad Rahman Amik,Zhanguang Zhang,Didier Chételat,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 STEP-KD 方法，利用中间设计阶段作为“跳板”进行渐进式知识蒸馏，将后布线时序预测能力迁移到后综合阶段，显著降低时序违规预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成电路设计流程包含逻辑综合、布局规划、布局、布线等多个阶段，各阶段耗时数小时至数周。若时序违规在流程后期才被发现，需回退到早期阶段重新设计，浪费算力并延迟产品上市。虽然从早期数据预测后布线时序可避免此类失败，但后综合的逻辑描述与后布线的物理布局之间存在巨大的抽象鸿沟，现有机器学习方法难以跨越。

Method: 提出 STEP-KD（Sequential Timing Evaluation via Progressive Knowledge Distillation）：不直接预测，而是利用中间设计阶段作为“跳板”进行渐进式知识迁移。具体在布线后、布局后、布局规划后三个阶段分别训练教师模型，再通过表示对齐（representation alignment）将它们的知识依次蒸馏到后综合阶段的学生模型中。

Result: 在多样化电路上的实验表明，STEP-KD 相比直接蒸馏和监督基线降低了时序预测误差，且在多数场景下优于业界标准的静态时序分析（STA）工具。STEP-KD 将总负裕量（Total Negative Slack）预测的加权平均绝对百分比误差降至 19.78%，而 STA 为 74.84%。

Conclusion: STEP-KD 通过渐进式知识蒸馏有效弥合了早期逻辑描述与后期物理布局之间的抽象鸿沟，能够更早识别时序问题，避免昂贵的后期重新设计，是迈向早期时序预测的实质性进展。

Abstract: Integrated circuit design involves multiple design stages: logic synthesis, floorplanning, placement, and routing, with each stage taking hours to weeks to complete. Discovering timing violations late in this flow forces costly iterations back to earlier stages, wasting computational resources and delaying product launches. While predicting post-routing timing from early-stage data could prevent these failures, existing machine learning approaches struggle with the massive abstraction gap between post-synthesis logical descriptions and post-routing physical layouts.
