We propose STEP-KD (Sequential Timing Evaluation via Progressive Knowledge Distillation), which leverages intermediate design stages as ``stepping stones'' for progressive knowledge transfer rather than attempting direct prediction. STEP-KD trains teacher models at the post-routing, post-placement, and post-floorplan stages, then sequentially distills their knowledge to a post-synthesis student model through representation alignment. Experiments on diverse circuits demonstrate that STEP-KD reduces timing prediction error compared to direct distillation and supervised baselines, and in most settings compared to the industry-standard Static Timing Analysis (STA) tool. STEP-KD reduces the weighted mean absolute percentage error of Total Negative Slack prediction to 19.78\%, compared with 74.84\% for STA. Our proposed method is step forward to identify timing problems earlier, avoiding expensive late-stage redesigns.

</details>


### [170] [MetaLearnNCA: Few-Shot Offline Meta-Learning via Interacting Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08479)
*Etienne Guichard,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaLearnNCA：一种基于耦合神经细胞自动机（NCA）动力学的去中心化少样本元学习框架，推理时无需梯度回传即可完成任务自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统少样本元学习或将任务自适应建模为通过展开计算图的分析梯度下降（推理需回传，代价高），或建模为对展平为一维特征向量的度量比较（丢弃原生二维空间几何结构）。本文希望避开这两种范式的局限。

Method: 将任务自适应分解为两部分：Active-NCA在连续二维空间记忆网格（称为spatial program）上执行任务推断；学习的Meta-NCA作为去中心化细胞优化器，通过在局部邻域扩散空间误差残差来动态更新该program。二者耦合交互，推理阶段不计算解析梯度。

Result: 在Omniglot上达到96.12%，与典型元学习器同分布性能相当；在MNIST、KMNIST、Fashion-MNIST上展现跨分布迁移增益，跨10个独立测试种子、1/5/10-shot设置下均有效（如10-shot MNIST较Prototypical Networks提升+10.54%，10-shot Fashion-MNIST较FOMAML提升+3.87%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，稳健的、免梯度的"学会学习"能力可以从非冯·诺依曼基底上的去中心化细胞动力学中涌现。

Abstract: Few-shot meta-learning traditionally formulates task adaptation either as analytical gradient descent through unrolled computational graphs or as metric-based distance comparisons over flattened 1D fea- ture vectors, which either incur costly test-time backpropagation or discard native 2D spatial geometry. In this work, we propose METALEARNNCA, a decentralized framework that achieves few-shot adapta- tion through the dynamical interaction of coupled Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs) without computing analytical gradients during inference. MetaLearnNCA decomposes task adaptation into an Active- NCA, which executes task inference conditioned on a continuous 2D spatial memory grid termed the spatial program, and a learned Meta-NCA, which acts as a decentralized cellular optimizer by diffusing spatial error residuals across local neighborhoods to dynamically update this program. METALEARN- NCA is competitive against canonical meta-learners in-distribution (96.12% on Omniglot) with Out-Of- Distribution transfer gains on MNIST, KMNIST, and Fashion-MNIST transfer across 10 independent testing seeds across 1-, 5-, and 10-shot regimes (e.g., surpassing Prototypical Networks by +10.54% on 10-shot MNIST and a +3.87% gain on 10-shot Fashion-MNIST over FOMAML). Our results establish that robust, gradient-free learning-to-learn can emerge from decentralized cellular dynamics on non-von Neumann substrates.

</details>


### [171] [PHBA: Prefix-State Hybrid Block Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08527)
*Ruijie Li,Jiaxi Hu,Shiyu Wang,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出前缀-状态混合块注意力（PHBA），用top-k块稀疏检索替代滑窗注意力，并让每个检索块绑定一个紧凑前缀状态，从而在单层内兼顾精确长程检索与压缩历史上下文，配套Triton实现保证效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性序列模型与softmax注意力混合架构（如NHA）虽能兼顾长上下文效率与精确检索，但其精确注意力被限制在固定局部窗口内，难以进行灵活的长程精确检索。

Method: 用top-k块稀疏检索替换NHA的局部滑窗注意力；在块边界处用门控线性递归构造前缀状态，并将其与对应token块一并检索，使精确的远距离证据与压缩历史上下文在统一层内融合；进一步开发硬件感知的Triton实现，流式处理被路由的token块与前缀状态，避免物化大型中间张量。

Result: 实验表明PHBA在长上下文与检索任务上优于强线性及混合基线，同时保持高效的训练与推理。

Conclusion: PHBA通过块稀疏检索与前缀状态的耦合，突破了混合注意力局部窗口的限制，在效率与长程精确检索能力之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining linear sequence models with softmax attention provide an effective balance between efficient long-context modeling and precise token retrieval. Existing designs such as Native Hybrid Attention (NHA) combine compressed long-term states with sliding-window attention, but their exact attention is restricted to a fixed local window. In this work, we introduce Prefix-State Hybrid Block Attention (PHBA), which replaces local sliding-window attention with top-k block-sparse retrieval and couples each retrieved block with a compact prefix state summarizing its preceding context. The prefix states are constructed by a gated linear recurrence at block boundaries and retrieved together with the corresponding token blocks, allowing the model to combine precise long-range evidence with compressed historical context within a unified layer. We further develop a hardware-aware Triton implementation that streams routed token blocks and prefix states without materializing large intermediate tensors. Experiments show that PHBA improves long-context and retrieval performance over strong linear and hybrid baselines while retaining efficient training and inference.

</details>


### [172] [How Bregman Divergences Shape Shampoo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08534)
*Bing Liu,Wenjie Zhou,Chengcheng Zhao,Hongtao Zhang,Boao Kong,Felix Dangel,Wu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一Bregman散度框架，将Shampoo类优化器的各类散度纳入同一体系，通过梯度二阶矩的谱分析揭示散度选择如何塑造Kronecker预条件及有限样本误差，并以GPT-2预训练验证其解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shampoo类优化器通过最小化Frobenius或KL散度来学习预条件子，但散度选择如何影响预条件效果尚不清楚，阻碍了进一步改进。

Method: 构建统一的Bregman散度框架，将流行散度统一连接以便联合研究；对梯度二阶矩进行经验谱分析，考察散度选择对Kronecker近似的影响及其与预条件中有限样本误差的交互。

Result: 部分散度能更好补偿经验二阶矩的有限样本低估，从而解释不同Shampoo变体行为差异；通过GPT-2预训练实验进一步验证了这一解释。

Conclusion: 将散度选择与实际训练行为相关联，为理解Shampoo基础并进一步改进其提供有原则的指导。

Abstract: Understanding the principles behind Shampoo has recently guided the development of more effective neural network optimizers. These methods learn a preconditioner by optimizing the Frobenius or Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence against the gradient second moment. In this work, we investigate how the choice of divergence shapes preconditioning, which remains unclear and blocks further improvements. To do so, we develop a unified Bregman divergence framework that connects all popular divergences, allowing us to study them jointly. Through empirical spectral analysis of gradient second moments, we examine how divergence choice shapes Kronecker approximation and interacts with finite-sample error in preconditioning. We find that some divergences can better compensate for finite-sample underestimation of the empirical second moment, helping explain the differing behavior of their corresponding Shampoo variants. We further validate this explanation through GPT-2 pretraining experiments. By connecting divergence choice to practical training behavior, we believe our framework provides principled guidance for understanding the foundations of, and further improving, Shampoo.

</details>


### [173] [FlowCF: Sparse Counterfactual Explanations for Mixed-Type Tabular Data using Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08537)
*Emmanouil Panagiotou,Eirini Ntoutsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowCF：模型无关生成式反事实解释方法，将CF生成视为从事实类到目标类的稀疏传输，用流匹配（含新型混合流算子）求解，并通过门控网络优化稀疏性。在六个基准数据集上取得最佳数值稀疏性与接近性，数值特征改变比例29% vs 基线89%，位移小70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法在稀疏性（sparsity）和接近性（proximity）上表现受限，尤其在数值特征上：模型无关与摊销方法、以及可完全访问模型的梯度方法均存在不足。而实用的CF解释应尽可能少地改变特征且改动幅度尽可能小。

Method: 提出FlowCF——一种模型无关的生成式方法：将CF生成建模为从事实类到目标类的稀疏传输（sparse transport）；用流匹配（flow matching）求解该传输，并扩展出新型混合流算子（mixed flow operator）以处理混合特征类型；利用所得传输几何结构，通过门控网络（gating network）最小化被改变的特征数量来优化稀疏性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，FlowCF产生最佳的数值稀疏性与接近性：仅改变29%的数值特征，而最佳基线改变89%；位移小70%；在其他评价指标上与其相当。

Conclusion: FlowCF验证了将CF生成表述为稀疏传输并结合流匹配与门控机制的可行性，在混合特征类型的数值稀疏性与接近性上显著优于现有方法，同时保持其他期望性质不劣化。

Abstract: In the field of Explainable AI (XAI), counterfactual (CF) explanations interpret a model's decision by suggesting the changes to the input that would lead to a more favourable outcome. To be useful in practice, such an explanation should change few features and change them as little as possible, properties known as sparsity and proximity. We observe that existing methods remain limited in this respect, especially for numerical features, whether they are model-agnostic and amortised, or gradient-based with full access to the model. In this paper, we propose FlowCF, a model-agnostic generative method that frames CF generation as sparse transport from the factual to the target class. We solve this transport with flow matching, which we extend to mixed feature types with a novel mixed flow operator, and exploit the resulting geometry to optimise for sparsity through a gating network that minimises the number of features the transport changes. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets demonstrate that FlowCF produces the best numerical sparsity and proximity, changing 29% of the numerical features where the best baseline changes 89%, at 70% smaller displacement, while remaining comparable on the other desiderata.

</details>


### [174] [From Shared Demand Patterns to Local Uncertainty: Probabilistic Load Forecasting by Mixing Compact Adaptations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08538)
*Haoran Li,Zhe Cheng,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的客户感知概率负荷预测框架，通过共享模型加少量低维自适应组件的方式，在保持低存储与推理成本的同时，提升客户级与变压器级负荷预测的精度与概率质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户级和变压器级负荷预测面临可扩展性挑战：负荷不确定性受用户行为、天气和混合负荷构成影响，单一共享模型难以捕捉异构模式，而为每个负荷训练独立概率模型又会带来训练、存储、更新和验证的高昂成本。

Method: 提出客户感知预测框架：不从为每个负荷建立独立模型或指派专用模型入手，而是学习一小组低维自适应组件，每个负荷根据自身预测特征对这些组件进行组合，从而在共享知识的同时保持对异构及混合负荷构成的灵活性。

Result: 在 SMART-DS 数据集的 590 条负荷曲线上，相较统计模型、神经网络、基于 Transformer 的模型以及预训练时间序列基线，该方法在确定性精度和概率质量上均取得一致提升，同时保持较低的存储和推理成本。

Conclusion: 基于低维自适应组件组合的共享建模策略，为大规模客户级概率负荷预测提供了一种兼顾精度、灵活性与成本效率的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic load forecasting has been widely studied for power-system operation and planning, but customer- and transformer-level forecasting introduces a distinct scalability challenge. At these levels, load uncertainty is strongly affected by customer behavior, weather, and mixed load composition, making it difficult for a single shared model to capture heterogeneous patterns. Using separate probabilistic models can improve local accuracy, but becomes costly to train, store, update, and validate at scale. To address this challenge, we develop a scalable customer-aware forecasting framework that learns common demand behavior through a shared model while adapting only a compact subset of parameters. Rather than using an independent model for each load or assigning each load to a specialized model, the proposed design learns a small bank of low-dimensional adaptation components and allows each load to combine them according to its forecasting characteristics. This preserves shared knowledge across customers while providing sufficient flexibility for heterogeneous and mixed load compositions. Experiments on 590 load profiles from the SMART-DS dataset show consistent improvements in deterministic accuracy and probabilistic quality over statistical, neural-network, Transformer-based, and pretrained time-series baselines, while retaining low storage and inference costs.

</details>


### [175] [DeltaTTT: Layerwise Optimization for Nonlinear Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08553)
*Yining Li,Dongchen Han,Jie Fu,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现非线性记忆的顺序测试时训练(TTT)并不总优于固定基线的并行TTT，将其归因于单次遍历中非线性记忆的优化困难，并提出分层学习的DeltaTTT来缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序TTT通过逐次更新计算内循环梯度，理论上应利用网络先前状态以更好整合新信息，但该优势在非线性记忆中并未稳定出现——固定基线的并行TTT反而优于其串行版本。作者旨在揭示其背后的优化困难并提出改进方案。

Method: 提出DeltaTTT：将两层记忆网络的联合内循环优化替换为逐层学习(layerwise learning)。每层分配局部预测目标，并通过状态相关的delta规则进行更新。该形式在保留非线性读出(readout)的同时支持分块并行计算(chunkwise parallel computation)。

Result: 在DeltaNet和LaCT骨干网络上，DeltaTTT在语言建模和检索任务上均较其循环(recurrent)基线取得提升。

Conclusion: 非线性记忆在单次序列遍历中的优化难度是顺序TTT未能发挥状态依赖优势的核心原因；通过逐层delta规则的DeltaTTT设计，可在保留非线性表达能力的同时实现并行计算，并稳定获得性能增益。

Abstract: Sequential test-time training adapts a memory network through successive updates, each computing an inner-loop gradient based on the network's previous state. Intuitively, this state dependence should allow each update to account for what the memory has already learned and better incorporate new information. However, we find that this expected advantage does not consistently materialize in nonlinear memories: a fixed-base parallel TTT baseline outperforms its serial counterpart. Our exploratory experiments point to a key underlying difficulty: nonlinear memories can be harder to optimize than linear ones within a single pass over the sequence. To alleviate this optimization difficulty, we introduce DeltaTTT, which replaces joint inner-loop optimization of a two-layer memory network with layerwise learning. Each layer is assigned a local prediction target and updated through a state-dependent delta rule. This formulation retains a nonlinear readout while enabling chunkwise parallel computation. Experiments on DeltaNet and LaCT backbones show improvements in language modeling and retrieval over their recurrent baselines.

</details>


### [176] [Reinforcement Learning for Hierarchical Reasoning Rewards: Minimax-Optimal Rates with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08561)
*Naoki Nishikawa,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究RL后训练中"在线策略探索+神经奖励模型"为何有效，将奖励建模为响应空间上的分层函数（由无穷多局部组件构成，需依次解决），证明Transformer的actor-critic算法在查询预算与正则强度上达到极小极大最优率，而固定参考分布的离线采样只能获得对数级遗憾衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL已成为语言模型推理任务后训练的标准工具，但其理论理解仍有限，尤其是"在线策略探索结合神经奖励模型"为何有效缺乏理论解释。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，量化在线策略探索对RL后训练的收益。

Method: 将奖励建模为响应空间上的分层函数：奖励由无穷多局部组件构成，每个组件仅在前序组件被解决后才变得相关。分析一个自然的基于Transformer的actor-critic算法，该算法在以下步骤间交替：(1) 从当前KL正则化策略中采样，(2) 用Transformer critic拟合观测到的奖励，(3) 更新策略。并与固定参考分布采样的离线奖励建模进行对比分析。

Result: 所分析的Transformer-based actor-critic算法在查询预算和正则强度上达到极小极大最优率（至多相差对数因子），且在固定提示数量下为极小极大最优。相反，从固定参考分布采样（如离线奖励建模）会将遗憾衰减限制在对数率。

Conclusion: 在线策略探索会逐步"聚焦"到奖励集中的区域，这一机制量化了其对RL后训练的收益，并从理论上解释了on-policy探索结合神经奖励模型的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard tool for post-training language models on reasoning tasks, where the policy is updated by reward feedback while exploring the space of responses. Despite its empirical success, theoretical understanding of RL post-training remains limited, in particular of why on-policy exploration combined with a neural reward model is effective. In this paper, we address this question by modeling the reward as a hierarchical function on the response space: the reward consists of infinitely many local components, each of which becomes relevant only after the preceding ones have been resolved. We show that a natural Transformer-based actor--critic algorithm, which alternates between sampling from the current KL-regularized policy, fitting a Transformer critic to the observed rewards, and updating the policy, achieves the minimax optimal rates in the query budget and in the regularization strength up to logarithmic factors, and is minimax optimal for a fixed number of prompts. In contrast, we prove that sampling from the fixed reference distribution, as in offline reward modeling, can limit regret decay to a logarithmic rate. These results show that on-policy exploration progressively zooms in on the region where the reward is concentrated, and quantify its benefit for RL post-training.

</details>


### [177] [Valid for Free: Homophily-Gated Conformal Prediction for Training-Free Node Classification with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08564)
*Nguyen Duy Long,Phung Minh Hien,Nguyen Trong Viet,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次对"以表格基础模型(TFM)作为图节点上下文预测器"的设定开展可靠性研究：证明冻结的上下文预测器可用分裂保形预测获得有限样本精确有效性，实证表明TabICL免训练后验的校准误差低于温度缩放GCN，并提出免训练扩散分数HG-DAPS以缩小预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将TFM用于图节点分类（把节点及邻域特征当作表行、与有标签上下文行并列）的工作只报告预测性能，未报告保形覆盖率或预测集大小，即缺乏对可靠性的评估。作者希望填补这一空白，首次对这一设定做可靠性研究。

Method: 以TabICL作为TFM，将每个图的一半节点作为带标签上下文；因预测器在校准前即固定，故冻结的上下文预测器可让分裂保形预测在有限样本下精确有效，无需训练、验证折或在目标图上调参。在十个图上做校准审计，比较TabICL免训练后验与温度缩放GCN(GCN+TS)的ECE。另提出HG-DAPS：一种免训练扩散分数，其同配门控只读取上下文标签以保证有效性。并在二分类、类别不平衡图上做预注册的陷阱实验，对比以原始同配度与调整后同配度门控的效果。

Result: 十个图中九个上TabICL免训练后验的ECE低于GCN+TS；十图平均ECE为0.019，比GCN+TS的0.029低约35%。HG-DAPS相较自适应预测集(APS)，在六个同配图上平均集合大小减少5.8%–17.1%，在四个异配图上变化小于1%。两个二分类、类别不平衡图上，预注册陷阱实验显示以原始（而非调整后）同配度门控会使低同配节点的覆盖率分别下降0.27和0.12，而边缘覆盖率仍保持名义0.90，从而掩盖了该下降。

Conclusion: 冻结的TFM上下文预测器配合分裂保形预测具备有限样本有效性，且免训练TabICL后验校准优于温度缩放GCN；HG-DAPS能在保持有效性的同时缩小同配图上的预测集。但门控若使用原始同配度会损害低同配节点的条件覆盖，且被持平的边缘覆盖率所掩盖，提示评估需关注条件覆盖而非仅看边缘覆盖。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) can classify the nodes of a graph without training on it, by reading node and neighborhood features as table rows next to labeled context rows. Work in this line reports predictive performance, not conformal coverage or prediction-set size. To our knowledge, we give the first reliability study of the setting, with TabICL as the TFM and half of each graph as labeled context. As for any predictor fixed before calibration, a frozen in-context predictor makes split conformal prediction exactly valid in finite samples, with no training, validation fold, or tuning on the target graph. An audit across ten graphs then shows that the training-free TabICL posterior has lower expected calibration error (ECE) than GCN with temperature scaling (GCN+TS) on nine of them. Its mean ECE over the ten graphs is 0.019, about 35 percent below the 0.029 of GCN+TS. We also introduce HG-DAPS, a training-free diffusion score whose homophily gate reads only the in-context labels, so the guarantee still holds. Relative to adaptive prediction sets (APS), it reduces mean set size by 5.8 to 17.1 percent on six homophilous graphs and changes it by under 1 percent on four heterophilous ones. On two binary, class-imbalanced graphs, a pre-registered trap case shows that gating on raw rather than adjusted homophily lowers coverage among low-homophily nodes by 0.27 and 0.12. Marginal coverage stays at the nominal 0.90 and masks this drop.

</details>


### [178] [Singular Value Decomposition: A Geometric Rediscovery, Where Proofs Become Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08565)
*Paul Agron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度重新发现奇异值分解(SVD):通过"单位圆映射为椭圆"的初等问题,以最大化拉伸并递归的方式构造出SVD,进而证明谱定理;并将该构造对应到幂法、PageRank、梯度下降停止准则与核PCA等机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常教材用$A=U\Sigma V^T$并借助$A^TA$的谱定理来证明SVD,虽正确但缺乏启发性——为得到一个本质关于椭圆的结论而预先假设了强定理。作者希望通过反转论证顺序,让SVD的几何本质自然浮现,并揭示其构造正是众多机器学习方法背后的机制。

Method: Part I反转论证顺序:线性映射将单位圆映为椭圆,考察哪些输入方向映到椭圆的轴,逐例发现这些方向互相垂直;在平面中通过旋转标架、追踪像偏离垂直的程度,由符号变化迫使出现恰好垂直的标架,该处也是拉伸最大的方向。将"最大化拉伸并递归"推广到n维,奇异值按序自然产生,且该构造反过来证明谱定理而非假设它。Part II将各构造付诸应用:最大化-递归对应幂法与PageRank;定位最大化者的引理对应梯度下降的停止准则;$A^TA$与$AA^T$的对偶对应核PCA核心的传输。

Result: 作者以几何构造方式重新推导出SVD,并证明该构造同时也证明了谱定理;并建立SVD构造与机器学习方法的对应:最大化-递归→幂法/PageRank,最大化者定位引理→梯度下降停止准则,$A^TA$与$AA^T$对偶→核PCA的传输。每项联系都标注了其适用边界,说明分解提供了什么、以及何处由其他思想接管。

Conclusion: SVD可以被几何地重新发现,其构造本身构成了众多机器学习方法背后的机制——不仅结论可迁移,证明本身也可作为过程来执行。前提仅为标准的二年级课程,示例小到可手算验证。

Abstract: This article is a geometric rediscovery of the singular value decomposition, with a further claim: the construction it builds is the machinery behind much of machine learning. The same argument that answers an idle question about ellipses is the algorithm behind principal component analysis, kernel methods, and PageRank, and it is not only the results that transfer but the proofs themselves, run as procedures.
  The usual introduction states $A = UΣV^T$ and justifies it via the spectral theorem applied to $A^T A$. This is correct but unilluminating, since it assumes a powerful theorem to reach a result that is, in the end, about ellipses. Part I reverses the order. A linear map sends the unit circle to an ellipse; one asks which input directions map to its axes, and finds, example after example, that they are perpendicular. In the plane this can be watched: rotate a frame, track how far its images are from perpendicular, and a sign change forces a frame where they are exactly perpendicular, which is also where the map stretches hardest. Maximizing the stretch and recursing generalizes this to n dimensions, with singular values falling out in order, and the construction proves the spectral theorem rather than assuming it.
  Part II puts each construction to work: maximize-and-recurse becomes the power method and PageRank; the lemma locating the maximizer becomes the stopping rule of gradient descent; the duality between $A^T A$ and $A A^T$ becomes the transport at the heart of kernel PCA. Each connection is stated with its boundary, saying what the decomposition supplies and where another idea takes over. Prerequisites are the standard sophomore sequence, and the worked examples are small enough to check by hand.

</details>


### [179] [Less Is More: A Leakage-Controlled Study of Dermoscopic Preprocessing for Joint Skin Lesion Classification and Segmentation with YOLO26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08570)
*Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Chi Hai,Nguyen Phuc Nguyen,Ngo Hoang Tu,Vo Nguyen Quoc Bao,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在严格按病灶去重、控制数据泄漏的评估协议下，对皮肤镜图像采用最小预处理加在线增强，比复杂确定性预处理（离线类别均衡、DullRazor-CLAHE、混合视图）具有更好的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工预处理在皮肤镜自动分析中广泛用于抑制伪影、增强病灶可见性，但其对现代实时模型的实际贡献并不明确；现有评估协议往往未充分控制同一病灶图像间的相关性，导致数据泄漏，结论不可靠。

Method: 基于HAM10000（10,015张，质控后10,013对有效图像-掩码，来自7,468个唯一病灶），按病灶身份划分互斥数据集；固定nano级YOLO26n-seg模型、分辨率、训练预算与评估协议，在三个随机种子下比较四种输入策略：最小预处理+在线增强（基线）、离线类别均衡、DullRazor-CLAHE预处理、原始-预处理混合视图，并联合进行多类别病灶分类与实例分割。

Result: 在病灶去重测试集上，原始基线达到mask mAP$_{50:95}$=0.5636±0.0234、Dice=0.9356±0.0024、macro-F1=0.6917±0.0202；离线增强未提升平均性能，组合策略与混合视图反而降低了类别感知的分割与分类精度。模型仅2.69M参数，推理速度约50 FPS。

Conclusion: 在泄漏受控、病灶去重且其他非输入因素固定的协议下，最小预处理的皮肤镜图像配合标准在线增强，比日益复杂的确定性预处理提供更优的精度-效率权衡；在HAM10000上，复杂预处理在三个种子上均无一致性的联合收益。

Abstract: Handcrafted preprocessing is widely employed in automated dermoscopic analysis to suppress imaging artifacts and enhance lesion visibility. Nevertheless, its actual contribution to modern real-time models remains unclear, particularly when evaluation protocols do not adequately control correlations among images of the same lesion. This study presents a leakage-controlled, lesion-disjoint evaluation of dermoscopic preprocessing and augmentation for joint multi-class lesion classification and instance segmentation using a fixed nano-scale YOLO26 segmentation model (YOLO26n-seg). From HAM10000 (10,015 images), quality control yields 10,013 valid image-mask pairs from 7,468 unique lesions, partitioned into mutually exclusive sets by lesion identity. With the architecture, resolution, training budget, and evaluation protocol held fixed, we compare minimally processed images plus online augmentation against offline class balancing, DullRazor-CLAHE preprocessing, and raw-processed hybrid views, over three random seeds. On the lesion-disjoint test set, the raw baseline achieves a mask mAP$_{50:95}$ of $0.5636 \pm 0.0234$, a Dice score of $0.9356 \pm 0.0024$, and a macro-F1 score of $0.6917 \pm 0.0202$. Offline augmentation does not improve the mean performance, while the combined and hybrid strategies reduce both class-aware segmentation and classification accuracy. At only 2.69 million parameters, the model runs at approximately 50 frames per second. Under a leakage-controlled, lesion-disjoint protocol with all non-input factors held fixed, minimally processed dermoscopic images combined with standard online augmentation deliver a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off than increasingly complex deterministic preprocessing, which yields no consistent joint benefit across three seeds on HAM10000.

</details>


### [180] [How Learning Governs Unlearning across the Memorization-Generalization Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08577)
*Hwiyeong Lee,Hyelim Lim,Ingyu Bang,Hoki Kim,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型的学习方式如何影响后续的遗忘（unlearning），从记忆化（memorization）与泛化（generalization）两种策略出发，发现泛化型模型在遗忘时对保留集的损害更大，且该趋势在LLM遗忘任务中同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘研究多关注如何消除模型习得的不良能力，但鲜有工作考察"模型如何学习"这一过程本身如何塑造其后续的遗忘行为。作者希望填补这一空白，从记忆化与泛化这两种代表性且相互竞争的习得策略入手，理解学习动态与遗忘效果之间的联系。

Method: 1) 利用模加法任务中的grokking现象，将模型划分为记忆主导型与泛化主导型，并比较它们的遗忘响应；2) 提出分桶模加法（bucketed modular addition），在记忆-泛化谱系上显式控制两种策略的贡献比例，进行更细粒度的验证；3) 将该分析扩展到LLM遗忘，涵盖逐字回忆（verbatim recall）与事实回忆（factual recall）两种设定。

Result: 记忆主导型模型在遗忘后保留集性能下降更小；泛化主导型模型遭受更大的保留集损害（retain damage）。引入分桶模加法后，该趋势依然存在且近乎单调。在LLM遗忘的逐字与事实回忆设定中，同样的关系依然成立。

Conclusion: 学习策略（记忆化 vs 泛化）会系统性地影响遗忘效果，泛化程度越高，遗忘对保留集的损害越大。作者据此提出两条面向遗忘方法设计的实用启示，强调在遗忘研究中必须考虑模型的学习动态。

Abstract: While unlearning seeks to negate undesired capabilities acquired through learning, little research has examined how the way models learn shapes their subsequent unlearning. In this paper, we investigate this connection from the perspectives of memorization and generalization, the two most representative yet competing strategies that models employ during training. We first classify memorization- and generalization-heavy models using grokking in modular addition and compare their responses to unlearning, showing that the latter suffer greater retain damage, i.e., a larger performance drop on the retain set. Furthermore, we conduct a finer-grained analysis by introducing bucketed modular addition, in which the respective contributions of the two strategies can be explicitly controlled across the memorization-generalization spectrum. In this setup, we reaffirm that the same trend persists and is nearly monotonic. We further demonstrate that this relationship also holds in LLM unlearning across verbatim and factual recall settings. Finally, we provide two practical insights for developing better unlearning methods, highlighting the importance of accounting for learning dynamics in unlearning.

</details>


### [181] [Random Feature Gaussian Process Attention: Linear-Time Probabilistic Attention with Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08578)
*Amir Mohammad Mahfoozi,Zi Yang,Ying Li,Michael Minyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出即插即用的随机傅里叶特征高斯过程注意力（RFF-GPA），将注意力建模为平稳核高斯过程并用随机傅里叶特征近似，把后验均值与方差的计算复杂度从三次/二次降至线性，同时改善校准性并保持预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer建模能力强，但校准性差，限制其在安全关键场景中的可靠性。将注意力解释为高斯过程后验可实现有原则的不确定性校准，然而核矩阵求逆带来序列长度的三次复杂度；解耦GP变体虽降至二次，实际仍难以承受。

Method: 提出RFF-GPA模块：将注意力表示为使用平稳核的高斯过程，并用随机傅里叶特征对该核做低秩近似，从而以线性时间复杂度近似GP后验均值与方差。该模块为即插即用设计，可嵌入现有Transformer结构。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实证结果表明，该注意力模块在保持预测精度的同时改善了校准性，并将计算复杂度降低为随序列长度线性增长。

Conclusion: 通过随机傅里叶特征近似平稳核GP注意力，可在显著提升可扩展性的同时兼顾校准性与精度，为Transformer在安全关键应用中的可靠部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transformers provide a state-of-the-art modeling framework, yet poor calibration limits their reliability in safety-critical applications. A promising direction addresses this issue by interpreting attention as a Gaussian process (GP) posterior, which enables principled uncertainty calibration but incurs cubic complexity in sequence length due to the inversion of the kernel; although decoupled GP variants reduced the cost to quadratic, the computation remains prohibitive in practice. In this paper, we propose the plug-and-play random Fourier feature Gaussian process attention (RFF-GPA) module, which represents the attention as a GP with a stationary kernel approximated by random Fourier features. This low-rank approximation results in linear-time complexity for approximating the posterior mean and variance, making it far more scalable compared to previous work. Empirical results on multiple real-world datasets show that our attention module improves calibration while maintaining predictive accuracy, and simultaneously reduces computational complexity to linear in the sequence length.

</details>


### [182] [CNet: A Complex-Valued Deep Learning Framework with Wirtinger Autodifferentiation and FFT--Hadamard Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08592)
*Marcel Crasmaru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CNet 是一个 C++/CUDA 复数神经网络（CVNN）框架，采用 Wirtinger 微积分与物理原生（Born 测量）范式，并提供 CPU 参考与 CUDA 实现。论文报告三项研究：复数因果 FNet 序列模型（基于 O(N log N) 因果傅里叶混合器）、无线调制分类与相干衍射成像的瓶颈分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习框架以实数运算与实数 logits + softmax 为核心，缺少对复数梯度（Wirtinger/CR-calculus）、酉算子（如 DFT）与物理测量（Born 规则）的原生支持，难以直接建模振幅/相位等物理结构。作者希望以“物理原生”立场构建复数神经网络工具与算子，并检验其在序列建模与物理反问题中的必要性与局限。

Method: 框架层面：C++/CUDA 实现，梯度下降基于 Wirtinger（CR-calculus）导数；网络为作用于振幅向量的复数（常为酉）算子级联，分类采用 Born 规则 p_k=|z_k|^2/‖z‖^2；每层配有 CPU 参考实现与经有限差分校验的 CUDA 核，计算图按 batch 克隆以支持 GPU。算子层面：基于 conv(x,k)=IFFT(FFT(x)·FFT(k)) 的信号处理原语构建可学习复数卷积网络，并实现 true-Adam 优化器与低内存推理模式。实验：构建 FNet 风格全复数因果序列模型，其核心为三角掩码 DFT（Bluestein/ chirp-z 分解）的 O(N log N) 因果傅里叶混合器；另在 RML2016.10a 与相干衍射成像傅里叶相位问题上做瓶颈分析。

Result: （1）复数因果 FNet 在字符级语言建模上，经充分调参后达到或超过参数匹配的实数因果 FNet，并以不到一半的训练步数达到实数模型的收敛质量。（2）与（3）在无线调制分类与相干衍射成像傅里叶相位问题上的瓶颈分析定位了复数网络仍需新算子的具体环节。三项研究均表明复数形式能够可证明地学到物理上正确的结构。

Conclusion: CNet 提供了支持复数梯度、酉算子与 Born 测量的端到端框架，验证了复数算子级联在序列建模与物理反问题中的有效性；同时指出复数网络并非万能，需针对特定任务设计新算子。代码已开源。

Abstract: CNet is a C++/CUDA framework for building and training deep complex-valued neural networks (CVNNs) and, more generally, for optimizing complex-valued functions by gradient descent with Wirtinger (CR-calculus) derivatives. It takes a physics-native stance: a network is a cascade of complex -- and often unitary (the DFT) -- operations acting on an amplitude vector, and classification is a Born-rule measurement $p_k = |z_k|^2 / \|z\|^2$ rather than a softmax over real logits. Every layer ships a CPU reference and a CUDA kernel checked against finite differences, and the computation graph is cloned across the batch for GPU execution. On top of the base layers we add signal-processing primitives that turn the identity conv(x,k) = IFFT(FFT(x) . FFT(k)) into a learnable complex convolutional network, together with a true-Adam optimizer and a reduced-memory inference mode.
  We report three studies. First, a fully complex-valued, FNet-style causal sequence model built on a new $O(N \log N)$ causal Fourier mixer -- a triangular-masked DFT evaluated by a Bluestein / chirp-z factorization: once properly tuned it matches or exceeds a parameter-matched real-valued causal FNet on character-level language modeling, reaching the real model's converged quality in under half the training steps. Second and third, bottleneck analyses on radio-modulation classification (RML2016.10a) and the Fourier phase problem of coherent-diffraction imaging, which isolate exactly where complex-valued networks still need new operators. Across all three the complex formulation provably learns the physically correct structure.
  Code: https://github.com/crasmarum/CNet

</details>


### [183] [Multi-Label Perceptual Bug Detection in Video Games using Deep Learning on Gameplay Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08593)
*Nahian Rifaat,Felix Morosov,Loutfouz Zaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ResNet-BiLSTM多标签感知缺陷检测模型，在基准数据集F1达85.78%，并发布含77,969片段、约120万帧、5类缺陷组合的新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工测试成本高、耗时长；现有工具难以在同一视频帧中同时检测多种感知缺陷，导致自动化缺陷检测在真实场景中面临检测困难。

Method: 构建深度学习多标签感知缺陷检测模型ResNet-BiLSTM，并将其与视频分类模型（Inflated 3D ConvNet、3D ResNet）进行对比；同时构建新的多标签感知缺陷数据集。

Result: ResNet-BiLSTM在基准数据集上取得85.78%的F1分数，结果表明时序依赖建模有助于提升基于视频的缺陷检测准确性。新数据集涵盖不同游戏类型，包含77,969个视频片段、约120万帧，涉及同一视频帧内5类缺陷的组合。

Conclusion: 多标签感知缺陷检测与时序建模对游戏视频缺陷检测有效，有望减少人工测试开销，且新数据集可支撑后续研究。

Abstract: Traditional approaches for automated bug detection in video games, such as manual testing, can be beneficial for the improvement of quality assurance, but they can be expensive and time-consuming. The scarce number of tools available to detect multiple perceptual bugs in the same video frame introduces detection challenges for automated bug detection tools in real-world scenarios. We propose a deep learning model for multi-label perceptual bug detection and compare it against video classification models such as Inflated 3D ConvNet and 3D ResNet. Our proposed model, ResNet-BiLSTM, achieved an F1 score of 85.78% on the benchmark dataset. Our results demonstrated that temporal dependency modelling is beneficial for accurate video-based bug detection. We believe this work with multi-label perceptual bug detection on gameplay videos will help save resources spent on manual testing workloads in video games. Furthermore, we introduce a new dataset with multi-label perceptual bugs in this work. The dataset contains 77,969 video clips across different genres of games with approximately 1.2 million frames, containing combinations from 5 classes of bugs in the same video frame.

</details>


### [184] [Early Memory Selection for Balanced Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08624)
*Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出从短程预训练中选取Adam共享记忆参数β的方法：基于局部模型推导三次记忆规则，用少量检查点的梯度探针估计系数，β选定后固定用于完整训练。在11个视觉与语言任务上，相对共享β=0.95网格代表，平均相对验证差距降低40.7%，最差四分之一平均差距降低44.3%，并比跨全部任务选出的最佳常数β低32.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam中共享记忆参数β（β1=β2）通常需人工设定或网格搜索，成本高且难以兼顾不同任务；希望以低成本方式为后续完整训练选出一个合适的固定β。

Method: 构建Adam归一化方向的局部模型，刻画采样变异与历史梯度平均所引入延迟之间的权衡，导出“三次记忆规则”；在若干预训练检查点上用梯度探针估计该规则的两个系数，估计时联合使用分子与分母以保留其协方差。流程采用200次更新的预训练与4个检查点、每点16个探针梯度，选定β后在完整训练中保持固定。

Result: 在11个视觉与语言工作负载上进行种子匹配的回顾性评估：相对共享β=0.95的网格代表，平均相对验证差距降低40.7%，最差四分之一平均差距降低44.3%；平均差距亦比在全部11个任务上选出的最佳常数β低32.3%。

Conclusion: 短程预训练加梯度探针即可有效估计Adam的共享记忆参数β，并以固定值用于完整训练；该方法在多种任务上稳定优于常用固定β及跨任务最优常数β，降低调参成本。

Abstract: We propose a method for choosing the shared memory parameter $β_1=β_2=β$ in Adam from a short pilot training. The selected $β$ remains fixed during the subsequent full training. A local model of Adam's normalized direction balances sampling variability against the delay introduced by averaging past gradients. This balance gives a cubic memory rule, whose two coefficients are estimated from gradient probes at a few pilot checkpoints. The estimator uses the numerator and denominator jointly, preserving their covariance. With a 200-update pilot and sixteen probe gradients at each of four checkpoints, a seed-matched retrospective evaluation on eleven vision and language workloads reduces mean relative validation gap by 40.7% and worst-quarter mean gap by 44.3% against the grid representative of shared $β=0.95$. The mean gap is also 32.3% lower than that of the best constant $β$ chosen across all eleven workloads.

</details>


### [185] [MemFLoRA: Memory-Floor LoRA for CNN Adaptation at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08669)
*Mehmet Emre Akbulut,Johannes Geier,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Memory-Floor LoRA (MemFLoRA)，一种以“激活内存下限”为设计准则的低秩 CNN 适配器，在保持或超越 CNN PEFT 基线精度的同时，将保存激活内存降低 98.5–98.7%、峰值训练状态内存降低 94.9–97.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧学习中，CNN 适配的真正瓶颈往往不是可训练参数量，而是反向传播前必须保留的激活状态；直接套用面向 Transformer 的 LoRA 思路并不能解决这一内存约束。

Method: 提出 activation-memory-floor 准则：可训练的反向计算不得依赖全宽度层输入。据此设计低秩 CNN 适配器：冻结下投影、训练尺度匹配的上投影，并结合 eval 模式骨干归一化与激活最小化的反向规则，将保存状态限制在低秩分支内。

Result: 在三个 HAR 数据集和两个 CNN 骨干上，针对受试者、身体位置与传感器放置偏移进行评估，MemFLoRA 相对全量微调降低保存激活内存 98.5–98.7%、峰值训练状态内存 94.9–97.3%，精度匹配或超过 CNN PEFT 基线。

Conclusion: 以内存优先而非参数量优先的设计可显著降低 CNN 端侧适配的训练内存开销，同时保持精度优势。

Abstract: On-device learning is necessary when the model encounters user-,sensor-, or environment-specific shifts after deployment. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, particularly Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) variants, enable efficient adaptation at the edge, the limiting resource for Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) adaptation is often not the number of trainable parameters but the activation state that must be retained until the backward pass. This paper introduces Memory-Floor LoRA (MemFLoRA), a low-rank CNN adapter built around a memory-first design principle rather than a direct application of transformer-oriented LoRA. Instead of merely reducing trainable weights, we define an activation-memory-floor criterion: trainable backward computations must not depend on full-width layer inputs. The resulting adapter freezes the down-projection, trains a scale-matched up-projection, and combines eval-mode backbone normalization with activation-minimal backward rules, reducing saved state to the low-rank branch. Evaluated on three Human Activity Recognition (HAR) datasets and two CNN backbones under subject, body-location, and sensor-placement shifts, MemFLoRA reduces saved-activation memory by 98.5-98.7% and peak training-state memory by 94.9-97.3% relative to full fine-tuning, while matching or exceeding CNN PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [186] [Principled Under Pressure: Post-Training Decides Whether LLMs Act on Their Own Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08670)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者构建了一个预注册的248个情境面板，通过同一模型在"自己行动"与"第三人称判断对错"两种提问方式下的选择差异，测量语言模型"知错仍犯"的价值-行为缺口，并发现该缺口取决于后训练配方而非预训练权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型日益作为智能体行动，而仅评估其"陈述价值"无法捕捉一类特定失败：模型明知某行为不对却仍然去做。这种"说一套做一套"的缺口需要独立于价值陈述的测量方式，以区分"知错仍犯"与"根本不知道更好"。

Method: 构建预注册的248个情境面板，覆盖五类压力；每个情境对同一模型提问两次——一次作为行动者选择做什么，一次以第三人称询问哪个选项正确，以模型自身判断为参照。每个情境配有去除压力的孪生版本，并设置阳性对照（由操作者命令其执行违规动作），以区分"缺失缺口"与"测量失灵"。在OLMo-3-7B-Instruct及四个instruct模型上测试，并考察后训练配方、聊天模板（在/不在模板内读取）、以及行动前对利害与规范的推理的影响。

Result: 在OLMo-3-7B-Instruct上，模型约在五分之一的施压情境中采取了它自己判定为错误的行为，且比在去除压力的同一情境中更频繁。四个instruct模型的缺口取决于后训练配方：OLMo-3与Meta的Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct存在该缺口；Tulu 3在全面板（约0.01概率以上）及其自身最高压力情境上均无缺口；Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct在全面板（约0.02以上）无缺口，在自身情境上结果不确定（0.083，-0.028至0.195）。Meta与AI2的Tulu 3起始自相同的Llama-3.1权重，仅Meta的配方存在缺口。在聊天模板之外读取聊天模型会使缺口符号反转（OLMo-3在无利害时为-0.038，模板内为+0.055），该失真出现在三种配方中的两种。在两个存在缺口的模型上，行动前对利害进行推理会把选择拉回模型自身判断，对照同等长度的非道德任务亦成立；在OLMo-3上，点名所受威胁的规范约贡献其中三分之一的效果。

Conclusion: 该价值-行为缺口是后训练配方可测量的目标，而非预训练权重的固定属性；因此可通过改进后训练与推理时提示来缩小缺口，同时强调了在正确聊天模板内评估模型的重要性。

Abstract: Language models increasingly act as agents. An agent that says an action is wrong and then takes it anyway is a different failure from one that does not know better, and evaluations of stated values cannot see it. We build a pre-registered panel of 248 scenarios across five kinds of pressure. Each scenario is posed twice to the same model, once as the agent choosing what to do and once in the third person asking which option is right, so the model's own judgment is the reference. Every scenario has a twin with the pressure removed, and every model gets a positive control in which its operator orders the violating action, so that a missing gap can be told apart from a blind instrument. On OLMo-3-7B-Instruct, the model takes the action it judged wrong on about one in five pressuring scenarios, more often than on the same scenarios with the pressure removed. Across four instruct models the gap depends on the post-training recipe: OLMo-3 and Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct carry it; Tulu 3 shows none on the whole panel (above about 0.01 in probability) or on its own most-pressuring scenarios; Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct shows none on the whole panel (above about 0.02) and is unresolved on its own (0.083, -0.028 to 0.195). Meta's recipe and Ai2's Tulu 3 start from the same Llama-3.1 weights, and only Meta's carries the gap. Reading a chat model outside its chat template reverses the sign of its gap with nothing at stake (-0.038 against +0.055 under the template on OLMo-3), a distortion present on two of three recipes. On both models that carry it, reasoning about the stakes before acting moves the choice back toward the model's own judgment, against a same-length non-moral task, with or without the pressure; on OLMo-3, naming the norm at stake does about a third of that. The gap is a measurable target for post-training recipes, not a fixed property of pretrained weights.

</details>


### [187] [Variance-Optimal Off-Policy Evaluation with Conjunct Effect Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08677)
*Nicolò Felicioni,Michael Benigni,Maurizio Ferrari Dacrema,Paolo Cremonesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VOCEM估计器，在OffCEM与DR之间插值并最小化方差，闭式求得总体最优插值系数，方差不超过两端点，在23个实验条件下均优于OffCEM和DR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文赌博机策略的离策略评估（OPE）中，动作级重要性权重会带来过大方差。双重稳健（DR）估计虽在共同支撑下无偏，但仍保留高方差的动作级权重；OffCEM用更稳定的簇级权重替代，却依赖奖励模型的局部正确性。因此需要一种兼具稳定性与无偏性的估计方法。

Method: 在DR与OffCEM所需假设下，证明存在一个无偏估计族，可在OffCEM与DR之间插值。据此提出VOCEM估计器，通过选择插值系数最小化方差，并推导出总体最优系数的闭式解。

Result: 理论上证明VOCEM的方差不超过OffCEM与DR任一端点。实验涵盖受控合成设置与两个大动作空间基准，在全部23个评估条件下VOCEM均优于两端点，表现出更强的稳定性与经验鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VOCEM通过方差最优插值统一了OffCEM与DR，在保持无偏的同时降低方差，实验验证其在多条件下的一致优越性。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) for contextual bandit policies becomes challenging when action-level importance weighting incurs excessive variance. Doubly robust (DR) estimation remains unbiased under common support but retains these high-variance action-level weights. A prior estimator, Off-policy evaluation with Conjunct Effect Model (OffCEM), replaces them with more stable cluster-level weights, at the cost of relying on local correctness of the reward model. In this paper, we show that, under the assumptions required by DR and OffCEM, there exists an unbiased family of estimators that interpolates between OffCEM and DR. Building on this result, we propose the Variance Optimal-CEM (VOCEM) estimator, which selects the interpolation coefficient to minimize variance. We derive the population-optimal coefficient in closed form and show that the resulting estimator has variance no larger than either endpoint, OffCEM or DR. Experiments in controlled synthetic settings and on two large-action benchmarks show that VOCEM improves upon both endpoints in all 23 evaluated conditions, exhibiting greater stability and empirical robustness.

</details>


### [188] [A Systematic Study of Small Language Models on Abstract Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08680)
*Nur A Zarin Nishat,Jens Lehmann,Andrei Aioanei,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在ARC-TGI基准上系统考察小型语言模型抽象推理能力的习得与迁移，发现高分不代表掌握了可迁移规则，性能高度依赖模型族、训练方式与评测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象推理基准上的端点准确率无法反映语言模型是否学到可迁移规则，还是仅拟合了分布特异的规律性。作者希望厘清这一区别，并揭示影响抽象推理表现的各类因素。

Method: 在ARC-TGI基准上，对decoder-only、encoder-decoder与mixture-of-experts三类模型族进行有监督微调，构建覆盖1000余次运行：分析技能习得的效率与稳定性、训练分布外的鲁棒性、模型族与任务表述的交互，以及伴随行为差异的逐层注意力特征；同时支持重采样、空间变换与跨基准迁移。

Result: 分布内准确率可达较高水平，但习得对优化敏感且在任务族间分布不均；训练分布外性能急剧下降，即使规则保留但网格尺度改变时亦然。增加训练集深度与广度的收益不均衡，额外上下文示例的作用依赖模型族。可执行规则归纳能解出直接网格生成未能得到的正确答案；注意力诊断显示特定任务上有不同的集中度与上下文依赖特征，但未能确立普遍因果机制。

Conclusion: 抽象推理得分是条件性的，取决于模型、适配机制、评测分布与响应格式，不能作为可迁移规则的直接证据。

Abstract: Endpoint accuracy on abstract-reasoning benchmarks does not reveal whether a language model has acquired a transferable rule or fit distribution-specific regularities. We study this distinction in small language models on the ARC-TGI benchmark, which organizes abstract grid transformations into controllable task families and supports resampling, spatial shifts, and cross-benchmark transfer. Across more than 1,000 runs, we profile decoder-only, encoder--decoder, and mixture-of-experts model families under supervised fine-tuning. We examine the efficiency and stability of skill acquisition, robustness beyond the training distribution, interactions with model family and task formulation, and layer-wise attention signatures that accompany behavioral differences. Substantial in-distribution accuracy is attainable, but acquisition is sensitive to optimization and unevenly distributed across task families. Performance deteriorates sharply outside the training distribution, including when the rule is retained but grid scale changes. Greater training-set depth and breadth yield uneven gains, while the effect of additional in-context examples depends on model family. Executable-rule induction also yields correct solutions not observed under direct grid generation. On selected tasks, attention diagnostics show distinct concentration and context-dependence profiles, but do not establish general causal mechanisms. Overall, abstract-reasoning scores are conditional on the model, adaptation regime, evaluation distribution, and response format.

</details>


### [189] [Probabilistic Counterfactual Inference for Discrete Outcomes in Gaussian-Process Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08689)
*Juliette Sinnott,Amir-Hossein Karimi,Mohammad Kohandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为含离散子节点的GP-SCM提出统一的反事实推断框架，通过将GP预测器与显式外生噪声机制配对，为二元、名义和有序变量分别推导精确的噪声绑架程序，并证明其可复现拟合模型的观测与干预分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GP-SCM反事实推断主要针对连续内生变量，难以适用于包含离散子节点、连续父节点的因果图，因此需要统一框架支持异质变量类型的反事实推断。

Method: 将GP预测器与显式外生噪声机制结合：二元变量采用均匀阈值，名义类别采用Gumbel-max竞争，有序变量采用潜高斯切点模型；推导精确条件噪声绑架，并在干预下传播绑架噪声，同时考虑GP潜函数的后验不确定性。

Result: 在已知真实反事实的合成SCM上评估精度、一致性与对耦合误设的鲁棒性；发现对有序数据误用类别耦合会使反事实误差约增至三倍，且不随数据增多而下降，误差趋于下限；反向对名义数据强加虚假顺序则会损害拟合方程本身。

Conclusion: 耦合机制的选择必须基于结构理由，而不能仅依据观测拟合优度来判断。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference in Gaussian-process structural causal models (GP-SCMs) has been developed primarily for continuous endogenous variables, limiting applicability to causal graphs that contain discrete child nodes with continuous parents. We introduce a unified probabilistic framework for counterfactual inference with heterogeneous variable types by pairing GP predictors with explicit exogenous noise mechanisms. For discrete outcomes, we derive exact conditional noise-abduction procedures using a uniform threshold for binary variables, a Gumbel-max race for nominal categories, and a latent Gaussian cut-point model for ordinal ones. In each case, we propagate abducted noise through interventions while accounting for posterior uncertainty in the GP latent functions, and prove that the resulting mechanisms reproduce the fitted model's observational and interventional distributions. On synthetic SCMs with known ground-truth counterfactuals, we evaluate estimation accuracy, consistency, and robustness to coupling misspecification. A key finding is that applying a categorical coupling to ordinal data inflates counterfactual error roughly threefold even when observational fit remains comparable, and that this error does not diminish with more data. As the training set grows, the fitted structural equation converges to the truth while the counterfactual error flattens onto a floor. In the reverse direction, forcing a false order onto nominal data instead degrades the fitted equation itself. The choice of coupling must therefore be justified on structural grounds rather than read off the fit.

</details>


### [190] [GeneICL: A Tabular Foundation Model for Bulk Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08694)
*Michael Bohl,Alexander Theus,David Wissel,Valentina Boeva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeneICL——一个4.2M参数的表格基础模型，通过从实测bulk表达谱构建的半合成预训练先验与参数高效循环架构，实现转录组学感知的预训练；并利用Cox偏似然残差将右删失生存预测无训练地归约为回归。在80个临床结局预测任务上，GeneICL在基础模型与调优基线中综合排名最优，参数量最多少387倍，推理无需梯度更新，笔记本CPU数秒完成预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达测量在生物医学中广泛应用，但临床结局预测受限于高维性、强特征相关性与标注数据稀缺。大型自监督转录组基础模型常常无法超越简单监督基线；表格基础模型虽通过上下文学习提供替代方案，却通常在通用合成数据上预训练，未纳入转录组结构。作者因此追问：缺失的关键是否是转录组学感知的预训练，而非规模。

Method: 提出GeneICL，一个4.2M参数的表格基础模型：结合由实测bulk表达谱构建的半合成预训练先验，以及参数高效的循环架构。此外，通过Cox偏似然残差将右删失生存预测无训练地归约为回归任务，从而支持生存分析。

Result: 在覆盖分类、回归与生存分析的80个临床结局预测任务上评估。表格基础模型一致优于自监督转录组模型；GeneICL在评估的基础模型与调优基线中获得最佳综合排名。GeneICL参数量最多减少387倍，推理时无需梯度更新，在笔记本CPU上数秒内给出预测。

Conclusion: 转录组学感知的预训练（而非单纯扩大规模）是提升临床结局预测的关键。GeneICL以极小参数规模、免训练推理与低成本部署，在多种临床预测任务上超越自监督转录组模型并取得最优综合排名。

Abstract: Gene expression is widely measured in biomedicine, yet clinical outcome prediction remains challenging due to high dimensionality, strong feature correlations, and limited labeled data. Large self-supervised transcriptomic foundation models often fail to outperform simple supervised baselines. Tabular foundation models offer an alternative through in-context learning, but are typically pretrained on generic synthetic data rather than transcriptomic structure. We ask whether transcriptomics-aware pretraining, rather than scale, is the missing ingredient. Towards this end, we introduce GeneICL, a 4.2M-parameter tabular foundation model combining a semi-synthetic pretraining prior built from measured bulk expression profiles with a parameter-efficient recurrent architecture. We further enable right-censored survival prediction via a training-free reduction to regression using Cox partial-likelihood residuals. We evaluate GeneICL on 80 clinical outcome-prediction tasks spanning classification, regression, and survival. Tabular foundation models consistently outperform self-supervised transcriptomic models, while GeneICL achieves the best overall rank among evaluated foundation models and tuned baselines. GeneICL does so with up to 387$\times$ fewer parameters, no gradient updates at inference, and predictions within seconds on a laptop CPU.

</details>


### [191] [Optimal and Efficient Online Inverse Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08735)
*Anupam Gupta,Guru Guruganesh,Honghao Lin,Vahab Mirrokni,Renato Paes Leme,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线逆线性优化问题，证明了Sakaue所提最优遗憾$O(\sqrt d)$可在多项式时间内达到：给出确定性算法，遗憾为$O(\sqrt d)$，运行时间关于$d$和$T$多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线逆线性优化中，学习者在每轮推荐一个动作，并观察一个在$\mathbb{R}^d$上最大化某个固定但未知线性目标的专家的选择，目标是在不观测目标的情况下学会优化该目标。Sakaue最近用随机算法取得了最优遗憾$O(\sqrt d)$，但每轮需$(dT)^{O(d)}$次线性优化，并公开提问能否在多项式时间内达到该最优遗憾。

Method: 本文给出一个确定性算法，是对Sakaue等和Cai等变量度量算法（variable-metric algorithms）的变体，其核心思想是：当查询点移动得离度量更新发生处足够远时，撤销该度量更新。

Result: 算法对任意时间跨度$T$均具有$O(\sqrt d)$的遗憾，且运行时间关于$d$和$T$均为多项式。

Conclusion: 正面回答了Sakaue的开放问题：最优遗憾$O(\sqrt d)$可以在多项式时间内达到，且可借助确定性算法实现。

Abstract: In online inverse linear optimization, a learner recommends an action and then observes the choice of an expert who maximizes a fixed, unknown linear objective on $\mathbb{R}^{d}$; the goal is to learn to optimize this objective without observing it. Sakaue recently obtained the optimal regret $O(\sqrt d)$ with a randomized algorithm making $(dT)^{O(d)}$ linear optimizations per round, and asked whether it can be attained in polynomial time. We answer positively: our deterministic algorithm has regret $O(\sqrt d)$ for every horizon $T$ and runs in time polynomial in $d$ and $T$. It is a variant of the variable-metric algorithms of Sakaue et al.\ and Cai et al., in which a metric update is revoked once the query point moves far enough from where the update was made.

</details>


### [192] [On the Computational Tractability of Robust Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08740)
*Vanessa Kosoy,Vinayak Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对不精确（鲁棒）赌博机问题，识别出一个可多项式时间学习的特例，达到Õ(√T)遗憾，并证明其若干小泛化均为NP-hard，表明该特例处于可处理性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当环境不属于学习器假设类时，通常用不可知（agnostic）学习保证处理，但在监督学习之外很难获得不可知保证。不精确/鲁棒赌博机被提出作为赌博机场景下不可实现学习的另一途径，已有Θ(√T)遗憾的学习器，但缺乏计算保证；本文旨在填补计算可行性方面的空白。

Method: 在不精确（鲁棒）赌博机框架下，识别出一个特殊情形，并为其设计多项式时间学习算法；同时通过归约证明该特例的若干小规模泛化问题是NP-hard的。

Result: 给出了该特例的多项式时间学习器，实现Õ(√T)遗憾；并证明该特例的几个小泛化均为NP-hard。

Conclusion: 该特例位于可处理与不可处理之间的边界。由于不可实现学习问题的高效学习器被认为对AI对齐问题至关重要，本工作朝此方向迈出了一小步。

Abstract: Learning when the environment does not belong to the learner's hypothesis class is typically handled using agnostic learning guarantees. However, for anything beyond supervised learning, agnostic guarantees are difficult to come by. Recently, imprecise bandits (Kosoy, 2025) (later renamed to robust bandits in Appel and Kosoy, 2025) were introduced as another approach to unrealizable learning in the bandits setting and a $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret learner was shown for a large class. However, no computational guarantees were provided. In this paper we identify a special case that admits a polynomial-time learner with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret. We also show that several small generalizations of this special case are NP-hard thus indicating that the special case is at the boundary of what is tractable. It has been recently suggested (Kosoy, 2018) that computationally efficient learners for unrealizable learning problems are crucial for solving the AI alignment problem. This work is a small step in that direction.

</details>


### [193] [Reinforcement Learning with Conformal Action Sets: An Application to Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08743)
*Wenwen Si,Honghao Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RLCP方法，用critic分数和在线阈值自适应调整保留动作集，理论分析剪枝对奖励的影响并在两个数据集上验证其多样性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐通常使用固定的候选列表大小，但在单个会话内有效备选动作的数量会发生变化，固定slate size不符合实际需求。

Method: Reinforcement Learning with Calibrated Pruning (RLCP)：利用critic分数与在线阈值自适应地确定保留动作集；阈值基于二元反馈（集合是否包含代理目标中的动作）更新；推导价值损失的精确分解（filtering损失与selection损失）。

Result: 证明了自适应轨迹上代理漏检率的确定性上界；在显式代理与critic近似条件下给出有限会话奖励界（考虑不完美选择与集合截断，无需学习参数收敛）；在KuaiRand-Pure和MovieLens 1M上，19个配置中每个至少有一个RLCP变体取得最高目录多样性，为最强基线的1.11×至5.21×，会话深度具竞争力且保留集不大于基线。

Conclusion: RLCP通过校准剪枝自适应调整动作集，在不增大保留集的前提下显著提升目录多样性，并提供了漏检率与奖励损失的理论保证。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders typically use a fixed slate size even though the number of useful alternatives changes within a session. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Calibrated Pruning (RLCP), which adapts the retained action set using critic scores and an online threshold. The threshold is updated from binary feedback indicating whether the set contains an action in a proxy target. We prove a deterministic bound on the observed proxy miss rate along adaptive trajectories. To quantify the effect of pruning on reward, we derive an exact decomposition of value loss into filtering and selection losses. Under explicit proxy and critic approximation conditions, this decomposition yields a finite session reward bound that also accounts for imperfect selection and set truncation, without requiring the learning parameters to converge. Experiments on KuaiRand-Pure and MovieLens 1M compare two RLCP implementations with four RL baselines. In each of the 19 configurations, at least one RLCP variant achieves the highest catalog diversity, reaching $1.11\times$ to $5.21\times$ that of the strongest baseline, with competitive session depth and no larger retained sets.

</details>


### [194] [Linear Bandits under Exact Sliding-Window Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08745)
*Seyed Mohammad Hadi Hosseini,Yasin Abbasi-Yadkori,Sattar Vakili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究精确滑动窗口约束下的线性bandit问题。离线情形证明凸性与循环平移不变性使平稳解最优；在线情形揭示仅靠几何结构无法学习，提出转移直径τ与稀有切换OFUL算法，达到Õ(d√T+τd+w)遗憾；进一步引入历史状态直径D处理一般约束，达到Õ(d√T+dD+w)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常要求任意连续动作块落在预定可行集内（如滑动窗口约束），但已有线性bandit研究多忽略此类精确约束。作者希望刻画该约束下离线最优结构，并探明在线学习是否可行、如何设计算法以保持严格可行性与低遗憾。

Method: 离线：利用凸性与循环平移不变性分析平稳解最优性（w|T时最优，否则O(w)加性间隙）。在线：定义转移直径τ量化可行可达性，设计稀有切换OFUL算法；去除循环不变性后，将近期动作历史视为有限记忆控制问题状态，定义历史状态直径D，结合乐观剩余时域规划与稀有策略更新。

Result: 在线仅凭几何结构不足以学习，次线性遗憾可能不可实现。稀有切换OFUL得到相对离线最优可行轨迹的遗憾Õ(d√T+τd+w)；一般约束下得到Õ(d√T+dD+w)。在真实与合成基准上，方法保持精确可行性，奖励与遗憾与基线相当，且策略更新显著更少。

Conclusion: 精确滑动窗口约束下的线性bandit需借助可达性度量（τ、D）才能实现有效学习；所提稀有切换/稀有更新算法在保证严格可行性的同时取得近优遗憾并大幅降低策略更新次数。

Abstract: We study linear bandits under exact sliding-window constraints, where every consecutive block of actions must belong to a prescribed feasible set. In the offline setting, where the reward function is known, we show that convexity and cyclic-shift invariance make a stationary solution optimal when $w\mid T$ and within an additive $O(w)$ gap otherwise. In the online setting, we show that geometric structure alone is insufficient for learning, and sublinear regret can be impossible. We introduce a transition diameter $τ$ that quantifies feasible reachability and develop a rare-switching OFUL algorithm with regret $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T}+τd+w)$ against the offline-optimal feasible trajectory. Finally, we remove cyclic invariance and consider general sliding-window constraints, where optimal behavior may be non-stationary. We represent recent action history as the state of a finite-memory control problem and introduce a history-state diameter $D$ that measures feasible communication between viable histories. Combining optimistic remaining-horizon planning with rare policy updates, we obtain a regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T}+dD+w)$. We evaluate our approach on real-world and synthetic benchmarks, showing that it maintains exact feasibility while achieving reward and regret comparable to baselines with substantially fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [195] [Neural Petri flows for chemical reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08750)
*Jose Eduardo Escrig Molina,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neural Petri Flow（NPF），一种每个权重取值下都严格保持Petri网语义的架构，将反应建模为价键网上的令牌博弈，仅学习触发速率律，其余层无参数硬编码，在原子映射、反应分类与正向预测上均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Petri网的反应学习模型与神经网络仅把网当作消息传递的脚手架，并不保证价键守恒、局部性与使能规则等化学语义。作者试图回答：什么样的架构对其任意权重都仍是一个真正的Petri网，从而在保证语义的前提下学习反应动力学。

Method: 从Petri网理论出发，证明守恒性强制触发形式 m′=m+Cσ、非负性在局部速率律上强制使能规则，并保持局部性（使能仅读取变迁的输入），因此仅速率律（各变迁触发倾向）可自由学习。据此提出Neural Petri Flow：用参数化网络学习该速率律或分类读出，其余部分硬编码为无参数层，在"价键网"上统一处理原子映射、反应分类与正向预测。

Result: 无训练时，最小触发向量在Golden集上映射准确率88.8%（RXNMapper 85.6%），在EnzymeMap酶反应上88.7%（77.9%）；在USPTO-480K上，NPF用这些触发向量训练可预测87.7%产物，仅用1%子集时达67.4%；ECREACT的EC号第三级预测准确率90.2%，领先最佳已发表方法5.6个百分点；以电子为令牌，同一令牌博弈对FlowER基元步骤的首次预测达90.5%，超越已发表基线，且所有top-1预测无需过滤即为有效分子。

Conclusion: 通过从理论推导Petri网的强制语义，NPF把所有可学习容量集中于速率律，实现了一个对任意权重都仍是Petri网的架构，将多个反应任务统一到单一触发向量上，在多项基准上取得领先且无需后处理过滤。

Abstract: Petri nets have been used to describe chemical processes such as reactions.They map well to chemistry: Places are the bonds between atoms and the free valence of each atom, a token is a unit of bond order, a transition forms or breaks a bond, the conserved quantities are the valence budgets of the atoms, and the enabling rule is the valence rule. These semantics are not guaranteed by learned models of reactions or neural networks that are built on Petri nets that use the net as a scaffold for message passing. Here, we ask what architecture remains a Petri net for every value of its weights. We find the answer in the theory, where all semantics of a net share the firing form $m^\prime=m+Cσ$, locality, as enabling reads only the inputs of a transition, and the enabling rule, and we prove that conservation forces the firing form and that non-negativity forces the enabling rule on local rate laws. This leaves free the rate law, which is the propensity of each transition to fire. We introduce Neural Petri Flow, which learns this rate law, or a readout for classification, and hard-wires the rest as parameter-free layers. On what we denote a valence net, atom mapping, reaction classification, and forward prediction become three tasks on one firing vector. Without training, the minimum firing vector maps 88.8% of the curated Golden set against 85.6% for RXNMapper, and 88.7 against 77.9% of the enzymatic reactions of EnzymeMap. On USPTO-480K, NPF trained on these firing vectors predicts 87.7% of the products and 67.4% when trained on a 1% subset of the training reactions. EC numbers of ECREACT are predicted at the third level for 90.2% of reactions, 5.6 points ahead of the best published method. With electrons as tokens, the same token game predicts 90.5% of the elementary steps of FlowER first, ahead of the published baseline, and every top-1 prediction is a valid molecule without a filter.

</details>


### [196] [Conformal Prediction Sets Quantify Information Gain: A Theoretical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08785)
*Kevin Zhang,Stephen Bates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为"预测集大小作为不确定性度量"这一直觉提供了信息论基础：通过决策论推广的熵，构建基于Conformal预测集大小与覆盖率的广义信息量族，证明Shannon互信息可精确表示为其积分，并给出集大小缩减的夹逼界与数据处理不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conformal预测能给出带有限样本覆盖保证的预测集，实践中常以预测集大小作为不确定性的启发式度量，但这种解释缺乏信息论依据。作者希望从理论上说明集大小缩减为何可视为信息增益。

Method: 提出面向集合值预测的决策论熵推广，定义一族基于Conformal预测集大小与覆盖率的广义信息度量；利用该族度量给出Shannon互信息的精确积分表示；在标准分类设定下推导集大小缩减的界与性质（夹逼、数据处理不等式），并控制有限样本校准与模型误差项。实证在11个分类场景验证，并进行贪心特征选择实验比较集大小缩减与Shannon互信息的特征排序。

Result: 理论上：Shannon互信息可由该度量族精确积分表示；额外信息带来的Conformal集大小缩减被校准相关的该族成员上下夹逼，且满足数据处理不等式（均至有限样本校准与模型误差项）。实证上：在11个分类设置中验证了理论，并发现集大小缩减与Shannon互信息在贪心特征选择中会对特征给出不同排序。

Conclusion: 该工作将Conformal预测与经典信息论量形式化地联系起来，为以集大小缩减作为信息增益度量提供了理论依据，同时提示二者在特征选择等任务中并非完全等价，需谨慎互换使用。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a popular tool for uncertainty quantification that outputs prediction sets with finite-sample coverage guarantees. While prediction set size is commonly used as a heuristic measure of uncertainty, the information-theoretic basis for this interpretation remains poorly understood. In this work, we provide such a foundation using a decision-theoretic generalization of entropy tailored to set-valued prediction. In particular, we introduce a family of generalized information measures based on the size and coverage of conformal prediction sets. Notably, Shannon mutual information admits an exact integral representation in terms of these measures. We then show that, in standard classification settings, the reduction in conformal set size from additional information (i) is sandwiched between calibration-dependent members of this family and (ii) obeys a data processing inequality, both up to finite-sample calibration and model error terms. Together, our results formally relate conformal prediction to classical information-theoretic quantities and justify using set-size reduction as an information gain metric. Empirically, we validate our theory across 11 classification settings and show that set-size reduction and Shannon mutual information can rank features differently in a greedy feature selection experiment.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [SAFESHIELD: A Decision-Organization Framework for Deployment-Time Safety of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07276)
*Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Aarti Choudhary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文将部署时安全重新表述为“决策组织”问题，提出SAFESHIELD系统，通过责任分解与显式协调四类安全决策，并用可审计的Decision Traces验证协调机制对端到端安全的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署框架虽提供越来越强的运行时护栏机制（输入审核、路由、检索验证、输出过滤），但对其产生的安全决策应如何显式组织、协调与审计缺乏指导。论文认为部署时安全不仅取决于单个护栏的能力，还取决于决策如何被组织和协调。

Method: 将部署时安全形式化为含两要素的决策组织问题：面向责任的决策分解与决策间的显式协调。在小语言模型上实例化为SAFESHIELD系统，组织四类反复出现的决策职责——准入（admission）、路由（routing）、证据（evidence）、放行（release），并将已提交决策记录为可审计的Decision Traces。评估采用机制级实验、聚合阶段消融、受控协调消融以及面向部署的压力测试套件。

Result: 机制级结果表明实例化护栏具备决策过程所需能力；聚合消融显示移除安全组织后端到端安全显著退化。更关键的是协调消融在保留各护栏机制的同时选择性切断其依赖：移除准入门控显著增加误放行，且若向放行决策隐瞒上游证据，放行准确率从96.0%降至69.5%。

Conclusion: 提供了系统级证据：部署时安全不仅依赖单个护栏的能力，也依赖其决策被组织和协调的方式。

Abstract: Deployment-time safety of language models is commonly implemented through runtime guardrails such as input moderation, routing, retrieval verification, and output filtering. Existing deployment frameworks provide increasingly capable mechanisms for these functions, but offer limited guidance on how the safety decisions they produce should be explicitly organized, coordinated, and audited. We formulate deployment-time safety as a decision-organization problem with two elements: responsibility-oriented decomposition of safety decisions and explicit coordination among them. We instantiate this formulation in SAFESHIELD, a deployment-time safety system for small language models that organizes four recurring decision responsibilities (admission, routing, evidence, and release) and records committed decisions in auditable Decision Traces. We evaluate SAFESHIELD through mechanism-level experiments, aggregate stage ablations, controlled coordination ablations, and a deployment-oriented stress suite. Mechanism-level results show that the instantiated safeguards provide the capabilities required by the decision process, while aggregate ablations show substantial degradation in end-to-end safety as the surrounding safety organization is removed. More importantly, dedicated coordination ablations preserve the participating safeguard mechanisms while selectively severing their dependencies: removing admission gating substantially increases false release, and withholding upstream evidence from the release decision reduces release accuracy from 96.0% to 69.5%. These results provide system-level evidence that deployment-time safety depends not only on the capability of individual guardrails, but also on how their decisions are organized and coordinated.

</details>


### [198] [Catching Developers in the Flow: Low-Latency Agentic Program Repair at Google Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07289)
*Celal Ziftci,Spencer Greene,Ray Liu,Livio Dalloro,Lorenzo Dini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍FlowAgent，一个部署于Google的AI智能体，在持续集成预提交阶段自动修复测试失败，采用ReAct式生成—验证循环与弃权过滤器，在严格延迟约束下提供高质量修复建议，并已大规模落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工修复程序失败耗时且打断开发流程，尤其在预提交阶段测试失败时；现有LLM自动修复技术多面向提交后离线流程，缺乏实时辅助开发者所需的低延迟能力。

Method: FlowAgent集成于Google内部工具Critique与Cider，采用ReAct式生成—验证循环，并设置严格的执行前与执行后弃权过滤器，以在严格延迟约束下保证建议质量。

Result: 195个真实测试失败的人工评估显示67.18%的修复准确率；Google全量部署后建议修复295,508个变更，开发者预览65,069个、应用28,554个。

Conclusion: 开发者访谈表明该智能体在建议正确修复方面有用，工业软件工程流程对自主修复智能体的集成接受度良好，但仍存在值得关注的问题与机遇。

Abstract: Manual repair of program failures is time-consuming and disruptive for software developers, particularly during the pre-submit phase where test failures occur within continuous integration systems. While Automated Program Repair has seen significant advancement through Large Language Models, existing state-of-the-art techniques primarily focus on post-submit workflows, operating offline without the low-latency requirements necessary to assist developers in real-time within their flow before they switch context.
  In this paper, we introduce FlowAgent, an AI agent deployed at Google to automatically repair test failures in the pre-submit outer-loop workflow inside continuous integration systems. Integrated into Google's internal developer tools, Critique and Cider,FlowAgent utilizes a ReAct-style generate-and-validate loop, as well as rigorous pre-execution and post-execution abstention filters to ensure high-quality suggestions under strict latency constraints.
  Based on our case studies, FlowAgent is highly effective. First, a manual evaluation conducted on 195 real-world test failures demonstrated 67.18% accuracy in suggesting correct fixes. Following its Google-wide deployment, FlowAgent suggested fixes on 295,508changes, of which developers previewed 65,069 and applied 28,554. Developer feedback from interviews indicate that the agent is useful in suggesting correct fixes, integration of autonomous repair agents into industrial software engineering workflows is received well, while interesting challenges and opportunities still remain.

</details>


### [199] [A Validated Dataset and Benchmark for Coherent Multi-Diagram SysML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07356)
*Ardalan Aryashad,Yan Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出SEMAADB数据集与基准，包含3,000个工程语境与15,000张SysML图（每语境五类相互关联视图），用于评估大语言模型生成连贯多图系统的能力，并在图表修复与跨图更新两项任务上评测三个语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统工程需要多张图共同描述系统结构与行为，须保证元素一致、图间协调；大语言模型可生成图（文本或代码），但其生成"连贯成套图"的能力缺乏理解，且现有数据集与基准无法大规模直接衡量该能力。

Method: 构建SEMAADB数据集：3,000个工程语境、共15,000张图，每语境含五类相连SysML视图（需求图、块定义图、活动图、状态机图、序列图）。对图组进行一致性与可渲染性校验，并人工核验100个语境构成基准测试集；在此基础上评测三个语言模型的两项任务——图表修复（diagram repair）与跨图更新（cross-diagram update），指标包括语义错误修复率与传播F1。

Result: 图表修复任务中，最强模型修复64.3%的语义错误；跨图更新任务中，当模型对相关图施加同一变更时，最佳传播F1为80.7%。结果表明语法层面的修复已接近解决，而语义修复与跨图一致性仍是模型的困难任务。

Conclusion: SEMAADB同时提供了大规模图资源和一套基准，可用于衡量连贯的多图SysML生成能力。

Abstract: Systems engineers use several diagrams to describe the structure and behavior of systems. Engineers create these diagrams together to make sure that they use the same elements and remain consistent with one another. Large language models can generate diagrams as text or code, which makes it possible to create system diagrams automatically. However, their ability to generate coherent sets of diagrams is not well understood, and existing datasets and benchmarks do not directly measure this ability at scale. We introduce SEMAADB (Systems Engineering Modeling Assistant with AI Dataset and Benchmark), a dataset of 3,000 engineering contexts and 15,000 diagrams. Each context contains five connected SysML views: Requirement, Block Definition, Activity, State Machine, and Sequence. Here, a view is a diagram that presents one aspect of a system. We checked the diagram sets for consistency and valid rendering. A set of 100 contexts is also human-verified and forms the benchmark test set. We evaluate three language models on two tasks. In diagram repair, the strongest model repairs 64.3% of semantic errors . In cross-diagram update the best propagation F1 is 80.7% when a model applies one change across related diagrams. The results show that syntax repair is nearly solved, but semantic repair and consistency across diagrams are still challenging tasks for models. SEMAADB therefore provides both a large diagram resource and a set of benchmarks for measuring coherent multi-diagram SysML generation.

</details>


### [200] [CogAdapt: Cognition-informed Sparse Adaptation of Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07446)
*Yueke Zhang,Zihan Fang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CogAdapt利用人类脑电(EEG)与眼动注意力数据学习程序级与token级先验，指导代码模型进行选择性稀疏微调，仅更新部分Transformer块，在减少86%以上可训练参数的同时提升pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成能力提升多依赖昂贵的全参数微调；已有类人学习(如认知信号引导)方法仍需调整大部分参数，训练成本没有实质降低。作者假设人类认知信号不仅指明学什么，还能指明在哪里做适配最有效，从而实现低成本且不牺牲性能的选择性适配。

Method: 提出CogAdapt认知引导框架：先从人类EEG和注意力数据中学习可迁移的程序级与token级先验；再将这些先验与冻结模型对当前编程任务的响应结合，决定分配的适配量以及需要更新的Transformer块；微调时仅更新被选中的块，推理阶段无需新的脑电记录。

Result: 在Qwen与GLM上均发现人类阅读行为与MoE计算之间存在一致性对应关系。CogAdapt在LiveCodeBench与BigCodeBench上均取得最优pass@1，其中在LiveCodeBench上相比常规微调基线分别提升10.86和6.29个百分点，同时将可接收梯度的适配参数减少86.21%-87.21%。

Conclusion: 人类理解信号可为代码模型的适配提供有价值的指导，使适配过程既更具选择性又更高效，证明认知先验可用于稀疏化微调而无需牺牲甚至提升性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become increasingly capable of generating code. However, achieving stronger code-generation performance still often relies on costly model adaptation, i.e., fine-tuning pretrained model parameters. Prior studies have shown correspondence between human code processing and neural models' attention or internal computation. Human-aligned learning approaches use cognitive signals to guide training, but typically adapt a large portion of the model, leaving training costs largely unchanged. Human cognitive signals may indicate not only what the model must learn from, but also where adaptation is most useful. We investigate whether human responses during code reading correspond to code-model behavior and can guide selective adaptation without sacrificing performance.
  We present CogAdapt, a cognition-informed framework for task-dependent sparse adaptation of code models. CogAdapt first learns transferable program-level and token-level priors from human Electroencephalography (EEG) and attention data, then combines these priors with the frozen model's response to each coding task to determine how much adaptation to allocate and which transformer blocks should receive updates. During fine-tuning, only the selected blocks are updated, while no new human recordings are required for inference. Across Qwen and GLM, we find consistent correspondence between human reading behavior and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) computation. CogAdapt achieves the best pass@1 across both LiveCodeBench and BigCodeBench, including gains of 10.86 and 6.29 percentage points over matched regular fine-tuning on LiveCodeBench, while reducing gradient-eligible adaptation parameters by 86.21-87.21%. These results suggest that human comprehension signals can provide useful guidance for making code-model adaptation both more selective and more effective.

</details>


### [201] [The EPIC Framework for Spec-Driven Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07534)
*Bruno Claudino,Ruizhe Xing,Yuanhao Zuo,Tyler Menezes,Anita Sarma,Kostadin Damevski,Zixuan Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究高质量规格驱动开发（SDD）规格应包含什么，基于 ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148 对 114 个开源 SDD 仓库进行评分，从高分仓库中提炼出 EPIC 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体会用自身假设填补指令中的空白，措辞细节（如 "must" 与 "should"）会影响其行为。SDD 要求开发者先写规格、计划和任务，但现有模板无法帮助开发者判断规格是否足够充分、清晰。

Method: 以 ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148 为标准，对 114 个开源 SDD 仓库的制品进行评分，并从得分最高的仓库中提炼实践，构建出包含 10 个质量维度、40 条实践的 EPIC 框架；同时通过 15 名 SDD 实践者进行认可度验证，并对比规格质量高低与仓库指标的关系。

Result: EPIC 的 40 条实践均获多数实践者（N=15）认可。规格质量排名前三分之一的仓库，其提交中用于缺陷修复的比例为 11.8%，后三分之一为 20.4%；前者的贡献者中位数为后者的 4 倍。

Conclusion: 开发者可用 EPIC 在智能体之前发现并填补规格中的空白，从而提升规格质量并可能降低缺陷修复负担、吸引更多贡献者。

Abstract: One practitioner we interviewed said their team writes "must" instead of "should" when instructing a coding agent, because the agent may treat "should" as optional. Small wording choices matter because agents often fill gaps in their instructions with their own assumptions. Spec-driven development (SDD) asks developers to write a specification, plan, and tasks before the agent writes code. SDD frameworks provide templates for these artifacts, but the templates do not help developers judge whether they have written enough or clearly enough. We studied what good SDD specifications contain. We scored the artifacts of 114 open-source SDD repositories against ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148 and derived practices from the highest-scoring ones. The resulting framework, EPIC, has 40 practices in 10 quality dimensions that guide developers in making expectations and decisions explicit in specifications, plans, and tasks for coding agents. The majority of SDD practitioners (N=15) endorsed every practice. Repositories in the top third of specification quality spent 11.8% of their commits on bug fixes, compared with 20.4% in the bottom third, and had a median of 4x as many contributors. Developers can use EPIC to find and fill the gaps in a specification before the agent does.

</details>


### [202] [CheckerBench: Can Long-Horizon Agents Synthesize Static-Analysis Checkers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07557)
*Hang He,Li Wang,Hao Chen,Yuchen Shao,Yuling Shi,Lisheng Wang,Peiyang Liu,Goose Lin,Zaiyuan Wang,Haiying Sun,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CheckerBench 基准（300 个源自 297 个 CVE、覆盖 167 个仓库、85 个 CWE 与五类语言生态的任务）及 CheckerLab 评估框架，系统评测编码智能体从头构建静态分析检查器的能力。在 21 种模型—框架配置、每种三次重复下，平均 Pass@1 为 32.30%，最佳为 45.33%，表明可靠可复用的检查器开发仍是当前智能体的难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析检查器合成要求智能体完成缺陷规格理解、仓库探查、分析器特定逻辑实现，并借助反复的编译与分析反馈迭代改进。然而现有编码智能体基准多聚焦补丁生成或漏洞检测，很少评估智能体能否在真实仓库中端到端开发出可用的检查器，因而缺少对这一能力的系统度量手段。

Method: 作者构建了两个互补组件：CheckerBench 是包含 300 个任务的可执行基准，任务源自 297 个 CVE，覆盖 167 个仓库、85 个 CWE 与五类语言生态；每个任务提供漏洞版本与修复版本、固定的分析环境以及检查器脚手架。CheckerLab 是统一评估框架，独立重建提交的检查器，并度量漏洞—修复诊断对比度、补丁定位、误报率与工具使用情况。评估覆盖 21 种模型—框架配置，每种配置独立重复三次。

Result: 在 21 种模型—框架配置、每种配置三次独立重复的评测设置下，平均 Pass@1 为 32.30%，表现最好的配置达到 45.33%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于当前编码智能体而言，开发可靠且可复用的静态分析检查器仍是一项具有挑战性的任务，端到端检查器合成的能力距离实用水平仍有明显差距。

Abstract: Static-analysis checker synthesis requires agents to interpret a defect specification, inspect a repository, implement analyzer-specific logic, and refine the checker through repeated compilation and analysis feedback. Existing coding-agent benchmarks focus on tasks such as patch generation or vulnerability detection and rarely assess whether an agent can develop a working checker in a repository from start to finish. We introduce CheckerBench, an executable benchmark of 300 tasks derived from 297 CVEs across 167 repositories, 85 CWEs, and five language ecosystems. Each task includes vulnerable and fixed revisions, a pinned analysis environment, and a checker scaffold. We further introduce CheckerLab, a common evaluation framework that independently rebuilds submitted checkers and measures vulnerable-fixed diagnostic contrast, patch localization, false positives, and tool use. Across 21 model-harness configurations and three independent repeats per configuration, mean Pass@1 is 32.30%, while the best reaches 45.33%. These results show that reliable, reusable checker development remains challenging for current coding agents.

</details>


### [203] [When Old Facts Return: Re-Reads, Reverts, and the Limits of Temporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07715)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究揭示"重读旧语句"与"真实回退"在观测序列上不可区分，导致基于身份的时间记忆机制出现严重误判，并据此提出区分观测与变更证据的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统可能因旧的相同语句再次出现而"注销"并随后"恢复"一个过时值；而重读旧源码与真实回退会产生相同的观测值序列，却需要相反的当前回答，这一歧义尚未被系统研究。

Method: 从软件修复中抽取130个原子转换（extractor-selected atomic transitions）进行实验，设定普通转换条件与"逐字重读旧语句"的构造条件，并测试一个拒绝重新激活已注销值的guard；另设两项支持性研究：向答案模型暴露已注销历史、以及为变更行为提供当前源码。此外开展707个软件修复的探索性抽取研究提供范围背景。

Result: 普通条件下基于身份的时间记忆达到98.5%模型判定准确率，陈旧值率为0；追加逐字重读后准确率降至10.8%，陈旧值率升至88.5%；guard在构造重读条件下将准确率恢复至97.7%，陈旧值率降至0.8%，但无法识别合法回退（缺少变更来源信息）。

Conclusion: 设计启示在于必须区分"对某值的观测"与"该值发生变更的证据"；结论受限于所选输入、仅聚合的答案记录、相关家族judge及失败后guard评估，仅适用于保留的实验。707修复的抽取研究仅提供范围背景，不构成普适覆盖率估计。

Abstract: A memory system can retire an obsolete value and later restore it merely because the same old statement appears again. A re-read of an old source and a genuine revert can produce the same observed sequence of values while requiring opposite current answers. We study this ambiguity on 130 extractor-selected atomic transitions derived from software fixes. In the ordinary transition condition, identity-based temporal memory reaches 98.5% model-judged accuracy with zero observed errors under a literal stale-value proxy. Appending a verbatim re-read of the old statement reduces accuracy to 10.8% and raises the stale-value rate to 88.5%. A guard that refuses to reactivate a previously retired value restores accuracy to 97.7% and reduces that rate to 0.8% in this constructed re-read condition. The guard cannot also recognize a legitimate revert without additional change provenance. Two supporting studies examine exposing retired history to the answer model and supplying current source for changed behavior. An exploratory extraction study over 707 software fixes provides scope context, not a universal coverage estimate. The design implication is to distinguish an observation of a value from evidence that the value changed. Selected inputs, aggregate-only answer records, related-family judges and a post-failure guard evaluation limit the conclusions to the retained experiments.

</details>


### [204] [Acquiring and Verifying Repository Norms for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07757)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Zhenxi Chen,Christoph Treude,Mingwei Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 RepoNorm，一种独立于编码任务、自动获取仓库显性与隐性规范的方法，并将规范包提供给现有编码代理以提升规范遵从率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理生成的改动虽能通过功能测试，却常不满足仓库的贡献要求；而仓库特定规范分散于各类来源中，需识别并解释其条件与例外。检索与文档方法只提供通用上下文，代理仍需自行判断哪些规范适用。

Method: RepoNorm 独立于编码任务获取显性与隐性仓库规范；利用仓库证据校验规范内容与适用性，必要时查询 Git 历史，并将规范包交付给现有编码代理。评估使用三个编码模型与 RepoNormBench 的 121 个任务。

Result: 相较无额外生成指导的 Raw 基线，整体规范遵从率（NCR）相对提升 7.42–10.77%，贡献 NCR 提升 31.64–45.44%，提示省略 NCR 提升 11.34–17.69%；三个模型在 RepoNorm 下的 NCR 均高于 CodeWiki 文档。功能成功率较 Raw 提升 5.79–9.09 个百分点，但配对比较未达显著性阈值；默认配置下采样精确率为 86%。

Conclusion: RepoNorm 能有效提升编码代理对仓库规范的遵从度，尤其在贡献类与提示省略类规范上收益显著，且优于通用文档方案；对功能成功率有正向趋势但未达统计显著。

Abstract: Changes produced by coding agents can pass functional tests while leaving repository contribution requirements unmet. Following repository-specific norms requires identifying guidance dispersed across repository sources and interpreting its conditions and exceptions. Retrieval and documentation approaches supply general context, but agents must still determine which norms apply. We introduce RepoNorm to acquire explicit and implicit repository norms independently of coding tasks. It checks norm content and applicability using repository evidence, consults Git history when needed, and delivers norm packages to existing coding agents. Our evaluation uses three coding models and 121 tasks from RepoNormBench. Against the baseline with no additional generated guidance (Raw), relative improvements are 7.42-10.77% for Overall Norm Compliance Rate (NCR), 31.64-45.44% for Contribution NCR, and 11.34-17.69% for Prompt-omitted NCR. All three coding models also obtain higher values on these NCR measures with RepoNorm than with CodeWiki documentation. Functional success rates show observed gains of 5.79-9.09 percentage points over Raw; the paired comparisons do not reach the significance threshold. Sampled precision is 86% with RepoNorm's default configuration.

</details>
