### [205] [ES-Trace: Auditing Ethical-Sourcing Disclosure of Code Generation Models Beyond Model Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07762)
*Zhuolin Xu,Haibo Wang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ES-Trace 框架，利用模型文档可追溯图（MDTG）审计代码生成模型的伦理来源披露，发现仅查模型卡得分偏低，扩展文档引用后披露显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成模型在软件开发中广泛应用，但其开发涉及知识产权、隐私、公平性、劳动实践与环境影响等伦理来源问题。已有工作虽定义了伦理来源标准，却尚不清楚现有模型披露了多少证据、这些证据位于何处。

Method: 提出 ES-Trace 伦理来源披露审计框架，通过模型文档可追溯图（MDTG）刻画模型、版本与文档制品之间的关系，从而追踪超越模型卡的披露证据。对来自10个发布者的26个模型、77份文档、20个 ES-CodeGen 维度进行审计。

Result: 仅审计模型卡的平均得分为 1.77/5，解析声明的引用后提升至 2.82/5，增幅主要来自发布者在结构化元数据中声明的文档。此外，仅模型卡审计会误判发布级别的披露变化；文档错配可能将证据关联到错误的模型或版本。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁采用引用感知的伦理来源披露审计、显式的模型—版本绑定与文档制品一致性，并加强对当前披露不足的社会与劳动相关维度的记录。社会与劳动相关维度即使扩展至全文档范围仍记录不足。

Abstract: Code generation models have been increasingly used in software development, but their development raises ethical-sourcing concerns involving intellectual property, privacy, fairness, labour practices, and environmental impact. Although prior work has defined ethical-sourcing criteria for code generation, it remains unclear how much evidence existing models disclose and where that evidence can be found. We introduce ES-Trace, a framework for ethical-sourcing disclosure audits that traces disclosed evidence beyond model cards using the Model Documentation Traceability Graph (MDTG), which represents relationships among models, versions, and documentation artifacts. We apply ES-Trace to 26 models from 10 publishers across 77 documents and 20 ES-CodeGen aspects. Model-card-only auditing yields a mean score of 1.77/5, while resolving the declared references increases it to 2.82/5, with most of the increase arising from documents that the publisher declares in structured metadata. The key findings of our study include: (1) social and labour-related aspects remain poorly documented, even when expanding the audit to the full documentation scope, (2) resolving documentation references substantially increases observed disclosure, raising the mean score from 1.77/5 to 2.82/5, (3) model-card-only audits can mischaracterize release-level disclosure changes, and (4) documentation mismatches can associate evidence with the wrong model or version, highlighting the need for explicit model--version binding and consistency across documentation artifacts. Our study calls for reference-aware ethical-sourcing disclosure audits, explicit model--version binding and consistency across documentation artifacts, and stronger documentation of currently underreported social and labour-related aspects.

</details>


### [206] [Harness Engineering for Software Engineering via Modular Executable Dev-Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07832)
*Haibo Jin,Xinjie Li,Peng Kuang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 Dev-Primitives 与 HERMES 框架，将代码库组件转化为由 LLM 赋能的主动参与者，通过依赖感知的动态激活与缺陷诊断机制缓解长程软件工程任务中的上下文爆炸与语义漂移问题，在四个基准上平均提升 12.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具备终端访问能力的 LLM 智能体在长程工作流中表现脆弱：需反复重建散落于源码、配置、测试、依赖与运行时行为中的程序状态，导致交互历史过长、上下文爆炸与语义漂移；且大型仓库中难以识别与任务相关的组件。

Method: 提出 Dev-Primitives（开发原语），一种模块化、可执行的抽象，将仓库组件由被动软件制品转变为软件工程中的主动参与者——每个原语将仓库制品与一个常驻 LLM 配对，基于其自身实现与依赖提供智能体原生接口，支持自然语言推理、组件间通信与局部自我修改。在其之上构建 HERMES（面向软件工程的 Harness Engineering 框架），通过依赖感知的动态激活机制与将执行证据映射回待修改组件的缺陷诊断机制，在仓库规模上实例化这些原语。

Result: 在四个软件工程基准上，HERMES 相比匹配的基线 harness 平均提升 12.4%；在与强激活与诊断模型配对时，即使 Dev-Primitives 使用 Qwen3-8B，也在全部四个基准上距同构 GPT-5.6 Sol 配置仅差 4.5%，同时在 Terminal-Bench 4.0 上降低推理成本 26.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明 harness 设计对软件工程智能体至关重要，模块化可执行抽象可在显著降低推理开销的同时逼近强同构配置的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) equipped with terminal access have demonstrated strong capabilities in automating software engineering tasks. However, existing agents remain brittle on long-horizon workflows, where they must repeatedly reconstruct program state scattered across source files, configurations, tests, dependencies, and runtime behavior, leading to increasingly long interaction histories, context explosion, and semantic drift. Large repositories further complicate the identification of task-relevant components. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{Dev-Primitives} (\emph{Development Primitives}), a modular and executable abstraction that transforms repository components from passive software artifacts into active participants in software engineering. Each Dev-Primitive pairs a repository artifact with a resident LLM, which gives the artifact an agent-native interface grounded in its own implementation and dependencies, enabling natural-language reasoning, inter-component communication, and localized self-modification. Building on Dev-Primitives, we propose \textbf{HERMES}, a Harness Engineering framework for software engineeRing via Modular Executable Dev-PrimitiveS, which instantiates these primitives at repository scale through a dependency-aware dynamic activation mechanism and a bug diagnosis mechanism that maps execution evidence back to the components that must be revised. Extensive experiments on four software engineering benchmarks demonstrate that HERMES outperforms matched baseline harnesses by 12.4\% on average. Moreover, when paired with strong activation and diagnosis models, HERMES, even with Qwen3-8B Dev-Primitives, remains within 4.5\% of the homogeneous GPT-5.6 Sol configuration across all four benchmarks, while reducing inference cost by 26.2\% on Terminal-Bench 4.0, highlighting the importance of harness design in software engineering agents.

</details>


### [207] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Dense and Mixture-of-Experts Models for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08173)
*Anvi Kalpesh Shah,Umamaheswara Sharma B*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出加权质量指数 QI，从正确性、可维护性、安全性与生成效率多维度评估自动程序修复，发现模型排名随权重变化，且 MoE 模型以更少激活参数达到与更大稠密模型相当的修复正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于语言模型的自动程序修复（APR）通常仅以生成补丁是否通过测试套件来评估，这种单一指标会掩盖补丁在可维护性、安全性及计算成本上的差异，因此需要一个多维度、可配置权重的综合评估框架。

Method: 受 ISO/IEC 25010 软件质量模型启发，构建加权质量指数 QI，综合功能正确性、可维护性、安全性和生成效率，并支持可配置的权重方案；在 40 个 QuixBugs 与 90 个 Defects4J 缺陷上，于相同硬件本地运行三个稠密 Qwen2.5-Coder 模型（3B、7B、14B）与 16B 参数（激活 2.4B）的 DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite MoE 模型进行评估。

Result: 模型排名随权重方案变化，表明单指标评估会掩盖权衡关系；MoE 模型在正确性上与 7B、14B 稠密模型无统计显著差异（McNemar 精确检验），但激活参数少 3–6 倍；而三个稠密模型随规模增大正确性显著提升。

Conclusion: 多维度加权评估能揭示单指标评估所掩盖的权衡；对于稀疏代码模型，激活参数量比总参数量更能反映其修复能力。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) with language models is usually evaluated by whether a generated patch passes the test suite, which can hide differences in maintainability, security, and computational cost. We propose a Weighted Quality Index (QI), inspired by the ISO/IEC 25010 software quality model, that combines functional correctness, maintainability, security, and generation efficiency under configurable weighting schemes. We evaluate three dense Qwen2.5-Coder models (3B, 7B, 14B) and the 16B-parameter DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model (2.4B active parameters) on 40 QuixBugs and 90 Defects4J bugs, all run locally on identical hardware to control for infrastructure effects. Model rankings change with the weighting scheme, showing that single-metric evaluation can hide trade-offs. The MoE model shows almost no statistically significant difference in correctness from the 7B and 14B dense models (McNemar's exact test) while using 3-6 times fewer active parameters, whereas correctness increases significantly across the three dense scales. These results suggest that active parameter count can be a more informative lens than total parameter count for sparse code models.

</details>


### [208] [Newer and Bigger, but Safer? A Longitudinal Study of the Functionality-Security Gap in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08240)
*Thiago Santos de Moura,Fynn Matuschek,Flavio Toffalini,Yannic Noller*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对7个模型家族（含5个开源权重家族）共32个LLM（每个家族三代旗舰与紧凑版本）进行纵向评测，用CWEval（119任务、5种语言、31类CWE）衡量功能—安全差距。结果显示新模型绝对安全性提升但无一家族消除差距；开源权重家族普遍显著缩小差距，Gemini 3.1 Pro仍存在与Llama相当的差距；紧凑模型通常更不安全但有例外；CWE层面存在持续弱点与新增回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成代码的功能合理性持续提升，但常含安全漏洞，"功能—安全差距"（通过功能测试却未通过安全测试）依然存在。此前一项针对三个模型家族的纵向研究得出"模型变聪明但并未变安全"、且唯一纳入的开源权重家族停滞的结论。该结论是否适用于其他（开源权重）家族、尤其是否适用于紧凑模型尚不明确，作者因此开展更广覆盖的纵向研究。

Method: 构建纵向评测：选取7个模型家族、共32个LLM，其中5个为开源权重家族，每个家族覆盖连续三代发布并区分旗舰与紧凑版本；使用CWEval基准，包含5种编程语言下的119个任务与31类CWE；按家族、模型规模与编程语言比较功能—安全差距的时间轨迹。

Result: 更新的模型在绝对意义上更安全，但没有家族能消除功能—安全差距。与既有工作不同，开放性并未区隔家族：所有纳入的开源权重家族均显著缩小差距，而Gemini 3.1 Pro的差距仍与Llama所报告的同样宽。紧凑模型通常比旗舰版本生成更不安全的代码，但存在明显例外（如Gemini 3.7 Flash）。CWE层面确认了日志注入（CWE-117）与HTTP响应拆分（CWE-113）等持续弱点，并发现最新专有模型在内存与整数类弱点上出现回归，同时同一CWE在不同语言的危害程度差异很大。

Conclusion: 作者据此提出对LLM厂商、研究者与开发者的启示，尤其强调开发者不应假设升级必然提升安全性，也不应假设专有模型更安全；每次更换模型后都应重跑安全检查，并在模型的上下文中提供安全API。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used to generate code. Although their functional plausibility keeps improving, the generated code often contains security vulnerabilities. The functionality-security gap captures code that passes functional tests but fails security tests. A recent longitudinal study of three model families concluded that LLMs become smarter but not safer, with the only considered open-weight family stagnating. Whether this holds for other (open-weight) families and particularly for compact models remains open. We present a longitudinal study of the gap across 32 LLMs from seven model families (five open-weight), covering three successive releases per family in flagship and compact variants. Using CWEval with 119 tasks in five programming languages and 31 CWEs, we compare trajectories across families, model sizes, and languages. Newer models do become safer in absolute terms, although no family closes the gap. Unlike prior work, we find that openness does not separate the families: every considered open-weight family narrows the gap significantly, while Gemini 3.1 Pro keeps a gap as wide as the one reported for Llama. Compact models usually produce less secure code than their flagship counterparts, with notable exceptions (e.g., Gemini 3.7 Flash). At the CWE level, we confirm persistent weaknesses such as log injection (CWE-117) and HTTP response splitting (CWE-113) and regressions in the newest proprietary models on memory and integer weaknesses, and show that the same CWE carries very different risk across languages. From these results, we derive implications for LLM vendors, researchers, and developers. In particular, developers should assume neither that upgrades improve security nor that proprietary models are more secure; they should rerun security checks after each model change and provide secure APIs in the model's context.

</details>


### [209] [Learning from Failures: A Failure-Driven Prompt Refinement for LLM-Based Vulnerability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08405)
*Mandana Ghadamian,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出失败驱动提示词精炼（FDPR）方法，通过分析LLM漏洞分析中的重复失败模式来指导提示词改进，提升可靠性并提炼可复用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件漏洞分析中的效果高度依赖提示词设计，但现有研究多以聚合性能指标比较提示策略，难以解释模型为何失败，也缺乏系统性改进提示词的方法。

Method: 在DVJA（Damn Vulnerable Java Application）上识别重复失败模式（假阳性、假阴性、无依据推理、CWE错分类），将其转化为有针对性的提示词精炼；随后在Juliet测试套件上评估所得提示词，并进行跨模型验证以检验泛化性。

Result: 失败驱动精炼提升了基于LLM的漏洞分析可靠性，并产出可复用的提示词设计原则；跨模型验证表明其具有一定泛化能力。

Conclusion: 重复出现的模型失败可作为提示工程的原理性基础，支撑更可靠的LLM漏洞分析系统的系统性开发。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as promising tools for software vulnerability analysis, but their effectiveness depends heavily on prompt design. Existing research primarily compares prompting strategies using aggregate performance metrics, providing limited insight into why models fail or how prompts can be improved systematically. We propose Failure-Driven Prompt Refinement (FDPR), a methodology that analyzes recurring model failures to guide evidence-based prompt refinement. Using the Damn Vulnerable Java Application (DVJA), we identify recurring failure modes, including false positives, false negatives, unsupported reasoning, and CWE misclassification, and translate them into targeted prompt refinements. We then evaluate the resulting prompt on the Juliet Test Suite and perform cross-model validation to assess generalizability. The results show that failure-driven refinement improves the reliability of LLM-based vulnerability analysis while yielding reusable prompt design principles. More broadly, this work demonstrates that recurring model failures provide a principled foundation for prompt engineering, enabling the systematic development of more reliable LLM-based vulnerability analysis systems.

</details>


### [210] [RAPO-Sol: Retrieval-Augmented Preference Optimization for Repository-Level Solidity Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08429)
*Rongcun Wang,Shi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPO-Sol两阶段训练框架（RAFT+DPO）用于仓库级Solidity代码生成，通过训练期检索增强与语义锚点扰动偏好优化，在不增加推理检索成本的前提下提升三个代码大模型的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solidity合约管理资产、权限与不可逆状态变更，代码生成既高价值又安全攸关。仓库级生成要求模型在状态变量、修饰符、事件、继承、外部调用与访问控制逻辑间保持一致性，难度大、风险高，因此需要专门的训练方法来提升生成正确性与安全性。

Method: RAPO-Sol采用两阶段训练：（1）检索增强微调（RAFT），为每个训练输入附加相似Solidity示例，使模型学习可复用的合约级模式，但推理阶段无需检索；（2）直接偏好优化（DPO），通过Solidity语义锚点扰动（SAP）构造被拒样本，扰动对象包括校验语句、可见性修饰符、数据位置关键字、上下文变量、支付操作与低级调用，从而让模型偏好参考合约而非语义有缺陷的近似样本。

Result: 在SolidityBench上，基于CodeLlama-7B-Instruct、DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct与Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct三个模型，RAFT较监督微调稳定提升；基于SAP的DPO进一步改善BLEU与SolidityScore；完整的RAFT+DPO流水线在三个模型上均取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 训练期检索增强与Solidity感知的偏好优化具有互补优势，二者结合可在不增加推理检索开销的情况下显著提升仓库级Solidity代码生成质量。

Abstract: Smart contracts written in Solidity manage assets, permissions, and irreversible state changes, making code generation both useful and security-critical. Repository-level Solidity generation is challenging because models must synthesize complete contracts or libraries while preserving consistency across state variables, modifiers, events, inheritance, external calls, and access-control logic. We present RAPO-Sol, a two-stage training framework for repository-level Solidity code generation. First, Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning (RAFT) augments each training input with similar Solidity examples, helping the model learn recurring contract-level patterns while remaining retrieval-free at inference time. Second, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) trains the model to prefer reference contracts over close but semantically flawed alternatives. We construct rejected samples using Solidity Semantic-Anchor Perturbation (SAP), which perturbs validation statements, visibility modifiers, data-location keywords, context variables, payment operations, and low-level calls. Experiments on SolidityBench with CodeLlama-7B-Instruct, DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct, and Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct show that RAFT consistently improves over supervised fine-tuning, while SAP-based DPO provides further gains in BLEU and SolidityScore. The full RAFT+DPO pipeline achieves the best performance across all three models, demonstrating complementary benefits from retrieval during training and Solidity-aware preference optimization without adding retrieval cost at inference.

</details>


### [211] [A Case Study in Assuring AI-Written Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08651)
*Lindsey Ferris,Sierra Bonilla*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个由无正规软件工程训练的操作者、借助编码智能体构建并治理的生产级医疗平台案例。随着工作流演进，形成了“人类主导的元智能体系统”：一个智能体写代码，其他智能体监督和审查，项目规则沉淀经验。操作者发现测试、监控与审查智能体均可能失效，因此人类控制的关键在于将目标、判断证据、智能体权限与最终人类决策统一绑定到同一底层目标上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程智能体既能让缺乏正规软件训练的人构建原本无法实现的系统，也能生成超出专家可有效审查能力的代码量。在这两种情形下，穷尽式代码审查无法作为人类控制的可靠唯一基础，因此需要研究在智能体大规模参与开发时，人类如何维持对系统的有效控制。

Method: 采用案例研究法，考察一个通过编码智能体构建、由无正规软件工程训练的操作者治理的生产级医疗平台。追踪其工作流随时间演变为人类主导的元智能体系统的过程，并分析其中测试、监控和审查智能体的实际表现与失效模式。

Result: 操作者发现测试、监控与审查智能体均不可靠：部分监控测量的是代理指标而非真实结果；部分审计静默失败；遗漏的检查从报告结果中消失；一次自动修复还引发了运营中断。

Conclusion: 在智能体参与软件开发与治理的场景中，人类控制不能依赖穷尽式代码审查或智能体监督的可靠性，而应确保预期结果、用于判断结果的证据、智能体权限以及最终人类决策全部绑定到同一底层目标上。

Abstract: Software-engineering agents can enable people without formal software training to build systems they could not otherwise implement and simultaneously can produce more code than even experts can meaningfully inspect. In both cases, exhaustive code review is not reliable as the sole basis for human control. We report a case study of a production healthcare platform built through coding agents and governed by an operator without formal software-engineering training. Over time, its workflow grew into a human-led meta-agent system where one agent wrote code, other agents supervised and reviewed it, and project rules carried lessons forward. The operator found that tests, monitors and reviewing agents used to supervise the system were fallible. Some monitors measured proxies rather than outcomes, some audits failed silently, missing checks disappeared from reported results and one automated repair caused operational disruption. In this case, human control depended on keeping the intended outcome, the evidence used to judge it, the agents' permissions and the final human decision were all tied to the same underlying objective.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Beyond Successor Accuracy: State Retention for Recursive Self-Improvement in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07105)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Zheng Lin,Wenhao Yuan,Jian Chen,Shujie Li,Edith Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究推荐系统递归自我改进（Rec-RSI）中的状态保留问题，提出"分布式进展"概念与跨代优势（CGA）指标，发现进展可能存在于代际关系中而非仅在最新模型内，并用秩分离统计量在无标签条件下选择更优的模型家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Rec-RSI评估仅以每轮最新模型为准，隐含假设后继模型完全整合了更新，但更新前后的模型可能保留互补的排序决策，因此单一最新模型无法完整代表本轮进展。

Method: 提出跨代优势（CGA），以边际匹配的对比方式衡量跨代与同代模型对的差异；引入在选择时无需标签的秩分离统计量，用于预测应保留哪个模型家族。在四个数据集和三种序列推荐编码器（GRU4Rec、SASRec、FMLP）上进行验证。

Result: 最优保留机制随架构而异：跨代配对有利于GRU4Rec和SASRec，FMLP初始偏好同代配对，第二次更新后转向跨代配对。秩分离在12/12首次更新和5/6第二次更新的数据集-编码器设置中选出更强家族；在留出测试上，所选家族在34/36条轨迹中优于直接后继。五种迁移机制无法在单一模型中稳定复现这些增益。

Conclusion: 状态保留是Rec-RSI中一个独立问题：进展可能存在于代际关系之间，而不仅存在于最新模型中。

Abstract: Recommendation recursive self-improvement (Rec-RSI) feeds recommender outputs into subsequent training. Evaluating each round solely through its latest model assumes that the successor consolidates the update, although pre- and post-update models may retain complementary ranking decisions. We term this \emph{distributed progress} and quantify it using cross-generation advantage (CGA), a marginally matched contrast between cross- and within-generation model pairs. A rank-separation statistic, label-free at selection time, predicts which family to retain. Across four datasets and three sequential recommendation encoders, the preferred retention regime varies by architecture: cross-generation pairing benefits GRU4Rec and SASRec, whereas FMLP initially favors within-generation pairing and shifts toward cross-generation pairing after a second update. Rank separation selects the stronger family in 12/12 first-update and 5/6 second-update dataset-encoder settings; on held-out tests, the selected family outperforms the direct successor in 34/36 trajectories. Five transfer mechanisms do not consistently reproduce these gains in one model. These findings establish state retention as a distinct Rec-RSI problem: progress may reside in relations between generations as well as in the latest model. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Jinfeng-Xu/RecRSI}{https://github.com/Jinfeng-Xu/RecRSI}.

</details>


### [213] [RAGFlip: Measuring Query-Level Negative Flips in Retriever Upgrades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07266)
*Elyas Irankhah,Muhammad Arif*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究检索器升级中被聚合指标掩盖的"负翻转"（negative flips）现象：即BM25原本能正确检索到相关段落的查询，在新检索器下反而失败。作者在BEIR三个数据集上评估BGE-large、E5-large-v2和SPLADE，发现负翻转在所有设置下均存在且随语料、检索器和深度显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索器升级通常用聚合指标（如整体召回率或nDCG）来评估，但这类指标可能掩盖在旧检索器已正确处理的查询上出现的性能回退。作者希望揭示并量化这种被聚合指标隐藏的查询级不兼容问题，论证评估检索器更新时需同时关注查询级兼容性。

Method: 将负翻转定义为：BM25能检索到已标注相关段落的查询，而替代检索器未能检索到。评估BGE-large、E5-large-v2、SPLADE三个检索器，在BEIR的Natural Questions、HotpotQA、FiQA三个数据集上，覆盖五个检索深度进行实验。默认使用any-relevant支撑标签，并在HotpotQA k=10时额外采用"要求所有正qrel段落"的严格标签；此外测试了与BM25的固定预算简单组合，并设计了HotpotQA reader实验作为下游检验。

Result: 所有替代检索器都提升了整体检索覆盖率，但负翻转在每个设置中均出现，且随语料、检索器和深度大幅波动。在k=1时，各设置下有8.6%–37.5%的BM25成功案例被丢失。较大深度设置下负翻转率较低（因BM25支撑队列在每个深度单独定义）。在HotpotQA k=10使用严格标签时，负翻转率升至12.3%–17.3%。与BM25的简单固定预算组合能减少这些回退，HotpotQA reader实验显示部分检索翻转伴随答案回退。

Conclusion: 聚合检索质量的提升不能保证查询级兼容性，负翻转是普遍且可量化的现象。作者主张在评估检索器更新时，应将查询级兼容性（query-level compatibility）与聚合检索质量并列考量，以更全面地反映升级的真实收益与代价。

Abstract: Retriever upgrades are typically evaluated using aggregate metrics, which can hide regressions on queries the previous retriever already served correctly. We study these regressions as negative flips: queries for which BM25 retrieves a judged relevant passage and the replacement does not. We evaluate BGE-large, E5-large-v2, and SPLADE on three BEIR collections: Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and FiQA, across five retrieval depths. All replacements improve overall retrieval coverage. Negative flips occur in every setting and vary substantially by corpus, retriever, and depth. At k=1, 8.6-37.5% of BM25 successes are lost across the evaluated settings. Negative-flip rates are lower in the larger-depth settings, where the BM25-supported cohort is defined separately at each depth. These rates use the any-relevant support label. On HotpotQA at k=10, requiring every positive qrel passage raises the negative-flip rate to 12.3-17.3%. Simple fixed-budget combinations with BM25 reduce these regressions, and a HotpotQA reader experiment provides a limited downstream check in which some retrieval flips are accompanied by answer regressions. These results motivate evaluating retriever updates using query-level compatibility alongside aggregate retrieval quality.

</details>


### [214] [Rethinking Semantic ID Construction for Generative Recommendation: SimHash with Parallel Decoding and Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07402)
*Yuqing Liu,Huiyuan Chen,Yibo Wang,Wooseong Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了"哈希式语义ID必然劣于学习式量化"的共识，提出FLASH框架，通过并行解码与显式语义对齐重振免训练的SimHash分词，在多个数据集上取得SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义ID生成推荐中，构建兼具语义表达力与计算效率的语义ID是核心挑战。近期方法倾向复杂的学习式量化，而SimHash等简单哈希方法被普遍认为本质上劣于前者。作者希望探究这种性能差距是否源于哈希本身的固有缺陷，还是有其他可解决的结构性原因。

Method: 作者指出现有性能差距并非源于哈希固有局限，而是来自与自回归解码的结构性不匹配，以及刚性离散化过程中不可避免的信息损失。基于此提出FLASH——一个两阶段框架：采用并行解码，并引入显式语义对齐，使免训练的SimHash分词重新焕发效能，且无需任何分词器训练。

Result: FLASH在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能，无需训练分词器，并在冷启动场景中展现出更强的泛化能力。此外，实验表明语义对齐在不同范式下均为普遍有效的机制。

Conclusion: 在兼容的解码方式与语义接地条件下，简单高效的哈希分词器可达到与复杂学习式方法相当的生成推荐性能，呼吁重新审视简单分词器的价值。

Abstract: Semantic ID-based generative recommendation represents each item as a sequence of discrete tokens, enabling structured modeling of item semantics. A critical challenge is constructing semantic IDs that are both semantically expressive and computationally efficient. While recent approaches favor complex learned quantization, simple hashing-based methods such as SimHash are widely regarded as fundamentally inferior. In this work, we challenge this consensus by showing that the apparent performance gap does not stem from inherent limitations of hashing, but rather from a structural mismatch with autoregressive decoding, coupled with the inevitable information loss during rigid discretization. Based on this insight, we propose FLASH, a two-stage framework that revitalizes training-free SimHash tokenization through parallel decoding and explicit semantic alignment. Despite its simplicity, FLASH achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple datasets without requiring any tokenizer training, while exhibiting stronger generalization in cold-start scenarios. Notably, we demonstrate that semantic alignment acts as a universally effective mechanism across diverse paradigms. Our findings suggest that, with compatible decoding and semantic grounding, simple and efficient tokenizers can achieve performance comparable to complex learned counterparts in generative recommendation. Our code is available at https://github.com/KevinC2015/Flash.

</details>


### [215] [A Study of Prior Case Retrieval Using Lexical, Semantic, and Rhetorical Role Information in Indian Legal Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07437)
*Sayed Ayaan Ahmed Sha,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Anand Kumar Madasamy,Navya Binu,Aniket Mani,Rishu Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对印度法律先例检索（IL PCR）任务的三代检索系统进行实证评测，发现结合修辞角色优于单角色或全文检索，法条相似度不具区分性，LLM生成的训练数据导致重排序器泛化差；最终提出含查询不相交切分与冻结验证融合的四阶段流水线，Micro F1 0.2549、MRR 0.6081、nDCG@10 0.4187。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在印度法律判决中检索先例时，需区分真正相关的法律事实与仅词汇相似的内容：与查询判决共享同一法条的先前案例未必相关，因此单纯依赖词汇或法条匹配不足以判定相关性。

Method: 对IL PCR任务的三代连续检索系统设计进行实证评估：比较修辞角色（事实、判决理由、先例、论点、法条）的组合使用与单角色使用及全文检索；考察法条相似度的判别能力；使用大语言模型（LLM）生成训练数据训练法律蕴含重排序器；并提出带查询不相交切分（query disjoint splitting）与冻结验证融合（frozen validation fusion）的新设计，形成四阶段流水线。

Result: 修辞角色组合优于单角色与全文检索；法条相似度不具区分性；基于LLM训练数据的法律蕴含重排序器官方评测表现远逊于其内部验证分数；两个内部MRR高达0.97的重排序器在官方评测中表现很差（MRR低至0.14）。新设计取得Micro F1 0.2549、MRR 0.6081、nDCG@10 0.4187。

Conclusion: 法律先例检索中，内部验证分数与官方评测之间存在显著泛化差距，需通过查询不相交切分和冻结验证融合来缓解；所提出的四阶段流水线在官方指标上取得更稳健且明显的提升。

Abstract: For retrieving prior cases in Indian legal judgments, the problem involves distinguishing relevant legal facts from mere lexical similarities because a prior case that shares a statute with the query judgment is not necessarily relevant. In this paper, we describe an empirical evaluation of three consecutive designs of retrieval systems for the IL PCR(Indian Legal Prior Case Retrieval) task. We demonstrate that the combination of the rhetorical roles (Fact, Ratio Of The Decision, Precedent, Argument, Statute) is better than either using individual roles or performing full text retrieval, that statute similarity is not discriminative, and that a legal entailment reranker with training data produced by an LLM is much worse in terms of official evaluation than its internal validation score. Two rankers, with excellent internal MRR up to 0.97, performed poorly in terms of official evaluation (MRR as low as 0.14). Thus, we propose a new design with query disjoint splitting and frozen validation fusion. The result is a four stage pipeline with Micro F1 0.2549, MRR 0.6081, and nDCG@10 0.4187.

</details>


### [216] [DBRAG: Multi-Table Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Complex Database Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07622)
*Prince Larbi Ampofo,Ryoji Kubo,Djellel Difallah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DBRAG，一个面向多表问答的检索增强生成框架，通过离线表索引检索、查询相关行摘要增强、LLM 重排序以及程序辅助推理器，在全库多关系场景下提升表检索与多表问答效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于程序辅助工具的方法多局限于单表场景，或默认相关表已被提供；而实际用户常在整库范围内提出复杂数据探索查询，相关信息分布跨越多个关系，因此需要面向多表检索与推理的专门方法。

Method: DBRAG 采用四步流程：（1）利用离线表索引检索候选表；（2）用查询相关行丰富候选表摘要；（3）由 LLM 对候选表重排序；（4）程序辅助推理器选择所需表并在其完整内容上执行操作，从而保持初始提示上下文紧凑。

Result: 在 Spider、GeoQuery 和 ATIS 数据集上的实验表明，表检索与多表问答性能均有提升。

Conclusion: DBRAG 通过检索增强与程序辅助推理相结合，有效应对跨多关系的信息分布问题，在整库多表问答任务中取得改进。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models have introduced new capabilities for reasoning over structured data, particularly through program-aided tools that can analyze tables. However, many existing methods address single-table scenarios or assume that the relevant tables are already provided. In practice, users often issue complex data exploration queries over entire databases, where relevant information may be distributed across multiple relations. In this work, we introduce DBRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation framework tailored for multi-table question answering. DBRAG first retrieves candidate tables using an offline table index, enriches their summaries with query-relevant rows, and uses an LLM to rerank the candidates. A program-aided reasoner then selects the required tables and executes operations over their full contents, keeping the initial prompt context compact. Experiments on the Spider, GeoQuery, and ATIS datasets used in this study demonstrate improvements in table retrieval and multi-table question answering.

</details>


### [217] [Learning to Retrieve via Reinforcement Learning in Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07731)
*Qi Liu,Fengming Liang,Yiqun Chen,Erhan Zhang,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RELER（REinforcement Learning for Retrieval），用强化学习直接优化稠密检索的检索指标与下游任务奖励。它在归一化编码器输出为中心的vMF分布上采样嵌入动作，用RLOO的REINFORCE更新编码器，并提出条件均值投影（CMP）降低高维探索的采样噪声。在BRIGHT上后训练BGE-M3与Qwen3-Embedding均优于InfoNCE和LambdaLoss；在七个QA数据集的RAG中联合优化检索与答案奖励提升了检索与答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索模型通常以对比学习目标训练，虽能学到有效表示，但并不直接优化检索指标或下游任务性能，存在目标错配。作者希望让已有嵌入模型直接在嵌入空间中学习检索，并与任务特定奖励对齐。

Method: 提出RELER强化学习框架：从以归一化编码器输出为中心的von Mises-Fisher（vMF）分布中采样单位长度的查询与文档嵌入作为动作；将检索或下游结果作为奖励，用REINFORCE结合留一法基线RLOO更新编码器。针对高维嵌入空间探索噪声大的问题，提出条件均值投影（CMP）：将采样嵌入投影到由其编码器输出与所比较候选嵌入张成的低维子空间，在保持策略梯度期望不变的同时降低噪声。

Result: 在推理密集型检索基准BRIGHT上，后训练BGE-M3与Qwen3-Embedding时，RELER在平均nDCG@10上持续优于InfoNCE与LambdaLoss。在RAG评估中仅适配查询编码器、固定文档索引与生成器，跨七个QA数据集联合优化检索与答案奖励，既提升了平均检索性能，也提升了答案质量。

Conclusion: RELER表明可以用强化学习直接在嵌入空间中优化稠密检索，使其与任务特定奖励（检索指标或RAG答案质量）对齐；配合CMP可有效抑制高维采样的梯度噪声，性能优于传统对比与排序损失，并具备下游RAG实用价值。

Abstract: Dense retrieval models are typically trained with contrastive objectives that learn effective representations but do not directly optimize retrieval metrics or downstream task performance. To address this problem, we introduce RELER (REinforcement LEarning for Retrieval), a reinforcement learning framework that enables existing embedding models to learn to retrieve directly in embedding space and align to task-specific rewards. We train RELER by sampling unit-length query and document embedding actions from von Mises-Fisher (vMF) distributions centered on normalized encoder outputs, scoring the resulting retrieval or downstream outcomes as rewards, and updating the encoder with REINFORCE using a leave-one-out baseline (RLOO). As exploration in the high-dimensional embedding space is prone to sampling noise, we further propose conditional-mean projection (CMP), which projects each sampled embedding onto the low-dimensional subspace spanned by its encoder output and the candidate embeddings it is compared against, reducing noise in the policy gradient while preserving its expectation. We evaluate RELER on BRIGHT, a benchmark with reasoning-intensive queries that remain challenging for existing embedding models. RELER consistently outperforms InfoNCE and LambdaLoss in average nDCG@10 when post-training BGE-M3 and Qwen3-Embedding backbones. We further evaluate downstream utility through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where we adapt only the query encoder while keeping the document index and generator fixed. Across seven QA datasets, jointly optimizing retrieval and answer rewards improves both average retrieval performance and answer quality in RAG.

</details>


### [218] [Token-Budgeted Escalation for Financial Document QA: Cost Is Predictable, Benefit Is the Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07760)
*Junru Zhu,Yixin Yang,Xiaoqing Ding,Ruoyu Qi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 将选择性升级建模为有限批量分配问题：预测每次额外top-5调用的质量增益与token成本，按"每token增益"排序分配预算。在10%预算下，该策略比仅按增益排序提升裁决质量0.034，且token用量比一次性top-5减少46.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成可把困难查询路由到更深上下文，但批量部署需在成本随查询变化的多次调用间共享token预算。核心动机是验证"成本异质性"在紧约束下是否可利用，并厘清限制整体预算前沿扩展的瓶颈。

Method: 在FinanceBench的150个金融文档问答问题上，先对每个问题做top-1检索回答；用预测器估计可选的top-5调用的裁决质量增益与token成本；分配器按预测的"每token增益"对调用排序并分配共享预算，并与仅按增益排序、一次性top-5检索两种基线对比。

Result: 名义10%预算下，增益/每token分配使裁决质量较仅按增益排序提升0.034（95%文档自举CI [0.001, 0.072]），同时总token消耗比一次性top-5检索减少46.6%。额外调用成本可被准确预测（R²=0.93），但"有益升级"仍难以排序（AUROC=0.60）。

Conclusion: 在紧预算下，异构成本是可操作的分配信号，能带来实际质量收益并显著节省token；但要把收益拓展到完整的预算前沿，仍需更强的查询级增益估计（提升AUROC），即瓶颈在于"收益预测"而非"成本预测"。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems can route difficult queries to deeper context, but batch deployments must allocate a shared token budget across calls whose costs vary by query. We formulate selective escalation as finite-batch allocation for financial document question answering. Each of 150 FinanceBench questions first receives a top-1 retrieval answer. Predictors estimate the adjudication-quality gain and token cost of an optional top-5 call, and the allocator prioritizes calls by predicted gain per token. At the nominal 10% budget, gain-per-token allocation improves adjudication quality over gain-only ranking by 0.034 (95% document-bootstrap CI [0.001, 0.072]) while using 46.6% fewer total tokens than one-pass top-5 retrieval. Additional-call cost is accurately predictable (R-squared 0.93), whereas beneficial escalation remains difficult to rank (AUROC 0.60). These results show that heterogeneous cost is actionable under tight constraints, while progress across the full budget frontier depends on stronger query-specific benefit estimates.

</details>


### [219] [Contrastive Learning for Aspect Representation towards Explainable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07761)
*Emrul Hasan,Chen Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLARER 模型，将评论中的方面特征与评分信息融合，以提升推荐准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升推荐系统的准确性和可解释性，充分利用评论文本中的语义信息。

Method: 融合评分特征与方面特征学习用户和物品表示：评分特征由 MLP 学习，方面评论表示由 Transformer 编码器结合对比学习获得；解释生成使用 Transformer 解码器，以两类特征的最终表示为上下文。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，推荐准确性和解释生成均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合评分与方面特征的对比学习框架能有效提升推荐性能与可解释性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a novel recommendation model, CLARER (Contrastive Learning for Aspect Representation towards Explainable Recommendation) that integrates aspect features learned from textual reviews with rating information to improve the accuracy and explainability of recommendations. Our proposed framework learns user and item representations by combining rating-based features and aspect-based features from reviews. Specifically, rating-based features are learned through a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) model, while aspect-specific review representations are learned using a transformer encoder to capture the semantic information and contrastive learning to better distinguish user preferences. To provide explanations, we train a transformer decoder, using the final representations of users and items from both rating and aspect-based features as context. Experimental results in three benchmark data sets demonstrate that our model achieves superior performance compared to baseline methods in both recommendation (accuracy) and explanation generation.

</details>


### [220] [From Delivery to Stateful Exploration: Rethinking the Index for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07960)
*Deogyong Kim,Sunghwan Kim,Sangam Lee,Wonjae Lee,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 IndexAct——一种"索引原生语料交互"接口，将候选集精炼与源文本查看解耦：智能体通过倒排索引上的词法条件与集合操作构建并维护持久候选集，只接收可复用的状态引用与候选计数等统计反馈，仅在需要时单独请求文本段落。在五个基准上优于基线，并在 BrowseComp-Plus 上以更小的实时上下文获得更高证据覆盖，且在语料扩展时保持答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体检索接口常把候选筛选与源文本暴露耦合在一起：即使下一步决策只需关于候选集的反馈，接口也会返回匹配段落。这会带来不必要的上下文开销与噪声，缺乏可复用的候选状态，也不利于在语料扩展时稳定表现。作者因此希望设计一种解耦二者的交互接口。

Method: 提出 IndexAct 接口，实现索引原生的语料交互。智能体在倒排索引上通过词法条件与集合操作（并、交、差等）构建和操纵持久化的候选集；接口返回可复用的状态引用及候选数量等统计信息，而非匹配段落本身。这些精炼反馈指导后续操作；当需要新线索或证据时，再单独请求特定段落，其结果可用于对保留的候选集做进一步操作。

Result: 在覆盖智能体检索与多跳问答的五个基准上，IndexAct 均优于所评估的基线。在 BrowseComp-Plus 上，相较基于终端的语料接口，它以更小的平均实时上下文取得更高的证据覆盖率；随语料规模扩大，其答案准确率保持稳定。进一步分析表明：有信息量的精炼反馈与状态复用有助于持续发现证据，而单纯缩短上下文或减少检索步数并不能保证更优性能。

Conclusion: 将候选集精炼与文本检查分离的索引原生交互是有效的：统计型反馈与候选状态复用是性能提升的关键机制，而非仅靠压缩上下文或减少步数。该接口在多项基准与语料扩展条件下表现稳健。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic search have given large language model (LLM) agents finer control over corpus exploration. However, search interfaces often return matching passages even when feedback about the candidate set would suffice for the next decision, coupling candidate refinement with source-text exposure. We propose IndexAct, an interface for Index-Native Corpus Interaction that separates candidate-set refinement from text inspection. Agents construct and manipulate persistent candidate sets through lexical conditions and set operations over an inverted index, receiving reusable state references and statistics such as candidate counts rather than matching passages. This feedback guides further refinement, while separately requested passages provide new clues or evidence that can inform subsequent operations on retained candidate sets. Experiments on five benchmarks spanning agentic search and multi-hop question answering show that IndexAct outperforms the evaluated baselines on each benchmark. On BrowseComp-Plus, it also achieves higher evidence coverage with a smaller average live context than terminal-based corpus interfaces, and maintains answer accuracy as the corpus expands. Further analyses suggest that informative refinement feedback and state reuse support continued evidence discovery, while shorter contexts or fewer search steps alone do not ensure better performance.

</details>


### [221] [Adapting Generative Recommenders for Multi-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08136)
*Yu-Chen Den,Zhi Rui Tam,Yung-Yu Shih,Shih-Hsin Wang,Yun-Nung Chen,Pu-Jen Cheng,Eugene Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: INTEGER 通过路由 token、历史重锚定与行为回放，将生成式推荐扩展为多轮交互式推荐，在不牺牲准确率的前提下支持用户对话式纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器仅根据用户交互历史解码物品，用户无法纠正与其当前意图不符的推荐。虽然物品与文本共享同一输出空间，加入对话很自然，但训练对话能力可能会覆盖模型赖以工作的"历史到物品"映射关系。

Method: 提出 INTEGER（交互式生成推荐），包含三个核心机制：(1) 学习式路由 token，让模型自行决定何时进行推荐；(2) 历史重锚定，使每个物品的生成同时依赖过往行为与对话；(3) 行为回放与指令数据排练，防止适配过程中发生灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在 Amazon Beauty 与 Toys 数据集上，INTEGER 在准确率上达到或超过最强基线，同时具备有竞争力的对话质量；在 Amazon Beauty 上 Hit@10 提升 13.3%，并显著优于其初始的生成式推荐器。

Conclusion: INTEGER 学到了朴素适配无法获得的行为：在用户意图明确时进行推荐，并在推荐时刻保持对行为历史的关注；同时学到一种意图无关的物品替换机制，可抑制被拒绝的物品，但这也指向属性感知反馈是下一步的研究方向。

Abstract: Generative recommenders decode items from a user's interaction history, but offer no way for users to correct a recommendation that misses their current intent. Adding conversation is natural since items and words share same output space, yet training the model to converse may overwrite the history-to-item mapping it relies on. We introduce INTEGER (**INTE**ractive **GE**nerative **R**ecommendation), which extends generative recommendation to multi-turn interaction with a learned routing token that lets the model decide when to recommend, history re-anchoring that conditions each item on both past behavior and the dialogue, and behavioral replay with instruction-data rehearsal that prevents forgetting during adaptation. Users can thus give feedback on recommendations within the dialogue, while recommendations stay grounded in behavioral history and accuracy is not traded for fluency. On Amazon Beauty and Toys, INTEGER matches or exceeds the strongest baselines in accuracy with competitive conversation quality, improving Hit@10 by 13.3% on Amazon Beauty, and significantly outperforms the generative recommender it starts from. Our analyses show that INTEGER learns behaviors that naive adaptation fails to acquire, recommending once the user's intent is clear and staying attentive to behavioral history at the moment of recommendation. INTEGER also learns an intent-agnostic replacement over the item space, which suppresses rejected items but points to attribute-aware feedback as the next step.

</details>


### [222] [Behavior-Mining, Generative Conversations, and Collaborative Advisory: the Future of Travel and Tourism Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08232)
*Alejandro Bellogín,Linus W. Dietz,Francesco Ricci,Pablo Sánchez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于旅游推荐系统（TTRS）的立场/展望型论文。作者指出TTRS虽研究历史悠久但尚未普及，原因在于数据集陈旧稀疏、算法过度追求预测精度、忽视旅行者具体需求；而生成式AI正成为旅行者规划行程的主要入口，迫使TTRS重新设计。作者主张未来TTRS应从个性化信息过滤转向支持决策的灵活"顾问"，融合多类数据与AI技术，并透明地平衡旅行者、供应商、平台与本地社区之间的冲突目标，同时提出研究目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅游与旅行自电子商务早期起便是推荐系统设计的试验场，学术界已产出大量情境依赖、多利益相关方导向、并逐渐关注可持续性（如过度旅游）的TTRS原型。然而这些系统至今仍未广泛落地。作者希望解释这一"研究多、应用少"的落差，并应对生成式AI进入旅游信息与商业化平台生态这一颠覆性因素，重新思考TTRS的设计方向与核心技术。

Method: 本文为立场性/展望性研究，采用论证与框架构建的方法：首先归纳TTRS未能普及的三项局限——（1）训练与验证所用数据集陈旧且稀疏；（2）算法将预测精度置于新颖性、情境相关性等领域特定维度之上；（3）未能满足旅行者的具体需求。随后引入生成式AI作为颠覆性外部变量，论证其对TTRS设计范式的冲击，并据此提出未来TTRS的功能定位与研究目标，主张新旧推荐技术的有益结合。

Result: 作者论证指出：市场研究表明GenAI应用正成为旅行者规划行程的主要入口，因此TTRS的研究必须重新设计并整合核心技术。未来TTRS除提供个性化信息过滤外，应成为更灵活的"顾问"以支持决策，整合多种数据类型与AI技术（从数据挖掘到自然语言处理）。此外，系统必须透明地平衡旅行者、服务供应商、平台所有者与本地社区之间相互冲突的目标。文中进一步给出了构建更有效TTRS的研究目标。

Conclusion: TTRS研究需从单纯追求预测精度的信息过滤范式，转向融合多源数据、多类AI技术、并以决策支持为核心的可解释"顾问"系统。增量式研究可部分缓解现有局限，但生成式AI的介入要求对TTRS的设计原则与核心技术进行根本性重构。未来工作的关键在于：透明地协调多方利益相关者的冲突目标，并将传统推荐方法与新兴技术有机结合，以推动TTRS真正走向广泛应用。

Abstract: Since the early adoption of e-commerce, travel and tourism has been a lab for the design of recommender systems: tools that help travelers choose destinations, flights, accommodations, and combine them into itineraries. Data-driven recommendation techniques, ranging from case-based reasoning to reinforcement learning, have been adapted to travelers' needs. The research community has produced multifaceted prototypes of travel and tourism recommender systems (TTRSs), which are context-dependent, multistakeholder-oriented, and more recently, addressing sustainability issues, such as overtourism. Despite this enduring work, TTRSs are not widespread yet. We argue that three limitations can explain this: outdated and sparse data sets used to train and validate TTRSs, algorithms that prioritize prediction accuracy over domain-specific dimensions such as novelty and contextual relevance, and a failure to address the specific needs of travelers. Targeted incremental research could address these limitations, but a disruptive factor has meanwhile entered the ecosystem of tourism information and commercialization platforms: generative artificial intelligence. According to market research, GenAI applications are becoming the primary entry point for travelers planning their trips. This forces research to rethink how TTRSs should be designed and which core techniques should be integrated. We claim that future TTRSs, in addition to offering personalized information filtering, should become more flexible advisors that support decision making, integrating multiple data types and AI techniques, from data mining to natural language processing. Moreover, they must transparently balance the conflicting goals of travelers, service suppliers, platform owners, and local communities. We then outline research targets for building more effective TTRSs, fruitfully combining old and new recommendation techniques.

</details>


### [223] [Aligning Performance with Contribution: Towards Contribution-Aware Fair Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08245)
*Shuai Zhang,Hui Fang,Zun Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"贡献—性能公平性"新视角，要求推荐性能与用户群体（及同群体内可比贡献用户）的估计贡献相对齐，并给出可适配不同骨干模型的CPFR框架，在三个数据集和三种骨干上验证了精度—公平性的良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统用户公平性研究虽提出多种目标，但忽视了分配视角：用户对模型学习的贡献是否应反映在其获得的推荐收益中。作者认为，公平系统除既有保护外，还应考虑用户估计贡献与所得推荐性能之间的对齐，以激励持续且信息丰富的参与，支撑可持续的推荐生态。

Method: 提出CPFR框架：由训练相关的贡献构建有序用户群体，贡献综合考虑交互量、损失对齐与优化强度；随后在优化推荐精度的同时联合优化两项公平要求——跨用户群体的性能—贡献对齐，以及同一群体内贡献可比用户之间的公平性。该方法可挂载于不同骨干推荐器，并通过博弈论分析论证该对齐在自愿贡献下对激励与系统级精度的作用。

Result: 在三个数据集和三种骨干模型上的实验表明，按所提出的操作性指标衡量，CPFR取得了较强的精度—公平性权衡。博弈论分析进一步显示该对齐能增强贡献激励，并在用户自愿贡献场景下提升系统级推荐精度。

Conclusion: 将推荐性能与用户估计贡献对齐是一种可行且必要的新型公平视角，CPFR能够在保持推荐精度的同时实现跨群体与群体内公平，并有助于形成可持续、激励相容的推荐生态。

Abstract: Existing research on user fairness in recommender systems has developed diverse objectives. However, it has paid limited attention to a distinct distributive perspective: whether users' contributions to model learning should be reflected in the recommendation benefits they receive. We argue that, in addition to existing fairness protections, a fair system may account for the alignment between users' estimated contributions and the recommendation performance they receive. Such alignment can incentivize sustained and informative engagement, thereby supporting a sustainable recommendation ecosystem. To this end, we propose Contribution-Performance Fairness, a novel fairness perspective which requires recommendation performance to be aligned with estimated contribution across user groups and to remain equitable among users with comparable contributions within a same group. To instantiate this perspective, we introduce the Contribution-Performance Fair Recommender (CPFR), a framework applicable to different backbone recommenders. CPFR constructs ordered user groups from a training-dependent contribution considering interaction volume, loss alignment, and optimization intensity, and jointly optimizes recommendation accuracy with the two fairness requirements. A game-theoretic analysis shows that such alignment can strengthen contribution incentives and improve system-level recommendation accuracy under voluntary contribution. Experiments on three datasets and three backbone models demonstrate that CPFR achieves a strong accuracy--fairness trade-off under the proposed operational metric.

</details>


### [224] [Seeing the Context: Enhancing Recommender Systems with Image-Derived Contextual Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08407)
*Tal Cordova,Tomer Geva,Moshe Unger*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出从图像中抽取情境上下文（物理、社交、模态三类），并设计ICE-Fuse流水线将其融合进上下文感知推荐系统。图像上下文单独使用不如传统信号，但组合后可提升性能，表明其提供互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统从位置、时间或评论中获取情境信息，但未利用图像；多模态推荐系统多用图像来丰富物品或用户表示，而非识别情境上下文。作者希望探索图像作为情境信息新来源的价值。

Method: 提出一种基于图像的上下文表示，涵盖物理、社交、模态三类，通过视觉-语言模型学习；设计ICE-Fuse流水线，融合这些类别并集成到上下文感知推荐系统中，使用TripAdvisor数据和Review-aware Graph Contrastive Learning作为推荐算法。

Result: 图像上下文单独使用时无法超越现有信号，但与现有信号结合后可带来提升，表明二者包含互补信息。语义分析显示，图像与评论所推导的上下文捕捉了交互的不同方面。

Conclusion: 图像可以作为情境上下文的重要补充来源，与评论等传统信号结合能增强推荐效果，为多模态上下文感知推荐提供新方向。

Abstract: Contextual information, capturing the circumstances of a user-item interaction, is central to recommender systems. Prior work draws context from location, time, or reviews, but not images; multimodal recommender systems mainly use images to enrich item or user representations, not identify situational context. We propose a new representation of context derived from images, spanning physical, social, and modal categories learned via a vision-language model. We introduce ICE-Fuse, a pipeline for evaluating this representation that fuses these categories and integrates them into a context-aware recommender system, using TripAdvisor data and Review-aware Graph Contrastive Learning as the recommendation algorithm. Image context does not outperform established signals standalone, but improves them combined, indicating complementary information. Semantic analysis shows image- and review-derived context capture distinct aspects of the interaction, positioning images as complementary context.

</details>


### [225] [Disentangling Paradigm, Identifier, and Decoding in Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08716)
*Hicham Randrianarivo,Logan Renaud,Alexia Allal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究在NQ320K和MS300K上控制标识符长度与训练预算，公平对比自回归、掩码扩散与块扩散三种生成式检索范式，并系统比较多种解码方式。核心发现：解码方式单独就能使扩散模型Hit@1变动6.6-13.7点；提出的one-pass scoring在12个设定中有11个匹配或超过generate-and-match；自回归仍领先Hit@1，且在NQ320K上优势来自模型本身而非beam search；各范式在很大程度上是在记忆"哪个标识符对应哪个查询"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作用扩散替换自回归解码器时，同时改变了标识符类型、训练配方与解码方式，导致性能差异无法归因于范式本身，缺乏受控对比。

Method: 在NQ320K与MS300K上，固定标识符长度和训练预算，用残差量化(RQ)、乘积量化(PQ)与随机标识符，分别训练自回归、掩码扩散和块扩散模型，并对每个模型采用多种解码方式；提出one-pass scoring：模型一次性读取完全掩码的标识符，用各文档编码的概率对其打分；以generate-and-match（生成标识符后检索语料中最接近的标识符）为参考解码。

Result: 解码方式单独即可使扩散模型Hit@1变动6.6-13.7点；generate-and-match生成正确标识符的比例为NQ320K查询的14-21%；one-pass scoring在12个设定中的11个匹配或超过generate-and-match；自回归Hit@1仍领先，NQ320K上优势来自模型而非beam search；从单次采样标识符出发，one-pass scoring消除掩码扩散相对beam search差距的46-83%，而相对generate-and-match至多消除四分之一；NQ320K上随机标识符保留了RQ标识符83-90%的Hit@1，表明范式大量记忆"标识符-查询"映射；PQ标识符在自回归中领先RQ 3.4点，在扩散中为-0.7至+3.6点，跨解码方式AR差距比扩散高1.5-2.3点，接近2点阈值。

Conclusion: 范式比较必须在各自最优配方与最佳解码下进行；解码策略对扩散检索器影响巨大，不可与范式效应混淆；one-pass scoring是更优的扩散解码方案；现有基准下各范式很大程度上依赖标识符-查询记忆，标识符设计与解码选择的交互需谨慎评估。

Abstract: Generative retrieval trains a language model to generate the identifier of a relevant document. Recent work replaces the autoregressive decoder with diffusion, but changes identifiers, training recipe and decoding at once, so differences cannot be credited to the paradigm. On NQ320K and MS300K, we train autoregressive, masked-diffusion and block-diffusion models with residual-quantised, product-quantised and random identifiers. With identifier length and training budget fixed, we decode each model in several ways. Decoding alone moves a diffusion model's Hit@1 by 6.6 to 13.7 points. Our reference diffusion decoding, generate-and-match, generates an identifier, then retrieves the closest corpus identifiers. The generated identifier is right for 14-21% of NQ320K queries. We test one-pass scoring to decode diffusion retrievers: the model reads a fully masked identifier once, and each document is scored by its codes' probabilities. It matches or beats generate-and-match in 11 of 12 settings. Autoregressive models still lead in Hit@1; on NQ320K, the lead comes from the model, not beam search. Starting from one sampled identifier, one-pass scoring removes 46-83% of masked diffusion's deficit to beam search; from generate-and-match, at most a quarter. On NQ320K, every paradigm largely memorises which identifier answers which query: random identifiers keep 83-90% of the Hit@1 of residual-quantised ones. There, product-quantised identifiers lead residual-quantised ones by 3.4 points in the autoregressive model and by -0.7 to +3.6 in diffusion models; across decodings, AR's gap exceeds diffusion's by 1.5-2.3 points, around our 2-point threshold. Paradigm comparisons must report each paradigm at its own recipe and best decoding.

</details>


### [226] [A Systematic Study of Semantic ID Spaces for Generative Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08732)
*Alexia Allal,Hicham Randrianarivo,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究生成式信息检索(GIR)中数值型DocID的设计问题，提出统一PQ与RQ的框架及免训练内在指标，在MS MARCO 300K与NQ320K上分析DocID结构性质对检索性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GIR将检索从"检索-排序"转为序列到序列生成，模型直接预测DocID，DocID语义设计对性能至关重要。但"什么是好的DocID"这一根本问题研究不足：现有方法依赖昂贵的下游评测，难以系统分析与快速迭代。

Method: 提出统一框架，将Product Quantization (PQ)与Residual Quantization (RQ)及其混合变体纳入单一设计空间，从而系统研究DocID的关键性质（如层次式 vs 并行式）以及DocID长度、码本大小等超参数的影响。同时定义一套免训练(training-free)的内在指标，用于量化DocID质量并评估结构保真度，避免完整模型训练的开销。

Result: 在MS MARCO 300K和NQ320K上进行大量实验，分析了DocID的结构性质如何影响检索有效性。

Conclusion: 通过统一设计空间与免训练内在指标，可在无需昂贵下游训练评测的情况下系统刻画有效数值型DocID的性质、度量与权衡，为GIR的DocID设计提供快速迭代与理论指导。

Abstract: Generative Information Retrieval (GIR) has emerged as a transformative paradigm, shifting document retrieval from a traditional "retrieve-and-rank" workflow to sequence-to-sequence generation, where a model directly predicts document identifiers (DocIDs). While the semantic design of these DocIDs is known to be critical for performance, a fundamental question remains under-explored: what makes a good DocID? Current approaches rely heavily on computationally expensive downstream evaluations, hindering systematic analysis and rapid iteration. In this work, we address this challenge by presenting a comprehensive study on the properties, metrics, and trade-offs that define effective numerical DocIDs. Specifically, our contributions are threefold: First, we propose a unified framework that unifies Product Quantization (PQ) and Residual Quantization (RQ), and their hybrid variants within a single design space. This enables us to systematically study key DocID properties, such as hierarchy versus parallelism, as well as the impact of hyperparameters like DocID length and codebook size. Second, we define a suite of training-free, intrinsic metrics, to quantify DocID quality and evaluate structural fidelity without the overhead of full model training. Through extensive experiments on MS MARCO 300K and NQ320K, we analyze how these structural properties influence retrieval effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [GAMEGO: Training Game-Dev Agents with Synthetic Trajectories Anchored in Real-World Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06910)
*Haoyue Yang,Jingyao Li,Zhengfan Wu,Jing Liu,Xuanle Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GameGo框架，将简短游戏构想系统性地转化为符合行业实践的产品需求文档（PRD），并采用任务特定动态压缩以保留核心玩法约束。基于该流程构建了覆盖2D/2.5D/3D游戏的55,060条开发轨迹数据集GameGoData及124个查询的GameGoBench，训练出的GameGoCoder在游戏开发基准上超过同类基线并接近前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多依赖复杂的多轮工作流或关注静态游戏评测基准，缺乏面向真实世界游戏的直接端到端合成能力。仅凭稀疏用户查询直接生成复杂游戏会迫使编码智能体做出欠明确假设，导致机制不完整、玩法流程割裂、视觉美感有限。

Method: 提出GameGo可扩展框架：将简短游戏种子（brief game seeds）系统性地转化为基于行业游戏开发实践的综合产品需求文档（PRD）；为在不限制设计探索的前提下保留核心玩法约束，采用任务特定的动态压缩（task-specific dynamic compression）以最大化信息密度并保持指令遵循能力。

Result: 构建了GameGoData数据集，含55,060条覆盖2D、2.5D和3D游戏的开发轨迹；提出GameGoBench基准，包含124个多样化游戏查询；在GameGoData上训练的GameGoCoder在游戏开发基准上优于匹配基线，并与前沿模型表现相当。

Conclusion: GameGo为基于编码智能体的端到端真实世界游戏合成提供了可扩展方案，通过PRD转化与动态压缩缓解了稀疏查询下的欠指定问题。所有代码、数据集和模型将公开。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in web front-end execution, with browser-based game generation emerging as a particularly prominent frontier. While previous efforts frequently rely on complex multi-turn workflows or focus on static game evaluation benchmarks, this work targets direct end-to-end real-world game synthesis driven by coding agents. However, generating complex games directly from sparse user queries often forces coding agents to make underspecified assumptions, yielding incomplete mechanics, disconnected gameplay flows, and limited visual aesthetics. To resolve this issue, this paper presents GameGo, a scalable framework that systematically transforms brief game seeds into comprehensive Product Requirements Documents grounded in industry game-development practices. To retain core gameplay constraints without restricting design exploration, GameGo uses task-specific dynamic compression to maximize information density while preserving instruction following. Based on this pipeline, GameGoData is constructed with 55,060 development trajectories across 2D, 2.5D, and 3D games, alongside GameGoBench, a benchmark comprising 124 diverse game queries. Training GameGoCoder on GameGoData yields a model that outperforms matched baselines and is comparable to frontier models across gamedev benchmarks. All code, datasets, and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [228] [Text2Dashboard: A Governed Agent Architecture for Natural-Language Dashboard Generation over Enterprise DataBrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06914)
*Yiou Wu,Zezhi Tang,Ningwei Bai,Liuhaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Text2Dashboard 是一个面向 DataBrain 的原型系统，将自然语言分析请求转化为可审查的仪表盘，通过受限模型决策与确定性软件控制的组合实现可审计的自动化工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言到分析仪表盘的自动化需要兼顾灵活性与可控性；纯模型驱动的方案难以保证执行安全、可审计性与故障恢复，因此需要一个模型只负责决策提议、软件负责执行与状态记录的原型来验证这一方向。

Method: 系统由可安装的 Codex 插件与独立 Agent Runtime 组成，结合模式约束的模型决策、类型化工具、持久化状态和确定性 Hooks（审批、审计、检查点、恢复、失败处理）。流水线依次进行实体解析、元数据发现、只读 SQL 强制、仪表盘组合，并施加静态检查、动态预检与浏览器检查。

Result: 在冻结的真实 DataBrain 任务与受控 Hook 故障上评测：元数据与 SQL 任务严格成功 6/8（元数据选择 4/4；四个 SQL 任务均满足语义标准，2/4 满足精确输出列契约）。最终版本通过 4/4 单面板仪表盘任务、1 个双面板任务和 1 个既有仪表盘精修；一个参数化任务超出步数上限。十个故障场景均达到预期结果且无未经批准的外部副作用。模型推理占每组观测运行时间的 97% 以上。

Conclusion: 这些规模较小的 DataBrain 专用结果不足以证明生产可用性、通用 text-to-SQL 准确性，也不构成相对人工构建仪表盘的效率优势；结论受任务规模与领域特异性限制。

Abstract: Text2Dashboard is a DataBrain-specific prototype that turns natural-language analytic requests into inspectable dashboards. An installable Codex plugin and standalone Agent Runtime combine schema-constrained model decisions with typed tools, persistent state, and deterministic Hooks for approval, audit, checkpointing, recovery, and failure handling. The pipeline resolves entities, discovers metadata, enforces read-only SQL, composes dashboards, and applies static checks, dynamic preflight, and browser inspection. The model proposes actions while deterministic software controls execution and records state transitions.
  We evaluate the workflow on frozen real-DataBrain tasks and controlled Hook faults. Strict success was 6/8 on metadata and SQL tasks: metadata selection passed 4/4, all four SQL tasks met semantic criteria, and 2/4 met the exact output-column contract. The final release passed 4/4 single-panel dashboard tasks, one two-panel task, and one existing-dashboard refinement; a parameterised task exceeded its step limit. All ten fault scenarios met their specified outcomes without unapproved external side effects. Model inference accounted for over 97\% of observed runtime in every reported group. These small, DataBrain-specific results do not establish production readiness, general text-to-SQL accuracy, or an efficiency advantage over manual dashboard construction.

</details>


### [229] [FluidPD: In-Place Elasticity for SLO-Aware Prefill-Decode Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06917)
*Kartik Ramesh,Kaidi Fu,Zihan Zheng,Jiahuan Yu,Fabio Oliveira,Carlos Costa,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FluidPD 是一种面向 P/D 分离式 LLM 服务的 SLO 感知就地弹性系统，通过 FluidToken 缓解瞬时负载失衡、FluidRole 应对持续负载失衡，在 Azure 生产负载下将 SLO 达成率较静态 SGLang 提升至多 94.6 个百分点，且无需额外 GPU 配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P/D 分离架构因预填充与解码阶段执行模式和 SLO 目标不同而被广泛采用，但现有系统多采用固定的预填充/解码工作节点比例配合请求路由。真实负载同时存在短时突发和预填充-解码需求比的持续偏移，使得某一时刻配置合理的方案很快失配，即使在别处存在空闲容量也会导致延迟 SLO 违约；而现有自动扩缩容机制反应慢、需要备用 GPU，且无法直接解决短时间尺度的阶段失衡问题。

Method: FluidPD 提出 SLO 感知的就地弹性机制：一是 FluidToken，在解码侧存在空闲时，将有限部分的预填充计算卸载到解码工作节点，以应对瞬时失衡；二是 FluidRole，通过就地重新分配运行中的工作节点在预填充与解码角色之间切换，以应对持续性失衡，从而避免模型重载和引擎重启。两种机制均由轻量级压力指标引导，可在 SLO 违约出现之前暴露预填充与解码侧的资源压力。

Result: 在 Azure 生产 trace 负载下，FluidPD 相比静态 SGLang 将整体 SLO 达成率最高提升 94.6 个百分点，表明 SLO 感知的就地 P/D 弹性可在不额外配置工作节点的情况下提升服务质量。

Conclusion: SLO 感知的就地 P/D 弹性（FluidToken 与 FluidRole 结合压力指标引导）能够有效应对瞬时与持续的阶段负载失衡，在不增加 GPU 资源的前提下显著提升 LLM 服务的 SLO 达成率。

Abstract: Prefill-decode disaggregation is becoming a common architecture for LLM serving because it separates two phases with distinct execution patterns and SLO objectives. Existing systems typically combine a fixed prefill/decode worker ratio with request routing across workers. However, real-world workloads exhibit both short bursts and sustained shifts in the prefill-to-decode demand ratio. As a result, a configuration that is well provisioned at one time may quickly become mismatched, causing latency SLO violations even when idle capacity exists elsewhere. Existing autoscaling mechanisms can add capacity, but they react slowly, require spare GPUs, and do not directly address short-timescale phase imbalance.
  We present FluidPD, a P/D-disaggregated serving system that provides SLO-aware in-place elasticity. FluidPD introduces two complementary mechanisms. FluidToken handles transient imbalance by offloading a bounded portion of prefill computation to decode workers when decode-side slack is available. FluidRole handles sustained imbalance by reassigning running workers between prefill and decode roles in place, avoiding model reload and engine restart. Both mechanisms are guided by lightweight pressure indices that expose prefill and decode-side resource pressure before they appear as SLO violations. Across production Azure trace workloads, FluidPD improves overall SLO attainment over static SGLang by up to 94.6 percentage points, demonstrating that SLO-aware in-place P/D elasticity improves service quality without provisioning additional workers.

</details>


### [230] [Anchor Divergence for Semantic Geometry in Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06919)
*Akash Kannan,Kiho Park,Victor Veitch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"锚点散度"（Anchor Divergences），通过对比学习、指数族与信息几何的相互作用，在固定表示上建立"锚点分布↔Bregman几何"的对应关系，从而为特定上下文指定语义相似度几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相似度度量（如余弦相似度）只提供单一固定几何，而语义相似度本质上依赖上下文：两张图像可能因同一物体、相同风格或相关临床发现而相似。因此需要一种能在固定表示上指定上下文相关几何的方法。

Method: 利用对比学习、指数族分布与信息几何的相互作用，建立表示空间上"锚点上的概率分布"与"Bregman几何"之间的对应关系。基于该对应定义Anchor Divergences，用于在固定表示上指定特定上下文的语义几何；在此框架下，对锚点分布建模即等价于对几何本身建模。

Result: 检索（retrieval）实验表明，锚点散度能够有效且高效地指定上下文相关的语义相似度。

Conclusion: 对比表示天然蕴含一族可特化到特定语义结构的几何；通过建模锚点分布即可建模几何，从而以高效方式实现上下文相关的语义相似度度量。

Abstract: This paper concerns how semantic context determines geometry in learned vector representations. Similarity is typically measured using cosine similarity, which provides a single fixed geometry. Semantic similarity, however, is inherently context dependent: two images may be similar because they depict the same object, share a visual style, or are relevant to the same clinical finding. We show that contrastive representations naturally encompass a family of geometries that can be specialized to particular semantic structure. The key idea is to use an interplay between contrastive learning, exponential families, and information geometry to establish a correspondence between probability distributions over "anchors" and Bregman geometries on the representation space. We use this correspondence to define "Anchor Divergences", a method for specifying context-specific semantic geometries on fixed representations. Under this correspondence, modeling the anchor distribution models the geometry itself. Experiments on retrieval show that anchor divergences provide an effective and efficient way to specify context-specific semantic similarity.

</details>


### [231] [RadOnc-Agent: An LLM-Orchestrated Framework for AI Workflows Across the Radiotherapy Care Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06923)
*Caiwen Jiang,Shuoyang Wei,Songlin Zhao,Junyu Li,Jingyuan Chen,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RadOnc-Agent，一个将放疗流程形式化为四个临床阶段、通过对话界面调用 26 个函数的智能体 AI 框架，实现跨阶段纵向任务编排，单函数选择准确率 98.79%、脚本化跨阶段流程完成率 96.50%、真实患者流程完成率 96.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 在放疗中的应用呈现"任务碎片化"：能力分散在各临床阶段、软件环境和数据模态中，彼此孤立，与放疗从治疗决策到随访的纵向连续流程不匹配，缺乏统一协调异质能力与信息流的架构。

Method: 将放疗形式化为四个临床阶段，提供 26 个可调用函数与对话式接口；由大语言模型控制器将临床意图映射为 schema 约束调用，维护患者与工作流上下文，并将请求路由至专科服务。评估设计包括 2,600 条单函数请求（7,800 次重复执行）、200 个预设合成跨阶段场景（600 次执行）、以及来自 60 份去标识患者记录的 120 个工作流实例（360 次干净执行），覆盖"决策到计划"与"计划到自适应"环节；并开展消融实验。

Result: 单函数执行中选定目标函数比例 98.79%；脚本化跨阶段工作流完成率 96.50%；真实患者工作流执行完成率 96.67%。消融实验中，移除纵向状态使跨阶段完成率由 96.50% 降至 84.00%；禁用 schema 与身份校验使后端错误分发由 0% 升至 95.28%（重放/测试评估）。

Conclusion: 结果证明了 LLM 编排架构在纵向流程中协调异质放疗能力与信息的技术可行性；但未证明临床正确性、临床实用性或前瞻性获益，仍需进一步临床验证。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has advanced individual radiotherapy tasks, yet these capabilities remain separated across clinical stages, software environments and data modalities. This fragmentation contrasts with the longitudinal radiotherapy workflow from treatment decision-making through follow-up. Here we present RadOnc-Agent, an agentic artificial-intelligence framework that formalizes radiotherapy into four clinical phases and provides 26 callable functions through a conversational interface. A large-language-model controller maps clinical intent to schema-constrained calls, preserves patient and workflow context, and routes requests to specialist services. We evaluated system execution using 2,600 single-function requests (7,800 repeat executions), 200 prespecified synthetic cross-stage scenarios spanning four phases (600 executions), and 120 workflow instances from 60 de-identified patient records (360 clean executions) representing decision-to-planning and planning-to-adaptation. RadOnc-Agent selected the intended function in 98.79% of single-function executions, completed 96.50% of scripted cross-stage workflows, and completed 96.67% of real-patient workflow executions. In comparative ablations, removing longitudinal state reduced cross-stage completion from 96.50% to 84.00%, while disabling schema and identity validation increased mismatched backend dispatch from 0% to 95.28% in a replay/test evaluation. These findings establish the technical feasibility of an LLM-orchestrated architecture for coordinating heterogeneous radiotherapy capabilities and information across longitudinal workflows; they do not establish clinical correctness, clinical utility or prospective benefit.

</details>


### [232] [Metonymic Circuits for Abstract Concept Grounding in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06928)
*Jing Ding,Ziqiao Ma,Jiayuan Mao,Joyce Chai,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉Transformer在参考证据有限时如何将抽象概念（如"愤怒"）接地，提出并验证了"转喻式接地"机制：抽象预测由具体、可解释的锚点概念（如"火"）驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据提供的直接指称证据有限，Vision Transformers如何将抽象概念与视觉信号联系起来尚不清楚，需要揭示其内部接地机制。

Method: 在CLIP与DINO视觉编码器上应用Transcoders，恢复可与具体概念语义标签关联的中间特征，并追踪其在抽象概念识别电路中的贡献；同时使用精心构建的图标数据集，并通过因果干预进行验证。

Result: 实验揭示出结构化的转喻电路：感知基元主导早期层，类物体锚点先于抽象目标出现；包含渲染文本的图像则另招募一条从感知到文本的独立路径。因果干预进一步证实转喻中间特征在抽象概念接地中具有功能作用。

Conclusion: Vision Transformers通过具体的锚点概念以转喻方式接地抽象概念，且该机制在因果层面得到验证，为理解视觉模型抽象语义的形成提供了机制性解释。

Abstract: We study how Vision Transformers ground abstract concepts (e.g., angry) when training data provide limited direct referential evidence. We hypothesize a metonymic grounding mechanism in which abstract predictions are driven by concrete, interpretable anchor concepts (e.g., fire) that bridge visual signals to abstract semantics. By applying Transcoders on CLIP and DINO vision encoders, we recover intermediate features that can be associated with semantic labels for more concrete concepts, and trace their contributions in circuits underlying abstract concept recognition. Experiments on a carefully curated icon dataset reveal structured metonymic circuits, in which perceptual primitives dominate early layers and object-like anchors precede abstract targets. Images containing rendered text instead recruit a distinct perceptual-to-textual route. Causal interventions further validate that metonymic intermediates are functionally involved in grounding abstract concepts.

</details>


### [233] [Principles that Guide, Actions that Inform: Agent Evolution via Knowledge Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06964)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SAGA 框架，将 LLM 智能体的交互轨迹抽象为情景描述、可复用流程与带适用条件的原理，并构建"执行—抽象"反馈循环，在 ScienceWorld 与 ALFWorld 上提升任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体虽在交互环境中表现强，但持续从经验中进化能力有限。微调依赖参数访问与高算力，难以适用于大规模及闭源模型；外部记忆虽免于改参数，却因知识表示与具体任务、上下文强耦合而泛化性差。核心难题是如何把具体交互转化为抽象、可复用、可指导未来决策的知识。

Method: SAGA 逐层将交互轨迹转化为三类知识：情景描述、可复用流程、带显式适用条件的原理，并保留与执行证据的链接。推理时检索原理，将其实例化为任务特定指导，并通过纠正性反馈与重采样对候选动作进行修正、调控。

Result: 在 ScienceWorld 与 ALFWorld 上任务性能均得到提升；消融实验表明上下文实例化与动作调控对利用原理级知识至关重要。

Conclusion: 通过经验落地的分层知识抽象与执行—抽象反馈循环，智能体可在不改动模型参数的前提下积累、复用并持续更新知识，从而实现自我进化与更强的跨情境泛化。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in interactive environments, yet their ability to continually evolve from experience remains limited. Although fine-tuning enables adaptation, its dependence on parameter access and high computational costs restrict its flexibility, especially for large-scale and closed-source LLMs. External memory offers an alternative by allowing agents to accumulate experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing methods mainly focus on experience representation and organization, while the acquired knowledge remains tightly coupled with specific tasks and contexts, limiting generalization. A key challenge is how to transform concrete interactions into abstract and reusable knowledge that guides future decisions beyond individual experiences.
  To address this challenge, we propose SAGA (\underline{\textbf{S}}elf-evolving \underline{\textbf{A}}gents through Experience-\underline{\textbf{G}}rounded \underline{\textbf{A}}bstraction), a framework for experience-grounded knowledge abstraction and utilization in LLM agents. SAGA progressively transforms interaction trajectories into episodic descriptions, reusable procedures, and principles with explicit applicability conditions, while maintaining links to execution evidence. Retrieved principles are instantiated into task-specific guidance and used to refine candidate actions through corrective feedback and resampling. This creates an execution--abstraction feedback loop, where accumulated knowledge guides future interactions and new experiences continuously update hierarchical memory. Experiments on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld demonstrate improved task performance, with ablation studies highlighting the importance of contextual instantiation and action regulation for leveraging principle-level knowledge.

</details>


### [234] [AegisFlow: A Multi-Agent Agentic AI Framework for Autonomous Remediation and Self-Healing in Fragile Data Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06971)
*Muhammad Bilal Awan,Zubair Hussain,Abdul Shahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AegisFlow 是一个基于 LLM 的智能体自愈框架，通过 Watchdog 与 Repair 智能体及"并行影子补丁"机制实现数据管道的自动检测与修复，将 MTTR 从 170 分钟降至 3.2 分钟（提升 98.1%），补丁成功率 92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据管道脆弱，常因上游 schema 漂移、API 契约变更或网页 DOM 修改而失败；现有可观测性工具仅向人工工程师发出告警，导致修复平均时间（MTTR）高、运维疲劳严重。作者希望闭合"检测—解决"的环路，实现自动化自愈。

Method: 提出 AegisFlow 智能体框架：由 Watchdog 智能体采集运行时遥测数据，Repair 智能体基于大语言模型（LLM）自动生成、测试并部署代码补丁；核心为非侵入式执行模型"并行影子补丁"（Parallel Shadow Patching），基于 MAPE-K（监控、分析、规划、执行、知识）循环，在数字孪生环境中生成并验证补丁；框架以插件方式部署于现有管道编排系统。

Result: 在五类常见故障场景上评估：MTTR 平均从每补丁 170 分钟降至 3.2 分钟，提升 98.1%；补丁成功率 92%。分场景看，JSON schema 变更成功率 96%，标点漂移 98%，表现最差的 Shadow DOM 场景为 85%。约 98% 的数据工程 on-call 时间从"救火"中释放，转投创新工作。

Conclusion: AegisFlow 通过智能体化、闭环化的自愈机制显著降低数据管道运维成本与 MTTR，且具备部署无关性，可作为插件低侵入集成到现有编排系统，具有良好的工程实用性与可移植性。

Abstract: Traditional data pipelines are notoriously brittle, often failing due to upstream schema drift, API contract changes, or website DOM modifications. Present observability tools only raise alerts but for human engineers, resulting in a high Mean Time to Repair (MTTR) and operational fatigue. In this paper we propose AegisFlow (Agentic Engine for Intelligent Self-healing and Graph-driven Operations for Workload remediation), a novel agentic framework that closes the loop between detection and resolution. AegisFlow uses a Watchdog agent to collect runtime telemetry and has a Repair agent to automatically create, test and deploy code patches based on Large Language Models (LLMs). The framework presents the non-intrusive execution model called Parallel Shadow Patching, a non-intrusive execution model based on the Monitor, Analyze, Plan, Execute, Knowledge (MAPE-K) loop to generate and verify patches in digital twin environments. Through experimental testing, we have evaluated AegisFlow across five common failure scenarios, and see 98.1 percent improvement in MTTR (from an average of 170 minutes per patch to 3.2 minutes) and a patch success rate of 92 percent . In particular, the system is successful in dealing with changes in the JSON schema (96 percent ) and punctuation drift (98 percent ), and is least successful in Shadow DOM cases (85 percent ). AegisFlow frees up about 98 percent of data engineering on-call time from firefighting and reallocates it towards innovation. The framework is deployment agnostic consisting of a system that can be deployed in a plugin fashion into an existing pipeline orchestration system with minimal uplift to the existing system.

</details>


### [235] [EPOCH: Reliable Discovery through Evidence-Governed Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06986)
*Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Ruitong Li,Xinle Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EPOCH，一种证据治理架构，通过任务契约、类型化记忆、主动证伪、准入检查与独立复现，将评估反馈的解读与复用纳入治理，从而在程序、数学构造与证明搜索中避免将脆弱候选误判为发现，并在多个基准上取得领先或有利结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 研究代理通常在缺乏对评估反馈如何被解读、质疑与复用进行治理的情况下直接优化反馈，导致脆弱但看似有前景的候选被提升为“发现”，并将基准提升、有限证书与定理级声明相互混淆。

Method: EPOCH 实现证据治理的发现循环，结合显式任务契约、类型化记忆、主动证伪、准入检查以及独立复现，使每个候选方案都依据其支撑声明的主张强度与适用范围进行评估。

Result: 在 AlgoTune 上取得最优聚合表现，平均归一化得分显著超过最强基线（0.65 对 0.53）；在内部 Math14 套件上取得最高平均分（0.57）；在官方测试复现下表现出有利的留出行为，并在 AgentHPO 上领先描述性聚合。在十个发现问题上，EPOCH 取得显著的任务特定进展，包括改进的可执行构造、优化算法、反例以及有证明支撑的结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，证据治理是迈向 AI 研究代理的关键一步：不仅能产生更强的解，也能产生更可信的科学发现，将搜索转化为数学与计算领域的具体进展。

Abstract: AI research agents are increasingly used to search over programs, mathematical constructions, and proofs. However, existing systems typically optimize evaluator feedback without adequately governing how that feedback is interpreted, challenged, and reused. As a result, promising but fragile candidates can be promoted as discoveries, while benchmark improvements, finite certificates, and theorem-level claims are too easily conflated. We introduce EPOCH, an evidence-governed architecture designed to close this gap. EPOCH implements an evidence-governed discovery loop by combining explicit task contracts, typed memory, active falsification, admission checks, and independent replay, so that each candidate is evaluated against the strength and scope of the claim it supports. EPOCH achieves state-of-the-art aggregate performance on AlgoTune, substantially exceeding the strongest baseline in mean normalized score (0.65 vs. 0.53), and attains the highest mean score on the internal Math14 suite (0.57). It further shows favorable held-out behavior under official-test replay and leads the descriptive aggregate on AgentHPO. Across ten discovery problems, EPOCH delivers substantial task-specific advances, including improved executable constructions, optimized algorithms, counterexamples, and proof-supported results. These advances demonstrate its ability to convert search into concrete progress across mathematical and computational domains. Together, the results suggest that evidence governance is a necessary step toward AI research agents that produce not only stronger solutions, but also more trustworthy scientific discoveries.

</details>


### [236] [When better traffic forecasts fail to improve signal control: a layered diagnostic study of forecast-to-decision value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06992)
*Jianing Long,Xiaobin Li,Wuming Lei,Weiguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对"预测精度提升是否必然带来信号控制决策改善"这一缺口进行分层诊断研究，基于宣城29天重建需求数据（7天留作测试），系统评估点预测、保形区间、依赖感知情景与匹配闭环控制器。结果显示更高精度预测并未改善控制性能：因果预测与事件预言机反而使排队车·秒增加，说明预测价值取决于时间可观测性、动作可辨识性、动态一致性与目标对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通领域普遍假设预测精度提升会自然转化为更优的信号控制决策，但实践中这一假设常被违背。作者希望揭示预测提升无法转化为运行效益的原因，并提供一个可操作、可诊断的评估协议来定位问题环节。

Method: 采用分层诊断框架，使用宣城29天重建交通需求数据，7天为冻结测试期。分别评估入口级与流向级点预测、名义90%保形区间、依赖感知情景生成，以及与之匹配的闭环控制器；通过接口审计识别决策时刻的信息泄漏，并进行因果预测与5秒事件预言机（穷举联合动作搜索）的对比，另设置合成正对照验证未来信息对 rollout 成本的影响。

Result: 入口级与流向级预测相对历史均值分别降低平均绝对误差4.03%和3.92%；名义90%保形区间边际覆盖率90.72%，但在事后高需求子集上仅75.66%。接口审计发现决策时刻信息泄漏，且9个受控交叉口中仅2个存在多个有效动作。合成正对照表明未来信息可将内部 rollout 成本降低61.5%；而在冻结测试日，因果预测与事件预言机相对匹配的无未来 rollout 分别使排队车·秒增加6.09%和3.39%，预言机使溢流暴露降低3.78%，配对日bootstrap区间跨零。

Conclusion: 预测精度提升不等于控制收益，预测价值受时间可观测性、动作可辨识性、动态一致性与目标对齐四个条件制约。所提出的诊断协议可实际用于识别预测改进未能转化为运行效益的环节。

Abstract: Improved traffic forecasts do not necessarily yield better signal-control decisions. We investigate this gap through a layered diagnostic study using 29 days of reconstructed demand from Xuancheng, China, with seven dates reserved for testing. The framework evaluates point forecasts, conformal intervals, dependence-aware scenarios, and matched closed-loop controllers. Entry-level and movement-level forecasts reduce mean absolute error by 4.03% and 3.92%, respectively, relative to historical means. A nominal 90% conformal interval achieves 90.72% marginal coverage but only 75.66% on an ex-post high-demand subset. Interface audits identify decision-time leakage and reveal that only two of nine controlled intersections offer multiple effective actions. We correct the temporal interface and compare causal forecasts with a five-second event oracle using exhaustive joint-action search. A synthetic positive control demonstrates that future information can reduce the internal rollout cost by 61.5%. On the frozen test dates, however, causal forecasts and the event oracle increase queue vehicle?seconds by 6.09% and 3.39% relative to the matched no-future rollout, while the oracle reduces spillback exposure by 3.78%; paired-day bootstrap intervals cross zero. These findings indicate that forecast value depends on temporal observability, action identifiability, dynamics consistency, and objective alignment. The proposed protocol provides a practical way to diagnose where predictive improvements fail to translate into operational benefits.

</details>


### [237] [Joint upper-bound coverage and route-choice utility: an empirical evaluation on two urban proxy tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06995)
*Jianing Long,Xiaobin Li,Wuming Lei,Weiguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文在冻结协议下评估"更准的交通预测或更高的不确定性覆盖率是否改善路径决策"，用北京、成都路速数据构建离线代理任务，分别检验速度误差、联合候选路径上界覆盖率、路径选择与实现损失。结果显示联合覆盖率大幅提升，但迟到率与平均行程时间反而增加（C2），说明覆盖率并非下游路径效用的替代指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中常假定提高交通预测精度或不确定性覆盖率会自然带来更好的路径选择，但二者与下游决策效用之间的因果或代理关系尚不明确；作者希望剥离"速度误差—联合上界覆盖—路径选择—实际损失"各环节，检验覆盖率能否作为路径效用的可靠代理。

Method: 采用冻结协议，将速度误差、联合候选路径上界覆盖、路径选择与实现损失分离开来。基于北京与成都已处理的道路速度数据，构建离线代理任务：每城150个OD对、3条候选路径、14个测试日；在最小上界路径选择规则下，比较原始90分位路径时间上界与联合校准上界，并另设成都预测器对比实验。

Result: 联合覆盖率显著上升：北京M1由83.19%升至92.26%，成都M1由75.14%升至88.33%，成都M2由74.01%升至90.64%。但C2分别使迟到率增加0.1633、0.7848、0.9200个百分点，平均行程时间增加0.588、3.082、4.418秒。在成都预测器对比中，速度平均绝对误差降低14.91%伴随C0下迟到率下降1.4571个百分点。

Conclusion: 在这些冻结任务中，联合覆盖率不能作为下游路径效用的替代指标；离线结果不构成在线或因果收益的证据。提升不确定性覆盖率可能以牺牲准时性与行程时间为代价，评估应直接以下游损失为准。

Abstract: Whether more accurate traffic forecasts or higher uncertainty coverage improve route decisions is unclear. We evaluate this question with a frozen protocol that separates speed error, joint candidate path upper bound coverage, route selection, and realized loss. Using processed road speed data from Beijing and Chengdu, we construct offline proxy tasks with 150 origin destination pairs, three candidate paths, and 14 test days per city. We compare raw 90th percentile path time bounds with jointly calibrated upper bounds under minimum bound route choice. Joint coverage rises from 83.19% to 92.26% in Beijing M1, from 75.14% to 88.33% in Chengdu M1, and from 74.01% to 90.64% in Chengdu M2. Yet C2 increases lateness by 0.1633, 0.7848, and 0.9200 percentage points, respectively, and mean travel time by 0.588, 3.082, and 4.418 seconds. In a separate Chengdu predictor comparison, a 14.91% reduction in speed mean absolute error accompanies a 1.4571 percentage point reduction in lateness under C0. Joint coverage is therefore not a surrogate for downstream route utility in these frozen tasks the offline results do not establish online or causal benefits.

</details>


### [238] [Topology-Consistent Task Planning over Cellular Workflow Complexes for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07004)
*Sen Zhao,Jia Tang,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Ding Zou,Xinyu He,Xu Zhang,Junwei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoPlanner，将工具依赖图提升为胞腔工作流复形，作为拓扑感知上下文指导LLM工具规划，在四类工具规划基准上优于提示与图增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的任务规划器擅长处理顺序或DAG结构，但难以应对真实验证-纠错循环、分支汇聚、可复用中间状态等工作流模式，缺乏对复杂拓扑依赖的建模能力。

Method: TopoPlanner将工具依赖图提升为胞腔工作流复形；通过cosheaf一致性胞腔检索获取与请求相关的闭子复形；在检索拓扑上进行多维结构推理，并将所得胞腔表示接入规划LLM以生成工具序列。

Result: 在四个工具规划基准上，针对拓扑引导的循环、汇聚及循环-汇聚工作流，相比提示式与图增强基线在不同本地LLM主干上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 以拓扑一致的胞腔复形作为上下文可有效增强LLM工具规划对循环、汇聚等复杂工作流模式的处理能力，具有跨主干模型的鲁棒性与通用性。

Abstract: Task planning for LLM agents requires workflows that satisfy both user intent and complex sub-task dependencies. While existing planners work well for sequential or directed acyclic graph (DAG)-like structures, they struggle with workflow patterns such as verification-correction loops, convergent branch merging, and reusable intermediate states that arise naturally in real-world tool orchestration. We present TopoPlanner, a topology-consistent planning framework that lifts tool dependency graphs into cellular workflow complexes and uses them as topologyaware context for LLM tool planning. TopoPlanner retrieves a request-relevant closed subcomplex through cosheaf-consistent cellular retrieval, performs multidimensional structural reasoning over the retrieved topology, and interfaces the resulting cellular representation with the planner LLM for tool-sequence generation.
