### [272] [Evaluating Escalation Signals for LLM Routing: Targets, Controls, and Five Ways to Fool Yourself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07354)
*Ramin Pishehvar,Andrea Morandi,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究"何时将查询从小模型升级到大模型"的信号评估问题，提出一套评估清单，揭示语义熵等升级信号可能只是反映了问题难度，并给出验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 升级查询需要在大模型调用前预判升级是否有帮助的廉价信号。语义熵（衡量采样答案在语义上的分歧）是自然候选，但作者希望检验这类信号是否真的提供了额外信息，还是仅仅在追踪问题表面的难度。

Method: 在三个基准和两个模型家族上测试语义熵。关键手段是：在评估任何信号时，同时评分一个"仅基于问题的廉价难度估计"，看该信号是否在难度之外仍增加真实信息；并考察"升级成功"的两种合理定义、基准本身留给信号提升的空间、以及实时采样的真实成本。对复用缓存历史结果的更廉价替代方案，提出预测其在新数据集上是否有效的方法。

Result: 在GSM8K上，小/大约相差12倍的模型对中，语义熵能可靠区分小模型的错误（AUROC 0.871），在相同成本下比随机升级的路由准确率最多提升9个点。但一个合成基准上的早期结果被证明具有误导性：一个不使用模型的、仅基于问题难度的简单规则几乎完全匹配语义熵的表现。对复用缓存的替代方案，作者提前正确预测了AUROC从0.908崩溃到随机水平（0.518）。

Conclusion: 本文主要贡献是一套检查方法，用于在把升级信号报告为"真实有效"之前识破假象，包括：难度对照基线、升级成功的定义敏感性、基准天花板效应、实时采样成本，以及缓存替代方案的可迁移性预测。作者将其作为评估升级信号的通用清单。

Abstract: Deciding when to escalate a query from a small language model to a larger one requires a cheap signal that predicts, before the large model is called, whether escalating would help. Semantic entropy, originally developed to detect hallucinations, is a natural candidate: it measures how much a model's sampled answers disagree in meaning, and high disagreement often signals an unreliable answer. We test it across three benchmarks and two model families. On GSM8K, with a small/large pair about twelve times apart in size, semantic entropy reliably distinguishes the small model's mistakes (AUROC 0.871) and improves routed accuracy over random escalation by up to nine points at matched cost. An earlier strong-looking result on a synthetic benchmark proved misleading: a simple rule based only on question difficulty, with no model involved, matched semantic entropy almost exactly. This paper's main contribution is a set of checks that catch this before it is reported as real. We show that scoring a cheap, question-only difficulty estimate alongside any signal reveals whether the signal adds real information or just tracks how hard a question looks; that two reasonable definitions of "escalation worked" can produce very different results on the same data; that a benchmark can leave almost no room for any signal to beat simply always using the large model; and that the true cost of live sampling can make routing more expensive than calling the large model directly. For a cheaper alternative that reuses cached past outcomes, we show how to predict whether it will work on a new dataset -- confirmed by correctly forecasting a collapse from AUROC 0.908 to chance level (0.518) ahead of time. We offer these as a general checklist for evaluating escalation signals.

</details>


### [273] [Evaluate the Stack, Not the Layer: Do Deterministic and LLM Gates for Agent Actions Fail Independently?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07359)
*Chenglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在1,119条带标注的agent动作上检验运行时门控堆叠的“误差相乘”假设：任意两个LLM裁判组合仅相当于约1.2–1.4个相乘等价层，而规则层加单个裁判相当于1.86–2.09层；单独准确率无法预测某一层带来的边际覆盖增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时门控通常被堆叠使用，其前提假设是各层错误相互独立、因而以乘法方式衰减。该假设此前缺乏实证检验，作者希望量化每层的“相乘等价层数”，并检验堆叠收益是否如理论预期。

Method: 在三个语料库的1,119条标注agent动作上评估，无自适应对手。堆叠含一个确定性规则层与四个LLM裁判（三个重新采集并记录每次调用的服务模型）。以n_mult（相乘等价层数）度量堆叠，并给出完全耦合下的下界。采用两种漏检定义：STRICT（升级到人工记为未拦截）与PRIMARY（升级记为已捕获）。以φ系数衡量层间耦合，并用bootstrap区间检验。

Result: STRICT定义下：任意两裁判组合约1.2–1.4层（φ中位数+0.430，6/6对显著，下界1.02–1.17）；规则层+单裁判为1.86–2.09层（φ中位数+0.014，0/4显著，下界1.01–1.09）。PRIMARY下分别为1.21–1.57与1.80–2.13。合并数据上区间可分，但逐语料点估计结论分裂；第三方厂商裁判落入裁判带。单独准确率不预测边际贡献：某云端规则包将规则层单独漏检率降低20%，却未带来新的联合覆盖。裁判耦合中的难度占比不可识别（31.8%–61.8%，随探针与定义变化）。一个裁判层在112批中的50批由未请求的模型版本服务（集中于外部语料）。

Conclusion: 门控堆叠的乘法假设在实践中被削弱：同类型LLM裁判之间高度耦合，几乎不产生额外等价层，而规则层与裁判之间的增益更接近独立相乘。层数不能由单独准确率推断，耦合中的难度成分不可识别，故应直接测量联合覆盖而非依赖理论层数。服务模型版本漂移与评分口径变化可反转结论，需明确披露并报告两种口径。

Abstract: Runtime gates for agent tool calls are stacked on the assumption that their errors multiply. We test it on 1,119 labelled agent actions from three corpora, without an adaptive adversary. The stack has one deterministic rule layer and four LLM judges, three of them re-collected with the served model recorded on every call. We read each stack as a number of multiplication-equivalent layers, n_mult, with its floor under perfect coupling. Under the STRICT miss definition (escalation to a human scored as not stopped), any two judges compose to about 1.2 to 1.4 layers (φ median +0.430, 6 of 6 pairs significant, floors 1.02 to 1.17). The rule layer plus one judge composes to 1.86 to 2.09 layers (φ median +0.014, 0 of 4 significant, floors 1.01 to 1.09). Under PRIMARY (escalation scored as caught) the bands are 1.21 to 1.57 and 1.80 to 2.13. Intervals separate on the pooled data, point estimates split on each corpus, and a third-vendor judge lands in the judge band. Solo accuracy does not predict what a layer adds: a cloud rule pack lowers the rule layer's solo miss rate by 20% and adds no new joint coverage. The difficulty share of judge coupling is not identifiable: 31.8% to 61.8% depending on the probe and the miss definition. One judge tier was served by an unrequested model version in 50 of 112 batches, concentrated on the external corpus. That event overturned a pre-declared analysis rule, and the scoring of review verdicts reversed five conclusions. We report both.

</details>


### [274] [MemCo: Memory-Centric Collaboration for Generalizing LLM Agents to Unseen Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07376)
*Xinting Liao,Siyan Liu,Rabab K. Ward,Holger R. Roth,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemCo，一个以记忆为中心的协作框架，通过维护局部与全局互补记忆空间并按状态和决策阶段路由检索，帮助LLM智能体泛化到未见过的交互环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体需在交互环境中通过观察、行动和反馈进行序贯决策。记忆可帮助复用经验，但现有工作孤立地设计记忆，收集足够轨迹来填充记忆成本高昂；共享记忆方法虽可跨任务与环境汇集情节记忆，但检索面临粒度难题：检索到的记忆要么过于具体而无法保持当前情境的落地性，要么过于粗糙而无法支持下一步动作。

Method: MemCo维护互补的局部与全局记忆空间：局部保留环境特定细节，全局则从局部轨迹中归纳出可迁移的工作流。在线交互时，MemCo根据智能体当前状态与决策阶段路由相关的局部和全局记忆，使智能体能够复用其他智能体的经验，同时避免盲目迁移环境特定的细节。

Result: 在交互式决策基准上的实验表明，与孤立记忆和共享记忆基线相比，MemCo提升了任务成功率并减少了冗余探索。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MemCo通过局部-全局互补记忆与基于状态和决策阶段的路由机制，有效解决共享记忆检索的粒度困境，提升LLM智能体在未见交互环境中的泛化能力与决策效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate in interactive environments, where they need to make sequential decisions through observation, action, and feedback. Although memory can help agents reuse experience, existing work designs memory in isolation, where collecting enough trajectories to populate it is expensive. Existing shared-memory approaches mitigate isolated experience by pooling episodic memories across tasks and environments. However, retrieving shared memory is challenged by the granularity, where retrieved memories can be either too specific to preserve current grounding or too coarse to support the next action. In this work, we propose MemCo, a memory-centric collaboration framework for generalizing LLM agents to unseen interactive environments. It maintains complementary local and global memory spaces, preserving environment-specific details locally while promoting transferable workflows induced from local trajectories to global memory. During online interaction, MemCo routes relevant local and global memories in terms of the agent's current state and decision phase, enabling agents to reuse the experience of other agents without blindly transferring environment-specific details. Experiments on interactive decision-making benchmarks show that MemCo improves task success and reduces redundant exploration compared with isolate-memory and shared-memory baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/SYannL/nvdamas.

</details>


### [275] [Defense-in-Depth for LLMs: Evaluating Memory Gates Against Activation-Induced and Memory-Induced Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07403)
*Ritvij Sharma,Russell Dlugosz,Ryan Zhou,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个 $2\times2$ 纵深防御框架，将内部激活引导与外部记忆处理分离，并在 MemSyco-Bench 上评测四种开源权重模型。结果显示，选择性 Router Gate 记忆过滤在保持准确率方面显著优于完全删除记忆，且该优势在模型被引导至谄媚时依然存在；但逆向激活引导并未显示出对记忆过滤的额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使 LLM 能跨交互维持个性化上下文，但检索到的用户历史会诱发"记忆诱导谄媚"，使模型偏向存储的用户信念而非客观证据。现有防御主要作用于检索上下文，且很少与模型内部行为偏差联合评估，因此需要一种同时覆盖内外部机制的防御与评估方案。

Method: 提出 $2\times2$ 纵深防御框架，分离内部激活引导与外部记忆处理。从 100 对配对提示中提取谄媚引导方向，在 MemSyco-Bench（全部 1,550 题；防御条件在固定 250 题子样本上评判）上评估四种开源权重模型，覆盖 10 个引导系数与 5 种记忆防御配置，并使用三个 LLM 评判器。其中三种配置为新增：重写每条记忆、Router Gate（对每条记忆保留/重写/丢弃）、丢弃全部记忆；另两种为 MemSyco 基线。

Result: 选择性 Router Gate 过滤比完全移除记忆保留了显著更多的 MemSyco 平均准确率，且该分离在模型被引导至谄媚时仍然成立。在 Llama 3.1 8B + Router Gate 下，轻度逆向引导（$\alpha=-1.5$）将评判器平均谄媚率从 35.80% 降至 31.32%，同时平均准确率从 43.99% 变为 43.31%；该降低在三个评判器下方向一致，但在 149 题上不具统计显著性（配对 $p=0.08$ 至 $0.63$）。

Conclusion: 外部记忆过滤是该设计中稳健有效的部分；数据未表明逆向激活引导能在记忆过滤基础上带来额外收益。

Abstract: Long-term memory allows Large Language Models (LLMs) to maintain personalized context across interactions, but retrieved user history can induce memory-induced sycophancy, causing models to favor stored user beliefs over objective evidence. Existing defenses primarily operate on retrieved context and are rarely evaluated jointly with internal behavioral bias. We introduce a $2 \times 2$ defense-in-depth framework separating internal activation steering from external memory handling. We extract sycophancy steering directions from 100 paired prompts and evaluate four open-weight models across 10 steering coefficients and five memory-defense configurations on MemSyco-Bench (answers for all 1,550 items; defense conditions judged on a fixed 250-item subsample), with three LLM judges. Three of the five configurations are new (rewriting every memory, a Router Gate that keeps, rewrites, or drops each memory, and dropping all memory); the other two are MemSyco's baselines. Selective Router Gate filtering preserves substantially more of MemSyco's average accuracy than complete memory removal, and this separation persists when the models are steered toward sycophancy. On Llama 3.1 8B with Router Gate, mild inverse steering ($α= -1.5$) lowers judge-averaged sycophancy from 35.80% to 31.32% while average accuracy moves from 43.99% to 43.31%; this reduction has the same direction under all three judges but is not statistically significant (paired $p = 0.08$ to $0.63$ on 149 items). External memory filtering is the part of the design that holds up; our data do not show that inverse steering adds to it.

</details>


### [276] [2d-fet-bench: from spatial reasoning to fet design on flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07423)
*Dunzhi Zhou,Chengyu Zhu,Gang Qiu,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出2D-FET-Bench V2基准，用于评测语言模型智能体能否根据显微轮廓为二维材料薄片自动构建场效应晶体管版图，并给出六模型与七种工作流变体的评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剥离的二维薄片上的FET版图通常需按每个薄片的光学显微位置与轮廓手工绘制电极和栅极，缺乏可执行基准来检验语言模型智能体能否可靠完成此类薄片特定版图构建。

Method: 构建含128个版图任务的2D-FET-Bench V2，取自显微图像衍生的薄片轮廓（含带孔薄片与多薄片任务）。每个任务提供文本器件规格与轮廓坐标，智能体生成类型化多边形与路径操作并渲染为GDSII；采用确定性验证器检查几何与结构要求，另有完整性检查确认轮廓未被改动。以脚本参考版图验证任务可解性，并评测六个模型及GPT5.6-Luna的七种工作流/脚手架变体，每任务五次尝试。

Result: 脚本参考版图通过全部128个任务，证明任务均可解。六模型面板中表现最佳的GPT5.6-Luna配ReAct-3通过62.3%的尝试，覆盖率80.5%，五次全通过一致性43.8%。ReAct-3相比单次Plan-and-Execute在pass@1上高27.0个百分点，但token消耗为2.46倍。对每个已覆盖任务抽取一个通过验证的版图进行专家审核，在五种ReAct-3配置下接受率为56.4%至63.5%。

Conclusion: 该基准可评估FET版图的几何与结构构建能力，揭示当前智能体在薄片特定版图任务上仍有明显差距，且验证器通过并不完全等同于专家认可。

Abstract: Field-effect transistor (FET) layouts on exfoliated two-dimensional flakes are typically drawn by hand for each flake, placing contacts and gates to match its position and outline in optical micrographs. To our knowledge, no executable benchmark tests whether language-model agents can perform this flake-specific construction reliably. We introduce 2D-FET-Bench V2, a benchmark of 128 layout tasks built from microscopy-derived flake contours, including hole-containing flakes and multi-flake tasks. Each task supplies a textual device specification and contour coordinates. An agent generates typed polygon and path operations rendered to GDSII. A deterministic verifier checks geometric and structural requirements, and a separate integrity check verifies that the supplied contours remain unchanged. Scripted reference layouts pass all 128 tasks, showing that every task is solvable. We evaluate six models and seven workflow and scaffold variants of GPT5.6-Luna, with five attempts per task. The best-performing configuration in the six-model panel, GPT5.6-Luna with ReAct-3, passes 62.3% of attempts and solves 80.5% of tasks at least once (coverage) and 43.8% in all five attempts (consistency). ReAct-3 exceeds the one-pass Plan-and-Execute by 27.0 pass@1 points at 2.46 times the tokens. An expert audit of one sampled verifier-passing layout per covered task, across five ReAct-3 configurations, accepts 56.4% to 63.5% of them. The benchmark evaluates geometric and structural FET layout construction.

</details>


### [277] [Adaptive Gait Biofeedback With Participant-Held-Out Modeling and Participant-Specific Updating in Chronic Ankle Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07428)
*Jaeyoon,Kim,Veronika Lebisova,Jeniya Sultana,Jaeyoung Cho,Jaeho Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了用于慢性踝关节不稳自适应步态生物反馈的时序卷积分类器的模型性能与人体响应：LOSO交叉验证的AUROC为0.948，但特异性仅0.366；离线模型更新提升BAD类F1；非随机对照显示干预组Post期额面踝角低5.168度，但保留期结果不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应步态生物反馈可能支持慢性踝关节不稳患者的重复训练，但其评估需同时考虑模型性能与人体响应两方面，而现有研究往往只关注其中之一。

Method: 采用参与者留出的留一被试交叉验证（LOSO），在20名参与者上评估时序卷积分类器对协议定义、由角度推导的GOOD/BAD步态周期标签的分类表现；自适应干预组7名参与者三周完成9次训练，每次分析一次动作捕捉记录，并在同一会话验证子集及随后首次自适应会话记录上，将失败会话后的更新模型与父模型进行离线比较；同时将自适应组与10名顺序入组对照者在基线、Post和7天保留期的额面踝角进行对比。

Result: 20个留出折的平均折级AUROC为0.948，对超过角度阈值BAD周期的敏感度为0.941，对满足角度阈值GOOD周期的特异度为0.366。候选模型平均BAD类F1在同一会话子集上高0.187，在随后首次记录上高0.118。Post期自适应组基线校正后额面踝角比对照低5.168度（95%CI：1.766–8.569度），保留期对比不确定。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了重复生物反馈使用中的群体模型判别能力与离线参与者特异性更新，并发现非随机化的Post期额面踝角关联；但未建立独立的临床步态分类能力，也未证明模型更新具有因果获益。

Abstract: Adaptive gait biofeedback may support repeated practice in chronic ankle instability, but its evaluation must address model performance and human response. We evaluated a temporal convolutional classifier on protocol-defined, angle-derived GOOD/BAD gait-cycle labels using participant-held-out leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation in 20 participants. Seven participants in the adaptive-intervention group completed nine sessions over three weeks, with one motion-capture recording analyzed per session. Models updated after failed sessions were compared offline with their parent models on the same-session validation subset used for candidate selection and the first subsequent adaptive-session recording. Frontal-plane ankle angle was compared between the adaptive group and 10 sequentially enrolled controls at Baseline, Post, and 7-day Retention. Across 20 held-out folds, mean fold-level area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) was 0.948, sensitivity for angle-threshold-exceeding BAD cycles was 0.941, and specificity for angle-threshold-meeting GOOD cycles was 0.366. Mean BAD-class F1 was higher in candidate models by 0.187 on the same-session subset and 0.118 on the first subsequent recording. At Post, the adaptive group had a baseline-adjusted frontal-plane ankle angle 5.168 degrees lower than controls (95% confidence interval, 1.766-8.569 degrees lower); the Retention contrast was uncertain. These findings characterize population-model discrimination and offline participant-specific updating during repeated biofeedback use, alongside a nonrandomized Post frontal-plane ankle angle association. They do not establish independent clinical gait classification or a causal benefit of updating.

</details>


### [278] [When Does AI Supervision Help? A Role-Aware Study of Network Fraud Decision Management with Blockchain Auditability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07434)
*Saviz Changizi,Nasibeh Mohammadzadeh,Mohammad Shojafar,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出角色感知的Decider-Supervisor框架（含区块链可审计性），评估四种方向性配置。硬门控解决89.994%欺诈请求，非硬群体成为主要AI决策场景。条件校验校准未能在部署回放中产生稳定可迁移的监督优势；DS-3 QLoRA扰动最小但仍劣于其FedAvg主阶段；36种重加权流量下仅DS-4 Base在两种极端高欺诈场景减少总误差。结论：AI监督价值取决于角色分配、校准、升级策略、流量构成与生命周期控制，而非第二模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注的核心问题是：何时引入第二个AI组件能真正改善主网络欺诈决策，而非仅增加运维负担。传统观点常假定增加模型即提升性能，但作者希望澄清第二模型的价值条件，并检验监督机制在欺诈检测中的实际增益与代价。

Method: 构建角色感知的Decider-Supervisor（DS）框架，并引入区块链审计能力。评估四种方向性配置，组合集中式机器学习、FedAvg训练的联邦元模型，以及Base或QLoRA大语言模型变体。比较主决策与监督决策，指标涵盖非硬欺诈性能、干预负担、条件校准、流量混合与审查容量敏感性、可靠性测试，以及区块链生命周期控制；并通过36种重加权流量混合进行鲁棒性分析。

Result: 确定性硬门控解决89.994%的欺诈请求，非硬群体构成主要AI决策场景。条件校验校准在部署回放中未产生一致可迁移的监督优势。DS-3 QLoRA为扰动最小的监督配置，但在F1与总误差上仍劣于其FedAvg主阶段。36种重加权流量中，仅DS-4 Base在两种极端高欺诈场景下通过监督降低总误差。区块链测试支持摘要验证、篡改检测、授权、单次审查解析及最终化后完整性，同时暴露最终化前的单写限制。

Conclusion: AI监督的价值取决于角色分配、校准、升级策略、流量构成与生命周期控制，而非仅由第二模型的存在决定。第二AI组件并非普遍增益，其有效性高度依赖场景与机制设计。

Abstract: When does a second artificial intelligence (AI) component improve a primary network-fraud decision rather than add operational burden? We study this question through a role-aware Decider-Supervisor (DS) framework with blockchain auditability, evaluating four directional configurations that combine centralised machine learning, a Federated Averaging (FedAvg)-trained federated meta-model, and Base or Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation (QLoRA) large language model variants. The analysis compares primary-only and supervised decisions using non-hard fraud performance, intervention burden, conditional calibration, traffic-mix and Review-capacity sensitivity, dependability tests, and blockchain lifecycle controls. The deterministic hard gate resolves 89.994% of fraudulent requests, leaving the non-hard population as the main AI decision setting. Conditional validation calibration does not produce a consistently transferable supervisory advantage on deployment replay. DS-3 QLoRA is the least disruptive supervised configuration, but it still underperforms its primary FedAvg stage in F1 and total errors. Across 36 reweighted traffic mixtures, supervision reduces total errors only for DS-4 Base in two extreme high-fraud scenarios. Blockchain tests support digest verification, tamper detection, authorisation, single-use review resolution, and post-finalisation integrity, while exposing a pre-finalisation single-write limitation. The results show that the value of AI supervision depends on role assignment, calibration, escalation policy, traffic composition, and lifecycle controls rather than on the presence of a second model alone.

</details>


### [279] [Auditable Claims about AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07459)
*Yue Zhao,Jiate Li,Li Li,Yi Nian,Jinbo Liu,Xiaolin Zhou,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出判定AI代理声明"可审计"的框架：声明须先明确策略、范围、可裁定记录及记录生成方，并在裁决前固定决策规则且记录可获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织常对AI代理作各类声明（如每次外发邮件均有人审批、所有动作均被记录、评估显示代理可安全部署），但EU AI Act第12条仅要求高风险系统支持事件自动记录，未规定哪些记录能裁定某一声明。声明与证据之间缺乏可核验的对应关系。

Method: 借鉴鉴证业务（assurance engagement）的前提条件，将声明定义为可审计需满足：策略、范围、裁定记录、记录作者等要素及决策规则须在任何裁决前固定，且记录可获取。此方法把Auditable Agents框架中的Policy Checkability维度从单一动作扩展到声明层面，并针对代理增加三项条件：独立记录覆盖、授权绑定至每个动作的参数、超越完整性的完备性。在显式模型下证明：若假设成立，缺失任一条件则支持不可实现。以声明—核查表将方法应用于六类常见声明（以NIST、IETF、OWASP现行草案为依据），并通过一个贯穿五种证据状态的案例进行演示。

Result: 给出针对六类常见声明的声明—核查表；在显式模型下证明三项代理条件的必要性；通过五种证据状态的实例展示应用过程；并给出面向运营方、采购方、审计方与标准制定者的实践清单与步骤。

Conclusion: AI代理声明的可审计性取决于事前固定的策略、范围、记录、作者与决策规则，以及独立覆盖、参数级授权绑定和完备性条件；该框架为运营、采购、审计与标准化提供可操作路径。

Abstract: Organizations make claims about their AI agents: a person approves every external email, every action is logged, an evaluation shows the agent is safe to deploy. Article 12 of the EU AI Act requires high-risk systems to allow the automatic recording of events but does not say which records settle a given claim. The position is one sentence: to be checked, a claim about an agent must first name its policy, its scope, the records that would settle it, and who writes them. Adapting the preconditions of an assurance engagement, we call a claim auditable when these elements and a decision rule are fixed before any verdict and the records are obtainable. This extends the Policy Checkability dimension of our Auditable Agents framework from single actions to claims. Agents add three conditions: coverage by an independent record, authorization bound to each action's arguments, and completeness beyond integrity. Under an explicit model, we prove that support is impossible without each wherever its hypotheses hold. A claim-check table applies the method to six common claims, anchored in current NIST, IETF, and OWASP drafts. A worked case follows one claim through five evidence states. We close with a practice box and steps for operators, buyers, auditors, and standard setters.

</details>


### [280] [COMPASS: Finding Where Reasoning Lives in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07469)
*Pratyay Dutta,Kowshik Thopalli,Vivek Narayanaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPASS，一种推理时激活引导方法：利用模型直接作答正确与否作为信号，提取正确性方向，并通过logit空间归因分数定位少数可干预的注意力头，引导其激活以激发推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激发LLM推理的方法依赖预定义的推理刻画（如CoT提示设计、对比CoT方向或SAE推理特征），成本高且受限于先验假设。作者希望找到更简单的信号来激发推理。

Method: 以模型自身直接作答的正确性作为信号，构造一个可激发推理的潜在"正确性方向"；发现该方向在多数注意力头激活中可解码，但仅少数头可有效干预。COMPASS用logit空间归因分数识别这些头，并沿正确性方向对其激活进行引导，仅需每头的激活统计量。

Result: 在三个模型家族和多个数学基准上，COMPASS优于对比的激活引导基线，GSM8K准确率平均提升16个百分点，并以少20-70%的生成token接近CoT准确率。干预无需重新拟合即可迁移到未见基准。消融显示正确性方向与承载它的少数头均为必要，效果集中在极少数头上。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对于有可验证答案的数学推理，仅用直接作答正确性这一简单信号即可有效激发推理；关键在于少数可干预的注意力头。

Abstract: Explicitly eliciting reasoning substantially improves LLM performance. Existing approaches require a predefined characterization of reasoning, whether through CoT prompt design, contrastive CoT directions, or via SAE derived reasoning features. For mathematical reasoning with verifiable answers, we show that a much simpler signal suffices, which is the correctness of the model's own direct answer attempts. This signal yields a latent direction that elicits reasoning. This direction is decodable within the activations of most attention heads, but only a small subset of them can be effectively intervened. We introduce COMPASS, an inference-time steering method that identifies these heads using a logit-space attribution score and steers their activations along the correctness direction, requiring only per-head activation statistics. Across three model families and multiple math benchmarks, COMPASS outperforms the activation-steering baselines we compare against, improves GSM8K accuracy by 16 percentage points on average, and approaches CoT accuracy with 20-70\% fewer generated tokens. Interventions transfer without re-fitting to unseen benchmarks, and ablations show that both the correctness direction and the small set of heads carrying it are necessary, with the effect concentrated in remarkably few heads.

</details>


### [281] [PsyCIDRA: A Dual-Agent Framework for Psychiatric Interviewing and Diagnostic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07473)
*Milad Mohammadi,Fatemeh Akrami Shamsabadi,Zahra Mohseni,Amirhossein Safdarian,Malekfarhad Malek,Hadi Moradi,Hesham Faili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PsyCIDRA：双智能体框架将自由形式精神科访谈与诊断推理结合，在模拟与人类盲法研究中均优于直接提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床推理方面展现潜力，但精神科访谈需要引导一个不断演化的对话，其交互式评估能力研究不足，且需同时考察诊断推理、访谈质量与安全性。

Method: 提出 PsyCIDRA 双智能体框架：访谈智能体借助工具维护工作笔记、加载专家编写的技能、检索 ICD-11 参考以引导问诊；诊断推理智能体接收完整访谈转录，报告假设及支持、冲突与缺失证据，证据不足时不下最终结论。使用 PsyCPG 生成的患者档案进行模拟评估，并开展 101 名被试的盲法分臂人类研究。

Result: 模拟中 4 个模型在 53 个评估案例上诊断一致性高于直接提示；81 个留出模拟案例 rank-1 准确率 60.5% 对 51.9%；盲法研究中与心理学家在是否提出诊断假设上一致率 79.6%，直接提示为 65.4%。

Conclusion: 结果支持 LLM 智能体通过自由对话辅助精神科评估的潜力；通过联合考察诊断推理、访谈质量与安全性，增进了对精神科访谈智能体能力与局限的理解。

Abstract: Large language models show promise in clinical reasoning, but psychiatric interviewing requires guiding an evolving conversation. Their ability to carry out this interactive assessment remains less studied. We present PsyCIDRA, a dual-agent framework linking free-form psychiatric interviewing with diagnostic reasoning for expert review. Its interviewer agent uses tools to maintain working notes, load expert-written skills, and retrieve ICD-11 references to guide inquiry. Its diagnostic reasoning agent then receives the completed interview transcript and reports hypotheses alongside supporting, conflicting, and missing evidence, withholding a final hypothesis when none is sufficiently supported. Using patient profiles generated with PsyCPG, we first evaluate PsyCIDRA in simulation. Across four models on 53 evaluation cases, it achieves higher diagnostic agreement than direct prompting. On 81 held-out simulated cases, rank-1 accuracy is 60.5% versus 51.9%. In a blinded study of 101 human participants in separate arms, PsyCIDRA agrees with psychologists on whether to propose a diagnostic hypothesis in 79.6% of cases, compared with 65.4% for direct prompting. Together, these findings support the potential of LLM agents to assist psychiatric assessment through free-form dialogue. By examining diagnostic reasoning, interview quality, and safety together, this study contributes to understanding the capabilities and limitations of psychiatric interview agents.

</details>


### [282] [In With the Old: Enhancing 'Classical' Document Automation with Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07480)
*Marc Lauritsen,Hannes Westermann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨基于专家系统等符号方法的法律文档自动化服务与当前生成式AI如何相互增强，并结合初步实验分析其益处与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件法律辅助系统已采用多种知识表示与推理形式；作者希望厘清传统符号式文档自动化与新兴生成式AI之间的关系，探索二者互补融合的可能性。

Method: 概念性讨论与初步实验相结合：一方面分析符号式文档自动化与生成式AI相互增强的益处与挑战，另一方面利用大语言模型识别并修正非专业人士撰写的法律文本中的问题。

Result: 报告了使用大语言模型识别和修正外行文本问题的初步实验结果，并归纳了符号方法与生成式AI结合的可能收益与挑战。

Conclusion: 基于专家系统风格及其他符号方法的文档自动化服务，能够与生成式AI相互促进；初步实验表明LLM在辅助外行法律文本纠错方面具有潜力，但相关益处与挑战仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Software-based legal assistance systems have leveraged many different forms of knowledge representation and reasoning. This article explores how document automation services rooted in expert system style and other symbolic approaches can usefully enhance and be enhanced by current generative AI approaches. We discuss the possible benefits and challenges, and report on preliminary experiments in using large language models to identify and fix issues in texts written by laypeople.

</details>


### [283] [Does Muon Need Fine-Grained Spectral Shaping?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07497)
*Meher Chaitanya,Tianyi Zhou,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究Muon优化器的谱更新是否需要精细谱细节，发现大部分奇异模式低于噪声边缘，提出两带谱重加权框架BulkBoost，仅用单一bulk-to-spike增益即可媲美精细谱方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon的理想化极更新给每个奇异方向相同权重（平坦谱），而近期优化器用精细谱映射为每个方向分配独立增益。作者追问：这种谱细节对Muon更新究竟有多必要。

Method: 谱诊断显示约94–97%的奇异模式低于估计的噪声边缘，但整体与参考梯度正相关。提出BulkBoost两带谱重加权框架，含固定秩与噪声校准两种变体：后者用分裂小批量梯度差为Muon的Nesterov输入校准Marchenko–Pastur参考边缘，将边缘以下的bulk与以上的spike分离；两变体通过共享增益提升bulk相对权重，同时保持各矩阵未重加权方向的Frobenius范数。理论给出将权重移向bulk可降低损失的一阶条件，并量化两带能捕获的最大一阶改进率比例。

Result: 在Pythia-14M至410M、六个语料的30个持续预训练设置中，两带重加权与Freon的精细幂律谱竞争并优于Spectra。相对Muon平坦谱，Freon平均降低最终损失0.022%，而两带变体降低0.073–0.147%。

Conclusion: 对平坦谱的有效偏离是低维的：单一bulk-to-spike增益即可获得至少与精细谱Profile相当的收益，说明精细谱细节对Muon更新并非必需。

Abstract: Muon combines current and past gradients into matrix momentum. For $M=UΣV^\top$, the idealized polar update $Q=UV^\top$ gives every singular direction the same weight. We refer to this as the flat profile. Several recent optimizers replace this flat profile with fine-grained spectral maps that give each direction its own gain. We ask how much of this spectral detail a Muon update needs. Our spectral diagnostics show that approximately $94$--$97\%$ of measured singular modes lie below an estimated noise edge, yet collectively align positively with a reference gradient.
  We introduce BulkBoost, a two-band spectral reweighting framework with fixed-rank and noise-calibrated variants. The latter uses split-minibatch gradient differences to calibrate a Marchenko--Pastur reference edge for Muon's Nesterov input, separating the bulk below the edge from the spikes above it. Both variants increase the bulk's relative weight through one shared gain while preserving the Frobenius norm of each matrix's unreweighted direction. For a fixed partition, our theory gives the first-order condition under which moving weight toward the bulk lowers the loss. It also quantifies the fraction of the maximal first-order improvement rate, over all per-mode reallocations, that two bands can capture. Across 30 continued-pretraining settings spanning Pythia-14M to 410M and six corpora, two-band reweighting is competitive with the fine-grained power-law profile of Freon and outperforms Spectra. Measured against Muon's flat profile, Freon reduces final loss by $0.022\%$ of the pre-adaptation loss on average, whereas the two-band variants achieve reductions of $0.073$--$0.147\%$. These observations suggest that useful departures from the flat profile are surprisingly low-dimensional: a single bulk-to-spike gain captures at least as much benefit as the fine-grained spectral profiles.

</details>


### [284] [MARS: Multi-resolution Adaptive Routing for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07505)
*Ming Yin,Sixun Dong,Yudong Liu,Wen-Yun Yang,Yunjiang Jiang,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了长历史推荐中缓存记忆的"时间混叠"失效模式，提出多分辨率用户记忆MARS，通过不同半衰期的循环状态轨道与稀疏路由读取器，在保持固定规模候选打分的同时提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长历史推荐器通常将用户历史压缩为紧凑、与候选无关的缓存记忆以复用打分。作者发现真实用户历史具有多尺度语义结构（短期意图、中期兴趣、长期偏好共存），而单一的整体缓存记忆对这些尺度保留不均：线性探针在恢复近期和中程内容时远差于长程内容。作者将此失效模式称为"时间混叠"，并希望解决该问题。

Method: 提出MARS：一种多分辨率用户记忆机制。它将完整历史写入锚定于不同半衰期的循环状态轨道，并使用稀疏路由读取器，通过为每个种子选择相关的时间分辨率来物化紧凑的种子记忆，从而保持固定规模的候选打分。作者还进行了组件匹配的消融实验与配对检验，比较硬窗口记忆与增加容量等替代方案。

Result: MARS在三个公开数据集上优于强基线，且增益随历史长度增长。消融实验表明，时间多样性与选择性路由各自的贡献超出硬窗口记忆或单纯增加容量所能提供的效果。在用户内行为变化之后，MARS相对其接口匹配基线的优势进一步扩大，且仅约为该基线热缓存服务延迟的1.02倍（每用户1000个候选）。

Conclusion: MARS通过多分辨率记忆与选择性路由，有效缓解时间混叠问题，在提升长历史推荐性能的同时保持接近基线的服务延迟，验证了多尺度记忆结构的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: Long-history recommenders often compress each user's history into a compact, candidate-independent memory that is cached and reused to score large candidate pools. We show that real user histories exhibit multi-scale semantic structure, with short-lived intent, medium-term interests, and long-term preferences coexisting in one sequence, and that monolithic cached memories preserve these scales unevenly: linear probes recover recent and mid-range content far worse than long-range content. We call this failure mode \textit{temporal aliasing}. We propose \textbf{MARS}, a multi-resolution user memory that writes the full history into recurrent state tracks anchored to different half-lives, and a sparse routing reader that materializes compact seed memories by selecting the relevant temporal resolutions for each seed, preserving fixed-size candidate scoring. MARS outperforms strong baselines on three public datasets, with gains that grow with history length. Component-matched ablations with paired tests show that temporal diversity and selective routing each contribute beyond what hard-window memories or added capacity provide. The advantage of MARS over its interface-matched baseline also widens after within-user behavioral shifts, at about $1.02\times$ that baseline's warm-cache serving latency for $1{,}000$ candidates per user.

</details>


### [285] [On Open-Ended Information Seeking for Information Elicitation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07509)
*Victor De Lima,Grace Hui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究11个LLM在开放式信息引出任务中对"信息价值"的判断差异，并通过受控仿真刻画这些判断如何塑造序贯信息搜寻的广度-深度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体式信息引出中，模型需在新信息不断涌现时持续决定该追求哪些信息，这类决策可能被委托给基础模型，但模型选择如何影响最终的信息搜寻行为尚缺乏研究。因此，作者希望厘清不同LLM对信息价值的判断差异，以及这些差异如何影响序贯式信息引出。

Method: 1) 在共享的信息集合与引出目标下，比较11个LLM（跨多个模型家族与参数规模）对信息价值的判断；2) 构建受控的信息引出仿真，让不同模型面对相同信息空间并使用相同选择规则，从而将这些价值判断与问题生成、被访者行为隔离开来；3) 基于此刻画模型特定信息搜寻偏好所导致的广度-深度行为；4) 进一步考察交互历史如何改变对候选信息的评估与后续选择；5) 通过敏感性分析与消融实验检验结论的稳健性与边界，涉及：引出者可获得的机会、用于操作化信息搜寻偏好的响应标签、交互历史的有无、以及冗余是否被显式纳入评估。

Result: 作者刻画了由模型特定信息搜寻偏好所涌现的广度-深度行为模式，发现交互历史会改变候选信息的评估及后续选择，并通过敏感性分析与消融实验界定了这些发现的适用边界与稳健性。项目代码、数据与轨迹文件已开源（https://github.com/infosenselab/open-elicitation）。

Conclusion: LLM对信息价值的判断存在跨模型差异，并会在受控条件下系统性地塑造序贯信息引出的广度-深度权衡；结论受交互历史、响应标签、可用机会及冗余相关性等因素影响，需在这些边界内理解。

Abstract: Information elicitation is an open-ended information-seeking problem in which an interaction can unfold in many potentially valuable directions, requiring an elicitor to continually determine which information to pursue as new information emerges. In agentic elicitation, these decisions may be delegated to a foundation model, yet how model choice shapes the resulting information-seeking behavior remains understudied. We study how judgments about information value vary across LLMs and how these differences shape sequential information seeking. We first examine these judgments across 11 LLMs spanning multiple model families and parameter scales, using a shared set of information and elicitation objectives. We then develop a controlled elicitation simulation in which different models encounter the same information space and use the same selection rule, isolating these judgments from question generation and respondent behavior. Using this setting, we characterize the breadth-depth behavior that emerges from model-specific information-seeking preferences over the course of elicitation. We further examine how interaction history changes the evaluation and subsequent selection of prospective information. We test the robustness and boundaries of these findings through sensitivity analyses and ablations over the opportunities available to the elicitor, the response labels used to operationalize information-seeking preferences, the presence of interaction history, and whether redundancy is explicitly relevant to the assessment. The project code, data, and trajectory files are available at https://github.com/infosenselab/open-elicitation.

</details>


### [286] [From Local Evidence to Safety Verdicts: Causal Tracing in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07514)
*Faezeh Dehghan Tarzjani,Mevan Wijewardena,Alexander Romanus,Sampad Mohanty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出SSU-Bench数据集，通过配对的安全/不安全图文组合及内部状态迁移实验，揭示视觉语言模型中联合安全判断的可干预层级转变规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型需结合图像与提示才能识别单独任一方都无法揭示的安全风险，但此类联合安全判断在模型内部何处形成尚不明确。论文旨在定位该判断的可及位置，并区分"可读取的模型决策"与"正确的安全判断"。

Method: 构建SSU-Bench数据集：通过单项提示编辑或带标注目标区域的图像编辑，生成配对的安全与不安全图文组合。使用三个视觉语言模型，在配对输入间迁移内部状态，测量安全判定结果的变化；并采用线性读出与跨模型比较分析。

Result: 跨模型与两类反事实编辑中，对"被改变输入位置"的干预在解码器早期层有效，而对"末位输入token"的干预在较晚层才有效；由其他样本估计的方向产生类似的晚期层效应。末位token状态的线性读出可预测模型自身判定（包括错误判断），跨模型比较显示反事实变化模式具有相似性。

Conclusion: 研究识别出联合安全判定中"干预可影响位置"的重复性层间转变，并将可读的模型决策与正确的安全判断区分开来。

Abstract: A vision-language model may need to combine an image with a prompt to recognize a safety risk that neither reveals alone. Where does this joint safety judgment become accessible inside the model? We introduce SSU-Bench, a dataset of matched safe and unsafe image-text combinations constructed using single-item prompt edits or image edits with annotated intended regions. Using three vision-language models, we transfer internal states between paired inputs and measure the resulting change in the safety verdict. Across models and both types of counterfactual, interventions at the changed input positions are effective in earlier decoder layers, while interventions at the final input token become effective later. Directions estimated from other examples produce similar late-layer effects. A linear readout of the final-token state also predicts the model's own verdict, including incorrect judgments, and cross-model comparisons reveal similarities in the patterns of counterfactual change. These findings identify a recurring transition in where interventions can influence a joint safety verdict and distinguish a readable model decision from a correct safety judgment.

</details>


### [287] [Grounding What Shapes the Plan: Rethinking Groundedness for Physical Intelligence in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07521)
*Minkyoung Cho,Zewei Zhou,Wenhao Ding,Shuhan Tan,Boyi Li,Yuxiao Chen,Yan Wang,Zheng Lian,Min-Hung Chen,Chaowei Xiao,Zhuoqing Mao,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Yulong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GroundAct框架，以物理实体为" grounding单元"，通过轻量引用token将实体连续状态接入符号推理，使推理真正作用于规划输出，在开环与闭环驾驶评测中均取得稳健表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶模型虽将因果、空间、感知与预测未来纳入推理，提升了推理与场景的对应性，但未回答一个根本问题：当模型最终输出的是"动作"时，groundedness应意味着什么。推理正确接地并不必然带来理想的驾驶结果，即"推理对"与"动作对"之间存在断裂。

Method: GroundAct以物理实体及其交互为建模出发点：以实体作为grounding的基本单元；为每个被选实体赋予轻量reference token，使其连续状态在符号推理过程中保持可寻址；仅让被引用实体与不断演化的候选规划之间的交互来修正规划，从而建立从"推理依据什么"到"规划做什么"的显式通路，作者称之为grounded planning。

Result: 在开环与闭环两类设置下评测，GroundAct在常规、分布外（OOD）及安全关键场景中均展现出较强的开环规划能力；闭环结果进一步在仿真驾驶中将该证据延伸，验证其实际价值。

Conclusion: 该工作重新定义了驾驶推理中groundedness在动作输出层面的含义，证明以实体为单元、以reference token承载连续状态、以实体交互修正规划的路径，能有效弥合推理与规划之间的差距，提升多场景下的规划性能。

Abstract: Driving models increasingly ground reasoning in causal relations, spatial structure, perceptual evidence, and predicted futures. These advances make reasoning more faithful to the driving scene, but leave a fundamental question unresolved: what should groundedness mean when the model ultimately outputs an action? Correctly grounded reasoning does not, by itself, ensure desirable driving outcomes. We introduce GroundAct, which starts from a simple premise: driving unfolds through physical entities and their interactions. Entities therefore become the unit of grounding; a lightweight reference token keeps each selected entity's continuous state addressable through symbolic reasoning; and only the referenced entities' interactions with the evolving proposal correct the plan. The result is an explicit path from what reasoning grounds to what the plan does, which we call grounded planning. To assess its practical value, we evaluate GroundAct in both open- and closed-loop settings. GroundAct shows strong open-loop planning across normal, out-of-distribution, and safety-critical scenarios, with closed-loop results extending this evidence to driving in simulation.

</details>


### [288] [A Systematic Investigation of Bias in Large Language Models for Advertising Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07544)
*Weiwei Wang,Yinchuan Xu,Jialu Gao,Youkow Homma,Jian Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了LLM广告相关性判断中的公平性问题，通过反事实框架检验广告主身份、受欢迎程度、输入语言与人口统计措辞对判断的影响，并探讨了推理与训练阶段的缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多用于判断广告与查询的匹配度，但其判断的公平性受到的关注有限；广告实践者需要识别公平性风险并找到合适的缓解方式。

Method: 构建反事实框架，考察广告主身份、可能受欢迎程度、输入语言和人口统计措辞的影响；以GPT-4o作为分类式相关性评判者和专门为相关性预测训练的Qwen-7B模型为对象；广告主与语言实验使用真实广告日志中采样的查询-广告对；使用受控合成查询研究就业、住房和信贷领域的人口统计关联；并进一步研究模型推理与训练阶段的缓解措施。

Result: 两个模型中，改变广告主身份或输入语言都会改变相关性评估；部分人口统计比较显示出与常见刻板印象一致的模式，尤其涉及性别与职业。缓解措施的有效性取决于广告主信息是否与查询相关，以及训练数据中广告主标签的分布。

Conclusion: LLM广告相关性判断存在公平性风险，缓解方法效果依条件而异；这些发现可帮助广告从业者识别公平性风险并为LLM相关性系统开发合适的缓解方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to judge how well an advertisement matches a query, but the fairness of these judgments has received limited attention. We conduct a systematic study of fairness in relevance judgments made by LLMs for queries and advertisements. Our counterfactual framework examines the effects of advertiser identity and possible popularity, input language, and demographic wording. We study GPT-4o as a categorical relevance judge and a Qwen-7B model trained specifically for relevance prediction. The advertiser and language experiments use query and advertisement pairs sampled from real advertising logs. Controlled synthetic queries are used to study demographic associations in employment, housing, and credit. For both models, changing the advertiser identity or input language can alter the relevance assessment. Selected demographic comparisons also show patterns consistent with common stereotypes, particularly those involving gender and occupation. We further study mitigation during model inference and training. The results indicate that its effectiveness depends on whether advertiser information is relevant to the query and how advertiser labels are distributed in the training data. These findings can help advertising practitioners identify fairness risks and develop suitable mitigation methods for LLM relevance systems.

</details>


### [289] [Decoupled Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07556)
*Xinle Wu,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeOrch 将多智能体编排解耦为"与工人无关的任务规划"和"具体工人选择"两阶段，实现条件信用分配、支持新工人免重训接入，在多项任务上性能优于既有自动编排方法且调用更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式编排方法存在两个缺陷：一是规划与固定工人池耦合，导致迁移能力受限；二是分解决策与协作决策共用同一终端结果进行训练，信用分配模糊、难以区分贡献。

Method: 提出 DeOrch 两阶段规划器：第一阶段在无工人信息下完成任务分解，第二阶段依据来自工人池的紧凑、与工人身份无关的"可匹配性"反馈选择协作操作，从而对分解与协作决策实施条件信用分配；另设轻量匹配器，通过固定探测集上的行为表现估计工人适配度，并用上下文老虎机在线自适应，使新工人无需重训规划器或匹配器即可接入。

Result: 在多种分布内与分布外任务上，DeOrch 性能超过先前自动多智能体系统编排方法，且工人调用次数少于同类的学习式编排器；在迁移到完全未见过的工人池时无需重训仍保持有效；消融显示两个组件均带来稳定增益。

Conclusion: 将规划与工人选择解耦是提升多智能体编排可迁移性、信用分配清晰度和工人池扩展性的有效途径，DeOrch 为此提供了可行方案与实证支持。

Abstract: Learned orchestration can automatically construct effective language-model multi-agent systems, but existing approaches couple planning to fixed worker pools and train decomposition and collaboration from the same terminal outcome, limiting transfer and obscuring credit assignment. We introduce DeOrch, which separates worker-agnostic planning from concrete worker selection. Its two-stage planner first decomposes the task without worker information, then chooses collaboration operations using compact, worker-identity-free matchability feedback from the pool, enabling conditional credit assignment to decomposition and collaboration decisions. A lightweight matcher estimates worker suitability from behavior on a fixed probe set and adapts online with a contextual bandit, allowing new workers to be incorporated without retraining the planner or matcher. Across diverse in- and out-of-distribution tasks, DeOrch outperforms prior automatic MAS orchestration methods with fewer worker calls than competing learned orchestrators, remains effective when transferred to an entirely unseen worker pool without retraining, and shows consistent gains from both components.

</details>


### [290] [Navigating Route Latent Space for Synthesizable Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07560)
*Tao Li,Tuan Vinh,Monika Raj,Yuan Fang,Zhichun Guo,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RouteFlow 将可合成分子设计重构为连续合成路径隐空间搜索，用奖励引导流匹配采样并引入循环一致性稳定微调，在 16 个 TDC 任务上取得最佳样本效率与可合成性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可合成性感知方法存在缺陷：要么把生成分子投影回可合成类似物，偏离目标；要么直接在离散合成空间中优化，缺乏连续景观、搜索效率低。作者认为瓶颈在于搜索空间而非优化器。

Method: 提出 RouteFlow：将可合成分子设计重构为连续合成路径隐空间上的搜索，每个隐向量映射回完整合成路线，从本质保证可合成性；采用奖励引导的流匹配作为高效采样器导向高性质区域；由于奖励优化可能使隐向量偏离真实合成路线流形、解码不可靠，引入循环一致性机制稳定微调。

Result: 在 Therapeutic Data Commons 的 16 个优化任务上，RouteFlow 在可合成性感知基线中取得最佳样本效率，同时具有最佳合成可及性和最高逆合成成功率。

Conclusion: 循环一致性机制能可靠地使优化保持在流形上并提升目标性质，支持有效的可合成分子发现，验证了以连续合成路径隐空间作为搜索空间的合理性。

Abstract: Goal-directed molecular design has advanced rapidly, yet a substantial proportion of designed molecules remain difficult to synthesize in practice, limiting their real-world utility. Prior synthesizability-aware methods either project generated molecules back to synthesizable analogs that deviate from the intended target, or optimize directly in discrete synthesis spaces that lack a continuous landscape for efficient search. We argue that this limitation mainly comes from the search space rather than the optimizer. To address this, we propose RouteFlow, a framework that reformulates synthesizable molecular design as a search over a continuous route latent space, where each latent maps back to a complete synthesis route and synthesizability is inherently preserved. To navigate this space, we adopt reward-guided flow matching as an efficient sampler that steers toward high-property regions. Since reward optimization may push latents off the manifold of real synthesis routes, where decoding becomes unreliable, we further introduce a cycle-consistency mechanism to stabilize fine-tuning. Across 16 optimization tasks from Therapeutic Data Commons, RouteFlow achieves the best sample efficiency among synthesizability-aware baselines, with the best synthetic accessibility and the highest retrosynthesis success rate. Our results also confirm that the proposed cycle-consistency reliably keeps optimization on-manifold while improving target properties, supporting effective synthesizable molecular discovery.

</details>


### [291] [Unanimously Wrong: Certified Abstention from How Medical LLM Consensus Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07570)
*Xiaoyang Wang,Tianrui Wang,Christopher C. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProbeGuard 是一个基于共识形成过程的认证弃权框架，通过过程特征、理由语义熵和主动探针来识别一致性投票中的错误，并用分层 Learn-then-Test 校准给出选择性风险的无分布界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床与智能体医疗问答系统中，一致性常被当作可靠性的证据。但一致性是正确性的脆弱代理：系统可能一致地出错，此时基于一致性的信号不含任何信息，因为它们只读取共识的最终状态而忽略了共识达成的方式。

Method: ProbeGuard 依据共识如何形成来决定弃权。过程特征追踪一致性轨迹、少数意见持续性和检索饱和；对于一致投票，理由语义熵检验投票背后理由是否连贯，主动探针检索反证并测量共识是否仍能维持；随后用分层 Learn-then-Test 校准将分数转化为选择性风险的无分布界。

Result: 在三个医学问答基准及一个困难前沿参考上，使用已发布的多轮智能体 RAG 底座，与六个弃权基线比较。MedQA 上 13.4% 的一致投票是错误的，且无任何基于一致性的信号能标记它们；过程信号将正确与错误共识的区分从随机水平提升到 0.696 AUROC；认证规则在一阶层问题上以 9.0% 的观测选择性风险回答了十分之六，当域内校准数据积累后提升至十分之九。

Conclusion: 基于共识形成过程而非仅最终一致状态的信号，能够有效识别一致但错误的答案，并在医学问答中实现有认证保证的选择性预测。

Abstract: In clinical practice, agreement among independent experts is treated as evidence of reliability, and multi-round consensus has become a core mechanism of agentic medical question-answering systems. When such a system must decide whether to trust its own answer, the prevailing signal is again agreement, now among the sampled answers. But agreement is a fragile proxy for correctness. A system can be unanimously wrong, returning the same incorrect answer on every sample, and on these questions agreement-based signals carry no information. The cause is that these signals read only the final state of the consensus and discard how it was reached. Agreement that was reached by resolving disagreement with evidence looks identical, at the end, to agreement that was present from the first sample because every sample shares one misconception. ProbeGuard is a certified abstention framework that bases the abstention decision on how the consensus formed. Process features trace agreement trajectories, minority persistence, and retrieval saturation. For unanimous votes, rationale semantic entropy checks whether the reasons behind the vote cohere, and an active probe retrieves counter-evidence and measures whether the consensus survives. A stratified Learn-then-Test calibration then converts these scores into a distribution-free bound on selective risk. We evaluate ProbeGuard on three medical QA benchmarks and a hard-frontier reference, with a published multi-round agentic RAG substrate, against six abstention baselines. On MedQA, 13.4% of unanimous votes are wrong, and no agreement-based signal can flag them. Process signals raise the discrimination of correct from incorrect consensus from chance to 0.696 AUROC. The certified rule answers six in ten unanimous-layer questions at an observed selective risk of 9.0%, and nine in ten once in-domain calibration data accumulate.

</details>


### [292] [Cooperating with Future Collaborators: Multi-Agent RL under Staggered Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07578)
*Jianglin Qiao,Siyi Hu,Thien Hoang Nguyen,Zehong Cao,Salah Sukkarieh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究协同多智能体强化学习中的"交错参与"(SP)设定：部分智能体更早行动并留下对后续智能体有用的信息，提出训练期增强方法SPL，通过前瞻性获取监督与结果监督的接收者学习分别解决"识别有用信息"和"后续智能体如何使用信息"两个问题，在60组对比中平均提升14.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同MARL通常假设智能体并发参与，但许多任务中智能体先后加入，早期智能体的行动会通过其留下的信息及后续策略的使用方式影响回报，形成跨时间、跨智能体的学习依赖，导致学习困难。

Method: 提出Staggered Participation Learning (SPL)，一种训练期增强方法，包含两部分：对早期智能体施加前瞻性获取(prospective acquisition)监督，促使其留下对未来有用的信息；对后期智能体进行结果监督的接收者学习(outcome-supervised receiver learning)，教会其有效利用这些信息。

Result: 在多种基于策略的MARL骨干、环境与交错参与模式下评估，60组MPE/RWAre骨干设定对比中每一组任务完成均值都更高，平均提升14.1%；增益也扩展到八智能体团队及Isaac Lab中的物理UAV-UGV环境。

Conclusion: SPL有效应对交错参与带来的跨时间、跨智能体学习依赖，在算法、时间与具身等多个层面均具泛化性与稳健性。

Abstract: In cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), agents are often trained under concurrent participation, while in many tasks some agents act earlier and leave task-relevant information that becomes useful to agents participating later. We study this setting as staggered participation (SP), which introduces a cross-time, cross-agent learning dependency because an early action may affect the return through the information it provides and the later policy that uses it. Learning under SP therefore requires both identifying what information is useful for future decisions and learning how later agents should use it. We propose Staggered Participation Learning (SPL), a training-time augmentation that addresses these two parts with prospective acquisition supervision for earlier agents and outcome-supervised receiver learning for later agents. We evaluate SPL across multiple policy-based MARL backbones, environments, and staggered-participation patterns. Across 60 MPE/RWARE backbone setting comparisons, SPL achieves higher observed mean task completion in every case, with an average difference of 14.1%. The gains also extend to eight-agent teams and a physics-based UAV-UGV environment in Isaac Lab, providing evidence across algorithmic, temporal, and embodied settings.

</details>


### [293] [LOGIC: An LLM Benchmark for Intent-Grounded Change Impact in Aerospace Electrical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07580)
*Muhammad Faraz Shoaib,Muhammad Qasim,Raisulhaq Mohammed Rizwan,Rahmatullah Safdar,Muzammil Adnan Shaik,Abdul Aleem Mohammed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出LOGIC基准与评估框架，将航空电气设计变更请求的候选选择与传播分离，用本地可部署语言模型结合确定性候选清单与类型化追溯图，实证区分选择错误与传播错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计修订请求常只授权部分检测到的变更，直接传播所有差异会产生过宽的影响报告；同时现有评测难以区分"候选选择错误"与"下游传播错误"，因此需要一个可控基准来解耦这两类误差。

Method: 构建LOGIC：168个场景（144选择、24弃权）；先由本地语言模型将请求锚定到确定性的候选变更清单，再沿类型化电气追溯图传播选中的变更。对比三类基线（意图不可知、词法、结构化证据）与oracle根上界，评估三个7–8B模型。

Result: 96个显式锚定选择案例上，仅门控结构化证据F1=1.0000，token词法0.9677；12个关系改写案例上，token词法0.1772、门控0.0000、大模型0.5000–0.6400。候选数从4增至64时模型锚定性能下降，而受影响元素与类型化路径准确率在约1K–100K节点图上较稳定。严格证据门控抑制假阳性但会删除正确的语义选择；探索性空证据弃权策略将严格弃权准确率提升至0.6667、不安全报告率降至0.1667，但可回答覆盖下降16.0–27.1个百分点。六个冲突请求中仍有四个对每个模型不安全。

Conclusion: 应结合字面证据与语言模型推理，并在意图无法可靠确立时引入工程人工审查，以平衡覆盖率与安全性。

Abstract: Aerospace electrical-design revisions can contain multiple genuine changes, although an engineering request may authorize only a subset. Propagating every detected difference can therefore produce overly broad impact reports. We present LOGIC, a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework in which locally deployable language models ground a request in a deterministic candidate-change inventory before selected changes are propagated through a typed electrical traceability graph. This separation permits candidate-selection errors to be distinguished from downstream propagation errors. LOGIC contains 168 scenarios, including 144 selection and 24 abstention cases. We evaluate three 7--8B models against intent-agnostic, lexical, and structured-evidence methods, with an oracle-root upper bound. On 96 explicitly anchored selection cases, gate-only structured evidence achieves candidate F1 of 1.0000, compared with 0.9677 for token-lexical matching. On 12 relational-paraphrase cases, token-lexical F1 is 0.1772 and gate-only F1 is 0.0000, compared with 0.5000--0.6400 for the large language models. Model grounding degrades as candidate inventories grow from 4 to 64 changes, while affected-element and typed-path accuracy remain comparatively stable when frozen selections are replayed over graphs of approximately 1K to 100K nodes. Strict evidence gating suppresses false positives but can remove correct semantic selections. An exploratory evidence-empty abstention policy raises strict abstention accuracy to 0.6667 for all three models and reduces unsafe-report rates to 0.1667, while decreasing answerable-case coverage by 16.0--27.1 percentage points. Four of six conflicting requests remain unsafe for each model. These findings support combining literal evidence and language-model reasoning with engineering review when intent cannot be established reliably.

</details>


### [294] [Representation Bias, Correction Transfer, and Resolution Sensitivity in Three-Dimensional Mitochondrial Morphometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07582)
*Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi,Timothy Oladunni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对三维线粒体形态计量进行经验性可靠性评估，串联表示偏差、受控处理干预、校正迁移与分辨率敏感性四条证据链，揭示"精度高≠可比"的系统性陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量成像流程可对同一对象产生精确但系统性不同的测量。作者希望区分"处理流程引入的描述符变化"与"候选生物学差异"，避免把流水线偏差误读为真实形态差异。

Method: 以 3D Mitochondria Shape Library for Optical Microscopy 的 2,720 个开发对象为基准：(1) 比较占用体素推导体积与参考网格体积，做边界位移分析；(2) 实施受控的标签流程重实现，移除深度偏移以观察误差变化；(3) 用冻结回归将由占用体素推导的特征迁移到 2,728 个未使用对象上，检验校正与不确定度迁移；(4) 在 MitoEM 的 550 个鼠皮层对象上，把面内间距从 8 nm 粗化到 24 nm 做分辨率敏感性测试。

Result: ICC 高达 0.994，但占用推导体积平均比参考网格体积高 3.665%，标签外移 0.00304 归一化单位。移除深度偏移使 55 个对象体积误差平均降低 1.57 个百分点，约占复现膨胀的 45%，余量来源未定。冻结回归把中位绝对百分比误差从 3.481% 降到 0.664%，但校准误差界仅覆盖总体 92.1%、低占用亚组 49.2%。8→24 nm 粗化使中位表面积变化 −10.60%、球形度 +11.76%，秩相关仍为 0.994。

Conclusion: 高相关/高精度指标不能保证跨流程、跨分辨率、跨来源的测量可比性；准确度迁移与不确定度迁移必须分别评估。该框架提供了区分处理诱导描述符变化与候选生物学差异的定量检查手段，但不主张生物学不变性，也不支持跨来源校正迁移。

Abstract: Quantitative imaging pipelines can produce precise but systematically different measurements of the same object. We present an empirical reliability assessment of three-dimensional mitochondrial morphometry that connects representation bias, a controlled processing intervention, correction transfer, and resolution sensitivity. Using 2,720 development objects from the 3D Mitochondria Shape Library for Optical Microscopy, we find that occupancy-derived volumes exceed reference mesh volumes by 3.665% on average despite an intraclass correlation coefficient of 0.994. Boundary analysis identifies an outward label displacement of 0.00304 normalized units. In a controlled label-pipeline reimplementation, removing the depth offset reduces volume error in all 55 analyzed objects by a mean of 1.57 percentage points, approximately 45% of mean reproduced inflation; the source of the remainder is not isolated. A frozen regression using occupancy-derived features reduces median absolute percentage error from 3.481% to 0.664% in 2,728 previously unused objects from the same resource. However, its calibrated error bound covers only 92.1% overall and 49.2% in a low-occupancy subgroup, demonstrating that accuracy and uncertainty transfer must be evaluated separately. In 550 rat-cortex objects from the MitoEM resource, coarsening in-plane spacing from 8 to 24 nanometers changes median surface area by minus 10.60% and sphericity by plus 11.76%, despite a rank correlation of 0.994. These results provide quantitative checks for distinguishing processing-induced descriptor changes from candidate biological differences, without establishing biological invariance or cross-source correction transfer.

</details>


### [295] [Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation with Cross-Platform User History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07588)
*Yu Xia,Jiangfan Zhang,Jun Xiao,Julian McAuley,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"个人代理中介推荐"这一新范式，并构建基准 MediateRec 与训练方法 PAMO，以在平台排序基础上用个人代理的跨平台历史进行中介，实现有效纠偏且平衡"救援"与"有害覆盖"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐正从平台中心化个性化转向用户主导的个性化：个人 LLM 代理可代表用户跨服务行动。但平台排序编码了个人代理无法观测的群体证据，因此中介必须在"有益救援"与"有害覆盖"之间权衡。现有工作缺乏对该信息边界与权衡的系统研究。

Method: 1) 形式化 Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation：平台推荐器用平台本地信息对候选集排序，个人代理利用用户授权的跨平台历史对该排序进行中介，产出最终 top-K 列表。2) 构建 MediateRec 基准，包含可扩展的代理式跨平台环境以及受控平台-代理信息边界下的真实跨平台测试。3) 提出 PAMO（Personal Attribution Mediation Optimization）：通过反事实遮蔽跨平台历史来估计"个人中介支持度"，并在平台相对价值下限约束下重新分配秩感知优势质量。

Result: 理论上证明 PAMO 保持截断层级的优势质量，且在不降低平均平台相对价值的前提下，在一阶重分配中具有局部最优性。MediateRec 实验表明：个人代理中介能带来有意义的平台纠偏，但即便是强专有 LLM 也会引入不可忽视的有害覆盖；PAMO 在已见与未见目标平台及真实跨平台测试上一致优于匹配的纯结果 RL，并取得更好的救援-危害平衡。

Conclusion: 个人代理中介推荐是一个有前景且需谨慎处理的方向：中介价值显著，但需专门优化以控制有害覆盖。PAMO 通过反事实归因与受约束的优势重分配，在保持平台相对价值的同时提升了跨平台泛化与救援-危害平衡。

Abstract: Modern recommendation is shifting from platform-centric personalization toward user-governed personalization, where a personal LLM agent can act on the user's behalf across services. We formalize this emerging paradigm as Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation: a platform recommender ranks a candidate set using platform-local information, and a personal agent uses user-authorized cross-platform history to mediate the resulting ranking and produce the final top-K slate. Such mediation is nontrivial: the platform ranking can encode strong population evidence that the personal agent cannot observe, so effective mediation must therefore balance beneficial rescues against harmful overrides. To study this trade-off, we introduce MediateRec, a benchmark that includes scalable proxy cross-platform environments and a real cross-platform test under a controlled platform-agent information boundary. To train the agent to use cross-platform history effectively, we further propose Personal Attribution Mediation Optimization (PAMO), which counterfactually masks that history to estimate personal mediation support and reallocates rank-aware advantage mass under a platform-relative value floor. We theoretically prove that PAMO preserves cutoff-level advantage mass and is locally optimal among first-order reallocations that preserve this mass without lowering average platform-relative value. Experiments on MediateRec show that personal-agent mediation enables meaningful platform corrections, yet even strong proprietary LLMs introduce non-negligible harmful overrides. PAMO consistently improves over matched outcome-only RL across seen and unseen target platforms and on the real cross-platform test, while achieving a better rescue-harm balance.

</details>


### [296] [LSC-DPO: Learning-Signal-Controlled Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07592)
*Yang Qu,Yusheng Han,Chengjia Feng,Handan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文从损失层面的几何视角研究DPO，发现sigmoid因子刻画了目标函数的局部敏感度，据此提出LSC-DPO，通过动态调控学习信号至目标区间，并在AlpacaEval 2、MT-Bench、Anthropic-HH上一致优于DPO及强基线，还给出信号预算补偿规则以降低初始化带来的性能波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO已成为无奖励模型的对齐标准方法，但随着训练中偏好间隔（preference margin）增大，logistic损失对进一步变化的敏感度逐渐下降，导致学习信号衰减、优化效率受限。作者希望从损失几何角度理解这一现象并加以调控。

Method: 作者从损失层面几何视角分析DPO，将sigmoid因子识别为刻画目标局部敏感度的学习信号，提出Learning-Signal-Controlled Direct Preference Optimization（LSC-DPO），将学习信号动态调节至目标区间；并通过log空间分析给出稳定跟踪目标学习信号区间的条件；进一步基于不同系数初始化导致不同瞬态学习信号轨迹的观察，推导信号预算补偿规则以调整目标学习信号。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2、MT-Bench和Anthropic-HH上，LSC-DPO持续优于DPO及强偏好优化基线；不同系数初始化即使后期信号水平相近，也会产生不同的瞬态学习信号轨迹，采用信号预算补偿规则后可显著降低跨系数初始化的性能差异。

Conclusion: 学习信号是理解与改进DPO的有效视角，动态控制学习信号可稳定提升对齐性能，且通过信号预算补偿可缓解初始化敏感性，提升方法鲁棒性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has become a standard reward-model-free approach for aligning language models with preference data. However, as the scaled preference margin grows during training, the logistic DPO loss becomes progressively less sensitive to further changes. We study DPO from a loss-level geometric perspective and identify the sigmoid factor as a learning signal that characterizes the local sensitivity of the objective. Based on this view, we propose Learning-Signal-Controlled Direct Preference Optimization (LSC-DPO), which dynamically regulates the learning signal near a target regime. A log-space analysis establishes conditions for stable tracking of the target learning-signal regime. Experiments on AlpacaEval 2, MT-Bench, and Anthropic-HH show that LSC-DPO consistently improves over DPO and strong preference-optimization baselines. We further find that different coefficient initializations induce distinct transient learning-signal trajectories even when their later signal levels become similar. Based on this observation, we derive a signal-budget compensation rule that adjusts the target learning signal to compensate for these transient differences. The resulting compensation substantially reduces performance variation across coefficient initializations.

</details>


### [297] [Beyond Scalar IoU: Structured Verification from Rollout Groups for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07601)
*Youngjae Cho,Won Young Jhoo,Jongsuk Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SUTURE，将验证从单条rollout扩展到rollout组，利用组内不一致性与位置覆盖度重分配奖励，可用于视频时序定位的RLVR，在五个基准上全IoU阈值提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR用于视频时序定位时，重叠验证器对每条rollout独立打分，忽略了同一组rollout之间的联合结构信息，导致验证信号信息量不足，也易出现视频起始锚定等偏差。

Method: SUTURE以rollout组为条件进行验证，在两个尺度利用组结构：用rollout间的不一致性控制目标重加权强度，用各位置的覆盖度决定奖励质量的重分配方向。其验证器可精确分解为标准IoU项与由rollout组决定的协方差修正项，并用局部梯度诊断分析响应偏好。

Result: 在五个时序定位基准上，SUTURE在每个报告的IoU阈值下均提升定位性能；梯度诊断发现所分析组中偏好覆盖支撑较弱目标区域的响应；训练后的策略推理轨迹中视频起始锚定减少，对较晚事件其首次时间提及更常与标注目标重叠。

Conclusion: rollout组的联合结构能够支撑更具信息量的时序验证器，为视频时序定位的RLVR提供了组级条件化奖励设计与可分解的理论解释。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) provides a natural framework for adapting pretrained models to video temporal grounding, where generated temporal intervals can be scored directly against ground truth intervals. Yet existing overlap verifiers typically score each rollout independently, leaving the joint structure of the rollout group unused. We introduce SUTURE, which conditions verification on the rollout group and exploits its structure at two complementary scales: disagreement across rollouts controls how strongly the target is reweighted, while coverage at each position determines where reward mass is redistributed. We show that the resulting verifier admits an exact decomposition into the standard IoU term and a covariance correction determined by the rollout group. A local gradient diagnostic finds a preference for responses covering relatively less supported target regions in the analyzed groups. Across five temporal grounding benchmarks, SUTURE improves grounding performance at every reported IoU threshold. Its trained policy also shows less video-start anchoring in reasoning traces: for later events, the first temporal mention more often overlaps the annotated target. Together, these results show that the joint structure of a rollout group can support a more informative temporal verifier.

</details>


### [298] [VALSE: Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficient Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07606)
*Jia-Dong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立垂直自适应跳层的理论框架，证明FLOPs闭式表达、函数空间包含关系及与MoE的结构对偶性，并提出VALSE逐样本非连续跳层方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层跳过方法多为连续或粗粒度跳过，缺乏对任意逐样本跳层计算代价与函数空间性质的理论刻画，也未被明确定位为宽度稀疏（MoE）的正交对应。本文旨在填补这一理论空白并指导方法设计。

Method: 理论层面：推导任意逐样本跳层调度的期望FLOPs闭式公式；证明跳层模型函数空间是完整层函数空间的真子集（superset与strict inclusion，含显式分离示例）；给出VALSE与MoE架构的结构对偶命题。方法层面：提出VALSE，用轻量难度估计器基于前几层打分，配合逐层门控选择性跳过冗余层，支持任意中间层跳过并保留更深层，实现逐样本、非连续跳层。

Result: 得到期望FLOPs的闭式表达；证明跳层模型严格包含于但能有效近似完整层函数空间；确立垂直深度稀疏与水平宽度稀疏的正交对偶关系；VALSE可行性在原型规模得到初步验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为垂直自适应跳层提供了理论基础，并将VALSE定位为与MoE正交的深度稀疏范式，原型实验初步支持其可行性与效率潜力。

Abstract: This paper establishes a theoretical framework for vertical adaptive layer skipping, proving three foundational results: (i) an Expected FLOPs formula (theorem 2) giving a closed-form expression for the computational cost of arbitrary per-sample skip schedules as a function of layer-wise skip probabilities; (ii) function-space superset (theorem 10) and strict inclusion (theorem 11) theorems showing that skip-layer models are strictly contained in---yet meaningfully approximate---the full-layer function space, with an explicit separating example; and (iii) a structural duality between VALSE and Mixture-of-Experts architectures (proposition 6), positioning vertical depth-wise sparsity as the orthogonal counterpart to horizontal width-wise sparsity. Building on this theory, we propose VALSE (Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficiency), a per-sample, non-contiguous layer skipping method: a lightweight difficulty estimator scores each input from the first few layers, and per-layer gates selectively skip redundant layers---including arbitrary middle layers while retaining deeper ones---so that only the necessary depth is activated for each input, whose feasibility is preliminarily assessed at prototype scale.

</details>


### [299] [BioStudyBench: Evaluating Agents on Post-Cutoff Biomedical Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07614)
*David Li,Shaamil Karim,Christian Gensbigler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BioStudyBench 基准，用 25 个长程生物医学分析任务评估 AI 智能体能否仅凭公开数据复现发表研究结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测无法有效区分模型的分析能力与其先验知识或对已发表答案的检索，导致难以判断智能体是否真正具备基于数据的独立分析能力。

Method: 构建 BioStudyBench：从 404,019 条 PubMed 记录半自动筛选出 25 个长程分析任务，均取自 2026 年 7—9 月发表的、晚于所评测模型知识截止日期的研究。任务仅提供中性研究问题，不提供数据文件，智能体需自行检索并下载相关公开数据、在仅返回截止日期前文献的工具中检索文献，并通过数据分析给出结论。每个任务在有/无数据与工具两种条件下各运行一次，以量化相对先验知识的增益。

Result: 在八个模型上，提供数据与工具平均使通过率较无数据基线提升 47 个百分点；开源权重模型整体落后于闭源权重模型，最佳开源模型通过率 81.3%，最佳闭源模型为 94.7%。

Conclusion: 该基准能有效隔离先验知识、控制答案泄漏，表明当前先进智能体在数据获取与实证分析上已具备较强能力，但开源与闭源模型之间仍存在明显差距。

Abstract: We evaluate whether AI agents can match the reported findings of published biomedical studies using public data. Existing evaluations do not consistently separate analysis from prior knowledge or retrieval of the published answer. We introduce BioStudyBench, a benchmark of 25 long-horizon analysis tasks drawn from studies first published between July and September 2026, after the developer-reported knowledge cutoffs of the models we evaluate, semi-automatically filtered down from 404,019 PubMed records. In each task, the agent receives a neutral research question but no data files, so it must find and download the relevant public data, search the literature through tools that return only records dated before its cutoff, and report findings through data analysis. To measure gains over prior knowledge, we run every task both with and without access to data and tools. Across eight models, access to data and tools raises the pass rate by 47 percentage points on average over the no-data baseline. Open-weight models across sizes trail closed-weight models, with the best open-weight model passing 81.3% of tasks against 94.7% for the best closed-weight model.

</details>


### [300] [Explore, Then Commit: Measurement-Efficient Scientific Law Discovery with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07620)
*Kautik Mandve,Dileepa Fernando*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在NewtonBench上评估"先探索后提交"协议：LLM提出假设、程序化规划器采集测量、新提示综合最终定律，结果显示启用解释器的规划器显著提升测量效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学定律发现需选择测量并将证据转化为支配方程，作者旨在评估一种测量高效的LLM驱动协议，解决如何在有限测量下准确推断物理定律的问题。

Method: 采用explore-then-commit协议，结合结构化探针、自动数值诊断、受限测量批次与可选解释器访问；在576次NewtonBench试验中，用GPT-4.1-mini及中等难度的GPT-4.1复现，对比8种配置于12个物理模块。

Result: 中等任务上，启用解释器的规划器每试验测量数为8.6对22.5（GPT-4.1-mini）、8.9对43.0（GPT-4.1）；平均基于幅度的RMSLE从2.514降至0.202、从0.626降至0.149。符号准确率提升跨模块不一致，随机采集与分歧评分相当；各模块均出现测量节省，但批次约束不均使节省无法完全归因于采集质量。

Conclusion: 完整协议是一种有前景的测量高效配置，但其因果组件及在非无噪声、非直接方程任务上的泛化性仍未解决。

Abstract: Scientific law discovery requires selecting measurements and converting evidence into a governing equation. We evaluate an explore-then-commit protocol in which a large language model proposes hypotheses, a programmatic planner gathers measurements, and a fresh prompt synthesizes the final law from fixed observations. The protocol combines structured probes, automatic numerical diagnostics, restricted measurement batches, and optional interpreter access. Across 576 NewtonBench trials, we compare eight configurations on 12 physics modules using GPT-4.1-mini and a medium-difficulty GPT-4.1 replication. On medium tasks, interpreter-enabled planners use 8.6 versus 22.5 measurements per trial for GPT-4.1-mini and 8.9 versus 43.0 for GPT-4.1. Their mean magnitude-based root-mean-squared logarithmic error falls from 2.514 to 0.202 and from 0.626 to 0.149, respectively. An additional audit retains incomplete and invalid submissions in a coverage-sensitive analysis. Observed symbolic-accuracy gains are less consistent across modules, and random acquisition is competitive with disagreement scoring. Measurement savings occur in every module, but unequal batch constraints prevent attributing them solely to acquisition quality. These results support the complete protocol as a promising measurement-efficient configuration, while leaving its causal components and generalization beyond noiseless direct-equation tasks unresolved.

</details>


### [301] [Learning to Outgrow a Theory: Experimental Discovery Beyond the Initial Hypothesis Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07627)
*SiYuan Ma,Albert Gao,Chunzheng Zhu,Xin Yan,Wenlong Zhang,Wenxin Zhang,Luqi Gong,Tianlin Li,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"实验性模型类修正"问题：发现策略联合提出结构编辑与诊断实验，在类级可区分性目标与随时有效序贯证据下仅在当前模型类被拒绝时修正结构，在400个动力学环境上以32次真实实验达89.5%精确恢复，并具备跨机制组合与库不足检测的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学发现系统在固定假设空间内优化实验，存在系统性失效模式：当所有候选机制都遗漏同一缺失机制时，候选间分歧会坍缩，即使模型类整体错误也无法被察觉，因此需要把"理论应表达哪些机制"与"在哪里采集证据"作为统一决策问题。

Method: 形式化实验性模型类修正任务，策略联合提议结构编辑与检验该编辑必要性的诊断实验；方法耦合类级可区分性目标（单一共享参数化须解释所有被选实验）与随时有效的序贯证据，仅当当前模型类被拒绝时才触发结构修正。

Result: 在400个留出受控动力学环境上，联合策略以32次真实实验预算达到89.5%精确恢复，较最强匹配基线提升10.0个百分点，且执行实验与候选拟合更少；学习到的"修正—实验"配对可迁移至未见机制组合、留出但可表达的基元、参数外推与实验代价偏移；当真实机制超出编辑文法时，88%情况下检测出库不足，误支持率5.5%；增益亦迁移至ODEBench、ODEBase模型库任务与DiscoverPhysics世界。

Conclusion: 结果表明，科学发现可被视为单一序贯决策问题：同时决定理论应使哪些机制可表达以及应在何处采集证据，从而克服固定假设空间下的系统性失效。

Abstract: Scientific discovery systems typically optimize experiments within a fixed hypothesis space. This creates a failure mode when all available candidates omit the same missing mechanism: candidate disagreement can collapse even while the model class is systematically wrong. We formulate experimental model-class revision, in which a discovery policy jointly proposes a structural edit and a diagnostic experiment that tests whether that edit is necessary. The method couples a class-level distinguishability objective, in which one shared parameterization must explain all selected experiments, with anytime-valid sequential evidence that triggers structural revision only after the current class is rejected. On 400 held-out controlled dynamical environments, the joint policy reaches 89.5% exact recovery with a budget of 32 real experiments, improving the strongest matched baseline by 10.0 percentage points while requiring fewer executed experiments and candidate fits. The learned revision-experiment pairing transfers across unseen mechanism combinations, held-out but expressible primitives, parameter extrapolation, and shifted experiment costs; when the true mechanism is outside the edit grammar, it detects library insufficiency in 88% of cases with a 5.5% false-support rate. Revision gains also transfer to ODEBench and ODEBase model-library tasks, as well as DiscoverPhysics worlds. These results support a view of scientific discovery in which deciding what mechanisms a theory should make expressible and where to collect evidence are treated as a single sequential decision problem.

</details>


### [302] [Measuring climate backlash in Twitter and Reddit archives: Lexical definitions, recorded responses and participant turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07634)
*Wentao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对四个Twitter与Reddit存档进行全量、非抽样的词汇规则处理，研究揭示框架共现、平台响应字段与观测边界如何塑造关于气候反弹的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体常被用于研究对气候行动的抵制，但"词语""响应计数"与"被观测参与者"并不测量同一社会过程，需要区分这些测量操作的定义与边界。

Method: 对供给的四个Twitter与Reddit存档全部注册文件不做抽样处理，采用透明、非排他的词汇规则；将框架共现与来源特定的时间及响应模型相链接，并把事件期变化分解为回归作者的变化与参与者更替；检验跨话语历史对预测的贡献。

Result: Reddit上可再生能源词与成本语言共现，但更窄的反弹短语显著削弱跨来源对比，并逆转《巴黎协定》对比的符号；跨话语历史不改善可入样主语境预测；否认/骗局词与Twitter记录点赞更高相关，而Reddit响应关联依赖框架、结果与作者设定；2019全球气候罢工前后，回归作者表达与参与者更替均提高抗议语言份额；存档终点阻碍相应Twitter迁移推断；多数跨簇估计缺区间、作者/月联合协方差估计失败。

Conclusion: 贡献在于给出档案层面的测量后果说明，而非对个体反对、说服或倡导引发反弹的度量；操作定义、平台特定响应字段与观测边界共同决定关于气候反弹的可主张范围。

Abstract: Social media archives are often used to study resistance to climate action, but words, response counters and observed participants do not measure the same social process. We examine four supplied Twitter and Reddit archives by processing all registered files without sampling and applying transparent, non-exclusive lexical rules. The study links frame co-occurrence to source-specific temporal and response models, then separates event-period changes among returning authors from participant turnover. Renewable-energy terms accompany cost-related language on Reddit, yet narrower backlash phrases sharply reduce cross-source contrasts and reverse the sign of the Paris Agreement contrast in submissions. Cross-discourse history does not improve eligible primary-context forecasts. Denial/hoax terms are associated with higher recorded Twitter likes, whereas Reddit response associations depend on frame, outcome and author specification. Around the 2019 global climate strike, returning-author expression and participant turnover both contribute to increased protest-language shares. An archive endpoint prevents the corresponding Climate Twitter migration inference. Most crossed-cluster estimates lack released intervals, and joint author/month response covariance estimates fail, restricting formal inference. These results show how operational definitions, platform-specific response fields and observation boundaries shape what can be claimed about climate backlash. The contribution is an archive-based account of these measurement consequences, rather than a measure of individual opposition, persuasion or advocacy-induced backlash.

</details>


### [303] [Learning Explainable Representations of Complex Game-playing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07638)
*Abhijeet Krishnan,Colin M. Potts,Arnav Jhala,Harshad Khadilkar,Shirish Karande,Chris Martens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种类似人类认知的强化学习智能体训练策略：从游戏行为序列中自动学习可执行程序以表示学到的策略，并在国际象棋和网格环境中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类玩家在玩复杂游戏时会发展出与规则一致的概念和策略抽象，用于解释他人行为和指导自身决策；理解对手策略是提升水平的关键，但耗时费力。论文希望让RL智能体也能以类似方式自动获取并表达这种可解释的策略知识。

Method: 提出一种类似人类认知的训练策略，将学习到的策略综合为可执行过程（程序）；通过从游戏行为动作序列中自动学习程序，从而让智能体学会玩国际象棋以及解决网格环境中的任务。

Result: 实验表明，学到的策略能够产生有效的动作，并且可以从游戏数据中学习获得。

Conclusion: 该工作说明将策略表示为可从 gameplay 数据中自动学习的可执行程序这一思路是可行的，为RL智能体从行为数据中学习可解释策略提供了途径。

Abstract: As part of learning to play complex games, human players develop develop abstractions for concepts and strategies of gameplay consistent with game rules to improve their performance. These concepts are applied to explain other players' actions, and to inform their own actions in-game. Understanding other players' strategies is a crucial part of such improvement, but requires time and effort. In this paper, we propose a strategy similar to human cognition for training RL agents to synthesize learned strategies and policies as executable procedures based on sequences of gameplay actions. We present methods to automatically learn such programs to play chess and to solve tasks in a grid-based environment. We show that the learned strategies produce effective actions, and can be learned from gameplay data.

</details>


### [304] [Towards the Automatic Synthesis of Interpretable Chess Tactics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07640)
*Abhijeet Krishnan,Chris Martens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于国际象棋的符号化子策略模型，借鉴棋术知识以提升可解释性，基于归纳逻辑编程系统PAL学习到的模式构建模型，提出散度度量对模型进行评测，并设计了与现成引擎结合的计算评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的强化学习智能体在棋类游戏（如国际象棋、围棋、星际争霸II）中已能超越人类专家，其优势不仅来自数字硬件带来的更快反应与计算能力，更在于采用了能带来更多胜利的更优策略。解读这些策略可为人类玩家提供提升棋艺的宝贵洞见。本文希望构建可解释的符号化模型来刻画这类策略，从而服务于人类学习与理解。

Method: 受国际象棋战术启发，作者提出一个符号化的子策略模型，通过引入领域知识来提升模型可解释性。具体做法是改造归纳逻辑编程系统PAL所学习到的模式，将其用于推导该模型；同时提出一个散度度量（divergence metric），用于将模型与随机基线进行对比评估；最后提出一种计算评估方案，将现成（off-the-shelf）国际象棋引擎与该模型结合进行评测。

Result: 评测发现，该模型所得到的一组战术能够给出与国际象棋初学者棋力相近的走子建议；提出的散度度量可用于有效地将模型与随机基线区分开来。

Conclusion: 本文作为一项初步工作，验证了以符号化、融入领域知识的方式来刻画棋类策略的可行性，所得战术集合在走子质量上接近人类初学者水平，并给出了散度度量与基于引擎的计算评估方案，为后续进一步提升可解释性与棋力奠定基础。

Abstract: State-of-the-art reinforcement learning agents are capable of outperforming human experts at games like chess, Go and StarCraft II. These agents do not simply take advantage of their digital hardware in being able to react and calculate faster than humans, but employ better strategies that lead to more victories. Interpreting these strategies would give human players valuable insight into how to improve their play. In this preliminary work, we propose a symbolic sub-policy model for playing chess. Inspired by chess tactics, our model attempts to incorporate domain knowledge to improve interpretability. We adapt patterns learned by an inductive logic programming system called PAL to derive our model. We contribute a divergence metric to evaluate our model against a random baseline, and find a set of tactics that is able to suggest moves of similar playing strength to a human beginner. Finally, we propose a computational evaluation scheme for the model by augmenting an off-the-shelf engine with it.

</details>


### [305] [Matching Object or Relation? Tracing Abstract Reasoning Inside VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07646)
*Gouki Minegishi,Hiroki Furuta,Takeshi Kojima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用心理学RMTS范式结合机制可解释性方法，揭示VLM抽象推理失败的原因：模型内部存在“早期表面特征回路”与“晚期抽象关系回路”的竞争，前者主导导致错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）基准只能记录其在抽象推理任务上的系统性失败，却无法解释失败原因或指出缺失的认知能力。作者希望从机制层面回答“为什么失败”以及“缺失哪种认知能力”这一空白。

Method: 借鉴比较与发展心理学的Relational Match-to-Sample（RMTS）范式，构建参数化可控刺激集，评测GPT、Claude、Gemini等前沿API模型及Qwen3.5、Gemma-4、InternVL3三个开源系列，操纵能力层级、模型规模、每场景物体数、逐物体刺激噪声四个变量；并对模型内部进行逐层表征相似性分析（RSA）与因果中介分析，定位相关回路；最后将分析扩展到ARC-AGI-1并做头消融验证。

Result: 四个变量共同推动VLM趋向关系匹配，形成类似人类“关系迁移”的发展轨迹；逐层RSA与因果中介分析显示，抽象推理由两个竞争回路实现——早期回路按表面物体特征组织图像，晚期回路按抽象关系组织图像；在ARC-AGI-1上，消融RMTS识别出的关系头比随机头造成更大性能下降，说明该关系回路可迁移到受控刺激之外。

Conclusion: VLM抽象推理由相互竞争的早/晚回路实现，其失败源于表面特征回路的强势；RMTS+机制分析提供了理解VLM抽象推理实现的机制级视角，是迈向该方向的一步。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) excel on visual benchmarks but fail systematically on tasks requiring abstract reasoning. Existing benchmarks document this failure but cannot say \emph{why} it happens or which cognitive capability is missing. We close this gap by adopting the Relational Match-to-Sample (RMTS) paradigm from comparative and developmental psychology and pairing it with a mechanistic analysis of the model's internals. On a parametrically controlled stimulus set evaluated across frontier API models (GPT, Claude, Gemini) and three open-source families (Qwen3.5, Gemma-4, InternVL3), we identify four levers that shift VLMs toward the relational match---capability tier, model scale, the number of objects per scene, and the absence of per-object stimulus noise---together producing a developmental-like trajectory that mirrors the human \emph{relational shift}. Opening up the model, a per-layer representational similarity analysis and a causal mediation analysis reveal that VLM abstract reasoning is implemented by two competing circuits: an early circuit that organises images by their surface object features, and a late circuit that organises them by their abstract relation. Extending the analysis to ARC-AGI-1, we find that ablating the relational heads identified on RMTS degrades performance more than ablating random heads, indicating that the relational circuit is recruited beyond our controlled stimuli. We hope this mechanism-level view serves as a step toward understanding how abstract reasoning is implemented in VLMs.

</details>


### [306] [Where Rules End and Judges Begin: Measuring the Judgment Boundary in Multi-Agent Systems Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07657)
*Shaswata Mitra,Raj Patel,Subash Neupane,Sudip Mittal,Md Rayhanur Rahman,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM多智能体系统（MAS）防御组织为五项原则，并实现为DEFER（确定性优先执行+残差判断），通过28项检查级联与四人评审小组，将四个领域的攻击成功率从约30.0%降至约3.0%，其中78%的拦截由确定性检查完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对LLM多智能体系统（MAS）的防御多为孤立评估，一次只针对一类攻击，导致成本高且难以审计。需要一套统一、可审计的防御框架来应对工具调用、共享记忆和任务委派中遇到的对抗性内容。

Method: 将防御归纳为五项原则，并实现为DEFER（DEterministic-First Enforcement with Residual judgment）：由28项检查构成的级联，能确定性阻断的直接阻断，其余交由四人评审小组（judges）判断。
