A heuristic is derived from each solver failure and accepted only after the solver repeatedly succeeds with that heuristic in context. These outcomes also provide feedback for the explorer to refine the difficulty of future tasks. Accepted heuristics are then consolidated into a memory bank for test-time use. Across AppWorld, $τ^2$-bench, and AutomationBench, DAEDALUS improves mean success rates by up to 15.9 points and pass^5 by up to 2.2x over a no-memory baseline, and is competitive with methods using training tasks, at a lower inference cost than most. We show that performance gains already emerge with a small exploration budget, and that its heuristics also benefit agents from other model families. Our ablations further reveal that solver traces provide the key information needed to derive effective heuristics, while factorizing early discoveries makes exploration more cost-efficient. Beyond memory construction, we find that the tasks generated by DAEDALUS can serve as a proxy for benchmark tasks when ranking models by performance. Code and artifacts: www.github.com/illuin-tech/daedalus.

</details>


### [340] [SpeedrunBench: Challenging LLM Agents with Video Game Speedrunning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08076)
*Yoshinari Fujinuma,Keisuke Kamahori,Ryuto Koike,Abdelrahman Madkour,Varun Prashant Gangal,Monty Bichouna,Martyna Markiewicz,Shivani Jain,Duncan Curtis,Rebecca Qian,Anand Kannappan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SPEEDRUNBENCH，用游戏速通评估前沿 LLM 智能体的策略形成与长时程优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 LLM 智能体已能解决人类已有可测解的任务，但能否超越人类既有解法尚不明确；当缺乏情境与训练数据时，发展复杂策略尤为关键。作者借助速通这一共同体实践来研究该能力，因为速通要求参与者深入理解并掌握游戏机制，发现非正统玩法。

Method: 构建 SPEEDRUNBENCH 基准，覆盖 9 款不同游戏；智能体需反复改进策略、反思表现、利用已获知识，并在长时程动作序列上推理，以不断刷新自身与全人类的最快完成时间。在实用预算下对前沿 LLM 智能体进行实验。

Result: 在简单的平台跳跃游戏中，前沿智能体接近人类世界纪录；但在更长、更复杂的游戏中，受实际预算限制，其表现仍落后于人类。

Conclusion: SPEEDRUNBENCH 可作为研究智能体策略形成能力的有效测试平台，也是一种抗饱和的评测指标，因为几乎总存在更快的完成时间等待被发现。

Abstract: Frontier LLM agents have been shown to be capable of solving increasingly complex tasks for which humans have measurable solutions. This begs the pertinent question of whether LLM agents can go beyond what humans have already solved. The ability to develop sophisticated strategies to tackle consequential problems becomes paramount as well-trodden, human-developed solutions become insufficient for problems for which we lack context or enough training data. We study agents' capability of such strategy formation through the communal practice of video game speedrunning. In speedrunning, practitioners compete to find the fastest way to complete a video game under certain conditions, and in so doing uncovering interesting unorthodox play styles that require a thorough understanding and mastery of the underlying game mechanics. We introduce SPEEDRUNBENCH, a benchmark that evaluates frontier LLM agents across 9 different games. To perform well in this benchmark, agents must repeatedly improve their strategy, reflect on their performance, exploit their gained knowledge, and reason across a long-horizon of actions to improve on an increasingly difficult problem: being faster than themselves and everyone else. Our experiments show that while frontier agents approach human world records in simple platformer games, they remain behind human performance on longer, more complex games under practical budgets. These results suggest that SPEEDRUNBENCH is a useful testbed for studying agents' strategy formation capabilities as well as being a saturation-resistant evaluation measure, as there is almost always a faster completion time waiting to be discovered.

</details>


### [341] [POLAR: Ontology-Guided Risk Prevention for Tool-Calling LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08082)
*Yunju Kang,Seonghyeon Cho,Irene Li,Yeo-Chan Yoon,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POLAR是一个面向小型工具调用智能体的护栏框架，通过双层本体对动作可逆性进行分级评分并剪枝高风险调用，在τ²-bench上部分场景提升奖励，但整体收益有限且存在回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM工具使用智能体处于动态环境，许多动作带有操作风险，但多数安全机制只在错误发生后反应。现有预防性方法要么微调智能体进行思维链推理，要么将自然语言护栏编译为运行时检查，均未提供结构化、可审计的判定结论。

Method: 提出POLAR护栏框架，基于结构化双层本体评估动作可逆性：为每个动作推导候选逆序序列，据此赋予分级可逆性分数，低于阈值的调用在执行前被剪枝。

Result: 在τ²-bench上对六个智能体模型评估：在airline域，六个智能体中有四个平均任务奖励提升0.11至0.18分；但十八个“模型—域”单元中仅八个整体改善，retail域及较强智能体常出现回退。

Conclusion: POLAR提供了可审计的结构化检查，并刻画了其任务效用权衡；奖励并非阻止危害的直接度量。

Abstract: LLM tool-use agents operate in dynamic environments where many actions carry operational risk. However, most safety mechanisms react only after errors manifest. Existing pre-emptive approaches either fine-tune the agent on chain-of-thought deliberation or compile natural-language guardrails into runtime checks, but they do so without exposing a structural, auditable verdict. We propose POLAR, a guardrail framework for small tool-calling agents that assesses reversibility through a structured two-layer ontology. POLAR assigns each action a graded reversibility score by deriving a candidate inverse sequence; calls failing a threshold are pruned before execution. Evaluated on $τ^2$-bench across six agent models, POLAR improves mean task reward by 0.11 to 0.18 points on airline for four of six agents, but only eight of eighteen model--domain cells improve overall; retail and stronger agents often regress. POLAR provides an auditable structural check and characterizes its task-utility trade-offs. Reward is not a direct measure of prevented harm.

</details>


### [342] [Natural Language Questions as an Interface for Knowledge Graphs: QRAKEN Graph Distillation and Semantic Self-Healing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08095)
*Remo Grillo,Lukas Klic,Giovanni Colavizza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QRAKEN是一种免训练、本体无关的神经符号流水线，通过离线蒸馏的TTQL（包含填充多跳模式、条件频率、路径条件字面量示例及类-属性共现矩阵）引导LLM生成SPARQL，并辅以确定性语法/词表/数据模型校验实现迭代精炼。在CK25基准上，GPT-4.1 mini与GPT-5.4分别取得严格F1 0.643±0.026和0.652±0.012，相对最强复现参赛系统提升30%和32%；消融显示TTQL模式为主驱动（较仅形状基线+0.31），且优于自动导出的SHACL达64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言访问RDF知识图谱是语义网的核心目标。LLM推动了Text-to-SPARQL进展，但在陌生图谱上常生成语法有效却违背实际数据模型的查询。现有方法依赖模式（schema）预期，而非图上的经验证据，导致生成结果与填充数据不符。

Method: 提出免训练、本体无关的神经符号流水线QRAKEN：离线蒸馏器生成TTQL——对已填充多跳模式、条件频率、路径条件字面量示例的紧凑描述，以及类-属性共现矩阵；在线阶段用TTQL引导LLM，并通过确定性语法、词表与数据模型检查提供诊断以支持迭代精炼（拒绝共现矩阵不支持的三元组模式）。

Result: 在CK25（首届International Text2SPARQL Challenge）上，按匹配条件在QLever快照重算：GPT-4.1 mini严格F1为0.643±0.026，GPT-5.4为0.652±0.012，相对最强重算参赛者提升30%与32%，超越使用同基座模型家族的系统。消融：TTQL模式为主驱动（较仅形状基线+0.31严格F1）；精炼循环为低成本安全网。相较自动导出SHACL，TTQL严格F1高64%。两个本地35B 4-bit开源权重模型在零边际成本下达到最强重算参赛者水平，且TTQL相对形状与SHACL基线优势持续。

Conclusion: 基于单个相对较小基准的结果提供了初步经验信号；主要局限是在非常开放的跨域图谱上进行单体式TTQL注入。方法证明经验填充模式优于单纯schema暴露，具备零成本本地部署潜力。

Abstract: Natural-language access to RDF knowledge graphs is a core Semantic Web ambition. Large language models (LLMs) have advanced Text-to-SPARQL, yet on unfamiliar graphs they often generate valid queries that misrepresent the populated data model. QRAKEN is a training-free, ontology-agnostic neurosymbolic pipeline grounding generation in empirical graph evidence rather than schema expectations. An offline distiller produces TTQL, a compact description of populated multi-hop patterns, conditional frequencies and path-conditioned literal examples, plus a class-property co-occurrence matrix. Online, TTQL guides the LLM, while deterministic syntax, vocabulary and data-model checks provide diagnostics for iterative refinement. On CK25 (First International Text2SPARQL Challenge), under matched-condition recomputation on a QLever snapshot, QRAKEN achieves strict F1 of 0.643 $\pm$ 0.026 with GPT-4.1 mini and 0.652 $\pm$ 0.012 with GPT-5.4: relative gains of 30% and 32% over the strongest recomputed participant, outperforming systems using the same base model family. Ablations identify TTQL patterns as the dominant driver (+0.31 strict F1 over a shape-only baseline); the refinement loop provides a cheap safety net, rejecting triple patterns unsupported by the co-occurrence matrix. Compared with auto-derived SHACL, TTQL yields 64% higher strict F1, supporting the value of empirical patterns beyond schema exposure. With two local 35B 4-bit open-weight models at zero marginal cost, the same pipeline matches the strongest recomputed participant, and TTQL advantages over shape-only and SHACL baselines persist. Results on a single, relatively small benchmark provide an initial empirical signal; monolithic TTQL injection on very open cross-domain graphs remains the main limitation.

</details>


### [343] [Beyond Corrected Memory: Execution Consistency in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08101)
*Zhe Yu,Zixuan Wang,Peidong Wang,Hehai Lin,Ruochen Zhao,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"执行一致性"定义，主张相同的记忆记录可能对应合规与违规两种执行，需保留回执、动作依赖、响应有效性等执行证据才能可靠判定任务履约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享记忆能协调智能体行为，但记录正确并不等于执行满足任务要求；记忆治理与故障诊断只规范或检查记录内容，无法本身判断记录是否足以判定任务职责，因此需要明确执行一致性的判定标准。

Method: 以支配状态使用、信息交接与最终状态一致性的"职责"定义执行一致性，并给出判定履约的显式证据条件；通过受控移除回执、动作依赖、响应有效性等证据进行消融分析，并用自然日志标注验证定义违反情况；进而构建CAVERT框架，从日志中抽取受支持的关系并应用上述准则。

Result: 受控移除证据后，82.4%的相反标签对变得不可区分；恢复证据后，97.9%的合并对被区分开。自然日志标注能在真实执行中识别所定义的违规。在全部12个基准-执行器设置中，CAVERT在诊断上一致优于合同提示LLM与规则基线；在相同门控与执行器限制下，在全部4个评估环境中优于规则引导恢复。

Conclusion: 这些发现识别出智能体记忆与执行接口应保留的执行证据，以支持可靠的执行一致性判定。

Abstract: Shared memory coordinates agents' actions, but correct records do not establish that those actions satisfy task requirements. Memory governance and failure diagnosis regulate or inspect recorded information; they do not by themselves establish whether it is sufficient to judge task duties. We define execution consistency through duties governing state use, information handoffs, and final-state agreement, with explicit evidence conditions for judging fulfillment. Our core claim is that identical retained records can correspond to compliant and violating executions under the same task rule. Controlled removal of evidence such as receipt, action dependence, or response validity leaves 82.4% of opposite-label pairs indistinguishable; restoration separates 97.9% of the merged pairs. Natural-log annotations identify the defined violations in actual executions. However, existing logs do not always explicitly represent the execution relationships needed for these judgments. To assess the definition's practical value, we use CAVERT, a framework for consistency diagnosis and recovery, to extract supported relationships from logs and apply these criteria. It consistently outperforms contract-prompted LLM and rule-based baselines in diagnosis across all 12 benchmark-executor settings. Under the same gate and executor limits, it also outperforms rule-guided recovery in all four evaluated environments. These findings identify execution evidence that agent-memory and execution interfaces should preserve for reliable judgment.

</details>


### [344] [DSV-Mem: Evaluating Multimodal Memory in Professional Workflows for MLLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08102)
*Jike Zhong,Ritwick Chaudhry,Xuanbai Chen,Tianchen Zhao,Linghan Xu,Yifan Xing,Nishant Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出DSV-Mem，一个评估多模态大模型智能体"密集有状态视觉记忆"能力的基准，包含专家审校场景与1000道题，覆盖五类任务。最强基线得分不足45%，表明状态演化（尤其治理性更新数量）是主要难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式基准多聚焦非正式日常互动与个人生活场景，特征为摄影类自然图像、孤立静态工件与回忆型问题。而专业工作流涉及结构化、信息密集的工件，需频繁修订与权限更新，并需在多版本间进行组合式推理与精确状态追踪，这一能力尚缺乏评估。

Method: 提出DSV-Mem基准，含专家审校场景与1,000道题，分五类用户导向任务：当前状态、过去状态、派生状态、变更历史、冲突/拒答。采用受Hartley启发的准则，偏好需更广泛视觉证据检视的问题。同时提出生成框架，将状态转移合成与对话填充解耦以生成评测套件。共评估27种配置，涵盖前沿与开源权重模型及记忆管理方法。

Result: 最强基线在DSV-Mem上得分低于45%。分析发现：1）多模态性与信息密度均增加难度，但状态演化（尤其治理性更新数量）是所测试的主导因素；原始对话/干草堆长度、OCR与算术并非主要瓶颈；2）模型常在回答前未对照先前状态更新核验用户前提；3）增加推理投入与记忆管理方法收益有限，而状态感知设计更有效。

Conclusion: DSV-Mem填补了专业场景下密集有状态视觉记忆评估的空白，揭示状态演化而非长度或OCR是核心难点，且状态感知设计优于单纯增加推理或记忆管理。基准与代码将公开释放。

Abstract: Conversational MLLM agents are increasingly expected to assist in professional workflows, from AI research and engineering design to product management and business operations. Yet this capability remains underexplored: existing benchmarks largely focus on informal, everyday interactions and personal-life scenarios featuring photographic natural images, isolated static artifacts, and recall-oriented questions. In contrast, professional scenarios often involve structured, information-heavy artifacts that undergo frequent revisions and authority updates, and compositional queries requiring reconciliation of many artifact versions while tracking state precisely. To address these challenges, we introduce DSV-Mem, a benchmark for evaluating Dense Stateful Visual Memory. DSV-Mem comprises expert-reviewed scenarios and 1,000 questions across five user-oriented categories (Current State, Past State, Derived State, Change History, and Conflict/Refusal). A Hartley-inspired criterion favors questions with broader visual-evidence inspection demands. We also introduce a generation harness that produces evaluation suites by decoupling state-transition synthesis from conversation filling. Evaluation over 27 configurations spanning frontier and open-weight models and memory management methods reveals that the strongest baseline scores below 45% on DSV-Mem. Analysis surfaces findings: 1) multimodality and information density both contribute to difficulty, but state evolution, particularly the number of governing updates, is the dominant tested factor. Raw conversation/haystack length, OCR, and arithmetic are not the primary bottlenecks; 2) models often fail to verify user premises against prior state updates before answering; 3) increased reasoning effort and memory management methods yield limited gains, whereas state-aware designs prove more effective. The benchmark and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [345] [ChartBmkAgent: Harness-Governed Multi-Agent Construction of Chart QA Benchmarks from Sparse Error-Taxonomy Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08106)
*Langxi Huang,Pingping Zhang,Lanyun Zhu,Chunyang Jiang,Jiawei Shao,Haocheng Yuan,Peilin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ChartBmkAgent，从稀疏错误分类法规格出发自动构造完整 Chart QA 诊断样本，以按需生成对齐指定能力缺口的目标化评测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 能力快速演进而基准建设滞后，难以及时探究新出现的能力缺口。此类探究需要富表达力的任务格式与按需构造流程，Chart QA 因信息丰富的图表适合探测感知-推理耦合。现有自动构造方法将目标引导与从零生成割裂：目标引导系统依赖预置数据、图表或模板，从零生成系统主要保证产物合法性，未显式控制新合成的要求与内容是否与外部指定的诊断目标保持一致。

Method: ChartBmkAgent 以中心化 harness 统筹多个专用智能体，从稀疏错误分类法规格构造完整 Chart QA 样本；构造过程中要求各阶段提供与原始错误类别对齐的证据，并记录每项接受决策的依据，从而将识别出的能力缺口转化为目标化诊断证据。

Result: 在 300 个覆盖全分类法的样本上，MLLM 准确率为 32.7%–84.3%，且呈现不同的类别画像，表明生成样本可揭示能力差异。三组源模型对比中，目标化后续样本得分 50.0% 对匹配对照组 82.2%（p=8.96×10⁻⁶）；全部六组跨模型对比方向一致，验证了目标化验证与诊断数据生成的有效性。多评估模型判定 86.4% 的样本确实测得其指定错误类别，为目标保持性提供实证依据。

Conclusion: ChartBmkAgent 通过带证据的阶段化控制实现了目标保持的按需 Chart QA 基准构造，可有效揭示 MLLM 能力差异并支持目标化诊断验证，缩短基准开发周期。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) advance rapidly, while conventional benchmark development lags behind, delaying investigation of newly observed capability gaps. Such investigation requires an expressive task format and an on-demand construction process: information-rich charts make chart question answering (Chart QA) suitable for probing coupled perception and reasoning. Automated Chart QA construction is intended to shorten the benchmark-development cycle by turning identified gaps into targeted samples on demand. Current methods, however, commonly separate target guidance from scratch generation: target-guided systems often require prepared data, charts, or templates, while scratch-generation systems primarily ensure artifact validity, without explicitly controlling whether newly synthesized requirements and content remain aligned with an externally specified diagnostic target. We introduce ChartBmkAgent, which turns an identified capability gap into targeted diagnostic evidence by constructing complete Chart QA samples from sparse error-taxonomy specifications. Throughout construction, a central harness governs specialized agents, requires stage-specific evidence of alignment with the original error category, and records the basis for each acceptance decision. On 300 taxonomy-wide samples, MLLM accuracies ranged from 32.7% to 84.3% with distinct category profiles, showing that generated samples reveal capability differences. Across three source-model comparisons, targeted follow-ups scored 50.0% versus 82.2% on matched controls ($p=8.96\times10^{-6}$); all six cross-model comparisons had the same direction, demonstrating targeted validation and diagnostic-data generation. Multiple evaluator models assessed whether each sample tested its specified error category; 86.4% met this criterion, providing empirical evidence of target preservation.

</details>


### [346] [Test-Time Agent Evolution for Long-Horizon Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08138)
*Haotian Chen,Shuaicheng Niu,Haocong Rao,Kaisong Song,Jun Lin,Lizhen Cui,Zhiqi Shen,Yonghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文面向长周期法律智能决策，提出免训练的测试时智能体自适应框架，包含"测试时记忆演化"与"准则对齐协作"两个组件，在J1-EVAL和LegalWorld上跨五种基座模型取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实法律部署中案件在事实、证据与程序情境上高度异质，静态智能体策略难以适应；同时法律推理在角色与程序阶段间本质相互依赖，全局可靠性不等同于孤立角色能力的叠加。

Method: 研究免训练的测试时智能体自适应：提出\method，其一是测试时记忆演化——从历史案件检索可复用经验、适配当前事实与程序情境、并巩固累积经验以供后续决策；其二是准则对齐协作——依据行为与程序准则校验并修订角色特定动作，实现跨角色与跨阶段协同决策。

Result: 在J1-EVAL与LegalWorld两个基准、五种基座模型上，相较代表性推理与智能体基线取得一致提升，且交互与计算开销合理。消融与案例研究表明两个组件在经验适配与跨角色协同上互补。

Conclusion: 测试时记忆演化与准则对齐协作互补，可提升长周期法律推理的可靠性与效率，验证了免训练测试时自适应在法律智能中的有效性。

Abstract: Legal intelligence aims to support reliable decision-making across long-horizon legal processes involving evolving case states and multiple roles. However, real-world legal deployment exhibits substantial case heterogeneity in facts, evidence, and procedural contexts, exposing the limitations of static agent strategies. Moreover, legal reasoning is inherently interdependent across roles and procedural stages, making global reliability fundamentally different from isolated role competence. To address these challenges, we study training-free test-time agent adaptation, where agents continuously exploit deployment-time signals from preceding cases and ongoing interactions without updating model parameters. We propose \method, which introduces \emph{Test-Time Memory Evolution} to retrieve reusable experience from previous cases, adapt it to the current factual and procedural context, and consolidate accumulated experience for subsequent decision-making. Further, \emph{Rubric-Aligned Collaboration} verifies and revises role-specific actions according to behavioral and procedural requirements, enabling coordinated decision-making across roles and stages. Extensive experiments on J1-EVAL and LegalWorld across five backbone models demonstrate consistent improvements over representative reasoning and agent baselines with reasonable interaction and computational costs. Ablation and case studies further show that the two components provide complementary benefits in experience adaptation and cross-role coordination, improving the reliability and efficiency of long-horizon legal reasoning.

</details>


### [347] [Partially Observable Zero-shot coordination by Predicting Intention of Partner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08142)
*Jinnyeong Yang,Yuhwan Jeong,Hoyong Kwon,Minseok Kim,Jihun Kim,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PIP方法，通过Joint-view VAE与伙伴状态信念网络联合解决具身零样本协作中伙伴间歇性不可见导致的表征模糊与隐藏状态不确定问题，在三个基准及人类评估中取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身环境下的零样本协作要求智能体在伙伴间歇性离开视野时仍能行动，现有方法因此面临伙伴表征模糊以及对伙伴隐藏状态的不确定性两大挑战。

Method: PIP包含两个组件：Joint-view VAE，从双方局部观测的并集中蒸馏更丰富的训练期证据，得到仅凭本地观测即可获得的伙伴表征；伙伴状态信念网络（Partner-state Belief networks），从自我智能体的交互历史中推断伙伴的隐藏位置与行为倾向。

Result: 在Burrito-PO、Overcooked-PO以及一个Melting Pot子环境三个基准上，PIP在所有对比方法中取得最高平均性能；人类评估与诊断分析进一步支持其与未见伙伴的协作能力，以及伙伴被遮挡时两个组件各自的贡献。

Conclusion: PIP通过联合建模伙伴表征与隐藏状态，有效缓解伙伴间歇性不可见带来的协作困难，在多种基准与人类评估中验证了其有效性与组件贡献。

Abstract: Zero-shot coordination in embodied settings requires acting while the partner is intermittently out of view, leaving existing methods with ambiguous partner representations and uncertainty over hidden partner states. We propose Predicting Intention of Partner (PIP) to jointly address these challenges. PIP uses a Joint-view VAE to distill richer training-time evidence from the union of both agents' local observations into a partner representation available from local observations alone. Partner-state Belief networks further infer the partner's hidden location and behavioral tendencies from the ego agent's interaction history. We evaluate PIP in Burrito-PO, Overcooked-PO, and a Melting Pot substrate, together with a human evaluation in Burrito-PO. PIP attains the highest mean performance among the compared methods across all three benchmarks. Human evaluation and diagnostic analyses further support coordination with unseen partners and the contributions of both components under partner occlusion.

</details>


### [348] [Which alloy composition,what process parameters? Inferring the recipe from optimized metallic microstructure and texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08165)
*Mahish K. Guru,Jan Bohlen,Louam Lemjid,Marius Tacke,Roland Aydin,Noomane Ben Khalifa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在107组镁合金挤压条件数据上比较三类微结构与织构描述符，发现传统晶粒/织构统计量最能反推合金成分（65% vs 基线17%），学习型嵌入反而低于30%；工艺参数可部分恢复，微结构使温度误差减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合金开发正向链路（配方→结构→性能）已成熟，但从优化结构反推配方仍依赖专家经验；本文要检验这一逆向步骤是否可学习。

Method: 使用内部镁合金挤压数据集（14种合金、107个挤压条件），每个条件配光学显微图与X射线织构测量；对比三种微结构/织构描述符：传统晶粒与织构统计量、预训练图像编码器的视觉嵌入、晶粒网络上的图神经网络；每种描述符配两个预测头：由工艺预测合金成分（Task A）、由成分预测工艺参数（Task B）；采用5折交叉验证，并比较离散选项分类头与自由值回归头（含顺序约束）。

Result: 传统描述符在留出条件上正确识别合金的比例为65%，恒猜最常见合金为17%；学习型嵌入低于30%。工艺参数可恢复但噪声更大：相比仅用成分，加入微结构使温度误差大致减半。因合金与压机设置均为少数离散取值，尊重其顺序的离散选项选择头优于自由值预测头。

Conclusion: 微结构-织构到配方的逆向映射可部分学习，但简单传统统计量优于当前学习型表征；任务本质是离散且有序的，应采用带顺序约束的离散选择建模。

Abstract: The mechanical properties of a metallic alloy are set by its microstructure and texture: the size and shape of its grains and the orientation of their crystals. That structure is in turn set by a recipe, the alloy composition together with the processing parameters. Alloy development runs this chain forwards, tuning the structure until a target property is met. Running it backwards, from an optimized structure to the recipe that would produce it, still relies on expert knowledge. We ask whether this backwards step can be learned. On an in-house dataset of 107 magnesium alloy extrusion conditions across 14 alloys, each with optical micrographs and an X-ray texture measurement, we compare three descriptors of microstructure and texture: conventional grain and texture statistics, a vision embedding from a pretrained image encoder, and a graph neural network on the grain network. Each is paired with prediction heads for two tasks: the alloy composition given the process (Task A), and the process parameters given the composition (Task B). Under 5-fold cross-validation, the conventional descriptors identify the correct alloy for 65% of held-out conditions, against 17% for always guessing the most common alloy, while the learned embeddings stay below 30%. The process parameters are recoverable but noisier: compared with using the composition alone, the microstructure roughly halves the temperature error. Because only a few alloys were cast and only a few press settings were used, both answers are discrete, and heads that pick from these known options, while respecting their order, worked better than heads that predict a free value.

</details>


### [349] [LFHE: Local-First Heuristic Evolution for Bounded Local Topology Search in Decentralized Learning with Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08176)
*Yin-Kuan Liang,Yan Gao,Yang Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LFHE（Local-First Heuristic Evolution），一种仅依赖自我邻域与朋友的朋友（FoF）信息的表示驱动重连框架，在有限局部拓扑搜索下实现去中心化学习中的自适应对等选择，避免图级或全局状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习在非IID数据下对通信拓扑高度敏感。自适应对等选择可利用局部模型信息，但更广的对等发现需要越来越大的控制状态，而直接谱优化通常依赖图全局信息。因此需要研究介于逐对选择与全局拓扑优化之间的受限局部拓扑搜索设置。

Method: LFHE的候选发现与打分仅使用自我邻域和FoF信息。其结构分数通过图Dirichlet能量获得精确解释：各客户端结构分数之和等于两倍的表示Dirichlet能量，在标准线性共识动力学下支配表示分歧的瞬时耗散。LFHE将这种状态相关结构信号与早期探索和度控制相结合，代数连通度仅作为离线图诊断量。在受限稀疏度下，FoF候选状态保持局部，不扩展为全群体对等跟踪。

Result: 在四个图像、语音和文本基准上，LFHE取得有竞争力的去中心化学习性能。匹配协议对照表明结构项是主要的经验性拓扑选择信号；与更广对等发现的比较揭示出预测性能与发现状态局部性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 结果支持在逐对等选择与全局知情拓扑优化之间，采用状态感知的受限局部拓扑搜索。

Abstract: Decentralized learning is highly sensitive to communication topology under non-IID data. Adaptive peer-selection methods can exploit local model information, but broader peer discovery may require increasingly large control state, whereas direct spectral optimization typically relies on graph-wide information. We study the intermediate setting of bounded local topology search and propose Local-First Heuristic Evolution (LFHE), a representation-driven rewiring framework whose candidate discovery and scoring use only ego-neighborhood and friend-of-a-friend (FoF) information. The structural score admits an exact interpretation through graph Dirichlet energy: its sum across clients equals twice the representation Dirichlet energy, which under standard linear consensus dynamics governs the instantaneous dissipation of representation disagreement. LFHE combines this state-dependent structural signal with early exploration and degree control, while algebraic connectivity remains an offline graph diagnostic. Under bounded sparse degree, its FoF candidate state remains local rather than expanding toward population-wide peer tracking. Across four image, speech, and text benchmarks, LFHE achieves competitive decentralized learning performance. Matched-protocol controls identify the structural term as the principal empirical topology-selection signal, while comparison with broader peer discovery exposes a trade-off between predictive performance and discovery-state locality. Together, these results motivate state-aware bounded local topology search between pairwise peer selection and globally informed topology optimization.

</details>


### [350] [Mathematical Proof Assistants for Teaching Logic: The LogiKEy Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08214)
*Christoph Benzmüller,David Fuenmayor,Luca Pasetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍基于LogiKEy方法论的逻辑教学法：以经典高阶逻辑(HOL)作为通用元逻辑，通过语义嵌入将各类对象逻辑（经典与非经典）编码于单一证明助手(如Isabelle/HOL)中，使计算机科学、数学与哲学混合背景的学生能够在同一环境中学习、实验并比较不同逻辑系统，并以递进式课堂案例展示该教学路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向计算机科学、数学与哲学等混合背景学生的逻辑教学面临困难：不同逻辑系统通常需要不同工具与形式化框架，学生难以在同一环境中进行对比与实验。作者希望论证证明助手进入逻辑课堂的教学价值，并回应"将一切嵌入经典HOL是以一元论取代多元论"的质疑。

Method: 采用逻辑多元主义的LogiKEy方法论（已用于课程、暑期学校与辅导课十余年）。以经典高阶逻辑(HOL)作为通用元逻辑，通过定义语义对经典与非经典对象逻辑进行语义嵌入，使单一证明助手（如Isabelle/HOL）及其自动定理证明器与（反）模型查找器成为一个统一的学习、实验与比较逻辑的环境。教学上设计分层递进的课堂案例序列，每一环节的过渡由前一表示的局限、对更显式建模资源的需求或新应用所驱动：说谎者与讲真话者谜题（命题逻辑→模态逻辑）、智者谜题（→动态认知逻辑）、Boolos的奇特推理（说明高阶元逻辑的价值甚至对自动证明搜索的意义）、Chisholm悖论（→道义逻辑，并从标准道义逻辑到二元道义逻辑）、哥德尔本体论论证（→研究级形而上学论证）。

Result: 作者反驳了"将一切嵌入经典HOL是一元论而非多元论"的反对意见，反思了三年此类课程的教学经验，并概述了该方法超越Isabelle的可移植性。

Conclusion: LogiKEy式的语义嵌入路径为混合背景学生的逻辑教学提供了可行且具扩展性的方案：单证明助手环境即可支撑从命题逻辑到研究级形而上学论证的递进式学习与逻辑比较，且该方法论可移植至Isabelle之外的平台。

Abstract: We report on an approach to teaching logic to mixed groups of computer science, mathematics, and philosophy students, based on the logico-pluralistic LogiKEy methodology, used for more than a decade in courses, summer schools, and tutorials. LogiKEy uses classical higher-order logic (HOL) as a universal metalogic in which object logics, classical and non-classical alike, are encoded by defining their semantics; through these semantical embeddings a single proof assistant (e.g. Isabelle/HOL), with its automated theorem provers and (counter-)model finders, becomes one environment in which students learn, experiment with, and compare logics. After making the pedagogical case for proof assistants in the logic classroom, we present a graded sequence of classroom examples, each transition motivated by a limitation of the preceding representation, by a need for more explicit modelling resources, or by a new application. A liars-and-truth-tellers puzzle leads from propositional to modal logic; the Wise Men puzzle leads on to dynamic epistemic logic; Boolos's curious inference illustrates what a higher-order meta-logic buys, even for automated proof search; Chisholm's paradox takes the sequence into deontic logic, and from standard to dyadic deontic logic; and Gödel's ontological argument brings it to a research-level metaphysical argument. We then rebut the objection that embedding everything in classical HOL is monism rather than pluralism, reflect on three years of teaching such a course, and sketch the portability of the approach beyond Isabelle.

</details>


### [351] [Learn2Play Bench: How Well Do LLM Agents Learn from Experience in Unfamiliar Environments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08215)
*Yibo Li,Jinhang Qiu,Zhi Zheng,Qianyun Guo,Jiaying Wu,Shuo Ji,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Learn2Play Bench，一个由规则新颖或反直觉的文本游戏组成的基准，用于评估 LLM 智能体从交互经验中学习的能力，并基于实验得出三条关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经验中学习是 LLM 智能体适应陌生、动态环境的关键能力。现有基准多使用指令中已给出规则或预训练模型已熟悉的游戏，难以区分“从交互中学习”与“使用既有知识推理”，因此需要新的评测手段。

Method: 作者设计了规则新颖或反直觉的文本游戏，要求智能体通过交互获取知识；游戏提供可复现的反馈与自动评分，支持对多次尝试中的学习过程进行受控评测；同时通过改变游戏实例来测试知识迁移能力。实验评估了骨干模型、自进化方法和智能体框架（harness）对学习能力的影响。

Result: 三条发现：(1) 经验保留——保留完整的动作与反馈记录比将其总结为规则或策略更有利于学习；(2) 人机差距——顶尖人类玩家峰值分数高于所评估智能体，且策略更多样、重复动作更少；(3) 框架影响——在固定骨干模型的情况下，更换框架可提升性能并降低估计推理成本。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解 LLM 智能体如何从经验中学习提供了新基准与实证洞见，并指出了提升其学习能力的未来方向。

Abstract: Learning from experience is essential for LLM agents to adapt to unfamiliar and dynmaic environments. Evaluating this ability is therefore important for understanding how effectively agents acquire and use new knowledge. Existing benchmarks have sought to evaluate this ability, but they primarily evaluate tasks whose rules are provided in the instructions or already familiar to pretrained models, making it difficult to distinguish learning from interactions from reasoning with existing knowledge. To address this, we introduce Learn2Play Bench, a benchmark of newly designed text-based games, whose rules are novel or counterintuitive, requiring agents to acquire knowledge through interaction rather than rely solely on pretrained knowledge. These games provide reproducible feedback and automatic scoring, enabling controlled evaluation of learning across repeated attempts. We also vary game instances to test whether agents can apply what they have learned to new situations. Therefore, we evaluate how backbone models, self-evolving methods, and agent harnesses affect agents' learning ability, revealing three findings: (1) Experience retention: Retaining complete records of actions and feedback can support more effective learning than summarizing these experiences into rules or strategies. (2) Human agent gap: Top-performing human players achieve higher peak scores than the evaluated agents. Human explore more varied strategies, and repeat actions less. (3) Harness matters: With the backbone fixed, changing the harness can improve performance while reducing estimated inference cost. Together, these findings provide insights into how LLM agents learn from experience and suggest directions for future work to improve their learning ability. Project website: https://liushiliushi.github.io/learn2play-bench-website/

</details>


### [352] [Quantum Entangled Multimodal Fusion Networks (QEMFN): Resource-Aware Hybrid Vision-Language Fusion via Trainable Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08216)
*Srikar Alla,Ali Shiri Sichani,Chi-Ren Shyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出量子纠缠多模态融合网络（QEMFN），将参数化纠缠作为多模态融合的结构化归纳偏置，在匹配参数预算和冻结CLIP骨干下，于COCO-5k与Flickr30k检索任务上超越多种经典融合基线，并通过量子中心化分析与真实超导设备验证其可行性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视觉-语言系统通常采用拼接、注意力、双线性池化或张量交互等经典算子融合图像与文本嵌入，缺乏对融合机制的新颖归纳偏置与可解释性。作者希望探索将可训练纠缠作为融合的结构化归纳偏置，并在当代量子硬件可及规模上评估其效果，而非主张量子计算优势。

Method: QEMFN为混合量子-经典框架：将预训练视觉与文本特征投影至紧凑潜空间，编码为角度参数化量子态；经模态内与成对跨模态纠缠电路处理；测量得到融合表示用于检索。在匹配参数预算、相同冻结CLIP骨干下与经典基线对比，并通过消融实验剥离量子模块贡献；量子中心化分析涵盖Meyer-Wallach纠缠能力、可表达性、梯度方差与贫瘠高原界、熵-性能相关性（控制训练进度）及纠缠组件干预研究；执行于基于采样的估计、噪声建模的模拟后端及带零噪声外推的真实超导设备。

Result: 在COCO-5k与Flickr30k上，QEMFN优于多层感知机、张量融合、FiLM、交叉注意力、紧凑Transformer及去量子化的成对拓扑对照等经典融合基线；消融验证量子模块的贡献；量子中心化指标给出纠缠能力、可表达性与梯度行为；并在真实超导设备上通过零噪声外推成功执行。

Conclusion: 该工作不主张量子计算优势，其贡献在于提出QEMFN框架及受控的经验与量子中心化评估，将可训练纠缠定位为一种可解释、可在硬件执行的融合机制，且规模适用于当代设备。

Abstract: Multimodal vision-language systems typically fuse image and text embeddings through classical operators such as concatenation, attention, bilinear pooling, or tensor interactions. We propose Quantum Entangled Multimodal Fusion Networks (QEMFN), a hybrid quantum-classical framework that introduces parameterized entanglement as a structured inductive bias for multimodal fusion. Pretrained visual and textual features are projected into compact latent spaces, encoded as angle-parameterized quantum states, processed through intra-modal and paired cross-modal entangling circuits, and measured to produce fused representations for retrieval. Under matched parameter budgets and identical frozen CLIP backbones, QEMFN outperforms classical fusion baselines on COCO-5k and Flickr30k, including multilayer perceptron, tensor fusion, FiLM, cross-attention, compact transformer, and a dequantized paired-topology analogue. An ablation suite isolates the quantum module's contribution from the surrounding classical projections, and quantum-centric analyses report Meyer-Wallach entangling capability, expressibility, gradient variance against barren-plateau bounds, and entropy-performance correlation under controls for training progress alongside an intervention study on the entangling component. QEMFN is executed under shot-based estimation, a noise-modeled fake backend, and a real superconducting device with zero-noise extrapolation. This work does not claim quantum computational advantage; the contribution is the framework together with a controlled empirical and quantum-centric evaluation that positions trainable entanglement as an interpretable, hardware-executable fusion mechanism at scales accessible on contemporary devices.

</details>


### [353] [OSFP4: Joint Optimization of Diagonal Smoothing and Block Scales for NVFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08231)
*Neriah Ben David,Ori Meir,Or Ordentlich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OSFP4量化方案，通过联合优化对角平滑矩阵与块缩放来最小化NVFP4矩阵乘量化误差，在保持约94-97%厂商预填充吞吐的同时取得最高平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVFP4具备紧凑存储与原生张量核加速优势，适合大模型推理，但要保持精度需要精细的量化设计；现有量化方案在NVFP4下精度不足。

Method: 针对每个线性投影引入对角平滑矩阵，其元素以最小化NVFP4下矩阵乘量化平方误差为目标进行优化，并考虑所用舍入方式（最近舍入或GPTQ式逐次干扰消除）；将平滑元素与块缩放进行联合优化，通过分析一个乘性抖动FP4量化器（而非固定确定性量化器）使优化可行。

Result: 在相应量化设置下，OSFP4在所评估的对比方法中取得最高平均精度，并在实测工作负载上保持约94-97%的厂商NVFP4预填充吞吐。代码已开源。

Conclusion: OSFP4是一种面向NVFP4的高精度量化方案，兼顾精度与推理效率，可作为LLM推理实用量化方法。

Abstract: NVFP4 is an attractive datatype for large language model (LLM) inference, offering compact storage and native tensor-core acceleration. However, preserving accuracy using NVFP4 requires careful quantization. In this work we develop a novel quantization scheme called Optimized Smoothing and Scaling for NVFP4 (OSFP4). For each linear projection it uses a diagonal smoothing matrix whose entries are optimized to minimize the squared matrix-product quantization error under NVFP4, taking into account the rounding procedure that is used (either round-to-nearest, or GPTQ-style successive interference cancellation). This requires performing joint optimization on the smoothing entries as well as the block scales, which is facilitated by analyzing a multiplicative-dither FP4 quantizer instead of the fixed deterministic one. Experiments show that OSFP4 achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated competitors in the corresponding quantization settings, while retaining approximately 94-97\% of vendor NVFP4 prefill throughput on the measured workloads. Our code is available in https://github.com/neriahbd/OSFP4

</details>


### [354] [Sensor-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08244)
*Yuekai Xu,Zitao Shuai,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Sensor-Language-Action (SLA) 建模框架，以语言为语义接口统一多模态传感器感知与动作决策，并构建大规模基准与 OpenSLA 模型，在临床预测、手术室和代谢健康任务上超越现有方法，展现零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器模型大多止步于感知，仅做状态识别或结果预测，动作被单独建模，依赖任务特定且封闭的标签空间，导致感知与行动割裂、动作表示难以跨域迁移和解释。

Method: 提出 SLA 建模框架，将多模态传感器观测、自然语言与动作集成于统一模型，用语言作为感知与行动之间的语义接口，使异构动作可被表示、预测和解释，同时仍锚定于底层传感器证据；构建覆盖 116,000+ 个体、79 种传感器模态、60 个动作组的大规模基准及多维度描述生成（captioning）流程，对齐用户上下文、传感器动态与动作证据；基于此提出统一模型 OpenSLA，支持分层动作预测、状态理解与动作解释。

Result: 在临床预测、手术室与代谢健康等真实世界任务上的大量实验表明，OpenSLA 性能优于当前最先进方法；并展现出语言引导的证据定位（evidence grounding）以及对未见动作和人群的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: SLA 框架与 OpenSLA 模型验证了以语言为语义接口统一传感与行动建模范式的有效性，为跨模态、跨任务的感知-行动一体化建模及可解释、可泛化的动作预测提供了新方向。

Abstract: Sensors are useful not only for understanding the world but also for deciding what to do next. Existing sensor models however largely stop at perception: they recognize states or predict outcomes, leaving actions modeled separately through task-specific and often closed label spaces. We introduce Sensor-Language-Action (SLA) modeling, a framework that connects multimodal sensor observations, natural language, and actions within a unified model. SLA uses language as a semantic interface between sensing and acting, allowing heterogeneous actions to be represented, predicted, and explained while remaining grounded in the underlying sensor evidence. We build a large-scale SLA benchmark consisting of datasets that span more than 116,000 individuals, 79 sensor modalities, and 60 action groups, together with a multi-faceted captioning pipeline that aligns user context, sensor dynamics, and action evidence. Building on this framework, we present OpenSLA, a unified SLA model for hierarchical action prediction, state understanding, and action explanation. Extensive experiments on real-world tasks in clinical prediction, operating rooms, and metabolic health verify its superior performance over the state-of-the-art. OpenSLA also demonstrates intriguing capabilities including language-guided evidence grounding and zero-shot generalization to unseen actions and cohorts.

</details>


### [355] [LeanPlan: Optimal Planning with LLM-Generated Heuristics and Admissibility Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08246)
*André G. Pereira,Augusto B. Corrêa,Felipe Meneguzzi,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanPlan是首个利用LLM生成启发式函数并对其可采纳性进行机器验证的规划系统，能在可满足规划中获得最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM可生成引导搜索的启发式函数，在可满足规划中达到SOTA，但这些启发式不保证可采纳性，可能导致次优解。因此需要一种方法在利用LLM生成启发式的同时保证其可采纳性并得到最优规划。

Method: 给定领域描述与训练任务，使用agentic loop结合规划器反馈迭代改进可复用的领域特定启发式、其可采纳性证明及所需领域假设。LeanPlan在Lean 4中实现了该启发式、其证明以及具备机器验证的grounding与搜索的高效规划器。

Result: 在IPC十个领域及三个新领域上评估，测试任务对象数最多达训练任务的57倍。使用GPT-5.6 Sol在agentic loop中，成功为所有领域生成启发式及其可采纳性证明。所得启发式下，LeanPlan通常比SOTA的Scorpion规划器扩展更少状态，并整体解决更多任务。

Conclusion: LeanPlan证明机器验证的可采纳性证明可与LLM生成的启发式结合，从而在最优规划中达到优于SOTA的性能。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) can generate heuristic functions that guide search to achieve state-of-the-art performance in satisficing planning, where any plan is acceptable. However, these heuristics are not guaranteed to be admissible and can lead to suboptimal plans. We introduce LeanPlan, the first planning system that finds optimal plans with LLM-generated heuristics whose admissibility is machine-checked. Given a domain description and training tasks, an agentic loop uses planner feedback to iteratively improve a reusable domain-specific heuristic, its admissibility proof and the required domain assumptions. LeanPlan implements the heuristic, its proof and an efficient planner with machine-checked grounding and search in Lean 4. We evaluate LeanPlan on ten domains from the International Planning Competition and three new domains, using test tasks with up to 57 times as many objects as the training tasks. With GPT-5.6 Sol in the agentic loop, we successfully generate heuristics and admissibility proofs for all these domains. With the resulting heuristics, LeanPlan usually expands fewer states than the state-of-the-art Scorpion planner and solves more tasks overall.

</details>


### [356] [MASC: A Multi-Agent Self-Calibration Framework with Latent Construct Alignment for Consistent Client Role-Playing in Psychological Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08250)
*Shixin Peng,Kun Jiang,Jiaxing Zheng,Qihao Yang,Jingying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MASC 多智能体自校准框架与 CRPC-Bench 基准，用于心理咨商中一致性的来访者角色扮演与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被用于来访者模拟以支持咨询师培训与心理咨商研究，但可靠模拟要求来访者在长程交互中保持心理一致性。现有角色扮演方法多依赖静态画像提示，易出现人设漂移、不真实配合，以及心理状态、沟通行为与情绪的不一致；同时缺乏统一测试平台来同时评估稳定的来访者特征与动态心理演化。

Method: 提出 MASC（Multi-Agent Self-Calibration）框架，结合潜构念对齐，通过构念引导生成、协同精炼、一致性验证与基于记忆的修正构成闭环校准回路，在对话展开过程中检测并纠正不一致。同时提出 CRPC-Bench 基准，覆盖会话级画像信息与大五人格特质，以及轮级心理状态、沟通行为与情绪表达；基准包含 38 个动机式访谈来访者画像，并附人格与情绪标注。

Result: 实验表明，MASC 在画像、人格、接受性（receptivity）与轮级一致性上均优于现有方法，其中异构配置（heterogeneous configuration）取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: MASC 与 CRPC-Bench 为 AI 辅助咨商研究与培训中开发与评估心理一致的来访者模拟提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate clients for counselor training and psychological counseling research, but reliable simulation requires clients to remain psychologically coherent across extended interactions. Existing role-playing methods largely rely on static profile prompts and may exhibit persona drift, unrealistic cooperativeness, or inconsistent psychological states, communicative actions, and emotions. Existing evaluations also lack a unified testbed for both stable client characteristics and evolving psychological dynamics. We propose MASC, a Multi-Agent Self-Calibration framework with latent construct alignment for consistent client role-playing in psychological counseling. MASC combines construct-guided generation, collaborative refinement, consistency verification, and memory-based revision in a closed calibration loop that detects and corrects inconsistencies as dialogue unfolds. We further introduce CRPC-Bench, a benchmark covering session-level profile information and Big-Five personality traits, as well as turn-level psychological state, communicative action, and emotion expression. CRPC-Bench contains 38 motivational interviewing client profiles augmented with personality and emotion annotations. Experiments show that MASC outperforms existing methods across profile, personality, receptivity, and turn-level consistency, with the heterogeneous configuration achieving the strongest overall performance. MASC and CRPC-Bench provide a unified foundation for developing and evaluating psychologically coherent client simulations for AI-assisted counseling research and training.

</details>


### [357] [zkLLMPoT: Efficient Zero Knowledge Proof of Training for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08258)
*Junkai Liang,Zhanpeng Guo,Pengfei Wu,Qingni Shen,Jiaheng Zhang,Zhonghai Wu,Haiyang Xue,Shengfang Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 zkLLMPoT 零知识框架，通过前向评估证明已训练检查点在审计者指定序列上的目标值，认证成本与训练迭代数无关且不泄露模型权重或私有数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练结果审计困难：权重与训练数据私有，而完整训练过程的密码学证明在 Transformer 规模下成本过高。需要一种不依赖训练轨迹、成本可控的审计方法。

Method: 两阶段协议：1) 训练方固定架构并承诺模型权重，审计方随即选取挑战序列，防止训练方根据审计数据篡改检查点；2) 训练方证明承诺模型在这些序列上达到的目标值。基于 sumcheck 与 lookup 论证来证明 Transformer 计算，支持下一 token 损失及任务特定审计目标。

Result: 在四个模型家族上，算子级基准测试显示：1.1–1.5B 参数模型证明时间 41–59 秒，13B 模型覆盖算子证明时间 131 秒，序列长度 512 时验证时间低于 0.5 秒。

Conclusion: zkLLMPoT 将审计目标从前向轨迹验证转向前向评估认证，使证明成本与训练迭代数解耦，在保密权重与数据的前提下实现可行的大模型训练结果审计。

Abstract: Auditing the claimed outcomes of large language model (LLM) training is challenging when model weights and training data are private, while cryptographically proving the full training process is prohibitively expensive at Transformer scale. We present zkLLMPoT, a zero-knowledge framework that certifies auditor-defined properties of a trained checkpoint through forward evaluation rather than verification of its optimization trajectory. zkLLMPoT includes 2 phases: 1) The trainer fixes the architecture and the model weights are committed. Then the auditor selects challenge sequences, preventing the trainer from modifying the checkpoint in response to the audit data. 2) Then the trainer proves the objective value attained by the committed model on those sequences. This formulation makes the certification cost independent of the number of training iterations, without revealing model weights or requiring access to private training data. We build on sumcheck- and lookup-based arguments to certify Transformer computations, while supporting next-token loss and task-specific audit objectives. Across four model families, operator-level benchmarks yield proving times of 41-59 seconds for 1.1-1.5B-parameter models and 131 seconds at 13B for the covered operators, with verification below half a second at a sequence length of 512.

</details>


### [358] [CoDe-LoRA: Mitigating the Orthogonality Dilemma in Continual Learning of LLMs via Knowledge Consolidation and Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08312)
*Maoqi Liu,Quan Fang,Yufei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免回放的CoDe-LoRA方法，将持续学习解耦为通用知识巩固与任务特定知识解耦，缓解正交投影带来的"正交性困境"，在四个骨干模型和三个基准上取得最佳平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需顺序适应不断演化的任务，持续学习至关重要。为缓解灾难性遗忘，近期方法采用带正交投影的低秩适配（如O-LoRA）来隔离任务参数。但作者揭示严格几何约束会引发"正交性困境"：僵化的参数隔离阻碍了语义相关任务间共享表征的迁移与积累。

Method: 提出免回放方法CoDe-LoRA，将学习过程解耦为"通用知识巩固"与"任务特定知识解耦"两个阶段，并借助自适应零空间投影机制与语义路由，在知识积累与任务特定适配之间取得平衡。

Result: 在四个骨干模型和三个持续学习基准上的实验表明，CoDe-LoRA取得了最佳的平均准确率。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 严格正交约束存在正交性困境，CoDe-LoRA通过巩固通用知识与解耦任务特定知识、结合自适应零空间投影与语义路由，有效平衡知识积累与任务适配，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for Large Language Models (LLMs) to sequentially adapt to evolving tasks. To mitigate catastrophic forgetting, recent advances implement low-rank adaptation with orthogonal projections (e.g., O-LoRA) to isolate task parameters. However, we reveal that such strict geometric constraints trigger an "Orthogonality Dilemma": rigid parameter isolation impedes the transfer and accumulation of shared representations across semantically related tasks. In this work, we propose a new replay-free method, called Consolidation and Decoupling LoRA (CoDe-LoRA), for CL of LLMs. CoDe-LoRA disentangles the learning process into Consolidating Universal Knowledge and Decoupling Task-Specific Knowledge. To achieve this, CoDe-LoRA leverages an adaptive null space projection mechanism and semantic routing to balance knowledge accumulation with task-specific adaptation. Experimental results across four backbones and three CL benchmarks show that CoDe-LoRA achieves the best average accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/Estrellajer/CoDe-LoRA.

</details>


### [359] [The Standardization Trap: Certifying Joint Label Processing in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08314)
*Duong Nguyen,Nicolas Chesneau,Milan Bhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文检验了预训练表格基础模型（TFM）是否遵循"固定权重"解释，提出两种仅依赖标准化标签预测值的证书，否定了固定权重预测与独立非线性标签变换求和两类解释，发现五种公开TFM均表现出"联合处理"行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性回归与核平滑为上下文学习提供了可解释的固定权重解释（特征决定每个上下文标签的权重），但该解释是否适用于预训练表格基础模型尚不明确。直接使用导数检验会遇到"标准化陷阱"：公开TFM包在模型见到标签前先做标准化，而普通导数会反映标准化标签集合之外的行为，导致即使所有预测都符合固定权重映射，模型也会显得非线性。

Method: 提出两种证书，仅依据标准化标签处的预测值，分别用于拒绝两类解释：(1) 固定权重预测；(2) 独立非线性标签变换之和。在五种公开TFM上进行评估。

Result: 在评估的五种公开TFM上，证书显示改变一个上下文标签会改变其他标签对预测的影响，即"联合处理"（joint processing）。进一步发现联合处理随训练出现，且注意力分数承载了大部分测得的交互。

Conclusion: 需要能够刻画"上下文标签如何改变单个样本影响力"的TFM解释框架，而非固定权重假设。

Abstract: Linear regression and kernel smoothing offer tractable explanations of in-context learning: in both, the features determine the weight assigned to each context label. However, whether this fixed-weight account describes pretrained tabular foundation models (TFMs) remains unclear. Testing this account using derivatives runs into a standardization trap: public TFM packages standardize the labels before the model sees them, yet ordinary derivatives also reflect behavior outside the set of standardized labels, making a model appear nonlinear even when every prediction it makes agrees with a fixed-weight map. We propose two certificates that depend only on predictions at standardized labels and can reject two distinct explanations: fixed-weight prediction and sums of independent nonlinear label transformations. Across the five public TFMs that we evaluate, our certificates show that changing one context label alters how other labels influence the prediction, a behavior we call joint processing. We further find that joint processing emerges with training and that attention scores carry most of the measured interaction. Together, these findings motivate TFM explanations that account for how context labels change the influence of individual examples.

</details>


### [360] [SCOPE: Certified Theorem Proving with a Language Model as the Policy Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08319)
*Hanchao Zhou,Jialei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCOPE 通过"模型规划算子、符号引擎执行数值、编译器渲染证明"的分工范式，在 Lean 形式化证明中显著优于直接生成和大型证明模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Lean 等证明助手中，生成的证明必须通过机器编译校验，因而无需人工评分即可评估。但直接生成方法在多步数值命题上失败：证明有效当且仅当每个内容整数都正确，导致通过率受每整数精度的 k 次方约束。本文旨在突破这一"幂律"瓶颈。

Method: SCOPE（State-Conditioned Operator Planning and Execution）强制天然分工：模型在算子词汇表上做规划，符号引擎执行数值运算，编译器渲染证明。方法层面还调整了决策帧中的引擎状态（用当前状态替换滞后状态），并考察了链端损失加权等变体。评估在版本冻结、独立复核与反向验证下进行。

Result: 1）对 2,617 条参考证明的受控损坏实验证实了幂律关系。2）在 218 题套件上，135M 骨干的 SCOPE 认证 191/218（87.6%）；7B 的 DeepSeek-Prover-V1.5-RL 仅认证 18/218，且消耗 27.5 倍 token 和 37.5 倍挂钟时间；DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B 在双向对偶套件上认证数为零。3）多步思考平均每题 6.12 个离散决策动作，且不产生自然语言思考文本。4）用当前引擎状态替换滞后状态使通过率从 117/218 提升至 191/218，而链端损失加权则有害。5）在公开 Lean-Workbook 库上，3,536 道可评分可采纳问题中 2,132 道通过认证（60.29%），主套件零退化。

Conclusion: 限制自由生成并保持决策时信息可见，比单纯扩大模型规模是更直接的路径。该方法在极小骨干下实现高认证率，且在多套件上具备泛化性且无退化。

Abstract: In proof assistants such as Lean, a generated proof must pass machine compilation checks, so evaluation needs no human scoring. Direct generation fails on multi-step numeric propositions: a proof is valid only if every content integer is correct, so the pass rate is bounded by the k-th power of the per-integer accuracy. Controlled corruption across 2,617 reference proofs confirms this power law. SCOPE (State-Conditioned Operator Planning and Execution) enforces the natural division of labor: the model plans over an operator vocabulary, a symbolic engine executes the numerics, and a compiler renders the proof. On a 218-problem suite it certifies 191/218 (87.6%) with a 135M backbone; the 7B DeepSeek-Prover-V1.5-RL certifies 18/218 at 27.5 times the tokens and 37.5 times the wall-clock, and DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B certifies zero on a bidirectional dual suite. Multi-step thinking costs 6.12 discrete decision actions per problem and produces no natural-language thinking text. Replacing the lagged engine state in the decision frame with the current one lifts the pass rate from 117/218 to 191/218, while up-weighting the chain-end loss hurts. On the public Lean-Workbook library, 2,132 of 3,536 gradeable admissible problems certify (60.29%) with zero regression on the main suite. All readings come from a version-frozen review with independent rechecks and reverse verification. Restricting free generation and keeping decision-time information visible is a more direct route than enlarging the model.

</details>


### [361] [MedZERO: Self-Evolving Agents for Open-Ended Medical Reasoning Through Controlled Knowledge Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08327)
*Xilin Dang,Weilin Ruan,Xue Yang,Jinghao Wang,Xiaowei Hu,Jinpeng Li,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedZERO自演化框架，通过Examiner生成前沿医学问答对、Reasoner结合外部知识工具多轮推理，并采用受控知识累积机制，在五个医学推理基准上以4B/8B模型取得最高13.7个准确率点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化方法主要面向数学、编程等可精确验证的领域，依赖精确答案或可执行程序进行校验；而医学推理具有开放式、知识密集型、仅部分可验证的特点，导致现有方法难以直接迁移，且LLM受静态参数化知识与高昂专家监督成本限制。

Method: MedZERO将Examiner（生成前沿医学问题-选项对）与Reasoner（借助外部知识工具进行基于证据的多轮推理）耦合，并引入受控知识累积，在推理中同时维护临时探索性知识与经筛选的持久性知识，以支撑可靠、持续的自我改进。

Result: 在五个公开医学推理基准、4B与8B规模基模型、开放式评估设置下，MedZERO一致优于基座模型及已有自演化基线，相较次优自演化基线平均准确率最高提升13.7个百分点。

Conclusion: MedZERO表明自演化框架可有效适配开放式医学推理，通过问题生成与工具增强推理的耦合及受控知识累积，在不依赖昂贵专家监督的情况下实现持续性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in medical question answering and clinical reasoning, yet their improvement remains constrained by static parametric knowledge and costly expert supervision. Self-evolving agents offer a promising alternative by enabling models to improve through iterative task generation and problem-solving. However, most existing self-evolving methods are designed for easily verifiable domains such as mathematics and coding, where solutions can be checked by exact answers or executable programs. Medical reasoning is fundamentally different: it is open-ended, knowledge-intensive, and often only partially verifiable. We present MedZERO, a self-evolving framework for open-ended medical reasoning. MedZERO couples an Examiner that generates frontier medical question-option pairs with a Reasoner that solves them through evidence-grounded multi-turn reasoning with external knowledge tools. To support reliable, continual improvement, MedZERO adopts controlled knowledge accumulation, which maintains temporary exploratory knowledge and curated persistent knowledge in reasoning. We evaluate MedZERO on five public medical reasoning benchmarks using 4B- and 8B-scale base models under open-ended evaluation. Across all settings, MedZERO consistently outperforms the underlying base models and prior self-evolving baselines, achieving up to 13.7 average accuracy-point gains over the next-best self-evolving baseline.

</details>


### [362] [An AI-Assisted Formalization of the Poincaré Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08329)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Axel Delaval,Leheng Chen,Jinxuan Chen,Jie Xu,Yuxuan Liao,Jiedong Jiang,Chunlei Liu,Bin Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了利用AI辅助在Lean 4中形式化庞加莱猜想的工作。项目通过结合数学家的证明蓝图与明确的里程碑声明来组织工作，实现并行智能体协作，并识别了工作流程中的人类干预与组织决策，为几何分析领域的形式化提供了可复用基础设施的起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该项目启动时，针对庞加莱猜想证明背后的几何分析，可复用的形式化基础设施非常有限，因此需要一种有效的方式来组织大规模形式化工作。

Method: 将数学家准备的证明蓝图与明确的里程碑声明相结合，用里程碑支持并行智能体（agent）工作，并为数学家提供定位阻塞点和给予有效数学指导的清晰节点；同时分析工作流程中的人类干预与组织选择。

Result: 成功组织并推进了庞加莱猜想的Lean 4形式化工作，明确了人类干预与组织决策在工作流程中的作用，并形成了相关方法论。

Conclusion: 该项目为未来形式化项目提供了可复用基础设施的起点，此类基础设施一旦建成，有望降低几何分析中数学结果验证的成本。

Abstract: We present an AI-assisted Lean 4 formalization of the Poincaré conjecture. The project began with limited reusable formal infrastructure for the geometric analysis behind the proof. To organize this work, we combined a proof blueprint prepared by mathematicians with explicit milestone statements. These milestones enabled parallel agent work and gave mathematicians clear points to locate blockers and provide effective mathematical guidance. Our analysis identifies the human interventions and organizational choices behind this workflow. The project provides a starting point toward reusable infrastructure for future formalization projects; such infrastructure, once developed, could eventually reduce the cost of verifying mathematical results in geometric analysis.

</details>


### [363] [MoF: Preference-Aware Mixture Modeling for Black-Box LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08330)
*Hun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a wide range of tasks, yet aligning their outputs with diverse user preferences remains challenging. Existing personalization approaches for black-box LLMs often rely on user-specific scoring heads, causing the number of personalized parameters to grow linearly with the number of users and requiring additional adaptation for unseen users. To address these limitations, we propose Mixture-of-Facets (MoF), a scalable personalization framework for black-box LLMs that models user preferences as compositions of shared latent preference facets rather than dedicated user-specific parameters. MoF performs personalization through history-conditioned routing over shared facet heads, enabling personalization for users unseen during training without additional parameter updates. Across diverse personalization tasks, MoF delivers stronger personalization performance while maintaining a more scalable and parameter-efficient design than prior approaches. Additional analysis indicates strong generalization to unseen users.

</details>


### [364] [Explainable Failure Prediction and Prevention in Maritime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08363)
*Dionisis Kalogeropoulos,Georgia Sovatzidi,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了海事系统中用于可解释故障预测与预防的关键AI技术，提出了一个集成数据采集、时间序列预测、异常检测、风险评估、决策与可解释AI的闭环概念架构，并讨论了相关研究的方法、优势与局限及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事系统运行于高度动态环境，设备故障会威胁安全、可靠性与运营效率。尽管AI、机器学习、数字孪生与预测性维护可实现主动故障预测与预防，但在安全攸关的海事应用中，确保可信且可解释的决策仍是重大挑战。

Method: 采用文献综述与概念架构设计方法：首先回顾海事系统可解释故障预测与预防所需的关键AI技术；随后提出一个支持自主或人在回路纠正措施的闭环概念架构，该架构整合数据采集、时间序列预测、异常检测、风险评估、决策和可解释AI；最后围绕架构各组件对相关海事研究进行综述与讨论。

Result: 围绕所提架构组件梳理了相关海事研究，归纳了各研究的方法、优势与局限，并识别出当前面临的主要挑战，包括不确定性与鲁棒性、模型泛化能力、可解释性、海事数据集有限以及实际部署问题。

Conclusion: 海事领域实现可信AI辅助决策仍面临多方面挑战，未来研究应着力于提升模型的不确定性与鲁棒性、泛化能力、可解释性，扩充海事数据集并推进实际部署，从而走向可信的AI辅助海事决策。

Abstract: Maritime systems operate in highly dynamic environments where unexpected equipment failures can compromise safety, reliability, and operational efficiency. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning, digital twins, and predictive maintenance enable proactive failure prediction and prevention. However, ensuring trustworthy and explainable decision-making remains a major challenge in safety-critical maritime applications. This chapter reviews key AI technologies required for explainable failure prediction and prevention in maritime systems and presents a conceptual architecture capable of supporting autonomous or human-in-the-loop corrective actions. This architecture integrates data acquisition, time-series forecasting, anomaly detection, risk assessment, decision-making, and explainable AI into a closed-loop framework. With reference to the architectural components, a review and discussion of relevant maritime studies is performed, outlining their methods, advantages, and limitations. Furthermore, it highlights current challenges, including uncertainty and robustness, model generalization, explainability, limited availability of maritime datasets, and operational deployment, and identifies future research directions toward trustworthy AI-assisted maritime decision-making.

</details>


### [365] [Transect: Retaining Observability for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08364)
*Toby D. Pilditch,Konstantinos Voudouris,Alexandra Abbas,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Transect 是一个基于 Inspect Scout 的开源工具包，用于对长时程多智能体评估的转录文本进行可复现、可审计的行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 AI 评估日益采用开放式、智能体化、长时程任务，其转录可达数百页，涉及复杂多智能体网络的输出与动作，导致评估者可推断智能体行为的"可观测包络"不断收窄。语言模型助手虽可辅助分类与解释，但也给人类评估者带来显著的分析自由度，威胁基于语言模型的转录分析的可复现性与可审计性。

Method: Transect 建立在 Inspect Scout 之上，用户以可复用的"评估族配置"指定任务上下文与行为词表，并单独提供评判模型与分析设置。其可导航报告将记录事件、token 使用、子智能体活动与模型生成的行为标签对齐到统一的基于轮次的时间线上，支持标签/事件溯源至源轮次，并可导出底层数据表以进行跨运行分析。作者在一个产生近 1300 万 token 的 AI 研发评估中演示了该工作流。

Result: 该 AI 研发评估将智能体工作划分为与研究技能分类一致的行为阶段，以及子智能体委派与交互、token 使用。组合视图显示智能体聚焦于操作性工作与手稿产出，缺乏持续性假设生成阶段的证据——而这可说是高质量科学产出所必需的一环。

Conclusion: Transect 灵活、可定制的转录分析流水线可帮助评估者跟上更长、更复杂、更频繁的 AI 评估，同时支持科学严谨性、透明度与可复现性。

Abstract: Frontier AI evaluations increasingly use open-ended, agentic, long-horizon tasks whose transcripts can span hundreds of pages of outputs and actions from complex multi-agent networks. The observability envelop-the range of what evaluators can reliably infer about an agent's behaviours-is therefore narrowing. Language model assistants can help classify and interpret agent behaviour but also afford human evaluators significant analytical degrees of freedom, threatening the reproducibility and auditability of language-model-based transcript analysis. Transect is an open source package built on Inspect Scout to help evaluators understand how a long agent run unfolded, identify behaviour worth investigating, and check interpretations against the transcript. Users specify task context and behavioural vocabulary in a reusable evaluation-family configuration, with judge models and analysis settings supplied separately. Transect's navigable reports align recorded events, token use, sub-agent activity, and model-generated behavioural labels on a common turn-based timeline. Reviewers can quickly grasp a run's narrative, trace any label or event to its source turns, and export the underlying data tables for cross-run analysis. We demonstrate the workflow on an AI R&D evaluation that generated almost 13 million tokens, dividing the agents' work into behavioural phases aligned with research-skill classifications, sub-agent delegations and interactions, and token use. The combined view shows a focus on operational work and manuscript production, with little evidence of a sustained hypothesis generation stage-arguably a necessary component for high-quality scientific outputs. Transect's flexible, customisable transcript-analysis pipeline will enable evaluators to keep pace with longer, more complex, more frequent AI evaluations while supporting scientific rigour, transparency, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [366] [EMHO: EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization via Experience Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08432)
*Hyun Jung Lee,Jungtaek Kim,Jongwon Jeong,Tae-Eui Kam,Donghyun Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出EMHO框架，在保持具身模型冻结的前提下，通过分析执行轨迹与历史记录，让智能体的"harness"（规划、上下文、工具使用控制层）自我进化，并引入EMHO-Merge支持跨子任务共享单一harness，在EmbodiedBench上持续提升Qwen 9B和27B的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提升具身智能体的方法集中于训练/优化底层模型，而负责规划、上下文与工具使用的harness通常依赖人工工程化设计。作者探究harness能否在稀疏环境反馈下，直接从经验轨迹中自我改进。

Method: 提出EMHO（Embodied Agent Harness Optimization）自我进化框架：冻结具身模型，迭代分析执行轨迹和历史harness记录来修订harness，优化范围超出技能或恢复提示，涵盖进度监控、视觉工具使用、观测接地（grounding）与失败响应。进一步提出EMHO-Merge，利用episode级的增益与损失，指导何时、如何应用修订行为的基于证据的细化，从而在多个子任务间共享单一harness并处理联合优化中的权衡。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench的导航与操作任务上评估，EMHO对Qwen 9B和27B模型均能持续提升任务成功率。定性分析显示，EMHO不仅限于从失败与无效动作中恢复，还重塑了具身智能体解读和交互环境的方式。

Conclusion: harness可通过经验轨迹实现自我优化，无需训练底层模型即可提升具身智能体表现；EMHO-Merge使单一共享harness适用于多子任务；该方法超越了单纯的失败恢复，改变了智能体与环境的交互范式。

Abstract: Improving embodied agents often focuses on optimizing the underlying model through training, while the surrounding agent harness that controls planning, context, and tool use is typically engineered. We ask whether this harness can instead improve itself directly from experience traces under sparse environmental feedback. We propose EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization (EMHO), a self-evolving framework that keeps the embodied model frozen and iteratively revises its harness by analyzing execution trajectories and prior harness history. EMHO optimizes beyond skills or recovery prompts, modifying how the agent monitors progress, uses vision tools, grounds observations, and responds to failures. To support multiple subtasks with a single harness, we introduce EMHO-Merge, which addresses trade-offs in jointly optimizing a single shared harness across subtasks by using episode-level gains and losses to guide evidence-supported refinement of when and how revised behaviors are applied. We evaluate EMHO on EmbodiedBench across navigation and manipulation tasks, and EMHO consistently improves task success for both Qwen 9B and 27B models. Qualitative analysis shows that EMHO goes beyond recovering from failures and unproductive actions to reshape how the embodied agent interprets and interacts with its environment.

</details>


### [367] [AssemState: Manual and Physical-State-Guided Reasoning for Zero-shot Furniture Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08446)
*Zhiyuan Qi,Jierui Li,Yifan Shen,Cheng Qian,Jiateng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AssemState——一个零样本（zero-shot）的家具装配框架，通过锚点引导的装配状态分解说明书并结合迭代式后状态反馈细化（SE(3)）位姿更新，提升了装配树恢复与局部位姿精度，但揭示MLLM在3D空间关系推理上仍存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉理解上进展显著，但结合物理环境的精确3D空间推理仍困难。家具装配不仅需从图示说明书恢复步骤级操作，还需将语义连接关系转化为6D位姿更新，使零件能与环境和已装配部件发生物理交互。作者旨在研究这一问题。

Method: 提出AssemState零样本框架，用于说明书与物理状态引导的家具装配。方法包含两部分：（1）采用锚点引导的边界装配状态，将说明页分解为单零件操作并恢复装配树；（2）使用迭代式后状态反馈细化，指导连续的SE(3)更新与修正，并通过基于仿真的释放测试（release tests）验证其物理合理性。

Result: 相比最强基线，装配树恢复的F1从38.58%提升至62.80%，Tree Exact Match从28.24%提升至53.92%。在243个独立评估的零件级操作上，迭代细化使评审通过操作从0提升至5.3%，并将平均Chamfer距离从5.4111降至1.7744。但视觉上合理的候选位姿仍可能出现碰撞、悬浮、镜像朝向错误、未完全就位及错侧附着等问题。

Conclusion: AssemState改善了操作-结构恢复及部分局部位姿指标，但MLLM在空间关系推理方面仍然受限，视觉合理性并不等同于物理可行性与正确的空间关系。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding, but precise 3D spatial reasoning integrated with physical environment remains difficult. Furniture assembly requires not only recovering step-level operations from diagrammatic manuals, but also translating semantic attachment relations into 6D pose updates that enable parts to physically interact with the environment and previously assembled components. To study this problem, we propose AssemState, a zero-shot framework for manual and physical-state-guided furniture assembly. It firstly employs anchor-guided boundary assembly states to decompose manual pages into single-part operations and recover an assembly-tree. Then, it uses iterative after-state feedback refinement to guide successive (SE(3)) updates and corrections, and validates their physical plausibility through simulation-based release tests. Experiments show that compared with the strongest prior baseline, AssemState improves F1 from 38.58\% to 62.80\% and Tree Exact Match from 28.24\% to 53.92\% for assembly-tree recovery. On 243 independently evaluated part-level operations, our proposed iterative refinement improves judge-accepted operations from 0 to 5.3\% and reduces mean Chamfer distance from 5.4111 to 1.7744. However, visually plausible candidate poses may still suffer from collision, floating, mirror-orientation errors, incomplete seating, and wrong-side attachment. These results show that AssemState improves operation-structure recovery and selected local pose metrics, while MLLMs remain limited for spatial relationship reasoning.

</details>


### [368] [Cylindrical Geodesic Flow Matching for Quasiperiodic Physiological Signal Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08510)
*Onur Selim Kilic,Afra Nawar,Cem Okan Yaldiz,Michael J. Cho,Ahmet Rasim Emirdagi,Demet Tangolar,Amirali Aghazadeh,Amit J. Shah,Omer T. Inan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"柱面测地线流匹配"(cylindrical geodesic flow matching)用于准周期心血管波形的配对翻译，用相位-幅值柱面上的闭式测地线替代标准仿射插值路径，消除中间幅值与瞬时频率畸变，并转化为稠密几何一致的速度监督。在零样本PPG与有限支持SCG适配基准上，Hilbert变换、L2与DTW距离最高降低约15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将可穿戴设备在不同身体部位采集的心血管信号互相翻译（由源信号恢复目标振荡信号）是心血管信号解读的核心问题。该源-目标映射具有内在几何结构：相位沿周期环绕、须视为圆变量，幅值严格为正，且逐拍对齐会随周期和受试者不可预测地漂移。现有深度网络虽用于相位估计与复值信号建模，但未显式学习配对信号间的相位输运；因此端点监督回归与流匹配中的标准仿射路径均未考虑这种相位-幅值结构。

Method: 提出柱面测地线流匹配：指出流匹配中的标准仿射路径在准周期信号间插值时会扭曲中间幅值与瞬时频率；改用相位-幅值柱面上的闭式测地线路径，从而消除这些伪影，并把每个训练对转化为稠密、几何一致的速度监督。

Result: 在零样本光电容积描记(PPG)与有限支持心震图(SCG)适配基准上，该方法持续优于插值基线，并达到或超过直接监督预测，相比最强竞争基线将Hilbert变换距离、L2距离与动态时间规整(DTW)距离降低最多约15%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，桥接路径的几何(bridge geometry)是在振荡信号翻译任务上进行流匹配的关键归纳偏置。

Abstract: Paired translation between quasiperiodic physiological waveforms (i.e., recovering a target oscillatory signal from the source) is central to the interpretation of cardiovascular signals derived from wearables placed at different body locations. This source-to-target mapping in these problems carries inherent geometric structure: the phase wraps around the cycle and must be treated as a circular variable, the amplitude remains strictly positive, and the beat-to-beat alignment can drift unpredictably across cycles and subjects. While deep neural networks have been used for phase estimation and complex-valued signal modeling, prior work does not explicitly learn phase transport between paired signals. Consequently, neither endpoint-supervised regression nor the standard affine path used in flow matching accounts for this phase--amplitude structure. We introduce \emph{cylindrical geodesic flow matching} for paired cardiovascular waveform translation. We show that the standard affine path used in flow matching distorts intermediate amplitude and instantaneous frequency when interpolating between quasiperiodic signals; replacing it with a closed-form geodesic on the phase--amplitude cylinder eliminates these artifacts and converts each training pair into dense, geometry-consistent velocity supervision. On zero-shot photoplethysmography and limited-support seismocardiography adaptation benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms interpolation baselines and matches or exceeds direct supervised prediction, reducing Hilbert Transform, $L_2$, and Dynamic Time Warping distance by up to ${\sim}15\%$ over the strongest competing baseline. These results suggest that bridge geometry is a critical inductive bias for flow matching on oscillatory signal translation.

</details>


### [369] [Toward Alignment Scaling Laws: A Framework and First Preregistered Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08540)
*Jeremy Canale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将"对齐难度随模型规模如何变化"重构为一族可测量的标度关系：对每个风险类别 r，将维持固定安全目标所需的对齐负担建模为 B_r(N)=a_rN^{α_r}（N 为能力代理），并以与 N 成正比的预算为参照，判定 α_r<1 时规模扩大有利、α_r≈1 时持平、α_r>1 时累积"对齐债务"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于"对齐随模型增大变易或变难"的争论多基于孤立发现，隐含把对齐当作单一属性。作者认为应将其视为一族可测量的标度关系，从而可对不同风险类别分别给出定量的规模依赖结论，并支持预注册式验证。

Method: 提出负担的三种操作化定义，并区分观测对齐、审计对齐与真实对齐；构建"修正消耗能力余量"的玩具模型；给出四条理论结果（最大指数而非平均值决定长期机制；α>1 时任何维持余量下限的策略需超指数增长；正幂律混合负担导致小模型拟合低估大尺度指数；无假阳性的审计不会高估真实对齐）；提出可预注册协议，并以缩减版本做两次实证：Pythia 分类器对抗训练数据复分析，以及 Qwen2.5 0.5B–72B（并在 Qwen3 0.6B–14B 复现）的预注册试点。

Result: Pythia 复分析：将攻击成功率压到 10% 以下所需算力按 N^{0.60} 增长。Qwen 试点：真实性指数 −0.05、陈述倾向 0.48（按缩减规则均为"规模有利"，但顶端局部斜率趋近 1）；谄媚 0.89（72B 加两个种子后为 0.83）与植入后门均未定论——后门在触发器已知时被迅速移除，但在五分之四规模上能挺过盲安全训练。作者同时发布四款展示这些规律的浏览器游戏，并声明不对当前前沿模型属于哪一机制作断言。

Conclusion: 对齐应按风险类别以幂律指数分门别类地度量，长期机制由被修正风险中的最大指数决定；小模型外推可能系统性低估大尺度指数；审计结论仅在无假阳性等条件下可靠。实证显示不同风险类别指数差异显著（0.60、−0.05、0.48、0.89 等），部分类别仍难以判定，作者不对前沿模型所处机制下结论。

Abstract: Whether alignment gets easier or harder as models grow is often argued from isolated findings, as if alignment were one property. We treat it as a family of measurable scaling relations: for each risk category r, the alignment burden needed to hold a fixed safety target is modeled as B_r(N)=a_rN^alpha_r, with N a capability proxy; against a budget proportional to N, scaling helps if alpha_r<1, keeps pace if alpha_r~1, and accumulates alignment debt if alpha_r>1. We give three operationalizations of burden and distinguish observed, audited and true alignment. A toy model, in which corrections consume capability headroom, makes the consequences explicit. We prove that the largest exponent among corrected risks, not an average, sets the long-run regime; that above 1 any policy holding headroom above a floor must grow super-exponentially; that, for burdens that are positive mixtures of power laws, fits on small models underestimate large-scale exponents; and that an audit that uncovers hidden failures without false positives never underestimates true alignment. We propose a pre-registrable protocol and apply reduced versions of it twice. A preregistered reanalysis of public adversarial-training data for Pythia classifiers finds that the compute needed to bring attack success under 10% grows as N^0.60. A preregistered pilot on Qwen2.5 0.5B-72B finds exponents of -0.05 for truthfulness and 0.48 for stated dispositions (both scaling helps under its reduced rule, though local slopes approach 1 at the top; replicated on Qwen3 0.6B-14B), while sycophancy (0.89, or 0.83 with two seeds added at 72B) and a planted backdoor are undetermined: the backdoor is removed quickly when its trigger is known but survives blind safety training at four of five sizes. We release four browser games that play these laws (www.aisafety.fun). We make no claim about which regime holds for current frontier models.

</details>


### [370] [AnyBottle: A Recipe to Only Keep the Concepts You Really Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08552)
*Wolfgang Stammer,Sukrut Rao,Hevra Petekkaya,David Steinmann,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出AnyBottle方法，构建紧凑的任务特定概念瓶颈模型（CBM），无需概念标注，通过冻结骨干网络与无监督概念池，利用黑盒教师引导概念选择，实现小规模且自适应推理的概念瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CBM需要概念标注，而无标注变体通常采用大而静态的概念词表，导致瓶颈规模远超任务需求，难以检视与干预。

Method: AnyBottle仅需冻结骨干网络和无监督概念池（如稀疏自编码器），由同骨干训练的黑盒教师引导：每轮在教师-学生分歧区域中选择最能解释当前失败的概念；按选择顺序施加嵌套dropout训练，使任意概念前缀均可预测，实现推理时概念数量自适应。

Result: 在6个视觉和2个文本数据集、2种教师范式下，AnyBottle相比无标注基线获得更少概念、更高概念一致性的瓶颈，同时保持接近黑盒参考的性能。

Conclusion: 无标注不必意味着大规模：通过发现的小概念词表即可达到与大规模固定词表相当的表达能力，且各阶段与模态无关，换域换任务只需替换骨干和概念池。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make predictions inspectable and intervenable by routing them through human-interpretable concepts, but originally required concept annotations. Annotation-free variants remove this requirement, but typically use large concept vocabularies, static at both training and inference, producing bottlenecks larger than any task or prediction needs and harder to inspect. We propose AnyBottle, a single recipe for building compact, task-specific CBMs. AnyBottle assumes only a frozen backbone and an unsupervised concept pool, such as a sparse autoencoder. A black-box teacher trained on the same backbone then guides selection: each round adds the concept that best explains the bottleneck's current failures, with candidates restricted to regions of teacher/student disagreement. Trained with nested dropout over this selection order, the final bottleneck predicts accurately from any concept prefix, so inference spends fewer concepts on inputs it is confident about early and more on hard ones. Since no stage is modality-specific, a new domain and task requires swapping only the backbone and concept pool. Across six vision and two text datasets and two teacher paradigms, AnyBottle yields bottlenecks with fewer concepts and higher concept consistency than annotation-free baselines, while staying close to the black-box reference. Overall, AnyBottle shows that going annotation-free need not mean going large: a small, discovered vocabulary can be as expressive as a much larger, fixed one.

</details>


### [371] [Adaptive Power Sampling for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08563)
*Bingnan Xiao,Chenhao Yang,Bingcong Li,Wei Ni,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自适应幂采样（APS），在测试时按查询动态调整锐化指数，无需训练即可提升大模型推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列级幂采样对基座模型分布进行跨查询统一的锐化，忽略了查询难度差异及模型对各查询已有处理能力的差别，缺乏查询自适应性。

Method: 理论上证明进一步锐化的收益由正确与错误响应的自奖励差距决定；据此提出APS，在测试时依据答案一致性与模型自奖励之间的关系，逐查询调整锐化指数。

Result: 在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA等多样化推理任务上，APS持续优于固定锐化指数的幂采样，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的锐化指数调节能有效改进免训练幂采样推理，为推理时采样方法提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Sequence-level power sampling has recently emerged as a training-free approach to reasoning by sampling from a sharpened output distribution of a base large language model (LLM). Nevertheless, existing methods typically sharpen the base model distribution uniformly across queries, overlooking variations in query difficulty and in how well the base model already handles each query. The goal of this work is to equip power sampling with query adaptivity. Theoretically, we show that the benefits of further sharpening are determined by the self-reward gap between correct and incorrect responses. Based on this insight, we propose \emph{Adaptive Power Sampling} (APS), which adjusts the sharpening exponent on a per-query basis at test time using the relationship between answer agreement and the model's self-reward. Experiments across diverse reasoning tasks, including MATH500, HumanEval, and GPQA, show that APS consistently outperforms power sampling with a fixed sharpening exponent, without additional training.

</details>


### [372] [MINDSET: Energy-based Schema Evolution for Long Conversational Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08586)
*Sujato Dutta,Sreekruthy Tummala,Shashank Vanga,Ayushmi Pavani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MINDSET 将长对话记忆建模为受约束的状态管理：以不可变片段存储对话，并通过最小能量状态转移组织为带版本的模式，在长期记忆问答上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长对话代理需记住久远信息以高效完成任务，但指令与上下文随时间变化，代理须自适应。理想记忆系统需同时保留当前与历史状态、区分过时信息与活跃知识、按查询检索证据，并避免反复调用大模型重写交互。

Method: MINDSET 是一个记忆控制器：将对话存为不可变片段（immutable episodes），通过最小能量状态转移（minimum-energy state transitions）将其组织为带版本的模式（versioned schemas）。每个新片段可强化、取代、拆分或创建模式。转移决策在表示失真、矛盾、历史损伤、碎片化与内部不一致之间权衡，并用迟滞（hysteresis）机制防止孤立矛盾过早改写稳定记忆。

Result: 在 850 题可复现样本（700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench）上与 5 个记忆系统对比：MINDSET 取得最高 LoCoMo answer F1，并在检索排序（Recall@8、MRR、nDCG@8）上显著优于第二名 LightMem（Holm 校正后 p<0.01）；在 MemoryAgentBench 上取得最高分但相对差异较小。消融显示受控碎片化与模式感知分配对答案质量贡献最大。700 题跨模型评估（GLM-4.7 与 Gemma-4-31B）支持模型独立性。

Conclusion: 长期记忆更适合作为受约束的状态管理来处理，而非持续摘要（continual summarization）。

Abstract: Long conversational agents have become essential in our daily lives. They must remember what was said long back in order to help us efficiently complete a task without needing the user to repeat instructions and context repeatedly. However, the main issue is that instructions and context change over time and so the agents must be able to adapt accordingly. A useful memory system should preserve both current and historical states, distinguish stale information from active knowledge, retrieve evidence appropriate to the query and avoid repeatedly invoking a large language model to rewrite prior interactions. We introduce MINDSET, a memory controller that stores a conversation as immutable episodes and organizes them into versioned schemas through minimum-energy state transitions. Each incoming episode may reinforce, supersede, split or create a schema. The transition decision balances representation distortion, contradiction, historical damage, fragmentation and internal inconsistency, while hysteresis prevents isolated contradictions from prematurely rewriting stable memory. We evaluate MINDSET against 5 memory systems on a reproducible sample of 850 questions (700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench). MINDSET obtains the highest observed LoCoMo answer F1 while significantly improving retrieval ranking (Recall@8, MRR and nDCG@8) over the second best method LightMem (p<0.01 after Holm correction). It obtains the highest observed scores on MemoryAgentBench although the relative difference is low. Ablations identify controlled fragmentation and schema-aware assignment as the largest contributors to answer quality. Additionally, a 700-question cross-model evaluation with GLM-4.7 and Gemma-4-31B supported model independence. These results show that long-term memory can be better handled as constrained state management rather than continual summarization.

</details>


### [373] [Parallel Predictive World Models for Accurate and Efficient Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08627)
*Wanjin Feng,Baobin Zhang,Ao Yu,Shibo Feng,Xi Wang,Xingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并行预测世界模型（PPWM），一次性并行预测有限时域轨迹，在解码前进行未来表示间的因果交互，用并行因果轨迹预测替代逐步自回归展开，从而提升长时域预测精度与规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域世界模型规划通常依赖自回归展开：预测状态被反复回灌到模型中。这虽保留了时序结构，却产生了随时域长度线性增长的串行路径，并使后续预测暴露于递归的解码状态反馈之中，造成误差累积与规划效率下降。因此需要重新审视是否必须进行逐状态自回归。

Method: 提出PPWM：并行预测有限时域轨迹，同时保持未来表示间的因果交互。每个时域以其因果动作前缀为条件，未来表示在解码之前先进行交互，从而将时间因果性与逐状态输出递归解耦。理论上将自回归展开形式化为因果轨迹映射，并识别出PPWM所移除的解码状态反馈通路。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务上，PPWM在所评估的预测接口中取得最低的长时域预测误差与最高的CEM模拟器成功率；同时相比自回归的LeWM基线，CEM规划速度平均提升3倍以上。

Conclusion: 结果表明，准确且高效的长时域世界模型规划并不需要逐状态自回归，而可通过并行因果轨迹预测实现。

Abstract: Long-horizon world-model planning typically relies on autoregressive rollouts, where predicted states are repeatedly fed back into the model.
