This preserves temporal structure but creates a horizon-length sequential path and exposes later predictions to recursive decoded-state feedback. We introduce Parallel Predictive World Models (PPWM), which predict a finite-horizon trajectory in parallel while retaining causal interaction among future representations. Each horizon is conditioned on its causal action prefix, and future representations interact before decoding, separating temporal causality from state-by-state output recursion. We formalize this distinction by viewing autoregressive rollout as a causal trajectory map and identifying the decoded-state feedback pathway removed by PPWM. Across four visual-control tasks, PPWM achieves the lowest long-horizon prediction error and the highest Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) simulator success among the evaluated predictive interfaces. Meanwhile, PPWM achieves more than a 3$\times$ average CEM planning speedup over the autoregressive LeWM baseline. These results suggest that accurate and efficient long-horizon world-model planning does not require state-by-state autoregression, but can instead be achieved through parallel causal trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [374] [SquidAgent: Parallelize Wisely, Coordinate Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08647)
*Yexiong Lin,Shanshan Ye,Yu Yao,Zhen Fang,Bo Han,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于token成本的并行化决策准则与SquidAgent系统，消除多智能体并行中的重探索与对齐开销，实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统理论上并行可获近线性加速，但实际常慢于单智能体串行基线。作者将此归因于并行执行的两个隐性成本：重探索成本（并行worker重复重建orchestrator已拥有的上下文）和对齐成本（协调独立生成输出间不一致的开销）。

Method: 推导出原则性决策准则：仅当某层的关键路径成本加重探索与对齐开销低于对应串行成本时才并行。因LLM对wall-clock时间估计校准差，改用预测输出token衡量成本。据此提出SquidAgent：单次规划步骤估计所有token预算；每个worker直接从orchestrator会话分叉以消除重探索成本；用预生成的共享约定块（convention block）替代事后协调，将对齐转为有界的前置成本；确定性调度器逐层应用该准则。

Result: 相比Claude Code取得2.2倍平均吞吐提升和2.6倍平均wall-time加速；相比最强多智能体基线取得2.0倍吞吐提升。

Conclusion: 通过以token预测为成本度量、消除重探索与对齐开销并采用确定性逐层调度，SquidAgent使并行多智能体系统真正获得超越单智能体串行的加速效果。

Abstract: LLM-based agents solve complex multi-step tasks, but sequential execution incurs substantial latency. In principle, parallelizing work across multiple agents should yield near-linear speedups. Yet existing parallel multi-agent systems often run slower than a single-agent baseline. We attribute this gap to two hidden costs that parallel execution incurs but a serial agent avoids. First, there is a re-exploration cost: redundant effort spent by parallel workers reconstructing context that the orchestrator already possesses, such as prior decisions, that would otherwise be inherited implicitly in a serial execution. Second, there is an alignment cost: the overhead required to reconcile inconsistencies across independently generated outputs. We thus derive a principled decision criterion: a layer should be parallelized only when its critical-path cost, plus re-exploration and alignment overheads, is lower than the corresponding serial cost. While this criterion is naturally expressed in wall-clock time, we observe that LLMs are poorly calibrated when asked to estimate task duration. To address this, we instead measure cost in predicted output tokens, which we empirically find LLMs can estimate substantially more reliably than wall-clock time. Building on this token-based criterion, we propose SquidAgent. It estimates all token budgets in a single planning step, forks each worker directly from the orchestrator's session to eliminate re-exploration cost, and replaces post-hoc reconciliation with a pre-generated shared convention block that converts alignment into a bounded upfront cost. A deterministic scheduler then applies the criterion layer by layer. Empirically, SquidAgent achieves a 2.2$\times$ mean throughput improvement and a 2.6$\times$ mean wall-time speedup over Claude Code, and a 2.0$\times$ throughput improvement over the strongest multi-agent baseline.

</details>


### [375] [Coupled but Late: Turn-Taking Between Full-Duplex Speech Models in Unscripted Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08683)
*Lichen Zhu,Yueqian Lin,Yiheng Wang,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 两个 PersonaPlex-7B 实例在共享时钟上交换音频 token 进行无脚本对话，其话轮转换时机呈耦合关系，但换手明显晚于人类（中位 400–560 ms vs. 137 ms），且缺少人类式的话轮结束预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型越来越多地被用于模型与模型对话（自博弈数据生成、智能体社会、基于模型的评估）。在这种闭环中没有人类承受时序错误，每个模型的换手时机即为对方的输入，因此需要探究该闭环最终稳定在何种时序上。

Method: 让两个 PersonaPlex-7B 实例在共享时钟上交换音频 token 进行无脚本对话，并对它们与 Switchboard 人类对话统一施加一条话轮转移（floor-transfer）规则；通过重新配对说话人、对信道单向延迟等扰动来分析时序的来源。

Result: 模型间时序是耦合的：跨对话重新配对说话人会破坏这种耦合。但话轮换手偏晚，中位为 400–560 ms，而人类为 137 ms；在伙伴话轮最后 120 ms 内（人类预测性转移约有十分之一发生于此），模型仅有 1%。对信道一个方向施加延迟会使响应一对一平移，且其前的预备段为空。

Conclusion: 模型的行为符合在感知到对方话轮结束后进行反应式等待，而非人类时序所需的话轮结束预测机制。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models are trained to converse with a person, but they are increasingly made to converse with each other, in self-play data generation, agent societies, and model-based evaluation. In that loop no human absorbs a timing error: each model's turn-taking is the other's input. We ask what timing the loop settles into. Two PersonaPlex-7B instances exchange audio tokens on a shared clock in unscripted conversation, and one floor-transfer rule is applied to them and to Switchboard. Their timing is coupled: re-pairing speakers across conversations destroys it. But the floor changes hands late, at a median of 400-560 ms against 137 ms for humans, and the last 120 ms of the partner's turn, where human projection places a tenth of its transfers, holds 1% of theirs. Delaying one direction of the channel shifts the response one-for-one and leaves the run-up to it empty, consistent with a reactive wait after the perceived end rather than the turn-end projection human timing requires.

</details>


### [376] [ScienceClaw: Benchmarking Continual Self-Evolution of AI-for-Science Agents Across the Natural and Social Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08691)
*Mingda Zhang,Wenjin Liu,Tiesunlong Shen,Zikai Xiao,Zhenghong Lin,Qing Xu,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ScienceClaw 框架，将科学自动化中的程序自我进化形式化为固定参数程序自进化，统一任务求解、科学验证与程序更新，并构建涵盖23个学科、评估科学正确性、进化增益、保持性、跨数据集迁移和进化成本的 ScienceClaw-Eval 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体虽加速了科学自动化，但经过验证的执行结果很少转化为持久的程序级改进；且现有评测未在自然科学与社会科学中考察跨顺序任务的这一过程。

Method: 将 ScienceClaw 形式化为固定参数程序自进化；通过多轮交互修复可执行工作流，将经重执行验证的失败—成功轨迹转化为关联的 Skill 与 Operator 候选；仅当源任务重放能复现修复且独立科学任务得到改进时才保留该更新。

Result: ScienceClaw-Eval 覆盖23个学科，通过顺序流与独立重置评估衡量科学正确性、进化增益、保持性、跨数据集迁移和进化成本。代码已开源。

Conclusion: ScienceClaw 为 LLM 智能体在科学领域的程序级自我进化提供了统一的框架与系统性评测基准，强调验证驱动的更新保留机制。

Abstract: Large language model agents are accelerating scientific automation, yet verified executions rarely become persistent program-level improvements, and existing evaluations do not examine this process across sequential tasks in both the natural and social sciences. We formalize ScienceClaw as fixed-parameter program self-evolution that unifies task solving, scientific verification, and program updates. ScienceClaw-Eval spans 23 disciplines and measures scientific correctness, evolutionary gain, retention, cross-dataset transfer, and evolution cost through sequential streams and independent reset evaluation. Our framework repairs executable workflows through multi-turn interaction, converts re-execution-verified failure--success trajectories into linked Skill and Operator candidates, and retains an update only when source-task replay reproduces the repair and independent scientific tasks improve. Code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/ScienceClaw.

</details>


### [377] [nanoMuse: An Open-Source Personal Agent for Every Device You Own](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08699)
*Guangyi Liu,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"个人智能体"（personal agent）的五问三阶段定义框架，基于Meta公开资料与生产提示词副本剖析其商用产品Muse，并发布开源对应实现nanoMuse：通过可自托管中继共享单一会话、Sentinel管控动作、可读文件式记忆与自选模型，实现跨设备协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年9月Meta的Muse展示了面向单一个体的智能体：具备账户、设备、记忆与持续会话，但封闭于厂商云、单一国家。这类智能体被期望操作用户账户与设备、跨周记忆、适时主动发起并以行为负责，其本质是软件而非模型，此前缺乏开源对应物，故需定义并给出开放实现。

Method: 以五问三阶段的框架定义个人智能体；依据Meta公开记录及其生产提示词副本，逐条标注来源地解析Muse架构；随后提出并说明开源对应物nanoMuse（GPL-3.0）：用户拥有的每台设备各部署一个智能体，可操作手机屏幕与电脑，经任何人可自建的中继共享同一会话，所有动作经Sentinel校验，记忆为可读文件，模型可自选，并给出规模与成本的估算。

Result: 给出Muse带来源标注的构建解析；发布nanoMuse开源方案及其架构要素（跨设备单会话、Sentinel、可读记忆、模型自选）；提供其规模与成本估算。

Conclusion: 开放路线图包含四项目标：开放本体（open）、带来源溯源的记忆、面向"操作手"（hands）的评测套件，以及配套的开放模型。

Abstract: Assistants from 2011 answered and waited, and agents from 2023 did a task and stopped. In September 2026 Meta's Muse showed an agent for one person, with accounts, devices, memory and a conversation that lasts, closed, in a vendor's cloud, in one country. Such an agent is expected to act on a person's accounts and devices, remember them across weeks, speak first when it is worth it, and answer for what it did. It is a kind of software, not a model, and until now had no open counterpart. This report defines the personal agent in five questions and three horizons. It reads how Muse is built from Meta's public record and a copy of its production prompt, each statement marked by its source. It then presents nanoMuse, the open-source counterpart under the GPL-3.0, one agent on every device a person owns, with hands on the phone's screen and the computer's. They share one conversation over a relay anyone can run; every action goes through a Sentinel, memory is files the person can read, and the model is their choice. Its size and cost are given as estimates. What is open, memory with provenance, an evaluation suite for the hands and an open model for them, is set out as a roadmap.

</details>


### [378] [WorldSolver: Can LLM Agents Simulate the Physical Dynamics via Solver Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08720)
*Siru Jiang,Yongzhe Lyu,Shuo Lu,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Yue Liao,Bin Wang,Jian Liang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WorldSolver基准，含168个源自61篇经典图形学论文、覆盖7个物理域的仿真任务，要求LLM智能体在给定代码脚手架下补全求解器，并从可执行性、视觉保真度、物理合理性三维评估。前沿智能体表现有限，最高仅48.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解器编写需物理理解、数学推理与软件工程三重能力，而LLM智能体在该方向的能力仍缺乏系统评测，因此需要构建专门的基准加以检验与推动。

Method: 构建WorldSolver基准：采集61篇经典计算机图形学论文中的物理现象，形成168个仿真任务，覆盖7个物理域；每个任务提供固定仿真环境的代码脚手架，由智能体补全求解器实现。评估包含三个维度：Execution Checks（能否成功运行）、Visual Fidelity（渲染仿真中是否复现目标动态行为）、Physical Plausibility（生成动态的物理合理性验证）。

Result: 前沿智能体实验显示，生成可执行求解器本身即较困难，满足视觉与物理正确性更难；GPT-5.6-Sol和Claude-Opus-5相对较好，但总分仅为48.7%和46.7%。

Conclusion: WorldSolver是智能体求解器生成的早期探索，有望推动智能体忠实模拟动态物理世界的研究进展，代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly advancing scientific and engineering problem solving, with physics simulation emerging as a challenging yet practical testbed for reproducing complex physical phenomena with application in embodied AI, games and films. As the workhorse of such simulation, a solver computes how the state of a dynamic system evolves over time. Building such solvers requires physical understanding to identify appropriate models, mathematical reasoning to formulate the underlying dynamics, and software engineering to implement them as executable code, yet this capability of LLM agents remains underexplored. To this end, we introduce WorldSolver, a benchmark of 168 simulation tasks derived from physical phenomena in 61 classic computer graphics papers, spanning 7 physical domains. Each task contains a code scaffold that provides a fixed simulation environment for the scene, with the solver implementation left for the agent to complete. Specifically, we evaluate them along three dimensions: Execution Checks for successful execution, Visual Fidelity for reproducing the intended dynamic behavior in the rendered simulation, and Physical Plausibility for physics-grounded verification of the generated dynamics. Experiments on frontier agents reveal that producing executable solvers is difficult itself, and satisfying visual and physical correctness is even harder. GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude-Opus-5 perform comparatively better than the other evaluated agents, yet achieve overall scores of only 48.7% and 46.7%, respectively. WorldSolver is an early step toward agentic solver generation, and we hope it helps drive progress toward agents that can faithfully simulate the dynamic physical world. Code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/WorldSolver.

</details>


### [379] [Does an Agent's History Tell You When Compaction Will Hurt? A Modest, Bounded Effect on the TRACE Paired-Replay Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08722)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究利用 TRACE 公开语料（590 个 AppWorld 压缩边界）检验"压缩前行为能否预测压缩后损害"。结论：压缩前历史对压缩后损害仅有弱预测力，最佳可解释触发规则能避免 21% 有害边界并保留 84% 压缩机会，但计数优于随机而负担质量未优于随机，且无法在现有数据上评估其是否优于 token 预算规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体的上下文压缩通常采用全局规则（如 token 预算），对智能体当前正在执行的任务一无所知。作者希望验证：智能体近期行为是否可用于预测一次压缩何时会造成损害，从而为更"行为感知"的压缩触发提供依据。

Method: 基于 TRACE 公开语料中的 590 个 harness 触发的 AppWorld 压缩边界；对每个边界，从重放的前缀状态出发，分别在压缩前上下文与摘要后上下文下重放，并记录后续动作的"负担"（报错的调用或重复已执行过的调用）。比较预先设定的前缀位置对比、朴素"has-written"标签，以及 extension-protocol 触发与冻结可解释触发。

Result: 压缩前历史对压缩后损害仅弱预测：预设前缀位置对比为宽零结果，朴素"has-written"标签实际度量的是轨迹阶段。最佳 extension-protocol 触发在留出集上 AUROC 0.66（在同边界复现标签上 0.64），而同边界复现为 0.72。最佳冻结可解释触发避免 21% 有害（正负担）边界、保留 84% 压缩机会；其在计数上超过随机规则期望，但在负担质量上未超过（事后比较）。在匹配保留率下，最佳触发是否优于 token 预算规则无法在发布数据上评估。

Conclusion: 压缩前行为只能弱预测压缩后损害，现有发布语料不足以判定行为感知触发是否优于 token 预算规则。作者呼吁后续语料应公布可在匹配保留率下做该比较所需的数据。

Abstract: Many long-horizon agents compact their context on a global rule, usually a token budget, blind to what the agent was doing. We ask whether the agent's recent behaviour predicts when a compaction will hurt. TRACE's public corpus of 590 harness-triggered AppWorld compaction boundaries replays each boundary from a re-executed prefix state under the pre-compaction context and under the summary, and records the burden of the next actions: calls that error or repeat a call already made. We find that pre-boundary history predicts post-compaction harm only weakly. An internally prespecified contrast by prefix placement is a wide null, and the naive "has-written" label behind it turns out to measure trajectory phase. The best extension-protocol trigger reaches held-out AUROC 0.66 (0.64 on the replicate's own label) against a same-boundary replicate of 0.72; the best frozen, interpretable trigger avoids 21% of harmful (positive-burden) boundaries while keeping 84% of compaction opportunities, and exceeds the random-rule expectation on count but not on burden mass (a post hoc comparison). Whether the best trigger beats a token-budget rule at matched retention cannot be evaluated on the release. We state what corpora should ship to answer it.

</details>


### [380] [VeriFine: Scaling Verification for Self-Improvement in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08761)
*Zewei Zhou,Rachel Luo,Yulong Cao,Chaowei Xiao,Chensheng Peng,Boyi Li,Thomas Tian,Zheng Lian,Yan Wang,Jiaqi Ma,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Wenhao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VeriFine框架，通过策略、训练课程与评判器的协同进化来扩展验证能力，实现具身推理中策略与评判器的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进策略会不断产生新的失败模式，改变评判器需验证的内容；而现有固定评判器同时限制了优化反馈和有用训练样本的发现，阻碍进一步自我改进。该问题在具身推理中尤为突出，因为可靠评估需兼顾空间定位、因果推理与安全感知决策。

Method: VeriFine由两个循环组成：策略改进循环用评分标准评判器诊断反复出现的失败、构建自适应课程并优化策略；当进展停滞、验证成为瓶颈时，进入评判器改进循环，选择性地对信息量大的失败案例查询人类指导，并通过协同主动校准（人类与智能体消解分歧、趋向物理推理的客观评分标准）精炼评判器，修订后的评判器再指导下一阶段的数据选择与策略优化。

Result: 在驾驶和机器人导航任务上的实验表明，在强化学习和监督微调两种设置下，策略与评判器能力均实现持续自我改进。

Conclusion: 结果表明，扩展验证能力可以支持在策略失败模式不断演变时的持续自我改进。

Abstract: Self-improving policies continually expose new failure patterns, changing what their judges must be able to verify. However, current fixed judges constrain both optimization feedback and the discovery of useful training examples, limiting further self-improvement. This challenge is even more acute in embodied reasoning, where reliable evaluation must account for spatial grounding, causal reasoning, and safety-aware decision-making. We introduce VeriFine, an agent harness framework that scales verification through the co-evolution of the policy, training curriculum, and judge. The Policy Improvement Loop uses a rubric judge to diagnose recurring failures, construct an adaptive curriculum, and optimize the policy. When progress plateaus and verification becomes a bottleneck, the Judge Improvement Loop selectively queries human guidance on informative failure cases and refines the judge through coactive calibration, in which humans and agents resolve disagreements and converge toward the objective rubric of physical reasoning. The revised judge then guides the next stage of data selection and policy optimization. Experiments on driving and robot navigation tasks demonstrate continuous self-improvement in both policy and judge capability across reinforcement and supervised fine-tuning. These results show how scaling verification supports continuous self-improvement as policy failure patterns evolve.

</details>


### [381] [Agent in a Bottle: Can LLM Agents Turn Their Capabilities Into Cheap, Scalable Artifacts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08775)
*Ankit Sonthalia,Haritz Puerto,Alexander Rubinstein,Martin Gubri,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BOTTLED 基准，用于评估 LLM 智能体"装瓶"能力——即把通用能力转化为兼顾质量与摊薄成本的任务专用方案。实验发现零样本性能无法预测装瓶能力，但装瓶可带来显著成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽能解决许多窄领域任务，但对数百万个相关实例逐一查询代价过高。作者希望探究 LLM 智能体能否自主创造更廉价的解决方案，并将这种"将通用能力转化为任务专用方案"的能力称为"装瓶"（bottling），需要同时平衡答案质量与摊薄成本。

Method: 作者构建 BOTTLED 基准：智能体接收一整个无标注工作负载，需在固定的时间、算力与 LLM API 预算约束下完成；智能体可自主选择方案，如训练小模型或编写可复用程序。实验覆盖十个模型与三个任务；作者还设置了两个同 token 预算的小模型蒸馏基线，并与专为廉价重复推理设计的"系统一"模型 Jev 进行对比。

Result: (1) 强零样本性能无法可靠转化为强装瓶能力：零样本得分相近的模型在装瓶后差异显著；60 次装瓶运行中有 48 次低于其模型零样本性能 95% 置信区间下界。(2) 60 次运行中有 31 次劣于同 token 预算下两个小模型蒸馏基线中较强的那个。(3) 装瓶仍能带来可观节省：在查询-商品相关性分类上，Opus 5 保留约 82% 的零样本 macro-F1，而报告成本降低约 657 倍。(4) 装瓶与 Jev 具竞争力：同一任务上 Opus 5 恢复约 94% 的 Jev macro-F1，成本仅为 Jev 全工作负载预估成本的四分之一。

Conclusion: BOTTLED 为评估和改进智能体"在有限资源下为大规模重复性工作负载投资可复用方案"的能力提供了基础。装瓶能力独立于零样本能力，且可显著降低成本，值得作为单独的评价维度加以研究和优化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve many narrow tasks, but querying them separately for millions of related instances can be prohibitively expensive. Can LLM agents autonomously create cheaper solutions for such workloads? We call this ability "bottling": the ability to turn general capabilities into task-specific solutions that balance answer quality and amortised cost. We introduce BOTTLED, a benchmark in which agents receive an entire unlabelled workload and must complete it under fixed time, compute and LLM API budgets. Agents choose their own approach, such as training a small model or writing a reusable program. Across ten models and three tasks, we find that strong zero-shot task performance does not reliably translate into strong bottling capabilities. Models with similar zero-shot scores can differ substantially after bottling, and 48 of 60 bottling runs score below the lower bound of the 95% confidence interval of their model's zero-shot performance. Moreover, 31 of 60 runs underperform the stronger of two small-model distillation baselines with the same token budget. Nevertheless, bottling can yield substantial savings: on query-product relevance classification, Opus 5 retains about 82% of its zero-shot macro-F1 at roughly 657 times lower reported cost. Bottling is also competitive with Jev, a "system one" model built especially for cheap, repetitive inference: Opus 5 on the same task recovers about 94% of Jev's macro-F1 at a quarter of Jev's projected full-workload cost. BOTTLED provides a basis for evaluating and improving agents' ability to invest limited resources in reusable solutions for large, repetitive workloads.

</details>


### [382] [Sherpa: Teaching LLMs to Teach Adaptively](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08778)
*Weixian Xu,Yanzhe Zhang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Changyu Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Sherpa多轮强化学习框架，用大模型实例化多种学生原型并以其学习结果为直接奖励训练教师模型，使教师能自适应不同学生并显著提升教学效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽解题能力强，但"会解题"不等于"会教学"。现有教师模型训练依赖演示、偏好数据或预设教学标准，这些信号未与学生个体学习结果挂钩，而有效教学策略因学习者差异而显著不同。

Method: Sherpa是一个多轮强化学习框架：用LLM实例化多个具不同学习偏好的学生原型，教师模型在多轮对话中进行教学，直接以学生原型的学习结果（表现提升）作为奖励信号来优化教师策略，从而学会自适应教学。

Result: 经Sherpa训练的教师模型使所有学生原型表现平均提升20.5个百分点；在MathTutorBench上整体教学法分数从52.5%升至79.2%；人类研究中，训练后的教师在79.6%的成对比较中被优先选择。

Conclusion: Sherpa能以学习结果为奖励训练LLM教师适应多样化模拟学生，并更贴合人类教师偏好，为AI导师教学真实学生奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become increasingly capable problem solvers, but being able to solve a problem is not the same as being able to teach it. Existing approaches to training LLMs as teachers rely on demonstrations, preference data, or predefined pedagogical criteria that specify what good teaching looks like. However, these signals are often not grounded in individual student learning outcomes, where effective teaching strategies can vary substantially across learners. To address this, we introduce Sherpa, a multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that instantiates multiple student archetypes with LLMs conditioned on distinct learning preferences and trains a teacher model to adapt its instruction by directly maximizing their learning outcomes. Teacher LLMs trained with Sherpa improve instructed students' performance across all archetypes by an average of 20.5 percentage points. Under MathTutorBench's evaluation, Sherpa raises the overall pedagogy score from 52.5% to 79.2%, indicating better teaching responses. Our human studies show that the trained teacher is preferred over the base model in 79.6% of pairwise comparisons. Together, Sherpa trains LLM teachers to adapt to diverse simulated students and become better aligned with human teachers, paving the road towards AI tutors teaching real students.

</details>


### [383] [What Does Fréchet Distance Measure? A Directional Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05518)
*Yunghee Lee,Jaeyeon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出方向性Fréchet距离，将FID/FVD等指标分解为可解释的语义方向，揭示少量方向即可解释大部分分布差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fréchet距离（如图像的FID、视频的FVD）用单一标量概括生成分布与参考分布的差异，但这一标量视角掩盖了差异的具体来源，甚至出现指标与感知质量不一致的现象（如COCO中增加扩散采样步数提升ImageReward却增大FID）。

Method: 引入方向性Fréchet距离，即最优传输位移在给定方向上的期望平方投影；通过寻找贡献主要距离的少数可解释方向，并结合CLIP嵌入的语义概念进行分析。

Result: 在图像、视频与蛋白质三类案例中，发现少量可解释方向解释了大部分距离；据此解释了FID上升对应的语义概念，量化了FVD对逐帧外观的偏置，并重新审视了Protein FID的解释。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 方向性Fréchet距离提升了Fréchet距离的可解释性，为跨域生成模型的评估提供了更细粒度、更语义化的分析工具。

Abstract: The Fréchet distance is a de facto standard for evaluating generative models across domains, appearing as FID for images and FVD for videos. It summarizes the discrepancy between generated and reference distributions in a single scalar, with lower values typically interpreted as better generation quality. However, this scalar view can obscure what drives the comparison. For example, in COCO dataset, increasing the number of diffusion sampling steps improves ImageReward scores yet worsens (increases) FID. Motivated by this mismatch, we seek to make the Fréchet distance more interpretable by uncovering where the discrepancy lies. To this end, we introduce directional Fréchet distance, the expected squared projection of the optimal transport displacement onto a given direction. Across our image, video, and protein case studies, we find that a small number of interpretable directions account for much of the distance. We use these directions to explain the FID increase in terms of semantic concepts represented by CLIP embeddings, quantify FVD's bias toward per-frame appearance, and revisit the interpretation of Protein FID. We open-source our codebase at https://github.com/yhlee-add/directional-fd.

</details>
